# Servitor (remote control) P0 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A loopback Servitor CLI (`servitor`) can attach to a running Arcane editor, headless runtime, or dedicated server, call the P0 command set (including play/step), and get one JSON envelope back — the envelope, endpoints, and exit codes in the spec, not a private dialect.

**Architecture:** A new Runtime module, `ArcaneRemote` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/`, export macro `ARC_REMOTE_API`, namespace `Arcane::Remote`), owns the protocol, the command registry, a 127.0.0.1 HTTP listener, the descriptor, and the main-thread dispatcher. Each host arms that listener and drains the queue once per tick before it simulates. Frame-spanning work (screenshot, compare, wait, `play.step`) is a job: the command arms it and returns, and the dispatcher steps it once per later tick. A capture job copies into a per-frame-slot host buffer and completes when the render ticket that rode with that copy is published back from that slot, even if `host.frame` does not move; it does not call `DeviceWaitIdle`. The listener is one service thread running one `PollSockets` loop. The wire protocol (envelope, exit table, descriptor, redaction) is a second, small Runtime module, `ArcaneRemoteProtocol` (`ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API`, ArcaneCore only; rev 8.3 V1). `servitor.exe` (`Source/Programs/Servitor`) is a console app whose client code lives in the program itself, namespace `Servitor`, and links ArcaneCore, ArcaneNet and ArcaneRemoteProtocol only, so one Servitor talks to any host that speaks a protocol major it supports (spec AM9, rev 8.3). `Optional` and nested `$defs`/`$ref` come from Astra 1.0 lane step 6, which has NOT synced into this tree yet (see **Prerequisite**); this plan does not edit Astra. One capture or compare is in flight per host. A second call is `host.busy` with `detail.state` `capture-pending`, and the client retries that busy answer until `--timeout` (default 30 s; the flag is seconds and `ParseGlobals` stores milliseconds). Acceptance owns its child hosts through an RAII guard whose destructor kills every child. Schema `default` values are injected on the Arcane side by default-constructing the args struct and writing it with `ReflectionJsonWriter`. The settings-era `RemoteCVarService` and the editor's `DiagnosticStore` move into shapes every host can call.

**Tech Stack:** C++23, premake5 (descriptor-driven modules, `build/modules.lua`), MSBuild (VS 2026) on Windows and gmake on Linux/macOS, Catch2 v3, rapidcheck, nlohmann::json, Astra ECS, Win32 DACL / POSIX file modes, PowerShell.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-arc-cli-design.md`. Section 0 decisions 0.1–0.28 are accepted as recommended. Section 0.29 amendments AM1–AM9 are binding (AM7 is the 2026-10-13 naming decision: Servitor + ArcaneRemote; AM8 exit 9 and the engine root; AM9 the engine-version-independent client, rev 8.3). This plan is s13 P0 plus the "P0 additions (2026-10-09)" paragraph (play|stop|pause|resume|step, AM1–AM6, `ecs.archetypes`). It does not build P1–P4. Research `docs/research/2026-10-05-arc-cli-research.md` is background. Where this plan and the spec disagree on a wire field, an endpoint, or an exit code, **the spec wins**. Where they disagree on a line number or a type that has moved, the **Tree facts** below win — rev 8 re-read them off `feat/arc-cli-p0` in `D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc` (main `6a737609` plus docs). This is rev 8.3 of the plan (rev 8.3: the engine-independent client, spec AM8/AM9, change log below; rev 8.2: naming): rev 8 (`58775ae1`), a re-anchor of rev 7.1 (`a165d810` on this branch) onto the modular tree with the design unchanged, plus the pre-flight rulings in the **Rev 8.1 (pre-flight) change log** below. Earlier revs (rev 1 was `657bd773`, rev 2 was `ca3d3f61`, rev 3 was `6d4da827`, rev 4 was `1da89547`, rev 5 was `a8add14b`, rev 6 was `cef4dd01`, rev 7 was `5535ff3c`) are commits on `feat/arc-cli`. Rev 2 replaced the flattened envelope, the `/events` route, the blocking step, the zero-fill Astra defaults, and the hard-coded ABI 55/56. Rev 3 makes capture an async readback, moves schema defaults onto Arcane's `ReflectionJsonWriter`, drives refusal tests through the real accept path, blesses acceptance into a scratch reference, and gives `--follow` one SSE lifetime. Rev 4 keys capture completion on the render ticket rather than `host.frame`, extracts the BGRA row normalization, makes the listener one poll loop, and writes the `$defs` emitter in full. Rev 5 refuses a second capture or compare while one is in flight with `host.busy` `capture-pending` (the client retries that busy answer), makes acceptance child hosts an RAII guard, and moves the Astra `Optional` attribute, nested `$defs`/`$ref`, and the Astra sync out to Astra 1.0 lane step 6. Rev 6 makes that overlap a real host test (capture B is refused while A is pending, then retried only after A's render ticket retires, and both PNGs are valid), retries busy answers until `--timeout` (default 30 s) rather than twice, and keeps an Arcane `$defs` emitter only as Task 4's fallback when Astra's `Optional` attribute is absent. Rev 7 (Grok review AX1–AX6) makes `--timeout` seconds (`ParseGlobals` stores milliseconds, and acceptance argv stays in seconds), defines the client's hung/died exit 7 path (`--hang-main 60`, a 250 ms liveness and heartbeat watch, the newest `.arcdiag` written after the call started), asserts explain history as Console / 45 / empty module, adds the ServerAdmin sites at `RemoteCVarService.cpp:75` and `:161` plus an Editor-context explain of a non-protected Editor-audience cvar, keeps one `operator|` in `Arcane` and re-exports it from the editor header, and deletes the `CVarType::Quat` arm. Rev 7.1 (Codex verify AV1–AV3) threads an explicit `CVarContext context` through `RemoteCVarService`'s `ReadValue`, `List`, `RunCommand`, and `Audit` (header declarations, definitions, and every call site; `Handle` passes `request.context`; no helper reads a `ServerAdmin` literal), matches the hang acceptance to its own child (the status row whose `pid` equals `pi.dwProcessId`, then the call passes `--pid` of that pid), and makes `ParseGlobals` start from a default-initialized `GlobalOptions` (`timeoutMs` 30000) so an omitted flag restores the default, with the omitted-timeout test using a fresh object.

## Rev 8.3 (engine-independent client) change log

User decision, 2026-10-14 (spec s0.29 **AM9**; exit 9 and the engine root are **AM8**). Servitor's code does not depend on the engine build it ships beside. In Hub v1 it still ships per engine; this revision is about the code boundary, not distribution. Ids are unchanged. These rulings override any older text they contradict, including text inside a task's code blocks.

- **V1 Protocol module (new Task 31, executes after Task 6 and before Task 7).** A new Runtime module, `ArcaneRemoteProtocol` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemoteProtocol/`, `ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API`, namespace `Arcane::Remote`), depends on ArcaneCore and the third-party `nlohmann` only.
  - It takes `Protocol.hpp/.cpp` (Task 3) by `git mv`, keeping the include spelling `<Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>`, and `RedactSecrets` (out of Task 5's `Http.hpp/.cpp`, into `<Arcane/Remote/Redact.hpp>`). Task 7 creates `Descriptor.hpp/.cpp` there.
  - ArcaneRemote gains it as a PUBLIC dependency, so every existing include keeps compiling.
  - Everything Task 3's Produces block and Task 7's Produces block mark `ARC_REMOTE_API` becomes `ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API`. Everything else in ArcaneRemote keeps `ARC_REMOTE_API`.
- **V2 Process identity in ArcaneCore (Task 7).** `ARC_CORE_API bool Platform::IsProcessLive(std::uint32_t pid, std::uint64_t start)` in `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Process.hpp` holds the tells formerly planned for `EditorLock::IsLive`, both platforms. `EditorLock::IsLive(info)` is one line, `return Platform::IsProcessLive(info.pid, info.start);`. `IsStale` calls `Platform::IsProcessLive`. ArcaneRemoteProtocol never includes `<Arcane/Project/Project.hpp>`.
- **V3 The client is a program, not module code.** Everything the plan used to put in `Arcane::Remote::Cli` (Format, Client, Verbs, Discover, Launch, Commands, FanOut):
  - lives in `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/`, namespace `Servitor`, included as `<Servitor/X.hpp>`;
  - compiles into `servitor.exe` and, from source, into ArcaneTests, exactly as `arcbuild`'s sources are (`premake5.lua` ArcaneTests `files` list near `:1533`, include dir near `:1771`).
  - Task 14 gives both projects the glob `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/**.cpp` (`main.cpp` sits outside that folder and is the exe's alone), so a later task's new client `.cpp` needs no premake edit. It only re-runs generate.
  - Client code that names protocol types qualifies them (`Arcane::Remote::Envelope`) or opens `using namespace Arcane::Remote;` inside a `.cpp`, never in a header.
  - The client never includes an ArcaneRemote host header: `Registry.hpp`, `Schema.hpp`, `Http.hpp`, `Listener.hpp`, `Dispatcher.hpp`, `Jobs.hpp`, `HostSession.hpp`, `Commands/*`, `Worlds.hpp`, `TickGate.hpp`, `Follow.hpp`, `FrameStats.hpp`. Request `args` are built as JSON by field name. Tests may include both sides.
- **V4 No export macro on client code.** A `Servitor::` declaration carries no `ARC_*_API`. Where an older line still shows `ARC_REMOTE_API` on a `Servitor::` declaration, drop it. Ruling F32 applies to ArcaneRemote and ArcaneRemoteProtocol symbols only.
- **V5 Servitor's closure (Task 14).** The `Servitor` premake project is `arc_module_consumer(engine_modules, { "ArcaneCore", "ArcaneNet", "ArcaneRemoteProtocol" })`. That supersedes ruling C-2: `servitor.exe` no longer loads ArcaneEngine.
  - Task 14 adds the boundary test `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/ServitorBoundaryTest.cpp` (`[servitor][boundary]`). It lists the staged files in the sibling `../Servitor/` directory of the ArcaneTests exe and REQUIRES that the directory exists and holds `servitor` (`.exe` on Windows).
  - It also REQUIRES that no file there starts with `ArcaneRemote.`, `ArcaneEngine`, `ArcaneScene`, `ArcaneSim`, `ArcaneAssets`, `ArcaneCrypto`, `ArcaneClient`, `ArcaneRender`, `ArcaneEditor` or `ArcaneVerify`, with each platform's shared-library prefix and suffix stripped.
  - Task 31 adds the descriptor guard `[remote][protocol]`: `ArcaneRemoteProtocol.arcmodule` names `ArcaneCore` as its only module dependency.
- **V6 Versions and compatibility (AM9).**
  - **Protocol constants.** `kProtocolMinor = 0` sits beside `kProtocol` (`ARC_CONSTANT`). `Envelope` and `HostStatus` gain `std::uint32_t protocolMinor = kProtocolMinor`. `ToJson` writes `protocolMinor`. `TryParseEnvelope` reads it when present and sets it to 0 explicitly when absent (never the struct default, which follows `kProtocolMinor` after the first bump) and ignores unknown keys at the top level, in `host` and in `data`.
  - **remote-protocol.json.** `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemoteProtocol/remote-protocol.json` is `{ "major": 1, "minor": 0 }`. Task 31's test pins it to both constants. The Hub and phase 1's `sdk.json` (`remoteProtocol`) read that file; nothing else spells the numbers.
  - **Request keys.** The listener (Task 6) ignores unknown keys inside `options`. Unknown top-level keys and unknown `args` keys stay refusals.
  - **Client constants.**
    - `Servitor::kVersion` is `"0.1.0"`, `ARC_CONSTANT`.
    - `Servitor::kProtocolMajors` is `{1}`. A reply whose `protocol` is not in it is `protocol.mismatch` (exit 4), with remediation naming both sides.
    - A `command.unknown` reply keeps exit 2. The client appends the host's `protocol.protocolMinor` to the remediation.
  - **Version output.** `servitor --version` prints `Servitor <kVersion> (remote protocol <kProtocolMajors, comma-joined>)`. `Run` handles `--version` before `ParseGlobals` and verb matching, so it never reaches the unknown-flag refusal. Task 14's version case also calls `Servitor::Run` with `{"servitor", "--version"}` and requires 0. That run writes to stdout once, so render the text through a `VersionText()` function and assert that instead of capturing stdout (the F31 rule).
  - **Remediation text.** The `command.unknown` remediation names both the command and the host's `major.minor`; the test checks for both.
  - **Markers.** `ConstantScanRoots()` does not scan `Source/Programs/Servitor`, so the client's `ARC_CONSTANT` markers are documentation there, not a guard.
- **V7 Engine root (AM8; Tasks 14-16).**
  - `GlobalOptions` gains `std::string engine;` (`--engine <root>`; a missing value is exit 2).
  - Task 15 adds `std::optional<std::filesystem::path> HostsDir(const GlobalOptions& opt, Arcane::Remote::Envelope* error)`, the directory holding `ArcaneEditor/`, `ArcaneRuntime/` and `ArcaneServer/`. First match wins:
    1. `opt.engine`;
    2. `SERVITOR_ENGINE`;
    3. the source sibling `ExecutablePath().parent_path().parent_path()`, when it holds any of the three folders;
    4. the package root `ExecutablePath().parent_path().parent_path().parent_path()` (the exe is `<root>/Packages/servitor/servitor`), when `sdk/sdk.json` exists under it.
  - A root given by 1 or 2 maps like this:
    - a root with `sdk/sdk.json` maps to `<root>/Binaries/<engineConfig>-<os>-<arch>-md/`, with `engineConfig` read from that file;
    - a root without it is a source checkout and maps to `<root>/Binaries/<Servitor's own outputdir>/`, from the define `SERVITOR_OUTPUTDIR`, which the `Servitor` and ArcaneTests projects set from `outputdir`.
  - A package `sdk.json` without `engineConfig`, or no match at all, is `engine.unresolved` (exit 4) with remediation `pass --engine <root> or set SERVITOR_ENGINE`.
  - `HostDiagnosticsDir`, `HostLogFile` and `servitor launch` take their exe-relative paths from `HostsDir`, never from a hard-coded `parent_path().parent_path()`.
  - Tests use temp trees: the flag beats the env var; the env var beats the sibling; a package tree with `sdk/sdk.json` `{"engineConfig":"Release"}`; a package tree missing `engineConfig` gives exit 4. Tests set and restore the env var through an RAII guard.
- **V8 Static manifest (Task 17; AM8).** `StaticManifest()` returns an object, not an array:
  ```
  {version, protocolMajors, verbs:[{name, summary, access}],
   exitCodes:[{code, meaning, launcherOnly}]}
  ```
  - `exitCodes` lists 0-8, 9 and 130/143. Code 9 is `launcherOnly: true` and names `engine.not-found`, `package.not-installed` and `package.incompatible`. Servitor itself never returns 9.
  - The Task 17 test reads `manifest["verbs"]` where it read the array, and also checks the code-9 row.

## Rev 8.2 (naming) change log

User decision, 2026-10-13 (spec s0.29 **AM7**; binding mapping in `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-09-arc-cli-p0/RENAME-SERVITOR.md`). `arc` / `ARC_` is reserved for engine macros and backstage tools (`arcbuild`, `arccook`), so the tool and its engine side are renamed throughout this plan. Ids (tasks, rulings F/S3/R8/A-G) are unchanged; the plan file name and the `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-09-arc-cli-p0/` workspace keep their paths.

- **Client:** the tool is **Servitor**: premake project `"Servitor"`, `location "Source/Programs/Servitor"`, `targetname "servitor"`, output `Binaries/<cfg>/Servitor/servitor[.exe]`; prose `servitor status`, `servitor screenshot`, ...; P2 scenarios are `.servitor`; client Catch2 cases are `[servitor][client]`.
- **Engine side (generic names):** module `ArcaneRemote` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/`, `ArcaneRemote.arcmodule`, `ARC_REMOTE_API`, `ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp`, `ARC_MODULE(ArcaneRemote)`), namespace `Arcane::Remote` (the client is namespace `Servitor`, rev 8.3 V3), headers `<Arcane/Remote/...>`, tests `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/` with `RemoteTestSupport.hpp` / `RemoteTestHttp.hpp` / namespace `RemoteTest` and tags `[remote]...`; wire `/remote/v1/...`, headers `X-Remote-*`; host flags `--remote`, `--remote-descriptor`, `--remote-idle*`; descriptor dir `<project>/Saved/Remote/`; cvars `editor.remote.enable`, `remote.server.frameBudgetMs`, `remote.server.queueDepth`, `log.remote.ringLines`; env var `ARC_REMOTE_READ_ONLY`; `HostConfig`/`ServerConfig` fields `remote*`; error/diagnostic codes `remote.*`; `Arc*`-prefixed test helpers become `Remote*` (`RunArc` is `RunServitor`).
- **Done tasks:** Tasks 0-5 were renamed in place by one `refactor(remote)` commit (`git mv`, so history follows); the commit lines in those tasks now read as the scopes later tasks use (`feat(remote)` / `feat(servitor)`).
- **Unchanged:** `ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND`, every other `ARC_*` macro, `.arcproj` / `.arcmodule` / `.arcdiag` and the other engine extensions.

- **F1** Task 9 executes before Task 8 (both headings and Global Constraints say so); `Jobs.hpp` forward-declares `HostGate`; Task 9 creates the `HostGate` half of `Dispatcher.hpp/.cpp`, which includes `Jobs.hpp`. **F2** `Drain` calls `BindWaiter(JobId, id, undo)`, the `RemoteDispatcher` constructor calls `SetQueue`, and `StepAll` stamps `gate.Block()` plus the bound undo block and Completes the waiter; new dispatcher and job-table tests.
- **F3** `Log::LatestSeq()`; Task 2's test anchors on it. **F4** a follow job starts at the newest seq; the SSE test loops `RecvUntil`. **F5** `ApplyFollowEvents(JobTable&, RemoteListener&)`. **F6** `capture.no-frame` and any other non-empty host code exit 6. **F7** Task 4 uses `editor.nosuch.get`. **F8** `scene.tree` answers `command.host-kind` on a server, with a test.
- **F9** `const HostGate& Gate() const`, `GetConfig()`, `WaitPoll(..., started)`. **F10** `NoteReload(generation, ok, reason, frame)`. **F11** every `Register*`/`Set*` is safe twice (a later call replaces the pointer); `SetPlayGate` in the Task 29 cases. **F12** Tasks 16, 17, 19-29 wire their registrations into `HostSession::Start` and their hooks into the host program files, listed in Files and `git add`. **F13** `catalog.describe` / `schema.type` (Task 17). **F14** `--world` / `GlobalOptions::world` / `options.world` (Task 19).
- **F15** the gate refuses Drive under `gate.readOnly || call.readOnly`; `Call` posts `options.readOnly`. **F16** one `SetCommandWorlds(const WorldTable&)`; every world-taking command calls `ResolveWorld(CommandWorlds(), call.world)`; `SetEcsWorld`/`SetResourceWorld`/`SetSceneWorld` are gone. **F17** the verb table is ArcaneRemote `Cli/Verbs.cpp` (Task 14); `main.cpp` stays one line. **F18** `cvar.set` is get, set, explain through `Handle`; the explain case runs `cvar.explain`. **F19** `Desc::accessFor`; `capture.compare --bless` is Drive; a read-only bless refuses. **F20** the editor's host capture pair is its viewport pair.
- **F21** Task 30 verifies `render.vsync` on the server and otherwise probes a Core/Engine cvar. **F22** live server `--frames 1200` case with code. **F23** whole-transcript token check. **F24** every Catch2 filter is a tag or ends with `*`. **F25** Review Focus #5 names real cases. **F26** Task 13 Step 2 is a regression pin. **F27** `Descriptor::abi`. **F28** both `HostLogFile` branches tested. **F29** Task 6 log test: include, mutex, read after `Stop`, forged bearer checked. **F30** `AnyRunning({"play.step"})`, `renders` deleted. **F31** `RenderStaticManifest` declared and asserted; the unit test never prints. **F32** every setter/registrar a test calls is in Produces with `ARC_REMOTE_API`. **F33** every changed file is in Files and `git add`; no notes after a commit command. **F34** Task 30 issues the listed calls (`stop` exits 0, `stats frame` unchanged) and holds a `DescriptorSweep`.
- **S3** (1) vacuous assertions: F22, F23, F29, F30. (2) Task 4's fallback asserts `REQUIRE` the `required` keys, no `if`. (3) Task 29's loop model stays as documentation; F22 is the proof. (4) Task 10's `DistSymbolTest` is deleted (the `dumpbin` check is the test); Task 14's redaction case drives `--verbose` and `--format human`; Task 20 per F18. (5) Task 0 adds `ARC_CORE_API const char* ToString(SetBy)` used by `CVarRegistry.cpp` and `RemoteCVarService.cpp`; the socket helper is written once, `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/RemoteTestHttp.hpp` (Task 6), included by Tasks 22 and 29. (6) F29. (7) F31. (8) Task 30 uses `file_clock::to_sys`, not `clock_cast`. (9) `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/RemoteTestSupport.hpp` (Task 4) holds `ScopedHostKind` and `ScopedRemotePointer`, used by every test that sets the host kind or hands a stateful command a pointer; a command whose stored pointer is null answers `host.unavailable` (exit 5, added to Task 3's table); `HostSession::Stop` restores the host kind.

## Rev 8 change log

- **Re-anchor.** Every path, line anchor and symbol was re-read off `feat/arc-cli-p0` (main `6a737609`): the namespace facades, the flat API, `Source/<Type>/<Module>/` module DLLs, `Binaries/` + `Intermediate/Build/`, ABI 59 in `Core/AbiVersion.hpp`. Anchors that could not be verified became symbol references. The design is unchanged.
- **ArcaneRemote module.** Everything in `Arcane::Remote` moves out of ArcaneCore into a new Runtime module, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/` (`ARC_REMOTE_API`), created by Task 3 with its full dependency set. It never depends on a Client, Developer or Editor module; render capture, reference images, settle/shader-idle and the editor selection reach it through host function pointers or register from the editor program.
- **Platform work.** All new code compiles on Windows, Linux and macOS (P0 is functional on Windows): `poll(2)` and `SIGPIPE` suppression in the listener, `0600` descriptors, `EditorLock::IsLive` with both platform branches, `posix_spawn` for `servitor launch`, `file_clock::to_sys`/`from_sys`, portable tests, and a Windows-only acceptance file.
- **Rulings applied** (controller, rev 8):
  - A-1 / G-1: Aphelyon is not restamped by Tasks 0, 10 or 30; Task 30 checks the header against ReferenceProject only.
  - A-2 / D-1: packages are Dist monolithic, so the remote surface is compiled out of them; ArcaneRuntimeHost takes ArcaneRemote in the modular layout only; no `build/arcane.lua` edit.
  - A-3: `DiagnosticStore` moves to ArcaneCore, `<Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp>`.
  - B-1: cvar-default constants keep `ARC_CONSTANT` markers; Task 8 also registers `remote.server.queueDepth`.
  - B-2: Task 3 Step 3 creates the module scaffold.
  - C-1: ArcaneNet is a public dependency of ArcaneRemote (`sockaddr_in` in `Listener.hpp`).
  - C-2: `servitor.exe` loads ArcaneEngine, the same as `arcbuild`. **Superseded by rev 8.3 V5:** it links ArcaneCore, ArcaneNet and ArcaneRemoteProtocol only.
  - C-3: each new cvar gets a frozen-names line and inventory rows in the task that adds it (precedent `92c0e8fd`).
  - C-4: dispatcher tests write under a per-test temp subdirectory.
  - D-2: every Remote test file is wrapped in `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`.
  - D-3: read-only arming is the `ARC_REMOTE_READ_ONLY` environment variable only; no host `--read-only` token.
  - E-1: `console.exec` refuses a command without `ServerCanExecute` with `permission.denied` (exit 3), pinned by a test.
  - E-2: `Launch.cpp` has a `CreateProcessW` arm and a `posix_spawn` arm; the child's stdout/stderr go to a log file beside the host exe.
  - E-3: `Servitor::ReadLogFile` is declared in `Servitor/Client.hpp`; the dead host's log is `<exeDir>/Logs/<appName>.log`.
  - E-4: `EditorLock::IsLive` extracts both the Windows and the POSIX branches of `ReadLive`.
  - F-1: the registry answers `command.host-kind` for an `editor.`-prefixed name on a non-editor host.
  - F-2: one capture ring per graph context; one capture in flight per host stays enforced by `CaptureInFlight`.
  - G-2: the editor bless/compare hook calls the adapter-set `ResolveReference` overload.
  - G gaps: `PlayHooks::editorPlay` takes the topology (Task 29); `ResolveFn` returns `triedPaths` (Task 28); server `--frames N` counts sim advances under a remote pause (Task 29).
  - Also: `NormalizeCaptureRows` lives in ArcaneAssets `ImageIo.hpp`; `Sha256Hex` lives in ArcaneCrypto `Crypto.hpp`; `EditorLock::IsLive` is `ARC_ENGINE_API`; the JSON include is `<Json.hpp>`; `HostSession::Config` reserves the Task 16/19 fields; Task 14 wires `servitor stats frame`; the AX(2) `.arcdiag` location is `Paths::Location::DiagnosticsDir`.

**Rev 8 refresh (2026-10-13).** Rev 7.1 was written against Arcane `7e6f8881`. Since then the tree gained the namespace facades, the flat API (ABI 57), the Linux and macOS port, engine modularization (module DLLs under `Source/<Type>/<Module>/`), and the `bin/` -> `Binaries/` rename. Rev 8 re-anchors every path, line and symbol; it does not redesign. R8-1 to R8-8 below are binding Global Constraints. Spec s0 picks, AM1-AM6 and rulings P1-P4/AX/AV stand (R8-9). Every point the refresh raised for the controller is ruled, and the ruled text is written into the task it affects (change log above).

- **R8-1 Module home.** Everything this plan puts in `Arcane::Remote` lives in a NEW Runtime module `ArcaneRemote` at `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/`: the protocol/envelope/exit table, command registry, HTTP parser, listener, descriptor, dispatcher, job table, and the Runtime-only commands. **Rev 8.3:** the protocol/envelope/exit table, the descriptor and `RedactSecrets` live in the separate Runtime module `ArcaneRemoteProtocol` (V1; ArcaneCore only, a public dependency of ArcaneRemote), and the client lives in the Servitor program, namespace `Servitor` (V3).
  - Descriptor `ArcaneRemote.arcmodule`, type `Runtime`, shaped like `Source/Runtime/ArcaneNet/ArcaneNet.arcmodule`. `build/modules.lua` derives the macro `ARC_REMOTE_API` and the define `ARC_REMOTE_EXPORTS` from the name (`suffix` at `:95`; the define at `:378`).
  - Export header `src/Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp`, a copy of `Source/Runtime/ArcaneNet/src/Arcane/Modules/ArcaneNetApi.hpp` (`ARC_MONOLITHIC` / `ARC_REMOTE_STATIC` -> empty; `ARC_REMOTE_EXPORTS` -> `ARC_DLL_EXPORT`; else `ARC_DLL_IMPORT`).
  - Registration TU `src/Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteModule.cpp`, the three-line shape of `Source/Runtime/ArcaneAssets/src/Arcane/Modules/ArcaneAssetsModule.cpp` (`#include <Arcane/Core/ModuleAttach.hpp>`, `#include <Arcane/Core/ModuleApi.hpp>`, `ARC_MODULE(ArcaneRemote)`). It stays OUTSIDE the Dist guard: every consumer force-references `ArcModule_ArcaneRemote` (`build/modules.lua:643-652`), so the symbol must exist in Dist too.
  - Dependencies (ruling C-1), declared once, in full, by Task 3: public `ArcaneCore` and `ArcaneNet` (`Listener.hpp` names `sockaddr_in`); private `ArcaneEngine` (`EditorLock::IsLive`), `ArcaneCrypto` (tokens, `Sha256Hex`), `ArcaneScene` (`ReflectionJsonWriter`, `Identity`, `Transform`), `ArcaneSim` (ECS and play) and `ArcaneAssets` (`ImageIo`, `CompareImages`); third-party public `nlohmann` and `Astra`. Every one is a Runtime module. `allowed_edges` (`build/modules.lua:87-93`, enforced at `:215`) refuses any Runtime -> non-Runtime edge at generate time. **Cycle guard:** no existing module gains a dependency on ArcaneRemote. ArcaneEngine, ArcaneScene, ArcaneSim, ArcaneAssets, ArcaneCrypto and ArcaneNet stay below it (their descriptors name none of ArcaneRemote today, and no task adds it); only programs (`Servitor`, ArcaneServer, ArcaneRuntime, ArcaneRuntimeHost, ArcaneEditor) and ArcaneTests consume it.
  - Edits to EXISTING types go to the module that owns the type today (paths in **Tree facts** and the File map): `RemoteCVarService`, `CVarFormat`, `Log`, `Diagnostics` -> ArcaneCore; `Crypto::Sha256Hex` -> ArcaneCrypto; `EditorLock::IsLive` -> ArcaneEngine (`ARC_ENGINE_API`); `TcpSocket` -> ArcaneNet; `ImageIo` (`NormalizeCaptureRows`) -> ArcaneAssets; `HostConfig` -> ArcaneClient; `ServerConfig` -> the ArcaneServer program. `DiagnosticStore` (today the ArcaneEditor program's) moves to ArcaneCore beside `Diagnostics`, `<Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp>` (ruling A-3, Task 1), so every host can own one.
- **R8-2 Isolation.** ArcaneRemote never depends on a Client, Developer or Editor module; ArcaneServer imports it, and `scripts/ci/package.{sh,ps1}` on `feat/modularization-s6` fails a server that imports one. Render capture (the readback hook) stays in the Client module that owns that code today (`NriGraphContext` and `PickNode` are ArcaneRender) and reaches ArcaneRemote through the host function pointers this plan already describes. `ReferenceImages` is ArcaneVerify (Developer): compare reaches it the same way. Editor-only commands (`editor.selection.get`, editor-side `DiagnosticStore` use) register from the ArcaneEditor program, which owns `SelectionContext` and the editor's store today.
- **R8-3 Consumers.** Hosts and tests take modules through `arc_module_consumer(...)` in `premake5.lua`. ArcaneServer (`:887`) and ArcaneRuntime (`:970`) add `"ArcaneRemote"` to their lists; ArcaneRuntimeHost (`:445-447`, where `RuntimeApp.cpp` lives) adds it to its MODULAR branch only, `ARC_MONOLITHIC_LAYOUT and { "ArcaneImGui" } or { "ArcaneVerify", "ArcaneImGui", "ArcaneRemote" }` (ruling D-1, see **Dist**). ArcaneEditor (`:1096`) and ArcaneTests (`:1229`) consume `engine_modules.order`, which already includes every descriptor under `Source/`, so they need no edit. A linked module is a loaded module (forced `/INCLUDE:ArcModule_<Name>`, `-Wl,-u,...` off Windows); nothing beyond the module TU is needed.
- **R8-4 servitor.exe.** premake project `"Servitor"`, `location "Source/Programs/Servitor"`, `targetname "servitor"`, a copy of the current `arcbuild` project (`premake5.lua:578-659`): ConsoleApp, C++23, `staticruntime "off"`, `targetdir ("Binaries/" .. outputdir .. "/%{prj.name}")`, `objdir ("Intermediate/Build/" .. outputdir .. "/%{prj.name}")`, `arc_module_consumer(engine_modules, { "ArcaneCore", "ArcaneNet", "ArcaneRemoteProtocol" })` (rev 8.3 V5; the closure staged beside the exe is those modules and their third-party libraries, never ArcaneEngine), `files` the client sources under `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/` (V3), `includedirs` `Source/Programs/Servitor/src`, and `defines { "SERVITOR_OUTPUTDIR=\"" .. outputdir .. "\"" }` (V7). Output `Binaries/<Config>-<os>-<arch>-md/Servitor/servitor` (`servitor.exe` on Windows; `outputdir` is `premake5.lua:238-239`). Hosts resolve through `HostsDir` (V7: `--engine`, `SERVITOR_ENGINE`, the sibling `../ArcaneEditor`, `../ArcaneRuntime`, `../ArcaneServer`, then the package layout). Never on `PATH` in P0 (the Hub's launcher is the distribution lane's).
- **R8-5 ABI.** Today 59 (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Core/AbiVersion.hpp:10`). Expected: Task 0 -> 60, Task 10 -> 61, always re-read from `main` at execution time (see **ABI**).
- **R8-6 Layout.** `bin/` is `Binaries/`, `bin-int/` is `Intermediate/Build/`. Generate, build, test, ReferenceProject and golden-gate commands are the ones in this section; other slices say "build per Global Constraints".
- **R8-7 Platforms.** CI builds Windows, Linux and macOS on every PR, so every new TU compiles on all three; P0 must be functional on Windows. Each Win32-only primitive names its POSIX counterpart or an explicit `#if` refusal/stub. Reuse what the tree has (see **Platforms** below). A test that cannot run off Windows gets a `<exact test case name> | <reason>` line in `scripts/linux-test-exclusions.txt` and `scripts/macos-test-exclusions.txt` (the runner rejects a line without a reason).
- **R8-8 Output channels.** Engine code logs through the `ARC_*` macros (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Log.hpp:170-175`, i.e. `Log::Engine()`), never `Arcane::Logger::Get` (`Arcane/Util/Logger.hpp`, header-only, one registry per image). The engine logger writes to stderr (`Log.cpp:227-233`); stdout is a data channel. `servitor.exe` prints envelopes on stdout and nothing else there.

## Global Constraints

- **Task order (ruling F1, rev 8.3 V1).** Tasks execute in number order, except that **Task 31 executes after Task 6 and before Task 7**, and **Task 9 executes before Task 8**: Task 8's dispatcher holds a `JobTable&` and its tests arm jobs, so Task 9 (which also creates the `HostGate` half of `Dispatcher.hpp/.cpp`) lands first. The task numbers are unchanged; the order is 0-6, 31, 7, 9, 8, 10-30 (**Task 31**, the protocol split of rev 8.3 V1, executes after Task 6 and before Task 7).
- **Shared test helpers (rulings S3-5, S3-9).** `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/RemoteTestSupport.hpp` (Task 4: `RemoteTest::ScopedHostKind`, `RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<T>`) and `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/RemoteTestHttp.hpp` (Task 6: `RemoteTest::ConnectLoopback`, `SendAll`, `RecvUntil`, `HttpExchangeHost`, `HttpExchange`) are the only copies. A test that sets the process host kind, starts a `HostSession`, or hands a process-global command a pointer to a stack object holds a guard from `RemoteTestSupport.hpp`; a test that talks to a listener uses `RemoteTestHttp.hpp` and defines no socket helper of its own. A command whose stored pointer is null answers `host.unavailable` (exit 5) and never dereferences it. Every `Register*(ptr)` / `Set*(ptr)` is safe to call twice; a later call replaces the stored pointer (ruling F11).
- **Host wiring (ruling F12).** `HostSession::Start` is where a host's remote commands register. Each task that adds a command or a host hook wires it there (and in the host program file) in its own commit; Task 30 checks the wiring end to end and does not discover it.
- **Scope.** P0 only. Do not build the action registry (0.25, settings S4, P1), undo or entity writes (0.15, P1), `editor.selection.set` (P1), game-module hot-reload of commands (the registry *mechanism* is P0; loading them from `HotReloadPlugin` is P1), ImGui RemoteUi (P2), `servitor run` / `.servitor` (P2), public `servitor job` / `--detach` / `build` / `test` (P3), the `github` format (P3 — `--format github` is refused, not ignored), MCP, the Servitor shell (P4), or `POST /remote/v1/batch` (P1, s8.3). `net.*` is reserved (AM3) and is not implemented; `ecs.archetypes` is. Internal jobs exist from P0 (s7.7).
- **Protocol (spec s6, binding).** One remote envelope, not JSON-RPC. Protocol major `1`, minor `0` (`protocolMinor` on the envelope and `GET /status`, absent reads 0; rev 8.3 V6). Endpoints, exactly s6.1: `GET /remote/v1/status` (listener thread, every state), `POST /remote/v1/call` (main), `GET /remote/v1/stream?topics=...` (SSE), `GET /remote/v1/catalog` (listener snapshot). There is no `/events` route. The request object is s6.2: `{id, command, args, options:{world, readOnly, timeoutMs}}`. The field name is `command`, not `name`. The response body is the s6.3 envelope: `ok`, `protocol`, `id`, `command`, `host` `{kind, pid, world, frame, state, catalog, pluginGeneration}`, `data`, `errors`, `warnings`, `notifications`, `artifacts`, `undo`, `timing` `{queuedMs, ranMs}`. `ok:true` never coexists with a non-empty `errors`. The client adds `client` `{exitCode, retried, descriptor}` only when it prints. Bearer token on every endpoint, including `GET /status`. Loopback only.
- **Exit table (s6.6 + AM1 + AM6).** 0 ok (including compare verdicts Passed / PassedOnFallback / Skipped). 1 internal error in `servitor` itself (`internal`). 2 usage/refusal: unknown verb, flag, or argument; ambiguous target; schema failure; **and `capture.no-renderer` (AM1)**. 3 auth or permission: `auth.denied`, `auth.throttled`, `permission.denied`, `permission.read-only`, `cvar.protected`. 4 no host: nothing to attach to, a stale descriptor, a protocol-major mismatch, or `engine.unresolved` (rev 8.3 V7). 5 `host.busy`, provably not run, retryable — every `host.busy` state, including minimized, queue-full, and capture-pending (AM6). 6 the host answered and the operation failed (`entity.not-found`, `compare.missing-reference`, and the other ran-and-failed codes). 7 unreachable, died, or restarting. 8 a check ran and did not pass (`compare.failed` / verdict `Failed`, `wait` timeout) with `ok:true` and empty `errors`. 9 is reserved for the Hub's launcher and never returned by `servitor` (AM8). 130/143 signals. Worst-exit rank (AM2): `7 > 6 > 8 > 5 > 4 > 3 > 2 > 1 > 0`.
- **Nothing blocks the main thread (0.12).** A command that must wait for a frame, a tick, a settle, or a reload arms a job (s7.7) and returns. The dispatcher steps each running job **once** per `Drain`. `play.step N` does not call `Advance` N times inside one `Drain`. A capture or compare job does not call `NriGraphContext::ReadCapture` and does not call `DeviceWaitIdle`. It copies into one slot of a host buffer. It completes when the readback ticket stored with that copy is published (ruling P3 (a), below), not when `host.frame` advances.
- **Controller ruling P2 (binding).** (a) Capture reuses the PickNode async-readback pattern (`Source/Client/ArcaneRender/src/Arcane/Render/Nri/nodes/PickOutlineNodes.cpp` `RecordReadback` at 617, the drain-first comment at 628–635, the `memcpy` at 639–641): copy into a per-frame-slot host buffer and read that slot when the render ring reuses it; no fence query, no `DeviceWaitIdle`. The job does not complete on a sim frame. Ruling P3 (a) is the completion rule. (b) Schema defaults are injected on the Arcane side. Astra's change is only a correct `required` list (the `Optional` attribute) plus nested `$defs`/`$ref`. Remote's schema adapter default-constructs the args struct and writes it with `Arcane::ReflectionJsonWriter` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneScene/src/Arcane/Serialization/ReflectionJson.hpp:401`, constructor `:415`, `Visit` `:419`; ArcaneScene is therefore a private dependency of ArcaneRemote), then sets `"default"` per property. (c) The refusal tests drive the real listener accept path (a peer-check seam on the accepted socket's address, or a test listener whose peer predicate is injected) and send a forged `Host` header; each fails if its check is removed. (d) `SanitizeRelaunchLine` is called as `std::span<const std::string>` (`Source/Client/ArcaneClient/src/Arcane/Host/HostConfig.hpp:436`), with complete test code. (e) Acceptance compare blesses into a scratch reference, then requires exit 0 against it and exit 8 against a poisoned copy. There is no accepted-failure branch. The `crash-gpu` arm is gated like G1 (`ARCANE_DIAG_DESK`, `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/CrashWitnessTest.cpp:124`). (f) One follow path: the SSE `/stream` subscription owns the lifetime; the host-side follow job is keyed to that subscriber and cancelled when the stream closes; that is tested.
- **Controller ruling P4 (binding).** (a) ONE capture in flight per host: a capture/compare call arriving while one is pending answers host.busy{state:"capture-pending"} (exit 5); the client's existing automatic retry of provably-not-run busy answers covers it; test: two overlapping screenshot calls both succeed, and the second reports one busy retry. (b) the acceptance owns its child hosts through an RAII scope guard (destructor kills every child), so any REQUIRE abort cleans up; test the guard with a forced failure path. Task 27 owns the in-flight gate and the host overlap test: capture A is armed and not yet retired, capture B answers `host.busy` `capture-pending` and is not run, render frames advance until A's ticket retires, the retried B then succeeds, and both PNGs are valid. Task 28 applies the same gate to compare. Task 14's client retries exit 5 with backoff until `--timeout` (default 30 s, not a fixed two tries) and owns the mock-host test that stays busy for longer than 60 ms and then frees. Task 3 adds `capture-pending` to the exit-table examples. Task 30 owns the guard and the forced-failure test.
- **Controller ruling P3 (binding).** (a) capture completion is keyed to a RENDER submission / readback-slot ticket (the PickNode `m_ticket` pattern), never `host.frame` or a sim tick; tests: idle editor and idle headless runtime captures complete. (b) the capture hook reuses the existing normalization (row padding dropped, BGRA->RGBA) by calling or extracting the `NriGraphContext.cpp` ~2111-2132 code; test with a multi-colour, non-grey probe that would catch swapped channels. (c) the listener is one service thread running a non-blocking poll/select event loop with a per-connection state machine: `/status` is answered inline; a `/call` is parked until its main-thread result (or job completion) is published to the listener through a queue; SSE connections are written as frames arrive; the idle timeout applies per connection; the in-flight status test proves it. (d) The `$defs` emitter is written in full in Astra 1.0 lane step 6, which is not a task of this plan. Task 4's default path consumes that schema and does not contain an Arcane emitter. The Arcane emitter exists only as Task 4's fallback sub-task, and only when `struct Optional` is absent from `ThirdParty/Astra/include/Astra/Reflection/Attribute.hpp`. That sub-task's tests live there. Verified on this worktree (rev 8): `PickNode::RecordReadback` is `Source/Client/ArcaneRender/src/Arcane/Render/Nri/nodes/PickOutlineNodes.cpp:617`. The drain-first comment is `:628-635`. The `memcpy` is `:639-641`. `m_probeTicket = m_ticket[frameSlot]` is `:645`. `m_pending[frameSlot] = true` is `:689` and `m_ticket[frameSlot] = ticket` is `:692`. `layout.offset = frameSlot * m_readbackStride` is `:677`. The normalization loop is inline code inside `NriGraphContext::ReadCapture` (`:2081`) at `Source/Client/ArcaneRender/src/Arcane/Render/Nri/NriGraphContext.cpp:2111-2132` (`IsBgraFormat`, anonymous namespace, at `:79-82`; `DeviceWaitIdle` still at `:2101` on that cover path only). It is not a public function. `Source/Runtime/ArcaneNet/src/Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp` exports no poll and no `SetNonBlocking` (the `<fcntl.h>` comment at `:31` names `SetNonBlocking`, but no such function exists). `select` appears only inside `ConnectSocketWithTimeout` (`:276-396`, `select` at `:344`), which restores blocking mode before it returns (`:377-393`). Task 6 adds `SetNonBlocking` and `PollSockets` beside that function and tests them in `ListenerTest.cpp`.
- **Controller ruling AX (binding).** (1) `--timeout <s>` is seconds, decimal allowed (spec s4.6, line 361). `ParseGlobals` stores `timeoutMs = max(1, std::llround(s * 1000.0))`. `--timeout 2` is 2000, `--timeout 0.5` is 500, and an omitted flag stays 30000. Acceptance argv stays in seconds. Wire `options.timeoutMs` stays milliseconds. Nothing multiplies again on the way into `Call` or `WaitArgs`. (2) A hung or dead target is exit 7. The hang spawn is `--hang-main 60` (a bare flag stays 0 and never sleeps). While a call is pending, and before the body is sent, the client checks that pid's liveness and the descriptor heartbeat every 250 ms. Process gone is `host.died`. A heartbeat stale by the same `IsHung` rule (older than 10 s, which is under the 15 s hang) is `host.hung`. Both are exit 7. The envelope names the newest `.arcdiag` under the host's report directory, `Paths::Location::DiagnosticsDir` (`<project>/Saved/Diagnostics` when a project is open, otherwise `<exe dir>/diagnostics`; `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Paths.hpp:33`), written after this call started, when one exists (spec s4.7 line 370, s12.1 line 812). The acceptance test waits until `servitor status` reports that runtime hung, then calls, and requires exit 7. (3) The explain test asserts what `Explain` stores: `SetBy` `Console`, value 45, empty `module`. No caller field is added. (4) `RemoteCVarService.cpp:75` and `:161` are in the ServerAdmin list. An explain of a non-protected Editor-audience cvar from `CVarContext::Editor` succeeds. (5) One `operator|` lives in namespace `Arcane` beside `MaskFrom`. The editor header re-exports `MaskOf`, `MaskFrom`, `MatchesDiagnosticFilter`, and `operator|`. `EditorDiagnosticStoreTest.cpp` is not edited. (6) `CVarValueToJson` has no `CVarType::Quat` arm.
- **Controller ruling AV (binding).** (1) Thread an explicit `CVarContext context` parameter through `RemoteCVarService`'s `ReadValue`, `List`, `RunCommand`, and `Audit`: header declarations (`RemoteCVarService.hpp:67`, `:68`, `:69-70`, `:71-72`), definitions (`RemoteCVarService.cpp:60`, `:66-67`, `:81`, `:112-114`), and every call site. `Handle` (`:131`) passes `request.context`. No helper reads a `CVarContext::ServerAdmin` literal, and the new parameter has no default. (2) The hang acceptance matches its own child: find the status row whose `pid` equals `pi.dwProcessId`, and call with `--pid` of that pid. (3) `ParseGlobals` starts from a default-initialized `GlobalOptions` (`timeoutMs` 30000) so an omitted flag restores the default. The omitted-timeout test also uses a fresh object.
- **Units and build rules.** MKS. `/MD` (`staticruntime "off"`). No `/fp:fast`. UTF-8 without BOM. ASCII comments. Shaders stay data. Do not put `servitor` on `PATH` (P0 runs it from `Binaries/`).
- **Platforms (R8-7).** What the tree already has for each Win32 primitive this plan uses:
  - Non-blocking sockets: `ioctlsocket(FIONBIO)` on Windows, `fcntl(F_GETFL/F_SETFL, O_NONBLOCK)` on POSIX, both already inside `ConnectSocketWithTimeout` (`TcpSocket.hpp:295-309`). `SocketType`, `INVALID_SOCK` and `CloseSocket` are already per-platform (`:22-35`).
  - Polling: POSIX `select` is bounded by the fd VALUE (`FD_SETSIZE`, usually 1024), not by the connection count, so `PollSockets` uses `poll(2)` on POSIX; on Windows `select` (or `WSAPoll`) over at most the listener's planned `kMaxConnections` (16, Task 6) sockets stays under `FD_SETSIZE` (64).
  - Process liveness and identity (descriptor pid checks): `Platform::QueryProcess` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Process.hpp:55`; Linux `/proc/<pid>/stat`, macOS `proc_pidinfo`), exactly as `EditorLock::ReadLive`'s POSIX branch uses it (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneEngine/src/Arcane/Project/Project.cpp:878-889`).
  - Process spawn (client launch, acceptance): the only cross-platform spawner is arcbuild's program-private `ProcessRunner` (`Source/Programs/arcbuild/src/Process.hpp:111`; `CreateProcessW` / `fork`+`execv`). It is not a library and it blocks, so it is not reused. `servitor launch` (Task 16, ruling E-2) has two `#if` arms in `Launch.cpp`: `CreateProcessW` with `QuoteWindowsArg` and `CheckLaunchPath` on Windows, `posix_spawn` on POSIX; the child's stdout and stderr go to a log file beside the host exe (the `RuntimeLaunch` pattern), never to `servitor`'s stdout. The acceptance (Task 30) is Windows-only.
  - Descriptor DACL: nothing in the tree sets file ACLs or modes today. The POSIX counterpart is a `0600` file in a `0700` directory.
  - Named kernel objects and Win32-only acceptance mechanics (`pi.dwProcessId`, `TerminateProcess`) sit behind `#if defined(_WIN32)`, with the POSIX path either implemented or refused explicitly.
- **Dist.** The macro is `ARC_BUILD_DIST` (`premake5.lua` Dist filter: the module projects' `module_defaults` at `:346-347`, and each program project's own Dist filter). The spec's `ARCANE_DIST` spelling (s10.6) is stale; use `ARC_BUILD_DIST`. Every new `Arcane::Remote` translation unit is born inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, except ArcaneRemote's registration TU and its export header (R8-1). `servitor.exe`'s Dist `main` prints `Servitor is compiled out of Dist builds` and returns 2. A Dist host that sees `--remote`, `--remote-descriptor`, or `--remote-idle-exit` exits 2 with that same sentence.
  - **Remote tests (ruling D-2).** CI builds and runs ArcaneTests in Dist on all three platforms (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:148`), where no Remote symbol exists. Every file under `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/` is wrapped whole in `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)` ... `#endif` (after its includes; cases a later step appends go inside the guard), and every remote case added to an existing test file sits in that file's own `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)` block. The code blocks below show the cases; the guard is implied by this rule where a block does not spell it.
  - **Packages (ruling D-1).** Packaged games are Dist monolithic builds, so the remote surface is compiled out of them. The remote wiring in ArcaneRuntimeHost (`RuntimeApp.cpp`, `RuntimeApp.hpp`) sits under `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST) && !defined(ARC_MONOLITHIC)` (`ARC_MONOLITHIC` is the monolithic layout's workspace define, `premake5.lua:174`), so a package references no Remote symbol and links no ArcaneRemote. ArcaneRuntimeHost lists `"ArcaneRemote"` in its modular branch only. Do NOT edit `build/arcane.lua` (`ARC_PACKAGE_HOST_MODULES`; it belongs to the distribution lane). If a non-Dist package configuration is ever built, its runtime refuses `--remote` at startup with usage exit 2 (`the remote surface is not linked into packaged games`). Attaching to a packaged game is a later spec item.
- **ABI.** Next ABI after `main` at execution time. This worktree's `kGamePluginABIVersion` is **59** (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Core/AbiVersion.hpp:10`); the version ledger stays in `Source/Runtime/ArcaneEngine/src/Arcane/Plugin/PluginABI.hpp` (the v59 entry is the last, `:1172-1178`). Do not hard-code 60 then 61. At Task 0 and again at Task 10, re-read `main`'s `AbiVersion.hpp`, assign `that value + 1`, write the ledger entry for the integer you actually assigned, and restamp the manifests to it. Expected: Task 0 → 60 (`RemoteCVarRequest` / `RemoteCVarResponse`), Task 10 → 61 (`HostConfig` remote fields). New `Arcane::Remote` types (ArcaneRemote exports) are additive and do not bump. `ServerConfig` is the server exe's own struct, not the game ABI. Do not add Remote headers to `kGameFacingHeaders` (`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/ArcaneSpellingGuardTest.cpp:34`, walked at `:222`). Each game-ABI bump restamps `ReferenceProject/ReferenceProject.arcproj` (today `"abi": 59`, line 6) and the arcbuild fixture `Tests/ArcaneTests/data/arcbuild-fixture/Fixture.arcproj` (today 59), as the v59 ledger did. **Aphelyon is outside this plan's scope (rulings A-1 / G-1).** `D:\dev\starworks\Aphelyon\Aphelyon.arcproj` is at `"abi": 55` and its catch-up rides Aphelyon's `feat/engine-modularization` branch. Tasks 0, 10 and 30 never restamp or check it; Aphelyon is restamped when that branch merges and its game module is rebuilt. Each ledger entry says "Aphelyon.arcproj restamps with its next engine sync", following the v58/v59 entries.
- **Repos and branches.** Arcane work stays on `feat/arc-cli-p0` in `D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc`. The vendored stamp in `ThirdParty/Astra/VENDORED.txt` is commit `c46a90ede5584c5aae5692da02889e2f8f35425b`, branch `dev`, synced 2026-10-02 08:30:19, scope `include/` only, until Astra 1.0 lane step 6 syncs a newer one. This plan does not commit in `D:\dev\starworks\Astra` and does not edit `ThirdParty/Astra`. Never push. Never edit `ThirdParty/Astra` in place. Never touch Astra `vendor/Unreal`. The mirror is `scripts/sync-vendor.ps1 -Library Astra` (it stamps `VENDORED.txt`); `scripts/sync-astra.ps1` (`-Source` default `D:\dev\starworks\Astra`, switch `-DryRun`) is a shim that calls it.
- **Untracked Astra benches.** `bench-compare/*` (and any other untracked bench files) under `D:\dev\starworks\Astra` belong to the user. They never fail a task, never get staged, and never get deleted. This plan does not edit Astra. Stage named files only.
- **Every commit message ends with these two paragraphs** (`git commit -m subject -m trailer -m session`):
  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
  Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01Ertr3dpdimU1VjCXXAJSBi
  ```
- **Build Arcane** from PowerShell (MSYS mangles `/p:`). These are the steps `scripts/ci/build.ps1` runs (generate, engine, game, hosts); `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts\ci\build.ps1 Debug` runs all four, logging to `logs\build-*.log`. `/FS` is not in `premake5.lua`; pass it.
  - Generate (after any added or removed `.cpp`, a new `.arcmodule`, and premake edits; `build/modules.lua` discovers every descriptor under `Source/`): `$env:_APH_NOPAUSE = '1'; cmd /c "D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\GenerateProjects.bat"` (`_APH_NOPAUSE` skips `scripts\generate.bat`'s closing `pause`; `VCPKG_ROOT` must be set).
  - Build: `& "C:\Program Files\Microsoft Visual Studio\18\Community\MSBuild\Current\Bin\amd64\MSBuild.exe" D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Arcane.slnx /p:Configuration=Debug /m:8 /nodeReuse:false /p:CL=/FS /v:m`
  - Tests **from the exe dir**. PowerShell eats brackets, so:
    `Set-Location D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\ArcaneTests; cmd /c "ArcaneTests.exe \"[remote][tag]\""`
    Record the `Randomness seeded to:` line on failure. A Catch2 name filter without a wildcard matches one exact case name; every filter in this plan is a tag (`[remote][play]`) or a name ending in `*` (ruling F24).

- **ReferenceProject** (only Tasks 0, 10, and 30, and only because the game ABI moved or the acceptance runs the game): after the engine build, `& D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\arcbuild\arcbuild.exe build --project D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\ReferenceProject --config Debug --sdk D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc`, then build `Arcane.slnx` again so every host's post-build restages the game module (`scripts/ci/build.ps1` steps 3-4). `ReferenceProject/Binaries/` is one slot for every configuration; a Debug-CRT game DLL inside a Release host crashes. Other tasks build `Arcane.slnx` only.
- **Golden gate** is the acceptance phase gate, not a per-task requirement: in order, (1) the ReferenceProject `arcbuild build` above, (2) an incremental `Arcane.slnx` build, (3) delete the exe-dir `imgui.ini` in `Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\ArcaneEditor`, (4) ArcaneTests `"~[gpu]"` and `"[witness]~[shell]"` from the exe dir, (5) `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\scripts\golden-gate.ps1 -Configuration Debug` (the order is `scripts\golden-gate.ps1`'s header). Do not re-bless. `editor-ui` is a shared slot.
- **Export macros.** Every ArcaneRemote class, free function or variable that another module, a host program or ArcaneTests calls is declared `ARC_REMOTE_API` (`<Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp>`); ArcaneRemoteProtocol's are `ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API` (`<Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteProtocolApi.hpp>`, rev 8.3 V1); `Servitor::` client code carries none (V4); plain data structs and templates need none. A declaration given in prose below carries it too. Edits to existing types use their owner's macro: `ARC_CORE_API` (ArcaneCore), `ARC_ENGINE_API` (`EditorLock::IsLive`), ArcaneNet's `TcpSocket.hpp` and ArcaneCrypto's `Crypto.hpp` additions are header-inline and need none. Editor-program code (`Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Remote/`) takes no export macro.
- **Cvar macro.** Remote commands use `ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND`. `ARC_COMMAND` already registers console commands (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Config/CVarDecl.hpp:44`).
- **Repo guards.** ArcaneTests cases in the `~[gpu]` run that fail on new code unless it follows these rules:
  - A numeric constant in `Source/` is a setting, or carries `ARC_CONSTANT("why")` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Core/Constant.hpp`) on the nearest non-blank line above it (`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/ConstantGuardTest.cpp:170`). `scripts/constant-allowlist.txt` is closed under `Source/Runtime/`, `Source/Client/`, the host programs, `Source/Editor/` and the ArcaneEditor program (`:194-227`). A constant that is a cvar's default keeps its `ARC_CONSTANT` marker and is the one spelling of that default (ruling B-1; the pattern of `EditorThemeSettings.hpp:170`).
  - **Cvar inventory (ruling C-3; precedent `92c0e8fd`, `physics.events.hitThreshold`).** Every cvar the test process registers (except `tests.*`) is in `scripts/settings-frozen-names.txt` or the inventory's existing-cvar table (`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/SweepCoverageTest.cpp:47`). The task that adds a cvar also adds, in the same commit: one sorted line in `scripts/settings-frozen-names.txt`, a post-freeze amendment row and a Part 1 SETTING row in `docs/superpowers/audits/2026-10-03-settings-inventory.md`, and the Part 1 / Total SETTING counts. The remote cvars, all named in the user-approved spec: `log.remote.ringLines` (Task 2), `remote.server.frameBudgetMs` and `remote.server.queueDepth` (Task 8), `editor.remote.enable` (Task 11).
  - No C++ macro is spelled `ARCANE_*` (`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/MacroPrefixGuardTest.cpp:21`); only environment-variable names keep that prefix.
  - No engine, client, editor or host source builds a well-known location by hand (`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/PathsGuardTest.cpp:66`); use `Paths::Get` (`Paths.hpp:57`).
- **Threading.** The listener thread never touches the registry, the cvar pending store, undo, or ImGui. `GET /status` and `GET /catalog` read atomics. Everything else runs on the host main thread inside `Drain`, after the event pump and before the sim step. `Drain` returns after one step of each job; it does not sleep and it does not loop a job to completion. The listener is one `ServiceThread` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/ServiceThread.hpp:31`). Its body is one `PollSockets` loop. `GET /status` is answered on that thread. A `/call` is parked on that thread until `RequestQueue::Complete` publishes the result. The service thread does not call `RequestQueue::Wait`.
- **Tick (AM1).** On a fixed-tick host (ArcaneServer, or a headless runtime with no on-screen presenter), one "frame" is one sim tick. `host.frame` counts sim advances only. It is not `io.frameCount` (`Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeFrame.cpp` `CaptureTail` increments that, `:800`) and not a free-running render counter. Render-only idle iterations may pump and render; they must not increment `host.frame`.

## Review Focus

These five are the inputs most likely to bite a user that a happy-path test would miss. Each one has a pinning test in the task named, written in that task's own step.

1. **The listener forgets the bind address and calls `CreateListenSocket(port)`.** The default is `INADDR_ANY` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneNet/src/Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp:205-209`), so the port is reachable off-machine. A reasonable person expects the socket's bound address to be `127.0.0.1` and nowhere else. Pinned in Task 6 (`"the listen socket is bound to 127.0.0.1"`).
2. **A request carries the right bearer token and also an `Origin` header, or a `Host` that is not `127.0.0.1:<port>` or `localhost:<port>`, or the TCP peer is not `127.0.0.1`.** DNS rebinding and a future bind slip. A reasonable person expects refusal before the command runs. Pinned in Task 6 (`"a good token does not survive Origin"`, `"a forged Host is refused by the listener"`, and `"the accept loop closes a peer the predicate refuses"`). Removing the Origin check, the Host check, or the peer predicate makes its test fail.
3. **The editor is minimized.** A queued `cvar.get` must still run, a screenshot must return `host.busy` with `state:"minimized"` and exit 5 without grabbing pixels, and the waiter must return rather than hang. Pinned in Task 13 (`"minimized: cvar.get runs, screenshot does not, and the waiter returns"`).
4. **The token is written to a log line, an envelope, a descriptor dump, or `servitor --verbose`.** A reasonable person expects the secret absent from all four. Pinned in Task 14 (`"verbose and human output redact the bearer token"`), with the listener-side half in Task 6 (`"a refused request does not log the token"`).
5. **`play.step 10` on an `--remote` headless runtime that was not given `--frames`, or the same step on ArcaneServer.** A reasonable person expects `host.frame` to advance by exactly 10 and then stop, counting sim ticks rather than rendered frames, **and** expects `GET /status` to be answered while those 10 ticks are still in flight. Pinned in Task 29 (`"step 10 advances host.frame by exactly 10 while status still answers"` and `"server --frames counts sim advances, so a remote pause does not spend it"`) and in Task 30 (`stepHost("server")` inside `"P0 hosts answer the spec envelope"`, and the live `"server --frames 1200 ticks exactly 1200 across a remote pause, step and resume"`).

These are also pinned, in the task that owns them, and are not a sixth header line: Servitor's module boundary (Task 14, `[servitor][boundary]`; rev 8.3 V5), a newer protocol minor and an unknown major (Tasks 31 and 14; V6), a recycled pid (Task 7), a Protected cvar read from the Editor context (Task 20), `--all` worst-exit when one host is busy (5) and another is dead (7) (Task 18), a compare that ran and failed (`ok:true`, exit 8) (Task 28), auth exit 3 (Task 3), `capture.no-renderer` exit 2 (Task 3 and Task 27), the 30 s idle close (Task 6), and an idle capture that completes on a render ticket while `host.frame` stays put (Task 27's frame-stays-10 case, Task 30's idle editor bless and idle headless `screenshot window`). Also pinned the same way, not as a sixth numbered line: a second capture while one is in flight answers `host.busy` with `state` `capture-pending` and exit 5, and the retried call succeeds only after that ticket retires, with both PNGs valid (Task 27); the client keeps retrying a busy answer for longer than 60 ms, until `--timeout` (default 30 s) (Task 14); the acceptance host guard kills its children on a forced failure (Task 30); `--timeout` is seconds and the stored field is milliseconds (Task 14); a runtime launched with `--hang-main 60` is reported hung by `servitor status` on the row whose `pid` equals that child's `pi.dwProcessId`, and the following `cvar get --pid <that pid>` exits 7 with `host.hung` (Task 30).

```cpp
struct RemoteCVarRequest {
    std::string op;
    std::string name;
    std::string value;
    std::string callerId;
    CVarContext context = CVarContext::ServerAdmin; // LAST
};

struct RemoteCVarResponse {
    bool ok = false;
    std::string text;
    std::string valueJson = "null";
    std::string type;
    std::string setBy;
    std::string audience;
    std::string scope;
    std::string apply;
    std::string flags;
    std::string publishedJson;
    std::string pendingJson;
    std::string historyJson; // JSON array of {by, value, module}
};

ARC_CORE_API nlohmann::json CVarValueToJson(const CVarValue& value); // CVarFormat.hpp; ArcaneRemote calls it across the DLL boundary

// CVarTypes.hpp, directly after enum class SetBy (ruling S3-5). Defined once,
// in CVarRegistry.cpp, from the body of today's private SetByName switch.
[[nodiscard]] ARC_CORE_API const char* ToString(SetBy by) noexcept; // "Default", "EngineConfig", ..., "Console"
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Add to `RemoteCVarServiceTest.cpp`, and add `#include <Json.hpp>` beside the existing includes. Leave `Req()`'s 4-field aggregate as it is. `Knob` and `AuditLog` are the anonymous-namespace helpers at lines 16–35, visible to every case in this file. Do not invent a second registrar. Real fixture names already in that file: `test.server.tick` (Server, Int32 60), `test.server.password` (Server, Protected, `"hunter2"`), `editor.secret` (Editor, Protected, `"editorhorse"`). The new Editor case registers `editor.visible` through that same `Knob`.

```cpp
TEST_CASE("Editor context can read an Editor-audience cvar the ServerAdmin list hides", "[remote][cvar]") {
    CVarRegistry reg;
    Knob(reg, "editor.visible", CVarValue::Int32(3), Audience::Editor);
    Knob(reg, "editor.secret", CVarValue::String("editorhorse"), Audience::Editor, CVarFlags::Protected);
    RemoteCVarService svc(reg);
    RemoteCVarRequest req{"get", "editor.visible", "", "remote-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    const RemoteCVarResponse res = svc.Handle(req);
    CHECK(res.ok);
    CHECK(res.text.find('3') != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.valueJson == "3");
    RemoteCVarRequest hidden{"get", "editor.visible", "", "admin-1"};
    CHECK_FALSE(svc.Handle(hidden).ok);
}

TEST_CASE("Protected get is masked in the Editor context", "[remote][cvar]") {
    CVarRegistry reg;
    Knob(reg, "editor.secret", CVarValue::String("editorhorse"), Audience::Editor, CVarFlags::Protected);
    RemoteCVarService svc(reg);
    RemoteCVarRequest req{"get", "editor.secret", "", "remote-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    const RemoteCVarResponse res = svc.Handle(req);
    CHECK_FALSE(res.ok);
    CHECK(res.text.find("protected") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.text.find("editorhorse") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.valueJson == "null");
}

TEST_CASE("explain returns structured history and still masks Protected", "[remote][cvar]") {
    CVarRegistry reg;
    Knob(reg, "test.server.tick", CVarValue::Int32(60), Audience::Server);
    Knob(reg, "test.server.password", CVarValue::String("hunter2"), Audience::Server, CVarFlags::Protected);
    RemoteCVarService svc(reg);
    RemoteCVarRequest set{"set", "test.server.tick", "45", "remote-test", CVarContext::ServerAdmin};
    REQUIRE(svc.Handle(set).ok);
    RemoteCVarRequest exp{"explain", "test.server.tick", "", "remote-test", CVarContext::ServerAdmin};
    const RemoteCVarResponse res = svc.Handle(exp);
    CHECK(res.ok);
    CHECK(res.text.find("Console") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.setBy == "Console");
    CHECK(res.valueJson == "45");
    const auto history = nlohmann::json::parse(res.historyJson);
    REQUIRE(history.is_array());
    REQUIRE(history.size() >= 2);
    CHECK(history.front()["by"] == "Default");
    CHECK(history.front()["value"] == 60);
    CHECK(history.front()["module"] == "");
    CHECK(history.back()["by"] == "Console");
    CHECK(history.back()["value"] == 45);
    CHECK(history.back()["module"] == "");
    CHECK(res.historyJson.find("remote-test") == std::string::npos);
    RemoteCVarRequest prot{"explain", "test.server.password", "", "remote-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    const RemoteCVarResponse masked = svc.Handle(prot);
    CHECK_FALSE(masked.ok);
    CHECK(masked.text.find("hunter2") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(masked.valueJson == "null");
    CHECK(masked.historyJson.empty());
}

TEST_CASE("Editor context explains a non-protected Editor-audience cvar", "[remote][cvar]") {
    CVarRegistry reg;
    AuditLog audit;
    Knob(reg, "editor.visible", CVarValue::Int32(3), Audience::Editor);
    RemoteCVarService svc(reg, &AuditLog::Sink, &audit);
    RemoteCVarRequest set{"set", "editor.visible", "9", "remote-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    REQUIRE(svc.Handle(set).ok);
    REQUIRE(audit.records.size() == 1);
    CHECK(audit.records.front().context == CVarContext::Editor);
    RemoteCVarRequest exp{"explain", "editor.visible", "", "remote-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    const RemoteCVarResponse res = svc.Handle(exp);
    CHECK(res.ok);
    CHECK(res.text.find("unknown") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.valueJson == "9");
    CHECK(res.setBy == "Console");
    CHECK(res.audience == "Editor");
}

TEST_CASE("omitted context stays ServerAdmin", "[remote][cvar]") {
    RemoteCVarRequest req{"get", "test.server.tick", "", "tester"};
    CHECK(req.context == CVarContext::ServerAdmin);
}

TEST_CASE("ToString(SetBy) names every rung", "[remote][cvar]") {
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::Default)) == "Default");
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::EngineConfig)) == "EngineConfig");
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::Plugin)) == "Plugin");
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::Project)) == "Project");
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::EditorUser)) == "EditorUser");
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::User)) == "User");
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::CommandLine)) == "CommandLine");
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::Code)) == "Code");
    CHECK(std::string(Arcane::ToString(SetBy::Console)) == "Console");
}
```

Append the fields exactly as in **Produces**. Thread an explicit `CVarContext context` parameter through `ReadValue`, `List`, `RunCommand`, and `Audit`: the header declarations (`RemoteCVarService.hpp:67`, `:68`, `:69-70`, `:71-72`), the definitions (`RemoteCVarService.cpp:60`, `:66-67`, `:81`, `:112-114`), and every call site. `Handle` (`:131`) passes `request.context`. `ReadValue` passes `context` to `Execute` (`:62`). `Audit` assigns `record.context = context` (`:75`). `List` passes `context` to `CVarRegistry::List` (`:83`), `CanRead` (`:92`), and `Execute` (`:100`). `RunCommand` passes `context` to `Execute` (`:125`) and to its `Audit` call (`:127`). `Handle` passes `request.context` at every call: `List` (`:138`), `RunCommand` (`:145`), the get `Execute` (`:153`), the explain probe `Execute` (`:161`), the `cvar_explain` `Execute` (`:163`), `ReadValue` (`:171`, `:175`, `:181`), `Audit` (`:172`, `:187`), and the set `Execute` (`:176`). The `Execute` calls at `:153`, `:161`, `:163`, and `:176` are inside `Handle`, which has `request`, so those literals become `request.context`. An Editor-audience cvar stays absent outside `CVarContext::Editor` (`DefaultAccess`, `CVarRegistry.cpp:109-110`), and `ExplainCommand` (`CVarRegistry.cpp:590`) then returns `unknown cvar` (`:601-604`). Leaving the explain probe at `:161` as `ServerAdmin` fails the Editor explain case. The Protected early return at line 160 still runs first, so the Protected explain case does not pin 161.

`CVarValueToJson`: switch on the public field `value.type` (`CVarTypes.hpp:189`; accessors at 191–203). `CVarType` has no `Quat` and `CVarValue` has no `AsQuat`. bool → JSON boolean, integers → number, float/double → number, string → string, vec2/vec3/vec4 → number arrays of length 2/3/4. Color and Enum use `FormatCVarValue` wrapped as a JSON string, and so does the default arm. The Protected refusal assigns `valueJson = "null"` itself and does not call this function. glm quaternions in `ReflectionJson` / `SchemaFor` stay `[x, y, z, w]` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneScene/src/Arcane/Serialization/ReflectionJson.hpp:267`); that is a different type.

### Task 1: Move `DiagnosticStore` into ArcaneCore

No ABI bump: a new Core type plus a using-alias. The editor header keeps the old name so panels do not all change includes.

Ruling A-3: the store moves to ArcaneCore beside `Diagnostics` (which owns `Diagnostic`, `DiagSeverity` and the sink seam), at `<Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp>`, namespace `Arcane`. A program-local type cannot serve the runtime and the server, and ArcaneRemote would compile it out of Dist while the editor's Problems panel needs it in every configuration. Later tasks (Task 22) include `<Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp>`.

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.cpp`
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/DiagnosticStore.hpp` (becomes an alias)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/DiagnosticStore.cpp` (delete the file; its methods moved)
- Modify: `premake5.lua` (delete the ArcaneTests source-compile of that editor cpp: the comment at lines 1464–1467 and the file line at 1468)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/DiagnosticStoreMoveTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the class in `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/DiagnosticStore.hpp` as it is today. `Count(Arcane::DiagSeverity)` line 65. `Snapshot()` line 68. `Filtered(SeverityMask, std::string_view)` line 69. `InstallAsEngineSink()` line 79. `UninstallEngineSink()` line 80. `Publish(std::string_view key, std::span<const Diagnostic>)` line 61. `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` line 48. `SeverityMask` line 23. `Diagnostics::Publish(key, span)` at `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Diagnostics.hpp:819`. `ClearSinkIfCurrent(Sink, void*)` at `:816`. `SetSink` at `:807`. `DiagSeverity::{Info, Warning, Error}` at `:748`. `DiagScope` at `:750`. `Diagnostic` at `:786`.
- Produces: the same class in namespace `Arcane`, no ImGui include. Editor header:

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp>
namespace Arcane::Editor {
using DiagnosticStore = Arcane::DiagnosticStore;
using SeverityMask = Arcane::SeverityMask;
using Arcane::operator|;
using Arcane::MaskOf;
using Arcane::MaskFrom;
using Arcane::MatchesDiagnosticFilter;
}
```

Move `SeverityMask`, `operator|`, `MaskOf`, `MaskFrom`, and `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` with the class, same signatures, into namespace `Arcane`. One `operator|` lives in `Arcane`, immediately after `SeverityMask` and before `MaskOf`. `MaskFrom` applies `|` (`DiagnosticStore.hpp:40-41`), and argument-dependent lookup for an enum whose home namespace is `Arcane` does not search `Arcane::Editor`. The editor header does not define a second `operator|`. `using Arcane::operator|;` re-exports the overload set. Mark `class DiagnosticStore` and `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` `ARC_CORE_API`. `operator|`, `MaskOf`, and `MaskFrom` stay inline constexpr in the header, with no export macro. `Clear` and `ClearAll` (`DiagnosticStore.hpp:62-63`) move with the class. `InstallAsEngineSink` still calls `Diagnostics::SetSink`. `UninstallEngineSink` still calls `ClearSinkIfCurrent(&DiagnosticStore::SinkTrampoline, this)`. Do not rename it to `Uninstall`. The process-wide sink stays last-writer-wins. The class holds a mutex and STL members. C4251 is already disabled workspace-wide (`premake5.lua:225`). Do not add a suppression. Every module's `files` glob is `src/**.hpp` + `src/**.cpp` (`build/modules.lua:391`), so the new cpp is picked up by `GenerateProjects.bat`. The moved code is portable C++ (`<mutex>`, `<map>`, `std::tolower`), so it compiles on all three platforms unchanged (R8-7).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/DiagnosticStoreMoveTest.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Base/Diagnostics.hpp>

TEST_CASE("Core DiagnosticStore installs, counts by severity, and uninstalls", "[remote]") {
    Arcane::DiagnosticStore store;
    store.InstallAsEngineSink();
    Arcane::Diagnostic row;
    row.severity = Arcane::DiagSeverity::Error;
    row.scope = Arcane::DiagScope::Project;
    row.code = "remote.move";
    row.message = "hello store";
    const Arcane::Diagnostic rows[] = { row };
    Arcane::Diagnostics::Publish("remote-move", rows);
    CHECK(store.Count(Arcane::DiagSeverity::Error) == 1);
    CHECK(store.Count(Arcane::DiagSeverity::Warning) == 0);
    const auto snap = store.Snapshot();
    REQUIRE(snap.size() == 1);
    CHECK(snap[0].message == "hello store");
    CHECK(snap[0].code == "remote.move");
    const auto filtered = store.Filtered(Arcane::SeverityMask::Error, "hello");
    REQUIRE(filtered.size() == 1);
    CHECK(Arcane::MatchesDiagnosticFilter(snap[0], Arcane::SeverityMask::Error, "hello"));
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::MatchesDiagnosticFilter(snap[0], Arcane::SeverityMask::Warning, "hello"));
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::MatchesDiagnosticFilter(snap[0], Arcane::SeverityMask::Error, "absent"));
    store.UninstallEngineSink();
    Arcane::Diagnostic later = row;
    later.message = "after uninstall";
    const Arcane::Diagnostic laterRows[] = { later };
    Arcane::Diagnostics::Publish("remote-move", laterRows);
    CHECK(store.Count(Arcane::DiagSeverity::Error) == 1);
    CHECK(store.Snapshot()[0].message == "hello store");
}
```

`MatchesDiagnosticFilter` is `noexcept` and returns bool (`DiagnosticStore.hpp:48`): the mask must include the row's severity and the search text must occur.
