### Task 2: Sequenced log ring

Not the crash backlog. `kBacklogLines` is 512 (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Log.hpp:109`) and the backlog is a torn read (`BacklogLineCount` / `BacklogLine`, `Log.hpp:132-140`). Do not reuse `FreezeBacklog` (`:119`). There is no `Log::Info`. The engine logger is `Log::Engine()` (`Log.hpp:35`), an `spdlog::logger*`. The log is ArcaneCore (R8-1); the ring stays there so every host and ArcaneRemote can read it.

**Files:**
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Log.hpp`
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Log.cpp` (the sink hook, in `Log::Init`)
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/LogRing.cpp`
- Modify: `scripts/settings-frozen-names.txt` (add `log.remote.ringLines`, in sorted position, after `jobs.workerThreads` and before `log.dir`)
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/audits/2026-10-03-settings-inventory.md` (a post-freeze amendment row and a Part 1 SETTING row for `log.remote.ringLines`, plus the Part 1 / Total SETTING counts; the shape of commit `92c0e8fd`'s `physics.events.hitThreshold` rows)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/LogRingTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Log::Engine()` (`Log.hpp:35`) and `AttachFileSink` (`Log.hpp:100`). The engine logger holds `[stderr, dist]` (`Log.cpp:122-125`); every attachable sink lives inside the `dist_sink_mt` created in `Log::Init` (`Log.cpp:242-243`), the way `BacklogSink` (`Log.cpp:82`, attached in `AttachFileSink` at `:425-428`) does.
- Produces (in `Log.hpp`; the record types in `namespace Arcane`, the functions in `namespace Arcane::Log`, which is the header's namespace at `:24`; every function `ARC_CORE_API`, because ArcaneRemote and ArcaneTests call it across the DLL boundary):

```cpp
namespace Arcane {
struct LogRecord {
    std::uint64_t seq = 0;     // starts at 1; never repeats in-process
    std::int64_t unixMs = 0;
    int level = 0;             // spdlog level as int
    std::string logger;
    std::string text;
};

using LogSinkFn = void (*)(const LogRecord& record, void* user);

struct LogSubscription { std::uint64_t id = 0; };
}

namespace Arcane::Log {
ARC_CORE_API LogSubscription Subscribe(LogSinkFn fn, void* user);
ARC_CORE_API void Unsubscribe(LogSubscription sub);
ARC_CORE_API std::vector<LogRecord> ReadSince(std::uint64_t seqExclusive, std::size_t maxLines);
ARC_CORE_API std::uint64_t LatestSeq(); // seq of the newest record pushed so far; 0 before the first line (ruling F3)
ARC_CORE_API std::size_t RingCapacity(); // default 4096
}
```

The ring is process-wide, mutexed, separate from the crash backlog. Overflow drops the oldest. `ReadSince(0)` returns the retained window. `ReadSince` returns the OLDEST `maxLines` records after the seq, so a reader that wants only what comes next anchors on `LatestSeq()` first (ruling F3): `LatestSeq()` reads the counter under the ring mutex and returns the seq the most recent push was given (it does not depend on what is still retained). Task 22's follow job anchors on it the same way. A subscriber runs on the logging thread and must not re-enter `Log`. Capacity is the cvar `log.remote.ringLines` (Dev, default 4096), read when the ring is first touched and on each publish if the cvar changed. Declare it with `ARC_CVAR` in the cpp (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Config/CVarDecl.hpp:26`; options are `CVarSpec<T>` designated initializers in field order): `ARC_CVAR(cvar_logRemoteRingLines, "log.remote.ringLines", std::uint32_t, 4096u, .flags = CVarFlags::Dev, .audience = Audience::Editor, .scope = SettingScope::PreferencesMachine, .help = "How many log lines the remote log ring retains.");`. Dist's registry refuses Dev cvars and the handle reads the declared default, so the ring then stays at 4096. The literal 4096 sits in the `ARC_CVAR` default, so it is a setting, not a bare constant; any other numeric constant in the TU needs `ARC_CONSTANT("why")` (see **Repo guards**). The registered name must be in `scripts/settings-frozen-names.txt` and the inventory, or `"sweep coverage: every registered cvar is a frozen SETTING name or an inventoried existing cvar"` (`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/SweepCoverageTest.cpp:47`) fails.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Base/Log.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

static int g_hits = 0;
static void OnLog(const Arcane::LogRecord& rec, void*) {
    if (rec.text.find("remote-ring-line") != std::string::npos)
        ++g_hits;
}

TEST_CASE("Subscribe observes a line and ReadSince resumes from seq", "[remote]") {
    g_hits = 0;
    // Anchor on the newest seq first (ruling F3): a full-suite run leaves
    // thousands of earlier lines in the ring, and ReadSince(0, n) returns the
    // OLDEST n of them.
    const std::uint64_t base = Arcane::Log::LatestSeq();
    const auto sub = Arcane::Log::Subscribe(&OnLog, nullptr);
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("remote-ring-line one");
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("remote-ring-line two");
    CHECK(g_hits == 2);
    // Another thread may log in between, so find our lines by text, never by
    // a fixed offset from base.
    const auto findText = [](const std::vector<Arcane::LogRecord>& recs, const char* text) {
        for (const auto& r : recs)
            if (r.text.find(text) != std::string::npos)
                return r.seq;
        return std::uint64_t{0};
    };
    const auto mine = Arcane::Log::ReadSince(base, 64);
    const std::uint64_t one = findText(mine, "remote-ring-line one");
    const std::uint64_t two = findText(mine, "remote-ring-line two");
    REQUIRE(one > base);
    REQUIRE(two > one);
    CHECK(Arcane::Log::LatestSeq() >= two);
    const auto tail = Arcane::Log::ReadSince(one, 64);
    REQUIRE_FALSE(tail.empty());
    CHECK(tail.front().seq == one + 1);
    Arcane::Log::Unsubscribe(sub);
    const int frozen = g_hits;
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("remote-ring-line three");
    CHECK(g_hits == frozen);
    const auto after = Arcane::Log::ReadSince(two, 64);
    CHECK(findText(after, "remote-ring-line three") > two);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate and Debug build per Global Constraints, then from the exe dir `cmd /c "ArcaneTests.exe \"Subscribe observes a line*\""`. Expected: FAIL to compile. `Log::Subscribe` does not exist.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the ring**

One TU (`LogRing.cpp`) owns the ring, the mutex, and the subscriber vector. On each line the engine logger emits, also push a `LogRecord` and invoke subscribers: the hook is a `spdlog::sinks::base_sink<std::mutex>` shaped like `BacklogSink` (`Log.cpp:82`), added to the `dist_sink_mt` inside `Log::Init`'s `call_once` (`Log.cpp:242-243`), so it sees every line from the first one on. Nothing touches the logger's own sink vector after `Init` (the comment at `Log.cpp:237-241` is the rule). Do not take the backlog lock and the ring lock in opposite orders; the ring has its own mutex and does not call `FreezeBacklog`. `seq` is a `uint64_t` incremented under the mutex. `ReadSince` copies out records with `seq > seqExclusive`, up to `maxLines`, in order (oldest first). `LatestSeq` returns that counter under the mutex (0 before the first push). Wire the cvar as specified. Changing `log.remote.ringLines` downward drops oldest until `size == capacity`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Same filter. Expected: PASS. Two delivered lines after the `LatestSeq()` anchor, monotonic `seq`, unsubscribe silence, and the third line readable via `ReadSince` after "two", however many lines the suite logged before this case. Then `cmd /c "ArcaneTests.exe \"[sweep][coverage]\""` and `cmd /c "ArcaneTests.exe \"[sweep][guard]\""`. Expected: PASS (`log.remote.ringLines` is frozen and inventoried; no unmarked numeric constant).

### Task 3: Envelope, error codes, exit table (s6.3, s6.4, s6.6, AM1, AM6)

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/ArcaneRemote.arcmodule` (the new Runtime module, R8-1; this is the first task that writes ArcaneRemote code, so it creates the module)
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp` (`ARC_REMOTE_API`)
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteModule.cpp` (the one `ARC_MODULE(ArcaneRemote)`)
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Protocol.cpp`
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/ProtocolTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

The module is discovered by `arc_modules_load { "Source", ... }` (`premake5.lua:287-290`, `**.arcmodule` under `Source`), so it needs no `project` block. ArcaneTests already consumes every module (`arc_module_consumer(engine_modules, engine_modules.order)`, project "ArcaneTests" in `premake5.lua`) and globs `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/**.cpp`, so `src/Remote/*.cpp` builds with no premake edit. ArcaneServer, ArcaneRuntime and `Servitor` gain `"ArcaneRemote"` in their own tasks (R8-3), not here.

Ruling B-2: Task 3, the first task that writes ArcaneRemote code, creates the module scaffold (Step 3) with its full dependency set (ruling C-1). Later tasks do not edit `ArcaneRemote.arcmodule`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nlohmann::json (`#include <Json.hpp>`, the workspace spelling; nlohmann is a public thirdParty of ArcaneCore and of ArcaneRemote). `ARC_CONSTANT` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Core/Constant.hpp`).
- Produces: namespace `Arcane::Remote` (engine-internal, not game API; unchanged by the flat API). The host envelope has no `result`, no `hostFrame`, and no top-level `exitCode`. Exit is derived by `ExitFromHost`. Every free function below is declared `ARC_REMOTE_API` (`#include <Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp>`); the plain data structs need no export.

```cpp
ARC_CONSTANT("wire protocol: remote protocol major (spec s6)")
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kProtocol = 1;
ARC_CONSTANT("wire protocol: s10.1 request body bound")
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxBodyBytes = 4u * 1024u * 1024u;
ARC_CONSTANT("wire protocol: s10.1 header block bound (16 KiB)")
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxHeaderBytes = 16u * 1024u;
ARC_CONSTANT("capacity: s10.1 connection cap, under Winsock FD_SETSIZE (64); POSIX polls with poll(2)")
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxConnections = 16;
ARC_CONSTANT("capacity: s10.1 SSE subscriber cap")
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxSseSubscribers = 8;
ARC_CONSTANT("a change would be a bug: the one spelling of remote.server.queueDepth's default")
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kQueueDepthDefault = 64;
ARC_CONSTANT("capacity hint: s4.3 default commands drained per tick")
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kFrameBudgetCommands = 8;
ARC_CONSTANT("a change would be a bug: the one spelling of remote.server.frameBudgetMs' default")
inline constexpr double kFrameBudgetMs = 4.0;
ARC_CONSTANT("wire protocol: s10.1 idle connection close")
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kIdleConnectionSeconds = 30;

enum class HostKind : std::uint8_t { Editor, Runtime, Server };
enum class HostState : std::uint8_t { Starting, Ready, Playing, Paused, Reloading, Stopping, Hung };
enum class Access : std::uint8_t { Observe, Drive };
enum class Mutates : std::uint8_t { None, Scene, Project, Preferences, PlayMode };
enum class PlayPolicy : std::uint8_t { Any, EditOnly, PlayingOnly };
enum class ExecThread : std::uint8_t { Main, Service, Job };

struct Error {
    std::string code;
    std::string message;
    std::string remediation;
    nlohmann::json detail = nlohmann::json::object();
    nlohmann::json locator = nullptr;
    std::vector<std::string> candidates;
    std::string check;
};

struct HostBlock {
    std::string kind;                 // "editor" | "runtime" | "server"
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::string world;
    std::uint64_t frame = 0;
    std::string state;
    std::uint32_t catalog = 0;
    std::uint32_t pluginGeneration = 0;
};

struct Artifact {
    std::string kind;
    std::string path;
    std::uint64_t bytes = 0;
    std::string sha256;
    std::optional<std::uint32_t> width;
    std::optional<std::uint32_t> height;
    std::string format;
};

struct UndoBlock {
    nlohmann::json label = nullptr;   // string or JSON null
    bool undoable = false;
    std::string reason;               // play-mode | runtime-host | not-undoable-command
    std::uint64_t transaction = 0;
};

struct Timing {
    double queuedMs = 0;
    double ranMs = 0;
};

struct Envelope {
    bool ok = false;
    int protocol = 1;
    std::string id;
    std::string command;
    HostBlock host;
    nlohmann::json data = nlohmann::json::object();
    std::vector<Error> errors;
    std::vector<Error> warnings;
    std::vector<Error> notifications;
    std::vector<Artifact> artifacts;
    std::optional<UndoBlock> undo;    // nullopt serializes as null
    Timing timing;
};

struct ClientBlock {
    int exitCode = 0;
    bool retried = false;
    std::string descriptor;
    std::string token; // print-time only; ToJson(Envelope) never emits this
};

struct HostStatus {
    std::uint32_t protocol = kProtocol;
    HostKind hostKind = HostKind::Editor;
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::uint64_t processStart = 0;
    std::uint16_t port = 0;
    std::string state;
    std::uint64_t frame = 0;
    std::string world;
    std::uint32_t abi = 0;
    std::string project;
    std::uint32_t catalogueGeneration = 0;
    std::uint32_t pluginGeneration = 0;
    bool reloadOk = true;
    std::string reloadReason;
    std::uint64_t reloadAtFrame = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> capabilities;
    std::int64_t heartbeatUnixMs = 0;
    bool minimized = false;
};

ARC_REMOTE_API const char* ToString(HostKind kind);
ARC_REMOTE_API nlohmann::json ToJson(const Envelope& env);
ARC_REMOTE_API nlohmann::json ToJson(const HostStatus& status); // no token field exists on HostStatus
ARC_REMOTE_API Envelope ParseEnvelope(const nlohmann::json& j);
ARC_REMOTE_API Envelope MakeOk(std::string command, nlohmann::json data, HostBlock host);
ARC_REMOTE_API Envelope MakeError(std::string command, std::string code, std::string message, std::string remediation);
ARC_REMOTE_API int ExitFromHost(const Envelope& env);
ARC_REMOTE_API int WorstExit(std::span<const int> codes);
```

`Protocol.hpp` includes `<Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Core/Constant.hpp>`, `<Json.hpp>`, `<cstdint>`, `<optional>`, `<span>`, `<string>`, `<vector>`. Each numeric constant carries the `ARC_CONSTANT` line shown: `ConstantGuardTest.cpp` ("constant guard: every numeric constant is a setting, marked, or allow-listed", `[sweep][guard]`) scans `Source/Runtime` and fails on an unmarked one.

Ruling B-1: `kQueueDepthDefault` and `kFrameBudgetMs` are the defaults of the Dev cvars `remote.server.queueDepth` and `remote.server.frameBudgetMs` (spec s4.3). They keep their `ARC_CONSTANT` markers, the pattern `EditorThemeSettings.hpp:170` uses for cvar defaults, and Task 8's `ARC_CVAR` declarations take these constants as their defaults, so each value has one spelling. `kFrameBudgetCommands` is a plain capacity hint with no cvar.

`ToString(HostKind)` lives in `Arcane::Remote`, so it does not collide with `Arcane::ToString(NetMode)`.

`MakeError` sets `ok = false`, pushes one `Error`, and leaves `data` an object. It does not take an exit code. `MakeOk` sets `ok = true` and clears `errors`.

`ExitFromHost` switches on the first error code. Empty errors and `ok` return 0, except: `data.verdict == "Failed"` or `data.timedOut == true` returns 8 (the check ran; `ok` stays true). A non-ok envelope with an empty code returns 2. **Rule (ruling F6, spec s6.6): any other non-empty host code is exit 6** -- the `switch`'s `default` arm returns 6, so `capture.no-frame`, `job.failed`, `reflect.unsupported`, `cvar.refused-weaker`, `play.edit-only`, `undo.unavailable` and every code a later task invents all exit 6 unless the table names them.

| code | exit |
|---|---|
| `internal` | 1 |
| `arg.invalid`, `arg.unknown`, `arg.missing`, `arg.inapplicable`, `command.unknown`, `command.unavailable`, `command.host-kind`, `target.ambiguous`, `component.ambiguous`, `entity.ambiguous`, `capture.no-renderer` | 2 |
| `auth.denied`, `auth.throttled`, `permission.denied`, `permission.read-only`, `cvar.protected` | 3 |
| `protocol.mismatch`, `target.none` | 4 |
| `host.busy` (any `detail.state`, including `minimized`, `queue-full`, `capture-pending`, `modal`, `reloading`, `connections`), `capture.minimized`, and `host.unavailable` (a command whose stored host pointer is null; nothing ran; ruling S3-9) | 5 |
| `entity.not-found`, `component.unknown`, `component.absent`, `compare.missing-reference`, `capture.no-frame`, `cvar.unknown`, `cvar.type-mismatch`, `reflect.unreadable`, any other code ending in `.not-found`, and **any other non-empty host code not listed in this table** (spec s6.6: the host answered and the operation failed; ruling F6) | 6 |
| `host.unreachable`, `host.died`, `host.restarting`, `host.hung` | 7 |
| `compare.failed`, `wait.timeout` | 8 |

`compare.failed` and `wait.timeout` are not put in `errors` on the success-shaped check path. `ExitFromHost` still maps those codes to 8 if a caller does put them in `errors`, and also returns 8 when `ok` is true, `errors` is empty, and `data.verdict` is `"Failed"` or `data.timedOut` is true. `capture.no-renderer` is exit **2**, not 6. Auth codes are exit **3**, not 2. The wire code for a bad token is `auth.denied`, not `auth.rejected`.

`ToJson(Envelope)` always emits `ok`, `protocol`, `id`, `command`, `host`, `data`, `errors`, `warnings`, `notifications`, `artifacts`, `undo`, `timing`, even when the arrays are empty. `undo` is `null` when the optional is empty. When `undo` is set, the object is `{label, undoable, transaction}` (spec s6.3 and s8.2), and it includes `reason` when `UndoBlock::reason` is non-empty (`play-mode`, `runtime-host`, `not-undoable-command`). An empty `reason` omits that key. `host` is an object with `kind`, `pid`, `world`, `frame`, `state`, `catalog`, `pluginGeneration`. `timing` is `{queuedMs, ranMs}`. `ToJson` does not emit `client`, `exitCode`, `result`, or `hostFrame`. `ok:true` with a non-empty `errors` is a programming error: `MakeOk` clears `errors`, and a test asserts that.

`WorstExit` rank is `7 > 6 > 8 > 5 > 4 > 3 > 2 > 1 > 0`. Unknown codes sort as 2. Empty span returns 0.

Whole header and cpp wrapped in `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)` except the includes. Provide an empty `#else` so the include is legal. Do not export the symbols from a Dist build. (`ARC_BUILD_DIST` is defined for module projects by `module_defaults()`'s Dist filter in `premake5.lua`.) The module TU `ArcaneRemoteModule.cpp` is the one file NOT wrapped: every consumer forces `/INCLUDE:ArcModule_ArcaneRemote` (`arc_module_consumer`, `build/modules.lua`), so the anchor must exist in every configuration, Dist included.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>

using namespace Arcane::Remote;

static Envelope Err(const char* code) {
    return MakeError("cvar.get", code, "m", "r");
}

TEST_CASE("exit table matches s6.6, AM1, and AM6", "[remote][protocol]") {
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("internal")) == 1);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("capture.no-renderer")) == 2);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("arg.unknown")) == 2);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("command.unknown")) == 2);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("target.ambiguous")) == 2);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("auth.denied")) == 3);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("auth.throttled")) == 3);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("permission.denied")) == 3);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("permission.read-only")) == 3);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("cvar.protected")) == 3);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("protocol.mismatch")) == 4);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("target.none")) == 4);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("host.busy")) == 5);
    auto minimized = Err("host.busy");
    minimized.errors[0].detail = nlohmann::json{{"state", "minimized"}};
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(minimized) == 5);
    auto full = Err("host.busy");
    full.errors[0].detail = nlohmann::json{{"state", "queue-full"}};
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(full) == 5);
    auto pending = Err("host.busy");
    pending.errors[0].detail = nlohmann::json{{"state", "capture-pending"}};
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(pending) == 5);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("capture.minimized")) == 5);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("host.unavailable")) == 5);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("entity.not-found")) == 6);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("compare.missing-reference")) == 6);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("capture.no-frame")) == 6);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("job.failed")) == 6);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("play.edit-only")) == 6);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("some.future-code")) == 6); // any other non-empty code (ruling F6)
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("")) == 2);                 // empty code on a non-ok envelope
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("host.died")) == 7);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(Err("host.unreachable")) == 7);
    HostBlock host;
    host.kind = "runtime";
    host.frame = 61;
    host.state = "ready";
    auto compare = MakeOk("capture.compare", nlohmann::json{{"verdict", "Failed"}, {"diffPixels", 4}}, host);
    CHECK(compare.ok);
    CHECK(compare.errors.empty());
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(compare) == 8);
    auto timed = MakeOk("host.wait", nlohmann::json{{"timedOut", true}}, host);
    CHECK(timed.ok);
    CHECK(ExitFromHost(timed) == 8);
}

TEST_CASE("ToJson is the s6.3 object and omits client and exitCode", "[remote][protocol]") {
    HostBlock host;
    host.kind = "editor";
    host.pid = 23180;
    host.world = "client";
    host.frame = 81234;
    host.state = "ready";
    host.catalog = 7;
    host.pluginGeneration = 3;
    Envelope env = MakeOk("entity.get", nlohmann::json{{"entity", "MeshCube"}}, host);
    env.id = "r-17";
    env.timing.queuedMs = 3;
    env.timing.ranMs = 0.4;
    const auto j = ToJson(env);
    CHECK(j["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(j["protocol"] == 1);
    CHECK(j["id"] == "r-17");
    CHECK(j["command"] == "entity.get");
    CHECK(j["host"]["kind"] == "editor");
    CHECK(j["host"]["frame"] == 81234);
    CHECK(j["host"]["catalog"] == 7);
    CHECK(j["data"]["entity"] == "MeshCube");
    CHECK(j["errors"].is_array());
    CHECK(j["warnings"].is_array());
    CHECK(j["notifications"].is_array());
    CHECK(j["artifacts"].is_array());
    CHECK(j["undo"].is_null());
    CHECK(j["timing"]["queuedMs"] == 3);
    CHECK_FALSE(j.contains("result"));
    CHECK_FALSE(j.contains("hostFrame"));
    CHECK_FALSE(j.contains("exitCode"));
    CHECK_FALSE(j.contains("client"));
    const Envelope back = ParseEnvelope(j);
    CHECK(back.command == "entity.get");
    CHECK(back.host.frame == 81234);
    CHECK(back.data["entity"] == "MeshCube");

    UndoBlock refused;
    refused.label = nullptr;
    refused.undoable = false;
    refused.reason = "not-undoable-command";

    CHECK(ju["undo"]["label"].is_null());
    CHECK(ju["undo"]["undoable"] == false);
    CHECK(ju["undo"]["transaction"] == 0);
    CHECK(ju["undo"]["reason"] == "not-undoable-command");

    UndoBlock recorded;
    recorded.label = "remote: cvar.set";
    recorded.undoable = true;
    recorded.transaction = 412;
    env.undo = recorded;
    const auto jb = ToJson(env);
    CHECK(jb["undo"]["label"] == "remote: cvar.set");
    CHECK(jb["undo"]["undoable"] == true);
    CHECK(jb["undo"]["transaction"] == 412);
    CHECK_FALSE(jb["undo"].contains("reason"));
}

TEST_CASE("WorstExit ranks a dead host above a busy host", "[remote][protocol]") {
    CHECK(WorstExit(std::vector<int>{0, 5, 7}) == 7);
    CHECK(WorstExit(std::vector<int>{8, 6}) == 6);
    CHECK(WorstExit(std::vector<int>{5, 8, 4}) == 8);
    CHECK(WorstExit(std::vector<int>{3, 2}) == 3);
    CHECK(WorstExit(std::vector<int>{}) == 0);
}

TEST_CASE("MakeOk never carries errors", "[remote][protocol]") {

    CHECK(env.ok);
    CHECK(env.errors.empty());

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `ToJson`, `ExitFromHost`, and `WorstExit`**

First create the module (R8-1, ruling B-2). `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/ArcaneRemote.arcmodule`, modelled on `Source/Runtime/ArcaneNet/ArcaneNet.arcmodule`. It declares the whole dependency set the P0 tasks need, once (ruling C-1): ArcaneNet is public because `Listener.hpp` (Task 6) names `sockaddr_in`; the private Runtime modules are ArcaneEngine (`EditorLock::IsLive`, Tasks 7 and 14), ArcaneCrypto (`MakeToken`, `Sha256Hex`, Tasks 5 and 8), ArcaneScene (`ReflectionJsonWriter`, `Identity`, `Transform`, Tasks 4 and 23-25), ArcaneSim (ECS and play) and ArcaneAssets (`ImageIo`, `CompareImages`, Tasks 27-28). Every one is a Runtime module (R8-2; `allowed_edges` refuses anything else), and none of them depends on ArcaneRemote, so there is no cycle. Later tasks do not edit this file:

```json
{
  "name": "ArcaneRemote",

  "dependencies": {

      "ArcaneCore",

      "nlohmann"
    ],
    "private": []
  },
  "platforms": {}
}
```

`src/Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp` is `build/templates/Api.hpp.in` with `@MODULE@` = `REMOTE` and `@API_MACRO@` = `ARC_REMOTE_API`, byte for byte (premake's `api-header` check in `arc_modules_load`, `build/modules.lua`, fails the generate on any difference):

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <Arcane/Core/ModuleApi.hpp>

#if defined(ARC_MONOLITHIC) || defined(ARC_REMOTE_STATIC)
    #define ARC_REMOTE_API
#elif defined(ARC_REMOTE_EXPORTS)
```

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {

struct Call {
    std::string id;
    std::string command;
    nlohmann::json args = nlohmann::json::object();
    std::string world;          // options.world
    bool readOnly = false;      // options.readOnly
    std::uint32_t timeoutMs = 30000; // milliseconds, copied from the wire; the listener does not scale
    std::string callerId;
};

struct CommandResult {
    Envelope envelope;
    std::uint64_t completesJob = 0; // non-zero: dispatcher holds the waiter until that job finishes
};

using CommandFn = CommandResult (*)(const Call& call, void* user);

struct Desc {
    std::string name;           // dotted wire name, the value of Call::command
    std::string summary;
    Access access = Access::Observe;
    Mutates mutates = Mutates::None;
    PlayPolicy play = PlayPolicy::Any;
    ExecThread thread = ExecThread::Main;
    bool needsFrame = false;
    std::string module;         // empty = host
    const Astra::TypeMeta* argsMeta = nullptr;
    CommandFn fn = nullptr;
    void* user = nullptr;
    // Per-call access (ruling F19). When non-null, Task 8's state gate and its
    // audit use accessFor(call) instead of the static `access` (for example
    // capture.compare answers Drive when --bless is set). Last field, so the
    // ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND brace below still compiles.
    Access (*accessFor)(const Call& call) = nullptr;
};

class ARC_REMOTE_API CommandRegistry {
public:
    static CommandRegistry& Get();
    bool Register(Desc desc, std::string* error);
    void UnregisterModule(std::string_view module);
    std::uint32_t Generation() const;
    const Desc* Find(std::string_view name) const;
    std::vector<const Desc*> List() const;
    nlohmann::json SchemaOf(std::string_view name) const;
    nlohmann::json Catalogue() const;
    CommandResult Invoke(const Call& call);
    void SetHostKind(HostKind kind);   // HostSession::Start sets it (Task 11); default HostKind::Editor
    HostKind GetHostKind() const;
};

ARC_REMOTE_API nlohmann::json SchemaFor(const Astra::TypeMeta& meta); // declared in Schema.hpp, re-included by Registry.hpp

#define ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND(ident, nameLit, summaryLit, access, mutates, play, thread, ArgsType, fn) \
    static const bool ident = ::Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Register( \
        ::Arcane::Remote::Desc{(nameLit), (summaryLit), (access), (mutates), (play), (thread), \
                            false, {}, ::Astra::GetMeta<ArgsType>(), (fn), nullptr}, nullptr)
}
```

`Registry.hpp` includes `<Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Remote/Schema.hpp>`, and `<Astra/Reflection/MetaRegistry.hpp>`. `CommandRegistry::Get()` is the one instance in `ArcaneRemote` (exported), so a registration from another module's static initializer lands in the same table.

`argsMeta` is `::Astra::GetMeta<ArgsType>()` (`ThirdParty/Astra/include/Astra/Reflection/MetaRegistry.hpp:904`), which returns a `const TypeMeta*`, or null while the type is unregistered. There is no `Astra::TypeMetaOf`. An `ArgsType`'s `ARC_REFLECT_TYPE` block (an `inline` static registrar) must appear in the TU before the `ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND` line, so it initializes first; otherwise `argsMeta` is null and `Register` refuses. `Register` returns false when `summary` is empty, `name` is empty or has an empty segment or a segment outside `[a-z0-9]+` split on `.`, the name is already registered, `argsMeta == nullptr`, or `access == Drive && mutates == None`.

`UnregisterModule("")` does **not** drop host commands (`module` empty). `UnregisterModule("fixture")` drops that module and increments `Generation()`. `Catalogue()` is `{"generation": N, "commands":[...]}` sorted by name. `Invoke` on an unknown name returns `MakeError(call.command, "command.unknown", ..., "servitor commands")` and lists up to 8 sorted names that share the first segment in `candidates`. Ruling F-1 (spec s6.4): when the name is unregistered, starts with `editor.`, and `GetHostKind() != HostKind::Editor`, `Invoke` returns `command.host-kind` instead (exit 2, the exit table's row), message `<name> is an editor command; this host is <ToString(kind)>`, remediation `attach to an editor host`. Editor commands register from the editor program (R8-2), so a runtime or server never carries the name; every other unregistered name stays `command.unknown`. A REGISTERED command that cannot run on this host kind answers `command.host-kind` from its own function, reading `CommandRegistry::Get().GetHostKind()` (spec: an unsupported host kind is host-kind; Task 25's `scene.tree` on a server is the P0 case, ruling F8). `Invoke` does not apply the state gate (Task 8 does) and does not check `readOnly` (Task 8's gate does, from `call.readOnly`, ruling F15). `Desc::accessFor` is stored and returned by `Find`; `Invoke` does not read it (Task 8 does, ruling F19).

**Process-global state (ruling S3-9).** `CommandRegistry::Get()` is a process singleton, and so are the host kind and every pointer a later task's `Register*`/`Set*` stores. Tests never leave them changed: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/RemoteTestSupport.hpp` (created here, full code in Step 1) holds `ScopedHostKind` (sets the kind, restores the previous one on scope exit) and `ScopedRemotePointer<T>` (calls a setter with a pointer, and with `nullptr` on scope exit). Every Remote test that sets the host kind or registers a stateful command uses them. A command whose stored pointer is null answers `host.unavailable` (exit 5, Task 3's table) and never dereferences it.

`SchemaFor` is ruling P2 (b). It calls `Astra::JsonSchemaGenerator` with default `Options` (`includeDefaults` stays false; the Astra string has no `"default"`). It parses that string with `nlohmann::json::parse`. When `meta.isDefaultConstructible && meta.defaultConstruct`, it placement-news one instance, walks `meta.fields`, and writes each `IsSerializable()` field with `Arcane::ReflectionJsonWriter` (constructor `ReflectionJson.hpp:415`, `Visit` `:419`, in `Source/Runtime/ArcaneScene/src/Arcane/Serialization/`). It then sets `"default"` on each property from that JSON, including `$defs` entries reached through `$ref`. A type that is not default-constructible gets no `"default"` keys and `defaultConstruct` is not called. After the defaults are stamped, a property whose field `typeHash` equals `Astra::TypeID<glm::vec2>::Hash()` (and vec3, vec4, quat, mat3, mat4) is rewritten to `{"type":"array","minItems":N,"maxItems":N,"items":{"type":"number"}}` where N is 2, 3, 4, 4, 9, 16, and an existing `"default"` is copied onto the rewritten object. Quat's 4 is `[x,y,z,w]`. The writer already emits that array (`Arcane::Detail::WriteGlm` at `ReflectionJson.hpp:184` for vec2/3/4, `WriteMatrix` `:223` for mat3/mat4 column-major, `WriteQuat` `:280` for `[x,y,z,w]`). Walk `meta.fields` and `properties` together; skip hidden fields on both sides.

**Default path (the lane check matched).** `SchemaFor` parses Astra's string and stops there for shape. It does not define `ApplySchemaAdapter`, `EmitDef`, or `FieldIsOptional`, and it does not rewrite `required` or `$ref`. Astra already omitted `Optional` fields from `required` and already emitted `"$ref": "#/$defs/<typeName>"` plus one top-level `$defs` object. A `$defs` entry is `type`, `properties`, `required`, `additionalProperties`, and it does not contain a `"$defs"` key. The Arcane code after the parse is only the `ReflectionJsonWriter` default stamp and the glm array rewrite. No other task edits Astra or grows a schema generator.

**Fallback sub-task (the lane check did not match). Execute this only then.** Today's `Generate` still lists every non-hidden field in `required`, still emits `"type":"object"` for a nested class, and still ignores `includeDefaults`. This sub-task wraps that current behaviour behind the same adapter the lane would have shipped. It is not part of the default path. No other task changes. Define this in `Reflection.hpp`'s `namespace Arcane::Attr` block, after `using ::Astra::Precision;` (line 41), instead of `using ::Astra::Optional`, and do not edit the vendored header:

```cpp
struct Optional : ::Astra::AttributeBase<Optional> {
    constexpr Optional() noexcept = default;
};
```

`SchemaFor` detects it with `field.HasAttribute<Arcane::Attr::Optional>()`. After `nlohmann::json::parse` and before the `ReflectionJsonWriter` stamp, call `ApplySchemaAdapter`. The adapter (1) removes fields with that attribute from `required` and (2) rewrites a nested non-enum, non-glm field whose `GetMeta(typeHash)` is non-null into `"$ref": "#/$defs/<typeName>"`, emitting one top-level `$defs` object. The required list inside a def skips hidden fields and `Optional` fields. Walk pending defs by index, because writing a def appends its children. Defaults stay on the `ReflectionJsonWriter` path. glm fields are not refs; the default path's rewrite turns them into arrays. `IsGlmWire` is the helper the default path already has for that rewrite. The adapter code and its test are under **Fallback sub-task** after Step 3. Do not paste them into the default `SchemaFor`.

`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/RemoteTestSupport.hpp` (test-only; ruling S3-9; Task 6's `RemoteTestHttp.hpp` includes it):

```cpp
#pragma once
#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)
#include <Arcane/Remote/Registry.hpp>
#include <functional>
#include <utility>

namespace RemoteTest {

// Sets the process registry's host kind; restores the previous kind on exit.
class ScopedHostKind {
public:
    explicit ScopedHostKind(Arcane::Remote::HostKind kind)
        : m_before(Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().GetHostKind()) {
        Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().SetHostKind(kind);
    }
    ~ScopedHostKind() { Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().SetHostKind(m_before); }
    ScopedHostKind(const ScopedHostKind&) = delete;
    ScopedHostKind& operator=(const ScopedHostKind&) = delete;
private:
    Arcane::Remote::HostKind m_before;
};

// Calls setter(value) now and setter(nullptr) on scope exit, so a process-global
// command never keeps a pointer to a test's dead stack object. The setter is
// any callable taking T* (a Register*/Set* function, or a lambda adapting one;
// its return value is ignored).
template <class T>
class ScopedRemotePointer {
public:
    template <class Fn>
    ScopedRemotePointer(Fn setter, T* value)
        : m_set([setter](T* p) mutable { (void)setter(p); }) {
        m_set(value);
    }
    ~ScopedRemotePointer() { m_set(nullptr); }
    ScopedRemotePointer(const ScopedRemotePointer&) = delete;
    ScopedRemotePointer& operator=(const ScopedRemotePointer&) = delete;
private:
    std::function<void(T*)> m_set;
};

}  // namespace RemoteTest
#endif
```

```cpp
TEST_CASE("registry rejects an empty summary, a bad name, and Drive with mutates None", "[remote][registry]") {

    bad.summary = "";
    CHECK_FALSE(reg.Register(bad, &err));
    bad.summary = "does a thing";
    bad.name = "Demo.Ok";
    CHECK_FALSE(reg.Register(bad, &err));
    bad.name = "demo..ok";
    CHECK_FALSE(reg.Register(bad, &err));
    bad.name = "demo.drive";
    bad.access = Arcane::Remote::Access::Drive;
    bad.mutates = Arcane::Remote::Mutates::None;

}

TEST_CASE("schema rewrites vec3 and stamps ReflectionJsonWriter defaults", "[remote][registry]") {

    std::string err;

    REQUIRE(reg.Register(ok, &err));
    const auto schema = reg.SchemaOf("demo.ok");
    const auto& pos = schema["properties"]["position"];
    CHECK(pos["type"] == "array");
    CHECK(pos["minItems"] == 3);
    CHECK(pos["maxItems"] == 3);
    CHECK(pos["default"].is_array());
    CHECK(pos["default"].size() == 3);
    CHECK(pos["default"][0] == 0);
    CHECK(pos["default"][1] == 0);
    CHECK(pos["default"][2] == 0);
    CHECK(schema["properties"]["n"]["default"] == 3);
    CHECK_FALSE(reg.Register(ok, &err));
    reg.UnregisterModule("");
    CHECK(reg.Find("demo.ok") != nullptr);
    ok.module = "fixture";
    ok.name = "demo.mod";
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(ok, &err));
    const auto gen = reg.Generation();
    reg.UnregisterModule("fixture");
    CHECK(reg.Find("demo.mod") == nullptr);
    CHECK(reg.Generation() == gen + 1);
    CHECK(reg.Find("demo.ok") != nullptr);
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "no.such";
    const auto missing = reg.Invoke(call);
    CHECK(missing.envelope.errors[0].code == "command.unknown");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(missing.envelope) == 2);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement registry, schema defaults, and the `using`**

Default path only. Implement the registry rules in **Produces**. `List()` sorts by `name`. `SchemaFor` is this function. It does not hand-format scalars. The writer is the only producer of default JSON. `Schema.cpp` includes `<glm/vec2.hpp>`, `<glm/vec3.hpp>`, `<glm/vec4.hpp>`, `<glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>`, `<glm/mat3x3.hpp>`, `<glm/mat4x4.hpp>`, `<new>` (`std::align_val_t`), `<Astra/Reflection/JsonSchema.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Serialization/ReflectionJson.hpp>` (ArcaneScene, a private dependency Task 3 already declared), and `<Arcane/Reflection.hpp>`. All of it is portable (no OS call), so it compiles and its tests run on Windows, Linux and macOS (R8-7). `Reflection.hpp` has `using ::Astra::Optional;`. There is no `ApplySchemaAdapter`, no `EmitDef`, and no `FieldIsOptional` on this path. Do not rewrite `required` or `$ref`.

```cpp
namespace {

bool IsGlmWire(std::uint64_t typeHash) {
    return typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec2>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec3>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec4>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::quat>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::mat3>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::mat4>::Hash();
}

int GlmItemCount(std::uint64_t typeHash) {
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec2>::Hash()) return 2;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec3>::Hash()) return 3;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec4>::Hash()) return 4;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::quat>::Hash()) return 4;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::mat3>::Hash()) return 9;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::mat4>::Hash()) return 16;
    return 0;
}

}  // namespace

nlohmann::json Arcane::Remote::SchemaFor(const Astra::TypeMeta& meta)
{
    Astra::JsonSchemaGenerator gen;
    nlohmann::json schema = nlohmann::json::parse(gen.Generate(meta));
    if (meta.isDefaultConstructible && meta.defaultConstruct)
    {
        void* instance = ::operator new(meta.size, std::align_val_t{meta.alignment});
        meta.defaultConstruct(instance);
        nlohmann::json written = nlohmann::json::object();
        Arcane::ReflectionJsonWriter writer(written);
        for (const Astra::FieldInfo& field : meta.fields)
        {
            if (!field.IsHidden() && field.IsSerializable())
                writer.Visit(field, instance);
        }
        if (meta.destruct)
            meta.destruct(instance);
        ::operator delete(instance, std::align_val_t{meta.alignment});

        auto stamp = [&](auto&& self, nlohmann::json& node, const nlohmann::json& value) -> void {
            if (!node.contains("properties") || !value.is_object())
                return;
            for (auto it = node["properties"].begin(); it != node["properties"].end(); ++it)
            {
                if (!value.contains(it.key()))
                    continue;
                (*it)["default"] = value[it.key()];
                if (!it->contains("$ref") || !value[it.key()].is_object() || !schema.contains("$defs"))
                    continue;
                const std::string ref = (*it)["$ref"].get<std::string>();
                const auto slash = ref.rfind('/');
                if (slash == std::string::npos)
                    continue;
                const std::string defName = ref.substr(slash + 1);
                if (schema["$defs"].contains(defName))
                    self(self, schema["$defs"][defName], value[it.key()]);
            }
        };
        stamp(stamp, schema, written);
    }
    if (schema.contains("properties") && schema["properties"].is_object()) {
        for (const Astra::FieldInfo& field : meta.fields) {
            if (field.IsHidden() || !IsGlmWire(field.typeHash))
                continue;
            const std::string fname(field.name);
            if (!schema["properties"].contains(fname) || !schema["properties"][fname].is_object())
                continue;
            auto& prop = schema["properties"][fname];
            nlohmann::json keptDefault = prop.contains("default") ? prop["default"] : nlohmann::json();
            const int n = GlmItemCount(field.typeHash);
            prop = nlohmann::json{{"type", "array"}, {"minItems", n}, {"maxItems", n},
                                  {"items", {{"type", "number"}}}};
            if (!keptDefault.is_null())
                prop["default"] = std::move(keptDefault);
        }
    }
    return schema;
}
```

`RemoteSchemaNoDefault` has `isDefaultConstructible == false`, so the `if` does not run and the dump contains no `"default"`. `RemoteSchemaOuter`'s `plain` default is 7 and `RemoteSchemaInner.n` default is 4 because the writer read the live member initializers. Do not zero the instance before `defaultConstruct`. The glm loop runs after the stamp and keeps `"default"` when it replaces `"type"`. Quat's count is 4 and the writer's array is `[x,y,z,w]`.

#### Fallback sub-task: Arcane `$defs` emitter

Skip this entire sub-task when the lane check matched (`struct Optional` is in `ThirdParty/Astra/include/Astra/Reflection/Attribute.hpp`). On that path, copy the test case below into `RegistryTest.cpp` and do not copy the adapter. The case then passes because Astra already emitted `$ref`, one `$defs` object, and a `required` list without `Optional` fields. `Schema.cpp` still has no `ApplySchemaAdapter`.

Execute this sub-task only when `struct Optional` is absent. No other task changes. Add the local `Arcane::Attr::Optional` from **Interfaces**. Paste `FieldIsOptional`, `EmitDef`, and `ApplySchemaAdapter` into the anonymous namespace next to `IsGlmWire`. Call `ApplySchemaAdapter(schema, meta)` immediately after `nlohmann::json::parse` and before the default-construct stamp. Add the test case below to `RegistryTest.cpp`. These are this sub-task's tests. They fail on today's generator and pass once the adapter runs. Do not keep the adapter if a later sync adds `struct Optional`; delete the three functions, the call, and the local attribute, and leave the test.

```cpp
bool FieldIsOptional(const Astra::FieldInfo& field) {
    return field.HasAttribute<Arcane::Attr::Optional>();
}

void EmitDef(nlohmann::json& defs, const Astra::TypeMeta& meta,
             std::vector<const Astra::TypeMeta*>& pending) {
    const std::string name(meta.typeName);
    if (defs.contains(name))
        return;
    nlohmann::json def = nlohmann::json::object();
    def["type"] = "object";
    def["properties"] = nlohmann::json::object();
    def["additionalProperties"] = false;
    nlohmann::json required = nlohmann::json::array();
    for (const Astra::FieldInfo& field : meta.fields) {
        if (field.IsHidden())
            continue;
        const std::string fname(field.name);
        def["properties"][fname] = nlohmann::json::object();
        if (!FieldIsOptional(field))
            required.push_back(fname);
        const Astra::TypeMeta* nested = Astra::GetMeta(field.typeHash);
        if (nested == nullptr || nested->isEnum || IsGlmWire(field.typeHash) || nested == &meta)
            continue;
        def["properties"][fname]["$ref"] = std::string("#/$defs/") + std::string(nested->typeName);
        pending.push_back(nested);
    }
    if (!required.empty())
        def["required"] = std::move(required);
    defs[name] = std::move(def);
}

void ApplySchemaAdapter(nlohmann::json& schema, const Astra::TypeMeta& meta) {
    if (!schema.contains("properties") || !schema["properties"].is_object())
        schema["properties"] = nlohmann::json::object();
    if (!schema.contains("$defs") || !schema["$defs"].is_object())
        schema["$defs"] = nlohmann::json::object();
    nlohmann::json required = schema.contains("required") && schema["required"].is_array()
        ? schema["required"] : nlohmann::json::array();
    std::vector<const Astra::TypeMeta*> pending;
    for (const Astra::FieldInfo& field : meta.fields) {
        if (field.IsHidden())
            continue;
        const std::string fname(field.name);
        if (FieldIsOptional(field)) {
            nlohmann::json kept = nlohmann::json::array();
            for (const auto& item : required) {
                if (!item.is_string() || item.get<std::string>() != fname)
                    kept.push_back(item);
            }
            required = std::move(kept);
        }
        const Astra::TypeMeta* nested = Astra::GetMeta(field.typeHash);
        if (nested == nullptr || nested->isEnum || IsGlmWire(field.typeHash))
            continue;
        if (!schema["properties"].contains(fname) || !schema["properties"][fname].is_object())
            schema["properties"][fname] = nlohmann::json::object();
        auto& prop = schema["properties"][fname];
        if (!prop.contains("$ref"))
            prop["$ref"] = std::string("#/$defs/") + std::string(nested->typeName);
        prop.erase("type");
        pending.push_back(nested);
    }
    if (required.empty())
        schema.erase("required");
    else
        schema["required"] = std::move(required);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < pending.size(); ++i)
        EmitDef(schema["$defs"], *pending[i], pending);
    if (schema["$defs"].empty())
        schema.erase("$defs");
}
```

```cpp
TEST_CASE("fallback adapter drops Optional from required and emits one $defs object", "[remote][registry]") {
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    std::string err;
    Arcane::Remote::Desc ok;
    ok.name = "demo.opt";
    ok.summary = "optional field";
    ok.module = "fallback";
    ok.argsMeta = Astra::GetMeta<RemoteDemoArgs>();
    ok.fn = &DemoFn;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(ok, &err));
    const auto flat = reg.SchemaOf("demo.opt");
    REQUIRE(flat.contains("required")); // `position` is required, so the list exists (ruling S3-2)
    CHECK(flat["required"].dump().find("\"n\"") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(flat["required"].dump().find("\"position\"") != std::string::npos);
    reg.UnregisterModule("fallback");
    CHECK(reg.Find("demo.opt") == nullptr);

    const auto schema = Arcane::Remote::SchemaFor(*Astra::GetMeta<RemoteSchemaOuter>());
    CHECK(schema["properties"]["child"]["$ref"] == "#/$defs/RemoteSchemaInner");
    CHECK(schema["$defs"]["RemoteSchemaInner"]["properties"]["n"]["default"] == 4);
    CHECK_FALSE(schema["$defs"]["RemoteSchemaInner"].contains("$defs"));
    const auto& innerReq = schema["$defs"]["RemoteSchemaInner"];
    REQUIRE(innerReq.contains("required")); // ruling S3-2: no `if`
    CHECK(innerReq["required"].dump().find("\"n\"") != std::string::npos);
}
```

`RemoteSchemaInner.n` stays in that def's `required` because the field is not `Optional`. `RemoteDemoArgs.n` does not, because it is. The `$defs` object has no nested `"$defs"` key. On the default path this case is only the consumption check described above.

### Task 5: HTTP/1.1 parser and the s10.1 header checks

Parser only. Sockets, the peer check, and the idle close are Task 6. The token compare and the redaction helper live here so Task 6 and Task 14 share them.

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Http.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Http.cpp`
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/HttpTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `kMaxBodyBytes`, `kMaxHeaderBytes` (Task 3). `Arcane::Crypto::GenerateSecureToken(size_t byteCount)` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCrypto/src/Arcane/Crypto/Crypto.hpp:64`, static member of `class Crypto`; ArcaneCrypto is the private dependency Task 3 declared, and `Http.hpp` does not include `Crypto.hpp`). `Arcane::Crypto::HexEquals(const std::string&, const std::string&)` (`Crypto.hpp:286`; it takes `std::string`, so `CheckTransport` builds strings from its `std::string_view` arguments).
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {

struct HttpRequest {
    std::string method;
    std::string target;
    std::string version;
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> headers; // names lowercased
    std::string body;
};

struct HttpResponse {
    int status = 200;
    std::string reason = "OK";
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> headers;
    std::string body;
    ARC_REMOTE_API std::string Serialize() const;
};

enum class TransportRefuse { None, Origin, Host, Auth, Bounds, Chunked, Version, Throttled };

struct TransportCheck {
    TransportRefuse refuse = TransportRefuse::None;
    std::string code;       // auth.denied or auth.throttled
    std::string message;    // never contains the token
};

ARC_REMOTE_API std::optional<HttpRequest> ParseHttpRequest(std::string_view bytes, std::string* error);
ARC_REMOTE_API TransportCheck CheckTransport(const HttpRequest& req, std::string_view portDecimal,
                                          std::string_view tokenHex);
ARC_REMOTE_API std::string Header(const HttpRequest& req, std::string_view nameLower);
ARC_REMOTE_API std::string RedactSecrets(std::string_view text, std::string_view tokenHex);
ARC_REMOTE_API std::string MakeToken(); // Arcane::Crypto::GenerateSecureToken(32); 64 hex chars
}
```

`Http.hpp` includes `<Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp>`, `<optional>`, `<string>`, `<string_view>`, `<utility>`, `<vector>`, and is wrapped in `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)` like Task 3's files. `Http.cpp` includes `<Arcane/Remote/Http.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>` and `<Arcane/Crypto/Crypto.hpp>`. The code is portable C++ with no socket or OS call, so it compiles and its tests run on Windows, Linux and macOS (R8-7); no exclusion entry.

`ParseHttpRequest` requires `HTTP/1.1` (anything else is `error = "version"`). It rejects `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` (`error = "chunked"`). `Content-Length` over `kMaxBodyBytes` or a header block over `kMaxHeaderBytes` returns nullopt with `error = "bounds"`. A short read returns nullopt with `error = "partial"`. Header names are stored lowercased. The body is the bytes after `\r\n\r\n`, length `Content-Length` (0 if absent). Non-JSON `Content-Type` on a POST with a body is `error = "content-type"` (the listener maps that to `arg.invalid`, exit 2, and does not queue).

`CheckTransport`, in order:

1. Any header named `origin` → `Origin`, code `auth.denied`, message `origin is not accepted`.
2. `Host` equals `127.0.0.1:` + `portDecimal` or `localhost:` + `portDecimal`, case-insensitive. Anything else → `Host`, `auth.denied`.
3. `Authorization` must be `Bearer <tokenHex>` with a single space. Compare with `HexEquals`. Mismatch or absence → `Auth`, `auth.denied`. Do not put the received token in `message`.

`RedactSecrets` replaces every occurrence of `tokenHex` with `<redacted>` and every `Bearer` + 64 hex chars with `Bearer <redacted>`.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <rapidcheck/catch.h>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Http.hpp>

TEST_CASE("parser rejects HTTP/1.0, chunked, and an oversized body", "[remote][http]") {
    std::string err;
    auto v = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest("GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\n\r\n", &err);
    CHECK_FALSE(v);
    CHECK(err == "version");
    auto c = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest(
        "POST /remote/v1/call HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n\r\n", &err);
    CHECK_FALSE(c);
    CHECK(err == "chunked");
    std::string big = "POST /remote/v1/call HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nContent-Length: 5000000\r\n\r\n";
    auto b = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest(big, &err);
    CHECK_FALSE(b);
    CHECK(err == "bounds");
}

TEST_CASE("a good token does not survive Origin or a foreign Host", "[remote][http]") {
    const std::string token = Arcane::Remote::MakeToken();
    REQUIRE(token.size() == 64);
    std::string err;
    const std::string okRaw =
        "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n\r\n";
    auto ok = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest(okRaw, &err);
    REQUIRE(ok);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::CheckTransport(*ok, "9", token).refuse == Arcane::Remote::TransportRefuse::None);

    const std::string originRaw = okRaw.substr(0, okRaw.size() - 2) + "Origin: http://evil\r\n\r\n";
    auto withOrigin = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest(originRaw, &err);
    REQUIRE(withOrigin);
    auto origin = Arcane::Remote::CheckTransport(*withOrigin, "9", token);
    CHECK(origin.refuse == Arcane::Remote::TransportRefuse::Origin);
    CHECK(origin.code == "auth.denied");
    CHECK(origin.message.find(token) == std::string::npos);

    const std::string foreign =
        "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 10.0.0.8:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n\r\n";
    auto fr = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest(foreign, &err);
    REQUIRE(fr);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::CheckTransport(*fr, "9", token).refuse == Arcane::Remote::TransportRefuse::Host);

    const std::string local =
        "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n\r\n";
    auto lh = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest(local, &err);
    REQUIRE(lh);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::CheckTransport(*lh, "9", token).refuse == Arcane::Remote::TransportRefuse::None);

    const std::string bad =
        "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + std::string(64, 'a') + "\r\n\r\n";
    auto denied = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest(bad, &err);
    REQUIRE(denied);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::CheckTransport(*denied, "9", token).code == "auth.denied");
}

TEST_CASE("redaction removes the token and not the surrounding text", "[remote][http]") {
    const std::string token = Arcane::Remote::MakeToken();
    const std::string line = "Authorization: Bearer " + token + " host=127.0.0.1";
    const std::string out = Arcane::Remote::RedactSecrets(line, token);
    CHECK(out.find(token) == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(out.find("127.0.0.1") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(out.find("<redacted>") != std::string::npos);
}

TEST_CASE("property: header order does not admit Origin", "[remote][http]") {
    rc::prop("origin anywhere is refused", [] {
        const std::string token = Arcane::Remote::MakeToken();
        const auto noise = *rc::gen::container<std::string>(rc::gen::inRange<char>('a', 'z'));
        const std::string raw =
            "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nX-Noise: " + noise +
            "\r\nOrigin: http://x\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n\r\n";
        std::string err;
        auto req = Arcane::Remote::ParseHttpRequest(raw, &err);
        RC_ASSERT(req.has_value());
        RC_ASSERT(Arcane::Remote::CheckTransport(*req, "9", token).refuse == Arcane::Remote::TransportRefuse::Origin);
    });
}
```
