```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {

ARC_REMOTE_API bool PeerIsLoopback(const sockaddr_in& peer); // true only for 127.0.0.1

class ARC_REMOTE_API RequestQueue {
public:
    explicit RequestQueue(std::size_t depth = kQueueDepthDefault);
    bool TryPush(std::uint64_t id, Call call);          // false when full; does not run the command
    bool TryPop(std::uint64_t& id, Call& call);
    void Complete(std::uint64_t id, CommandResult result);
    bool Wait(std::uint64_t id, CommandResult& out, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout);
    bool TryTake(std::uint64_t id, CommandResult& out); // never waits; false if that id is not complete
    std::uint64_t OldestPendingId() const;              // earliest TryPush id not yet Complete; 0 if none
    using WakeFn = void (*)(void* user);
    void SetWake(WakeFn fn, void* user);                // Complete calls fn after publishing; null is legal
    std::size_t Depth() const;
};

enum class SseLife : std::uint8_t { Opened, Closed };

struct SseEvent {
    std::uint64_t subscriberId = 0;
    std::string topic;             // the topics= query value, e.g. "logs"
    SseLife life = SseLife::Opened;
};

// std::atomic<std::shared_ptr> is missing from Xcode 16 libc++. Same split as
// CVarRegistry.cpp:247-255 (plain shared_ptr + std::atomic_load_explicit /
// std::atomic_store_explicit under __APPLE__, std::atomic<std::shared_ptr> elsewhere).
// store/load keep std::atomic's spelling, so callers write atoms.status.store(snap).
template <class T>
class SharedSnapshot {
public:
    void store(std::shared_ptr<const T> next) noexcept;  // release
    std::shared_ptr<const T> load() const noexcept;      // acquire
private:
#if defined(__APPLE__)
    std::shared_ptr<const T> m_ptr;
#else
    std::atomic<std::shared_ptr<const T>> m_ptr;
#endif
};

struct ListenerAtoms {
    SharedSnapshot<HostStatus> status;
    SharedSnapshot<nlohmann::json> catalog; // GET /catalog snapshot
    std::mutex sseMu;
    std::vector<SseEvent> sseEvents; // listener pushes; TakeSseEvents swaps
};

ARC_REMOTE_API std::vector<SseEvent> TakeSseEvents(ListenerAtoms& atoms);

class ARC_REMOTE_API LoopbackHttpTransport {
public:
    LoopbackHttpTransport(RequestQueue& queue, ListenerAtoms& atoms, std::string tokenHex,
                          std::chrono::milliseconds idleClose = std::chrono::seconds(kIdleConnectionSeconds));
    void Start();
    void Stop();
    std::uint16_t Port() const;
    std::uintptr_t ListenSocket() const;
    std::chrono::milliseconds IdleClose() const;
    using PeerPredicate = bool (*)(const sockaddr_in& peer, void* user);
    // Default predicate is PeerIsLoopback with a null user. The accept loop is the only caller.
    void SetPeerPredicate(PeerPredicate pred, void* user);
    bool EnqueueSse(std::uint64_t subscriberId, std::string_view data); // false if that subscriber is gone
};

class ARC_REMOTE_API RemoteListener {
public:
    RemoteListener(RequestQueue& queue, ListenerAtoms& atoms, std::string tokenHex,
                std::chrono::milliseconds idleClose = std::chrono::seconds(kIdleConnectionSeconds));
    void Start();
    void Stop();
    std::uint16_t Port() const;
    std::uintptr_t ListenSocket() const;
    const std::string& Token() const;
    std::chrono::milliseconds IdleClose() const;
    ListenerAtoms& Atoms();
    LoopbackHttpTransport& Transport();
    void SetPeerPredicate(LoopbackHttpTransport::PeerPredicate pred, void* user);
    bool EnqueueSse(std::uint64_t subscriberId, std::string_view data);
};
}
```

`Start` calls `InitSockets()` then `CreateListenSocket(0, "127.0.0.1")`, then `SetNonBlocking` on that listen socket. `getsockname` (length type `socklen_t`, which `WS2tcpip.h` also defines) is how `Port()` learns the port. It also opens a loopback wake pair (listen on `127.0.0.1:0`, connect, accept, close the temporary listener) and `SetNonBlocking` on both ends. The loopback TCP pair is portable as written; POSIX does not need `socketpair`. `RequestQueue::SetWake` is installed so `Complete` sends one byte on the write end. A `send` for which `IsSocketTimeoutError()` is true means the listener is already due to wake; ignore that error. Production hosts use the default `idleClose`, which is `kIdleConnectionSeconds` (30). Tests may pass a shorter duration; no production call site passes one.

POSIX (R8-7): a `send` to a peer that has already closed raises `SIGPIPE`, which kills the host by default, and nothing in the tree's socket code suppresses it. On Linux every listener `send` (responses, SSE frames, the wake byte) passes `MSG_NOSIGNAL`. macOS has no `MSG_NOSIGNAL`, so the listener sets `SO_NOSIGPIPE` to 1 on each accepted socket and on both wake-pair ends. Windows has no `SIGPIPE` and passes flags 0. A `send` that fails this way returns `EPIPE`, and the connection moves to `Closing`.

One `ServiceThread` runs the loop below. It is the only thread that accepts, reads, and writes these sockets. It never calls `RequestQueue::Wait`, `Invoke`, or `CommandRegistry`.

```cpp
enum class ConnState : std::uint8_t { Reading, Parked, Streaming, Sending, Closing };

struct Conn {
    SocketType sock = INVALID_SOCK;
    ConnState state = ConnState::Reading;
    std::string inbuf;
    std::string outbuf;
    std::uint64_t callId = 0;
    std::uint64_t subscriberId = 0;
    std::string sseTopic;
    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point lastRead{};
};
```

Each iteration builds a `std::vector<PollInterest>`: the listen socket `wantRead`, the wake socket `wantRead`, and each connection. A connection in `Reading`, `Parked`, or `Streaming` sets `wantRead`. A connection with a non-empty `outbuf` sets `wantWrite`. `PollSockets(fds, 50)` is the wait. `50` is milliseconds, so `Stop` and the idle clock are noticed without a socket event.

When the listen socket is readable, `accept`. `IsSocketTimeoutError()` true (`WSAEWOULDBLOCK` / `EAGAIN` / `EWOULDBLOCK`) means the poll raced and there is no socket; leave the loop running. Any other accept error is logged and the loop continues. The accept loop, and only the accept loop, does this on that new socket:

1. `getpeername` on **that accepted socket** into a `sockaddr_in`. Call `m_peerPred(peer, m_peerUser)`. The default predicate is `PeerIsLoopback`. If `getpeername` fails or the predicate returns false, close **the accepted socket**, log `peer refused` plus the numeric address (`inet_ntop`) through `RedactSecrets` and `ARC_INFO`, and do not read it. Do not queue. Tests inject the predicate with `SetPeerPredicate` before `Start`. They do not call an accept helper with the client socket.
2. Otherwise `SetNonBlocking` (Linux `accept` does not inherit `O_NONBLOCK` from the listen socket, so call it on every platform), set `SO_NOSIGPIPE` on macOS, and push a `Conn` in `Reading` with `lastRead = now`.

When a `Reading` connection is readable, `recv` appends to `inbuf`. `n == 0` or a hard error moves it to `Closing`. A non-blocking would-block (`IsSocketTimeoutError()`) leaves the buffer and stays `Reading`. When `ParseHttpRequest` returns a request, run `CheckTransport` on that connection. Origin or a `Host` that is not `127.0.0.1:<port>` or `localhost:<port>` → append HTTP 403, body `MakeError("", "auth.denied", ...)`, exit derived as 3, state `Sending`. These do not consume the auth budget. Auth failure → `m_authLimiter.Allow("remote-auth", m_authConfig)` (the dedicated limiter and the 20/10/10 `Config` above). While `Allow` is true, HTTP 401, code `auth.denied`. When `Allow` is false, HTTP 429, code `auth.throttled`. Neither message contains the token. `Allow` returns true unconditionally while the process-wide `g_rateLimitingEnabled` is false (`RateLimiter.hpp:73`); only `RateLimiterTest.cpp` flips it, and it restores it. A forged `Host` is refused even when the bearer is valid and no `Origin` header is present. State becomes `Sending`.

The idle clock runs only while `state == Reading`. If `now - lastRead >= idleClose`, close the socket and drop the `Conn`. `Parked` and `Streaming` are not idle-closed: a `/call` that takes longer than 30 s, and an SSE connection that is quiet, stay open. The 200 ms test connects and sends nothing, so it stays `Reading` and still closes (`recv` returns 0).

Routes, after the checks. Every JSON response is `Content-Type: application/json`, `Content-Length` set to the body size, and `X-Remote-Protocol: 1`. The bytes are appended to `outbuf` and the state becomes `Sending`. Nothing in this thread blocks in `send`.

- `GET /remote/v1/status` — answered inline on this thread from the status `shared_ptr` via `ToJson`. Does not touch the queue. This runs while another connection is `Parked`.
- `GET /remote/v1/catalog` — answered inline from the catalog `shared_ptr`. Does not call `CommandRegistry`.
- `GET /remote/v1/stream?topics=` — SSE. Topics P0 serves: `logs`, `problems`, `state`, `catalog`, `job:<id>`. `watch:` is refused with `arg.inapplicable` exit 2 (public watch is P3). Counts toward `kMaxSseSubscribers` (8); the 9th gets `host.busy` / `detail.state = "connections"` / exit 5 and is not held open. A kept stream appends `HTTP/1.1 200` with `Content-Type: text/event-stream` and moves to `Streaming`. The listener assigns a monotonic subscriber id, pushes `SseEvent{id, topic, Opened}` under `sseMu`, and on socket close (recv 0, error, or `Stop`) pushes `SseEvent{id, topic, Closed}` and drops the socket. It does not arm a job. `EnqueueSse(id, data)` appends `data: <payload>\n\n` to that connection's `outbuf` under the connection mutex and sends one wake byte, or returns false when the id is already closed. `TakeSseEvents` swaps the vector out under the mutex. Follow jobs are Task 22.
- `POST /remote/v1/call` — body is s6.2: `{id, command, args, options:{world, readOnly, timeoutMs}}`. Unknown top-level keys → `arg.unknown` exit 2, not queued. Unknown keys inside `options` are ignored (rev 8.3 V6: new request fields arrive in `options`, so an older host tolerates a newer client); a test sends `options.futureFlag` and the call still runs. Missing `command` → `arg.missing` exit 2, not queued. A `protocol` member other than 1 → `protocol.mismatch` exit 4, not queued. Absent `protocol` means 1. Otherwise assign a monotonic `std::uint64_t` starting at 1 (the JSON `id` string is copied onto `Call::id` and is not the queue key) and `TryPush` that key. On false: HTTP 200, `host.busy`, `detail.state = "queue-full"`, exit 5, command not called, state `Sending`. On true: store the key on `Conn::callId`, set state `Parked`, and write no response yet. `TryPush` and that state assignment finish before the service thread returns to `PollSockets`, so `Depth() == 1` means the connection is already `Parked`. Do not call `Wait`. The listener thread does not call `Invoke`. Copy a present `options.timeoutMs` integer onto `Call::timeoutMs`, a present `options.world` string onto `Call::world` (Task 19's `ResolveWorld` reads it; ruling F14) and a present `options.readOnly` boolean onto `Call::readOnly` (Task 8's gate reads it; ruling F15). Absent `options` or absent `timeoutMs` leaves 30000. That integer is already milliseconds. The listener does not multiply it, and neither does `host.wait` when it copies `Call::timeoutMs` into `WaitArgs::timeoutMs` (Task 16).
- `POST /remote/v1/batch` and any other path → `command.unknown` exit 2. Do not implement batch.

When the wake socket is readable, `recv` until it would block, then for each `Parked` connection call `TryTake(callId, result)`. True means the main thread has `Complete`d that id (or the job finish path has). Append the HTTP 200 body `ToJson(result.envelope)` and set state `Sending`. False leaves the connection `Parked`. Task 29's `"step 10 advances host.frame by exactly 10 while status still answers"` is this same shape on a live job. Do not remove that test.

`Sending` with `wantWrite`: `send` what the socket accepts, erase that prefix, and on a would-block (`IsSocketTimeoutError()`) keep the rest. When `outbuf` is empty and the state is `Sending`, a JSON response goes back to `Reading` and refreshes `lastRead` (keep-alive). A `Streaming` connection stays `Streaming` with the socket open.

Connection cap `kMaxConnections` (16): the 17th is accepted and answered `host.busy` / `state:"connections"` / exit 5, then closed. The poll set is then at most the listen socket, the wake socket, and 17 connections, which is under Winsock's `FD_SETSIZE` (64); POSIX uses `poll`, which has no descriptor-number ceiling. `Stop` calls `RequestStop`, closes the listen socket, and `Complete`s every parked id with `MakeError("", "host.died", "listener stopped", "relaunch")` so the wake loop writes exit 7. A client drop while `Parked` (`recv` returns 0) closes that connection; a later `Complete` for its id finds no `Conn` and the result is discarded.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`SocketType` is a global `using` alias and `INVALID_SOCK` / `CloseSocket` are macros in `TcpSocket.hpp` (`:22-24` Windows, `:33-35` POSIX). Do not write `Arcane::SocketType` or `Arcane::Net::SocketType`. These cases run on the Windows, Linux and macOS lanes (`[remote][listener]` is not `[gpu]`), so they are written portably: `socklen_t` lengths, `Arcane::SetSocketTimeout` (`TcpSocket.hpp:65`) instead of a `DWORD` `SO_RCVTIMEO`, `static_cast<int>` on `send` / `recv` results (POSIX returns `ssize_t`), and `SocketType` instead of `SOCKET`. No exclusion entry is needed.

`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/RemoteTestHttp.hpp` is the one copy of the loopback client helpers (ruling S3-5). Tasks 22 and 29 include it; nothing else defines `HttpExchange`, a connect helper or a receive loop:

```cpp
#pragma once
#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp>
#include "RemoteTestSupport.hpp"
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <string>

namespace RemoteTest {

// A blocking TCP socket connected to 127.0.0.1:port. REQUIREs success.
inline SocketType ConnectLoopback(std::uint16_t port) {
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType sock = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    REQUIRE(sock != INVALID_SOCK);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(port);
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(sock, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    return sock;
}

// Sends every byte. REQUIREs one full send (requests here are small).
inline void SendAll(SocketType sock, const std::string& bytes) {
    REQUIRE(static_cast<int>(::send(sock, bytes.data(), static_cast<int>(bytes.size()), 0)) ==
            static_cast<int>(bytes.size()));
}

// Reads until `marker` is in the accumulated bytes, the peer closes, or
// `budget` passes. Each recv waits at most 100 ms, so the loop never overruns
// the budget by more than that. Returns everything read.
inline std::string RecvUntil(SocketType sock, const std::string& marker, std::chrono::milliseconds budget) {
    Arcane::SetSocketTimeout(sock, 100);
    std::string raw;
    char buf[4096];
    const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + budget;
    while (raw.find(marker) == std::string::npos && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline) {
        const int n = static_cast<int>(::recv(sock, buf, sizeof(buf), 0));
        if (n > 0) {
            raw.append(buf, buf + n);
            continue;
        }
        if (n == 0 || !Arcane::IsSocketTimeoutError())
            break; // closed, or a hard error
    }
    return raw;
}

// One request/response on a fresh connection. `extraHeaders` is zero or more
// complete "Name: value\r\n" lines. Returns the raw HTTP response.
inline std::string HttpExchangeHost(std::uint16_t port, const std::string& token,
                                    const std::string& hostHeader, const std::string& extraHeaders,
                                    const std::string& requestLine, const std::string& body = {}) {
    const SocketType sock = ConnectLoopback(port);
    std::string req = requestLine + "\r\nHost: " + hostHeader +
        "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n";
    if (!body.empty()) {
        req += "Content-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: " + std::to_string(body.size()) + "\r\n";
    }
    req += extraHeaders;
    req += "\r\n";
    req += body;
    SendAll(sock, req);
    std::string raw;
    char buf[4096];
    for (;;) {
        const int n = static_cast<int>(::recv(sock, buf, sizeof(buf), 0));
        if (n <= 0) break;
        raw.append(buf, buf + n);
        const auto hdr = raw.find("\r\n\r\n");
        if (hdr == std::string::npos) continue;
        const auto key = raw.find("Content-Length:");
        if (key == std::string::npos || key > hdr) break;
        const int len = std::atoi(raw.c_str() + key + 15);
        if (static_cast<int>(raw.size() - (hdr + 4)) >= len) break;
    }
    CloseSocket(sock);
    return raw;
}

inline std::string HttpExchange(std::uint16_t port, const std::string& token,
                                const std::string& extraHeaders, const std::string& requestLine,
                                const std::string& body = {}) {
    return HttpExchangeHost(port, token, "127.0.0.1:" + std::to_string(port),
                            extraHeaders, requestLine, body);
}

}  // namespace RemoteTest
#endif
```

`ListenerTest.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Listener.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Base/Log.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp>
#include "RemoteTestHttp.hpp"
#include <chrono>
#include <mutex>
#include <span>
#include <thread>

using RemoteTest::HttpExchange;
using RemoteTest::HttpExchangeHost;

TEST_CASE("the listen socket is bound to 127.0.0.1", "[remote][listener]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<const Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>());
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    sockaddr_in bound{};
    socklen_t len = sizeof(bound);
    REQUIRE(::getsockname(static_cast<SocketType>(listener.ListenSocket()),
                          reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&bound), &len) == 0);
    char text[64] = {};
    inet_ntop(AF_INET, &bound.sin_addr, text, sizeof(text));
    CHECK(std::string(text) == "127.0.0.1");
    CHECK(listener.Port() == ntohs(bound.sin_port));
    CHECK(listener.Port() != 0);
    CHECK(listener.IdleClose() == std::chrono::seconds(Arcane::Remote::kIdleConnectionSeconds));
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::kIdleConnectionSeconds == 30);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("status requires the bearer and answers from the atomic", "[remote][listener]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    snap->frame = 4;
    snap->hostKind = Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime;
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    const std::string body = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "", "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(body.find("\"state\":\"ready\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(body.find("\"frame\":4") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(body.find(listener.Token()) == std::string::npos);
    const std::string denied = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), std::string(64, '0'), "", "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(denied.find("auth.denied") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(denied.find("auth.rejected") == std::string::npos);
    auto env = Arcane::Remote::ParseEnvelope(nlohmann::json::parse(denied.substr(denied.find("\r\n\r\n") + 4)));
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(env) == 3);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("a good token does not survive Origin", "[remote][listener]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    const std::string origin = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "Origin: http://evil\r\n",
                                            "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(origin.find("403") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(origin.find("auth.denied") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(origin.find("\"state\":\"ready\"") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(origin.find(listener.Token()) == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 0);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("a forged Host is refused by the listener", "[remote][listener]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    const std::string port = std::to_string(listener.Port());
    const std::string forged = HttpExchangeHost(listener.Port(), listener.Token(),
                                                "10.0.0.8:" + port, "",
                                                "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(forged.find("403") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(forged.find("auth.denied") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(forged.find("\"state\":\"ready\"") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(forged.find("Origin") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 0);
    const std::string ok = HttpExchangeHost(listener.Port(), listener.Token(),
                                            "127.0.0.1:" + port, "",
                                            "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(ok.find("\"state\":\"ready\"") != std::string::npos);
    listener.Stop();
}

struct RemoteSeenPeer {
    sockaddr_in peer{};
    int calls = 0;
};

static bool RemoteRefusePeer(const sockaddr_in& peer, void* user) {
    auto* seen = static_cast<RemoteSeenPeer*>(user);
    seen->peer = peer;
    ++seen->calls;
    return false;
}

TEST_CASE("the accept loop closes a peer the predicate refuses", "[remote][listener]") {
    sockaddr_in forged{};
    forged.sin_family = AF_INET;
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "10.0.0.8", &forged.sin_addr);
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::Remote::PeerIsLoopback(forged));
    sockaddr_in loop{};
    loop.sin_family = AF_INET;
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &loop.sin_addr);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::PeerIsLoopback(loop));

    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    RemoteSeenPeer seen{};
    listener.SetPeerPredicate(&RemoteRefusePeer, &seen);
    listener.Start();
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType client = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(listener.Port());
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(client, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    const std::string req = "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:" +
        std::to_string(listener.Port()) + "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + listener.Token() + "\r\n\r\n";
    REQUIRE(static_cast<int>(::send(client, req.data(), static_cast<int>(req.size()), 0)) == static_cast<int>(req.size()));
    Arcane::SetSocketTimeout(client, 2000);
    char buf[512];
    const int n = static_cast<int>(::recv(client, buf, sizeof(buf), 0));
    std::string raw = n > 0 ? std::string(buf, buf + n) : std::string();
    CHECK(seen.calls == 1);
    char text[64] = {};
    inet_ntop(AF_INET, &seen.peer.sin_addr, text, sizeof(text));
    CHECK(std::string(text) == "127.0.0.1");
    // FIN (0) or, when the request bytes were already queued on the refused
    // socket at close, a reset (-1). Never response bytes.
    CHECK(n <= 0);
    CHECK(raw.find("200") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(raw.find("ready") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 0);
    CloseSocket(client);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("an idle connection is closed and the production limit is 30s", "[remote][listener]") {
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::kIdleConnectionSeconds == 30);
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<const Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>());
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken(), std::chrono::milliseconds(200));
    CHECK(listener.IdleClose() == std::chrono::milliseconds(200));
    listener.Start();
    const SocketType client = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(listener.Port());
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(client, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    Arcane::SetSocketTimeout(client, 2000);
    char buf[8];
    const int n = static_cast<int>(::recv(client, buf, sizeof(buf), 0));
    CHECK(n == 0);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 0);
    CloseSocket(client);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("POST /remote/v1/call reads command and the 65th is queue-full", "[remote][listener]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue(64);
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<const Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>());
    atoms.catalog.store(std::make_shared<const nlohmann::json>(nlohmann::json::object()));
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    for (std::uint64_t i = 1; i <= 64; ++i) {
        Arcane::Remote::Call call;
        call.id = std::to_string(i);
        call.command = "demo.look";
        REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(i, call));
    }
    const std::string payload = R"({"id":"r-65","command":"demo.look","args":{}})";
    const std::string body = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "",
                                          "POST /remote/v1/call HTTP/1.1", payload);
    CHECK(body.find("\"code\":\"host.busy\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(body.find("queue-full") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(body.find("\"command\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 64);
    const auto env = Arcane::Remote::ParseEnvelope(nlohmann::json::parse(body.substr(body.find("\r\n\r\n") + 4)));
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(env) == 5);
    const std::string catalog = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "", "GET /remote/v1/catalog HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(catalog.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    listener.Stop();
}

// The subscriber runs on the listener's logging thread; the test thread reads
// the text only after listener.Stop() and Unsubscribe, under the mutex (ruling F29).
struct RemoteSpill {
    std::mutex mu;
    std::string text;
};

static void RemoteSpillSink(const Arcane::LogRecord& rec, void* user) {
    auto* spill = static_cast<RemoteSpill*>(user);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(spill->mu);
    spill->text += rec.text;
    spill->text.push_back('\n');
}

TEST_CASE("a refused request does not log the token", "[remote][listener]") {
    RemoteSpill spill;
    const auto sub = Arcane::Log::Subscribe(&RemoteSpillSink, &spill);
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<const Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>());
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    const std::string forged(64, 'b'); // the bearer this client actually sends
    const std::string denied = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), forged, "", "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(denied.find("auth.denied") != std::string::npos);
    const std::string realToken = listener.Token();
    listener.Stop();
    Arcane::Log::Unsubscribe(sub);
    std::string seen;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(spill.mu);
        seen = spill.text;
    }
    CHECK(seen.find(forged) == std::string::npos);    // the refused bearer is never logged
    CHECK(seen.find(realToken) == std::string::npos); // nor is the listener's own token
}

TEST_CASE("PollSockets reports one readable socket and times out the idle one", "[remote][listener]") {
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType listen = Arcane::CreateListenSocket(0, "127.0.0.1");
    REQUIRE(listen != INVALID_SOCK);
    sockaddr_in bound{};
    socklen_t len = sizeof(bound);
    REQUIRE(::getsockname(listen, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&bound), &len) == 0);

    Arcane::PollInterest before[1];
    before[0].socket = listen;
    before[0].wantRead = true;
    CHECK(Arcane::PollSockets(before, 0) == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(before[0].readable);

    const SocketType client = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    REQUIRE(client != INVALID_SOCK);
    REQUIRE(::connect(client, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&bound), sizeof(bound)) == 0);
    Arcane::PollInterest listening[1];
    listening[0].socket = listen;
    listening[0].wantRead = true;
    CHECK(Arcane::PollSockets(listening, 200) == 1);
    CHECK(listening[0].readable);

    sockaddr_in peerAddr{};
    socklen_t peerLen = sizeof(peerAddr);
    const SocketType accepted = ::accept(listen, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&peerAddr), &peerLen);
    REQUIRE(accepted != INVALID_SOCK);
    REQUIRE(Arcane::SetNonBlocking(accepted, true));
    REQUIRE(Arcane::SetNonBlocking(client, true));
    const char byte = 'Q';
    REQUIRE(static_cast<int>(::send(client, &byte, 1, 0)) == 1);

    const SocketType idleListen = Arcane::CreateListenSocket(0, "127.0.0.1");
    REQUIRE(idleListen != INVALID_SOCK);
    sockaddr_in idleBound{};
    socklen_t idleLen = sizeof(idleBound);
    REQUIRE(::getsockname(idleListen, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&idleBound), &idleLen) == 0);
    const SocketType idleClient = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    REQUIRE(::connect(idleClient, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&idleBound), sizeof(idleBound)) == 0);
    sockaddr_in idlePeer{};
    socklen_t idlePeerLen = sizeof(idlePeer);
    const SocketType idleAccepted = ::accept(idleListen, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&idlePeer), &idlePeerLen);
    REQUIRE(idleAccepted != INVALID_SOCK);
    REQUIRE(Arcane::SetNonBlocking(idleAccepted, true));

    Arcane::PollInterest both[2];
    both[0].socket = accepted;
    both[0].wantRead = true;
    both[1].socket = idleAccepted;
    both[1].wantRead = true;
    const int ready = Arcane::PollSockets(both, 200);
    CHECK(ready >= 1);
    CHECK(both[0].readable);
    CHECK_FALSE(both[1].readable);
    char got = 0;
    REQUIRE(static_cast<int>(::recv(accepted, &got, 1, 0)) == 1);
    CHECK(got == 'Q');
    char extra = 0;
    const int again = static_cast<int>(::recv(accepted, &extra, 1, 0));
    CHECK(again < 0);
#ifdef _WIN32
    CHECK(WSAGetLastError() == WSAEWOULDBLOCK);
#else
    CHECK(errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK);
#endif

    Arcane::PollInterest idleOnly[1];
    idleOnly[0].socket = idleAccepted;
    idleOnly[0].wantRead = true;
    const auto t0 = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    const int idleRc = Arcane::PollSockets(idleOnly, 50);
    const auto ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - t0).count();
    CHECK(idleRc == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(idleOnly[0].readable);
    CHECK(ms >= 40);
    CHECK(ms < 500);

    CloseSocket(client);
    CloseSocket(accepted);
    CloseSocket(listen);
    CloseSocket(idleClient);
    CloseSocket(idleAccepted);
    CloseSocket(idleListen);
}

TEST_CASE("a parked call does not stop status", "[remote][listener]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType caller = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(listener.Port());
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(caller, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    const std::string payload = R"({"id":"r-park","command":"demo.look","args":{}})";
    const std::string req = "POST /remote/v1/call HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:" +
        std::to_string(listener.Port()) + "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + listener.Token() +
        "\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: " + std::to_string(payload.size()) +
        "\r\n\r\n" + payload;
    REQUIRE(static_cast<int>(::send(caller, req.data(), static_cast<int>(req.size()), 0)) == static_cast<int>(req.size()));
    const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(2);
    while (queue.Depth() == 0 && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
    REQUIRE(queue.Depth() == 1);
    const std::string status = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "",
                                            "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(status.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(status.find("\"state\":\"ready\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 1);
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult done;
    done.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("demo.look", nlohmann::json::object(), {});
    const std::uint64_t parked = queue.OldestPendingId();
    REQUIRE(parked != 0);
    queue.Complete(parked, done);
    Arcane::SetSocketTimeout(caller, 2000);
    std::string raw;
    char buf[4096];
    const auto readDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(2);
    while (raw.find("demo.look") == std::string::npos && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < readDeadline) {
        const int n = static_cast<int>(::recv(caller, buf, sizeof(buf), 0));
        if (n > 0) raw.append(buf, buf + n);
        else break;
    }
    CHECK(raw.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(raw.find("demo.look") != std::string::npos);
    CloseSocket(caller);
    listener.Stop();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the poll primitives and the one-thread listener**

Add these inside `namespace Arcane` after `ConnectSocketWithTimeout` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneNet/src/Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp:276-396`), before the length-frame banner at `:398`. Do not change that function. Add `#include <span>` and `#include <vector>` to the standard includes (`:38-41`). On the POSIX branch, `<fcntl.h>` is already included (`:31`); add `#include <poll.h>` beside it.

```cpp
struct PollInterest {
    SocketType socket = INVALID_SOCK;
    bool wantRead = false;
    bool wantWrite = false;
    bool readable = false;
    bool writable = false;
    bool hungup = false;
};

inline bool SetNonBlocking(SocketType sock, bool nonBlocking)
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    u_long mode = nonBlocking ? 1u : 0u;
    return ::ioctlsocket(sock, FIONBIO, &mode) == 0;
#else
    const int flags = ::fcntl(sock, F_GETFL, 0);
    if (flags < 0)
        return false;
    const int next = nonBlocking ? (flags | O_NONBLOCK) : (flags & ~O_NONBLOCK);
    return ::fcntl(sock, F_SETFL, next) == 0;
#endif
}

inline int PollSockets(std::span<PollInterest> fds, int timeoutMs)
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    fd_set readSet, writeSet, errSet;
    FD_ZERO(&readSet);
    FD_ZERO(&writeSet);
    FD_ZERO(&errSet);
    for (PollInterest& fd : fds) {
        fd.readable = false;
        fd.writable = false;
        fd.hungup = false;
        if (fd.socket == INVALID_SOCK)
            continue;
        if (fd.wantRead)
            FD_SET(fd.socket, &readSet);
        if (fd.wantWrite)
            FD_SET(fd.socket, &writeSet);
        FD_SET(fd.socket, &errSet);
    }
    timeval tv{};
    timeval* tvp = nullptr;
    if (timeoutMs >= 0) {
        tv.tv_sec = timeoutMs / 1000;
        tv.tv_usec = (timeoutMs % 1000) * 1000;
        tvp = &tv;
    }
    const int sel = ::select(0, &readSet, &writeSet, &errSet, tvp); // Winsock ignores nfds
    if (sel <= 0)
        return sel;
    int count = 0;
    for (PollInterest& fd : fds) {
        if (fd.socket == INVALID_SOCK)
            continue;
        fd.readable = FD_ISSET(fd.socket, &readSet) != 0;
        fd.writable = FD_ISSET(fd.socket, &writeSet) != 0;
        fd.hungup = FD_ISSET(fd.socket, &errSet) != 0;
        if (fd.readable || fd.writable || fd.hungup)
            ++count;
    }
    return count;
#else
    // poll, not select: select's fd_set is indexed by descriptor NUMBER, and
    // FD_SET on a descriptor >= FD_SETSIZE (1024) overflows it.
    std::vector<pollfd> pfds(fds.size());
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < fds.size(); ++i) {
        PollInterest& fd = fds[i];
        fd.readable = false;
        fd.writable = false;
        fd.hungup = false;
        pfds[i].fd = fd.socket; // INVALID_SOCK (-1) is skipped by poll
        pfds[i].events = static_cast<short>((fd.wantRead ? POLLIN : 0) | (fd.wantWrite ? POLLOUT : 0));
        pfds[i].revents = 0;
    }
    const int rc = ::poll(pfds.data(), static_cast<nfds_t>(pfds.size()), timeoutMs);
    if (rc <= 0)
        return rc;
    int count = 0;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < fds.size(); ++i) {
        PollInterest& fd = fds[i];
        const short re = pfds[i].revents;
        fd.readable = (re & POLLIN) != 0;
        fd.writable = (re & POLLOUT) != 0;
        fd.hungup = (re & (POLLERR | POLLHUP | POLLNVAL)) != 0;
        if (fd.readable || fd.writable || fd.hungup)
            ++count;
    }
    return count;
#endif
}
```

`timeoutMs < 0` waits until a socket is ready (`tvp` is null on Windows; `poll` takes a negative timeout as infinite). `0` polls. A positive value waits that many milliseconds. Return `-1` on `select` / `poll` error (including `EINTR`; the listener loop simply iterates again), `0` on timeout, otherwise the number of interests that became readable, writable, or hung up. Windows ignores `select`'s `nfds` argument, so the Windows branch passes 0 (`ConnectSocketWithTimeout` passes `sock + 1` at `:344` only because its call is shared with POSIX). Both branches clear and refill `readable` / `writable` / `hungup` on every call.

Implement `SharedSnapshot<T>::store` / `load` inline in `Listener.hpp`: under `__APPLE__`, `std::atomic_store_explicit(&m_ptr, std::move(next), std::memory_order_release)` and `std::atomic_load_explicit(&m_ptr, std::memory_order_acquire)`; elsewhere `m_ptr.store(std::move(next), std::memory_order_release)` and `m_ptr.load(std::memory_order_acquire)` (the pattern at `CVarRegistry.cpp:1161-1165` and `:1477-1481`).

The listener is the state machine in **Interfaces**. Bind `"127.0.0.1"` only. Log every refusal through `RedactSecrets` and `ARC_INFO` (engine logs go to stderr). `TryTake` returns false immediately when that id has no published result. `Wait` stays for tests and other callers; a timeout of 0 returns immediately too, and the service thread still does not call it. `Complete` stores the result, then calls the wake function if one was set. `OldestPendingId` is the smallest key passed to `TryPush` that `Complete` has not yet been called with. The peer predicate runs on the address `getpeername` filled from the accepted socket. `CheckTransport`'s Host refusal is what answers the forged-Host test; deleting that branch turns the forged request into `"state":"ready"`. Deleting the inline `/status` path, or answering `/status` only after `Wait` returns, fails `"a parked call does not stop status"`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Filter `[remote][listener]`. Expected: PASS. `getsockname` is `127.0.0.1`. The forged Host response is 403 `auth.denied` and the same listener still answers a `127.0.0.1` Host with `"state":"ready"`. The injected predicate is called once with `127.0.0.1` and the accepted socket is closed (`recv` returns 0, or -1 when the close reset a connection whose request bytes were already queued) with no response bytes and queue depth 0. The 200 ms idle socket reads 0. The default `IdleClose()` on the first test is 30 seconds. The log subscriber, read under its mutex after `Stop()`, never sees the forged `bbbb...` bearer or the listener's token. The catalog GET is 200. `PollSockets` on the idle accepted socket returns 0 in at least 40 ms and under 500 ms, and the socket that was sent `'Q'` is readable. While a `POST /remote/v1/call` is parked (`Depth() == 1` and nobody has `Complete`d it), `GET /remote/v1/status` returns 200 and `"state":"ready"`. After `Complete(OldestPendingId())`, the parked client reads a 200 body containing `demo.look`.

  ```cpp
  namespace Arcane::Remote {
  struct HttpReply {
      int status = 0;
      std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> headers; // names lowercased
      std::string body;
  };
  // "POST <target> HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:<port>\r\nAuthorization: Bearer <token>\r\n
  //  Content-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: <n>\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n<body>"
  ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API std::string BuildPost(std::string_view target, std::uint16_t port,
                                                std::string_view token, std::string_view jsonBody);
  // The same shape for GET (no body, Content-Length: 0), used for /remote/v1/status and /catalog.
  ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API std::string BuildGet(std::string_view target, std::uint16_t port, std::string_view token);
  // Parses one complete response: status line, headers, a Content-Length body. nullopt sets *error
  // to "partial" (need more bytes; the host closes after each response, so EOF before the body is
  // complete is "truncated"), "truncated", or "malformed". Chunked bodies are "malformed".
  ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API std::optional<HttpReply> ParseHttpReply(std::string_view bytes, bool eof, std::string* error);
  }
  ```

A wrong port is refused as `Host`. `ParseHttpReply` reads `HttpResponse::Serialize()`'s output (Task 5) back to the same status and body. TEST_CASE("remote-protocol.json matches the protocol constants", "[remote][protocol]") {
```cpp
    std::ifstream in(Arcane::Test::RepoRoot() / "Source" / "Runtime" / "ArcaneRemoteProtocol" / "remote-protocol.json");
    REQUIRE(in.good());

}

TEST_CASE("ArcaneRemoteProtocol depends on ArcaneCore only", "[remote][protocol]") {
    std::ifstream in(Arcane::Test::RepoRoot() / "Source" / "Runtime" / "ArcaneRemoteProtocol" / "ArcaneRemoteProtocol.arcmodule");
    REQUIRE(in.good());

    CHECK(j.at("dependencies").at("public") == nlohmann::json::array({"ArcaneCore"}));
    CHECK(j.at("dependencies").at("private").empty());
    CHECK(j.at("thirdParty").at("public") == nlohmann::json::array({"nlohmann"}));
    CHECK(j.at("thirdParty").at("private").empty());

TEST_CASE("envelopes carry protocolMinor and tolerate unknown fields", "[remote][protocol]") {
    auto env = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("x.y", nlohmann::json{{"a", 1}}, {});
    auto j = Arcane::Remote::ToJson(env);
    CHECK(j.at("protocolMinor") == Arcane::Remote::kProtocolMinor);
    // A newer host: a later minor, plus fields this build has never heard of.
    j["protocolMinor"] = 7;
    j["futureTopLevel"] = "x";
    j["host"]["futureHostField"] = 3;
    j["data"]["futureDataField"] = true;
    const auto parsed = Arcane::Remote::TryParseEnvelope(j);
    REQUIRE(parsed.has_value());
    CHECK(parsed->ok);
    CHECK(parsed->protocolMinor == 7);
    CHECK(parsed->data.at("a") == 1);
    // An older host: no protocolMinor at all reads 0.
    j.erase("protocolMinor");
    const auto older = Arcane::Remote::TryParseEnvelope(j);
    REQUIRE(older.has_value());
    CHECK(older->protocolMinor == 0);
    // A present non-integer is malformed.
    j["protocolMinor"] = "seven";
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::Remote::TryParseEnvelope(j).has_value());
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EditorLock::Info` (`Project.hpp:174-178`), `EditorLock::Self()` (declared `Project.hpp:187`, defined `Project.cpp:800`; on POSIX it fills `start` from `Platform::QueryProcess`). `Platform::ProcessStat` / `Platform::QueryProcess(pid)` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Process.hpp:47-55`, declared only under `ARC_PLATFORM_POSIX`; `Project.cpp` reaches it through the anonymous `ReadProcStat` at `:793`). From `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Paths.hpp`: `Location` (`:26`; `ProjectSaved` `:30`, `EditorUserDir` `:31`), `ForProject` (`:49`), `Join` (`:54`), `Get` (`:57`), `EnsureDir` (`:59`). Task 3 `HostStatus`. `MakeToken` (Task 5).
- Produces (`IsProcessLive` in ArcaneCore; `IsLive` in ArcaneEngine; the rest in `Descriptor.hpp`, ArcaneRemoteProtocol, each carrying `ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API` (rev 8.3 V1)):

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Platform {
ARC_CORE_API bool IsProcessLive(std::uint32_t pid, std::uint64_t start); // start 0 = any start
}

namespace Arcane::EditorLock {
ARC_ENGINE_API bool IsLive(const Info& info); // return Platform::IsProcessLive(info.pid, info.start);
}

namespace Arcane::Remote {
struct Descriptor {
    std::uint32_t protocol = kProtocol;
    HostKind hostKind = HostKind::Editor;
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::uint64_t processStart = 0;
    std::uint16_t port = 0;
    std::string token;
    std::string project;
    std::int64_t heartbeatUnixMs = 0;
    std::string state;
    std::uint64_t frame = 0;
    std::uint32_t abi = 0;  // kGamePluginABIVersion of the host that wrote it (ruling F27); HostSession::Start writes it
};

ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API std::filesystem::path DescriptorPath(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot, HostKind kind, std::uint32_t pid);
ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API std::filesystem::path DescriptorDir(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot, bool projectless);
ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API bool WriteDescriptorAtomic(const std::filesystem::path& path, const Descriptor& desc);
ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API std::optional<Descriptor> ReadDescriptor(const std::filesystem::path& path);
ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API void TouchHeartbeat(const std::filesystem::path& path, std::int64_t unixMs, std::string state, std::uint64_t frame);
ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API bool IsHung(const Descriptor& desc, std::int64_t nowUnixMs); // heartbeat older than 10s
ARC_REMOTE_PROTOCOL_API bool IsStale(const Descriptor& desc); // !Platform::IsProcessLive(pid, processStart); never EditorLock (rev 8.3 V2)
}
```

`DescriptorPath` = `<projectRoot>/Saved/Remote/<hostKind>-<pid>.json` with `hostKind` `editor`, `runtime`, or `server`. Build it the way `EditorLock::FileFor` builds `editor.lock` (`Project.cpp:755-758`): `Paths::Join(Paths::Location::ProjectSaved, Paths::ForProject(projectRoot), std::filesystem::path("Remote") / name)`. Do not write `/ "Saved"`: `PathsGuardTest.cpp` scans `Source/Runtime` and fails on a hand-built `/ "Saved"` join. `DescriptorDir(root, false)` is the same `Join` with `"Remote"`. Projectless dir is `Paths::Get(Paths::Location::EditorUserDir) / "Remote"`. `WriteDescriptorAtomic` creates the parent directory.

`WriteDescriptorAtomic` writes `path + ".tmp"` and makes it owner-only **before** the rename (spec AM5), then renames it over `path`. Failure deletes the temp and returns false.
- Windows: apply SDDL `D:P(A;;FA;;;OW)(A;;FA;;;SY)` (`ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptorW`, then `SetFileSecurityW` or `SetNamedSecurityInfoW`) to the temp file **before** `MoveFileExW(..., MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING)`, then apply it again to the final path. advapi32 is already in every Windows module's link set.
- POSIX (`#else`): create the temp with `::open(tmp, O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_TRUNC | O_CLOEXEC, 0600)`, then `::fchmod(fd, 0600)` (an existing temp keeps its old mode otherwise), write, `::close`, then `std::filesystem::rename` (atomic replace on POSIX). This is the spec's AM5 POSIX rule.
- Do not call `EditorLock::ReadLive` on this file.

`Platform::IsProcessLive` is the three tells already in `ReadLive`, moved verbatim, both platforms (rev 8.3 V2; the tells below are written in terms of `info`, which is `{pid, start}`):
- Windows (`Project.cpp:847-877`): `OpenProcess`, `GetProcessTimes` creation equals `info.start` when `info.start != 0`, and the exit time is zero.
- POSIX (`Project.cpp:878-888`): `ReadProcStat(info.pid)` (`Platform::QueryProcess`) is `found`, its `start` equals `info.start` when `info.start != 0`, and it is not `exited`.

`abi` is a plain JSON number in the descriptor file; `ReadDescriptor` reads it back (a missing key reads 0). `ReadDescriptor` is tolerant (rev 8.3 V6). It ignores unknown keys and returns any non-negative integer `protocol` as written, never only 1. A newer host's descriptor therefore reaches Task 15's `Select`, which answers `protocol.mismatch` (exit 4) instead of `target.none`. Add a `[remote][descriptor]` CHECK: a descriptor with `"protocol": 2` and an extra `"futureField"` reads back with `protocol == 2`. Task 11's `HostSession::Start` writes it from `kGamePluginABIVersion` (`<Arcane/Core/AbiVersion.hpp>`), and Task 15's `StatusData` reads it into the status row (ruling F27).

A start flipped by 1 is not live on any platform. `ReadLive` parses the lock file and then returns `IsLive(*info) ? std::optional(info->pid) : std::nullopt`. `IsHung` is `nowUnixMs - heartbeatUnixMs > 10000`. A hung descriptor is reported, not deleted. The token is in the file and is not logged by `WriteDescriptorAtomic`. `ToJson` of a status snapshot has no token; do not add one.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Descriptor.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Http.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Project/Project.hpp>
#include <filesystem>

TEST_CASE("Self is live and a flipped start time is not", "[remote][descriptor]") {
    const Arcane::EditorLock::Info self = Arcane::EditorLock::Self();
    REQUIRE(self.pid != 0);
    REQUIRE(self.start != 0);
    CHECK(Arcane::EditorLock::IsLive(self));
    Arcane::EditorLock::Info recycled = self;
    recycled.start += 1;
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::EditorLock::IsLive(recycled));
}

TEST_CASE("descriptor write is atomic, owner-only, and a stale pid is not live", "[remote][descriptor]") {
    const auto dir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-desc-test";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(dir);
    const Arcane::EditorLock::Info self = Arcane::EditorLock::Self();
    const auto path = Arcane::Remote::DescriptorPath(dir, Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime, self.pid);
    Arcane::Remote::Descriptor d;
    d.protocol = 1;
    d.hostKind = Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime;
    d.pid = self.pid;
    d.processStart = self.start;
    d.port = 9;
    d.token = Arcane::Remote::MakeToken();
    d.state = "ready";
    d.heartbeatUnixMs = 1;
    d.abi = 61;
    REQUIRE(Arcane::Remote::WriteDescriptorAtomic(path, d));
    std::filesystem::path tmp = path;
    tmp += ".tmp";
    CHECK_FALSE(std::filesystem::exists(tmp));
#if !defined(_WIN32)
    namespace fs = std::filesystem;
    CHECK((fs::status(path).permissions() & fs::perms::all) == (fs::perms::owner_read | fs::perms::owner_write));
#endif
    auto back = Arcane::Remote::ReadDescriptor(path);
    REQUIRE(back);
    CHECK(back->token == d.token);
    CHECK(back->abi == 61);
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::Remote::IsStale(*back));
    back->processStart += 1;
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::IsStale(*back));
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::IsHung(*back, back->heartbeatUnixMs + 10001));
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::Remote::IsHung(*back, back->heartbeatUnixMs + 10000));
    std::filesystem::remove_all(dir);
}
```

### Task 8: Dispatcher — budget, audit, and one job step per drain (Task 9 executes before Task 8)

**Order (ruling F1): Task 9 executes before Task 8.** Task 9 creates `Jobs.hpp/.cpp` and the `HostGate` half of `Dispatcher.hpp/.cpp`; this task adds `RemoteDispatcher` to those two files. The task numbers are unchanged.

- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.cpp` (created by Task 9 with the `HostGate` methods; add the dispatcher, the gate, the audit and the two cvars)
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCrypto/src/Arcane/Crypto/Crypto.hpp` (ArcaneCrypto owns it; add `Sha256Hex` in the public section right after `HmacSha256Hex`, `:276-279`; add `#include <string_view>` to the includes at `:4-13`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RequestQueue` (Task 6), `CommandRegistry::Invoke`, `Desc::accessFor` (Task 4), `Envelope`, `UndoBlock` (Task 3), and from Task 9 (already executed, ruling F1): `HostGate` (`Dispatcher.hpp`), `JobTable`, `JobId`, `Job`, `JobState`, `JobTable::StepAll`, `BindWaiter(JobId, std::uint64_t waiterId, std::optional<UndoBlock> undo)`, `SetQueue(RequestQueue*)`, `Arm`, `Find` (`Jobs.hpp`). `ARC_CVAR` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Config/CVarDecl.hpp:26`). `CVarFlags::Dev` (`CVarTypes.hpp:42`), `Audience::Server` (`:111`), `SettingScope::Project` (`:117-122`).
- Produces (added to `Dispatcher.hpp`, ArcaneRemote, below Task 9's `HostGate`):

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {

class ARC_REMOTE_API RemoteDispatcher {
public:
    RemoteDispatcher(RequestQueue& queue, CommandRegistry& registry, HostGate& gate,
                  JobTable& jobs, std::filesystem::path auditPath);
    void Drain(double budgetMs, std::uint32_t budgetCommands);
    std::uint32_t LastDrainRan() const;
};

ARC_REMOTE_API double FrameBudgetMsSetting();        // published remote.server.frameBudgetMs
ARC_REMOTE_API std::uint32_t QueueDepthSetting();    // published remote.server.queueDepth; HostSession (Task 11) sizes its RequestQueue with it

}
```

The constructor calls `jobs.SetQueue(&queue)` (ruling F2), so every job finish path can `Complete` its waiter. A later dispatcher over the same `JobTable` replaces the pointer.

`Drain` order, every call:

1. `jobs.StepAll(gate)` — each running job's step function runs **at most once**. This is not charged against `budgetCommands`. When a step returns true, `StepAll` itself stamps `envelope.host = gate.Block()` (and the undo block the waiter was bound with) and `Complete`s that job's waiter (Task 9).
2. Then pop commands until the queue is empty, `budgetCommands` commands have been invoked, or `budgetMs` has elapsed (check the clock before starting each command; a command already started finishes).
3. A command with `ExecThread::Job` is invoked. Its `fn` arms a job and returns. If `CommandResult::completesJob != 0`, `Drain` does **not** `Complete` the waiter. It calls `jobs.BindWaiter(JobId{r.completesJob}, id, r.envelope.undo)` (ruling F2), where `id` is the queue key it popped, so the job's finish path (`StepAll` or `Finish`) `Complete`s that key with the job's final envelope. The main thread returns from `Drain` with that job still `Running`.

Two Dev cvars (spec s4.3; ruling B-1 adds the second), declared at namespace scope in `Dispatcher.cpp` with `ARC_CVAR` (designated initializers in `CVarSpec` order, `CVarDecl.hpp:14-16`). Each default is the Task 3 constant, so the value has one spelling:

```cpp
ARC_CVAR(cvar_remoteServerFrameBudgetMs, "remote.server.frameBudgetMs", double, kFrameBudgetMs,
    .flags = CVarFlags::Dev, .audience = Audience::Server, .scope = SettingScope::Project,
    .help = "Milliseconds of remote commands drained before the next tick.");
ARC_CVAR(cvar_remoteServerQueueDepth, "remote.server.queueDepth", std::uint32_t, kQueueDepthDefault,
    .flags = CVarFlags::Dev, .audience = Audience::Server, .scope = SettingScope::Project,
    .help = "Remote requests a host queues before it answers host.busy queue-full.");
```

`FrameBudgetMsSetting()` and `QueueDepthSetting()` return the published values (Dist's registry refuses Dev cvars and the handles read the declared defaults). A positive `Drain` argument wins over the budget cvar. Both names get their frozen-names line and inventory rows in this task (Files, ruling C-3).

Gate, before `Invoke`. The effective access is `desc.accessFor ? desc.accessFor(call) : desc.access` (ruling F19); the gate and the audit both use it:

- `(gate.readOnly || call.readOnly) && access == Drive` → `permission.read-only` exit 3. `fn` not called. (`gate.readOnly` is the host's `ARC_REMOTE_READ_ONLY` arming, D-3; `call.readOnly` is the request's `options.readOnly`, which Task 14's client posts; ruling F15.)
- `desc.play == EditOnly && state == "playing"` → `host.busy` `detail.state = "playing"` exit 5. `fn` not called.
- `desc.play == PlayingOnly && state` is neither `playing` nor `paused` → `host.busy` with the current state, exit 5. `fn` not called.
- `desc.needsFrame && gate.minimized` → `host.busy` `detail.state = "minimized"` exit 5. `fn` not called. (The check lands here so the flag has one implementation; Task 13's test is its regression pin.)

Every completed envelope gets `envelope.host = gate.Block()`, `envelope.command` left as the call's command, and `timing` filled from the queue time and the invoke time. Do not write `hostFrame` or a top-level `exitCode`.

Audit one ndjson line per command whose effective access (above) is **Drive** and that was actually invoked, at `auditPath` (`Saved/Remote/out`'s sibling `Saved/Remote/audit.ndjson`; the host that constructs the dispatcher builds it with `Paths::Join(Paths::Location::ProjectSaved, ...)`, never a hand-built `/ "Saved"`, which `PathsGuardTest.cpp` refuses under `Source/Runtime`). Spec s10.4 fields, these names: `time` (unix ms), `client` (callerId), `pid`, `command`, `argsDigest` (hex SHA-256 of canonical `args.dump()` with sorted keys), `outcome` (`"ok"` or `"error"`), `errorCode` (empty on ok), `frame`. Do not write the args. Observe commands are not audited. On construction, create `auditPath.parent_path()` if it is missing, then prune `auditPath.parent_path() / "out"` to the newest 200 files by `last_write_time`. Tests pass a per-test subdirectory, `temp_directory_path() / "remote-dispatch-<case>" / "audit.ndjson"`, never a file directly in the OS temp dir, so the prune only ever touches a directory the test owns (ruling C-4).

If one `Invoke` takes longer than `10 * budgetMs`, `ARC_WARN` once with the command name. Do not fail the command.

`Sha256Hex`:

```cpp
static std::string Sha256Hex(std::string_view bytes);
```

on `Arcane::Crypto` (a class of static members, not a namespace), next to `HmacSha256Hex` (`Crypto.hpp:276`), using the SHA-256 primitive HMAC already calls (private `SHA256_Raw(const std::vector<uint8_t>&)`, `:397`; build the vector from `bytes`) and the existing private `ToHex` (`:408`). Inline in the header. `Crypto.cpp` already exists and holds only `Detail::PlatformFillOverrideSlot`; do not add code to it, and do not add another `.cpp`. The dispatcher reaches `Crypto` through ArcaneRemote's private ArcaneCrypto dependency (R8-1).

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp>

static int g_ran = 0;
static Arcane::Remote::CommandResult Boom(const Arcane::Remote::Call& call, void*) {
    ++g_ran;
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult r;
    r.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(call.command, nlohmann::json::object(), Arcane::Remote::HostBlock{});
    return r;
}

static Arcane::Remote::Desc MakeDesc(const char* name, Arcane::Remote::Access access,
                                  Arcane::Remote::Mutates mutates, Arcane::Remote::PlayPolicy play) {
    Arcane::Remote::Desc d;
    d.name = name;
    d.summary = "dispatch test";
    d.access = access;
    d.mutates = mutates;
    d.play = play;
    d.module = "dispatch-test";
    d.argsMeta = Astra::GetMeta<RemoteDemoArgs>();
    d.fn = &Boom;
    return d;
}

TEST_CASE("a busy gate does not run the command", "[remote][dispatch]") {
    g_ran = 0;
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(MakeDesc("demo.boom", Arcane::Remote::Access::Drive,
                                  Arcane::Remote::Mutates::PlayMode, Arcane::Remote::PlayPolicy::PlayingOnly), &err));
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.SetState("ready");
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    const auto audit = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-dispatch-audit" / "audit.ndjson";
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit);
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "demo.boom";
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(1, call));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_ran == 0);
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult done;
    REQUIRE(queue.Wait(1, done, std::chrono::milliseconds(1000)));
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(done.envelope) == 5);
    CHECK(done.envelope.errors[0].code == "host.busy");
    CHECK(done.envelope.errors[0].detail["state"] == "ready");
    CHECK_FALSE(done.envelope.data.contains("hostFrame"));
    gate.SetState("playing");
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(2, call));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_ran == 1);
    reg.UnregisterModule("dispatch-test");
}

    look.command = "demo.look";

    REQUIRE(queue.Wait(1, denied, std::chrono::milliseconds(1000)));

}

static int g_steps = 0;
static bool StepOnce(Arcane::Remote::Job& job, Arcane::Remote::HostGate& gate, void*) {
    ++g_steps;
    gate.frame.fetch_add(1);
    job.progress.done += 1;
    job.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(job.command, nlohmann::json{{"done", job.progress.done}}, gate.Block());
    return job.progress.done >= job.progress.total;
}

TEST_CASE("one Drain steps a frame job once and still runs a queued command", "[remote][dispatch]") {
    g_ran = 0;
    g_steps = 0;
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(MakeDesc("demo.look", Arcane::Remote::Access::Observe,
                                  Arcane::Remote::Mutates::None, Arcane::Remote::PlayPolicy::Any), &err));
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    const auto audit = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-dispatch-job" / "audit.ndjson";
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit);
    const auto id = jobs.Arm("play.step", 10, &StepOnce, nullptr);
    Arcane::Remote::Call look;
    look.command = "demo.look";
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(1, look));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_steps == 1);
    CHECK(g_ran == 1);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(gate.frame.load() == 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 9; ++i)
        dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_steps == 10);
    CHECK(gate.frame.load() == 10);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_steps == 10);
    reg.UnregisterModule("dispatch-test");
}

// Drops the module even when a REQUIRE aborts the case (ruling S3-9).
struct RemoteUnregisterOnExit {
    const char* module;
    ~RemoteUnregisterOnExit() { Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().UnregisterModule(module); }
};

static bool ThreeSteps(Arcane::Remote::Job& job, Arcane::Remote::HostGate&, void*) {
    job.progress.done += 1;
    job.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(job.command, nlohmann::json{{"done", job.progress.done}},
                                       Arcane::Remote::HostBlock{});
    return job.progress.done >= job.progress.total;
}

static Arcane::Remote::CommandResult ArmThree(const Arcane::Remote::Call& call, void* user) {
    auto* jobs = static_cast<Arcane::Remote::JobTable*>(user);
    const Arcane::Remote::JobId id = jobs->Arm(call.command, 3, &ThreeSteps, nullptr);
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult r;
    r.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(call.command, nlohmann::json{{"job", id.value}}, Arcane::Remote::HostBlock{});
    Arcane::Remote::UndoBlock undo;
    undo.reason = "runtime-host";
    r.envelope.undo = undo;
    r.completesJob = id.value;
    return r;
}

TEST_CASE("a Job-thread command's waiter completes after its job's last drain", "[remote][dispatch]") {
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    RemoteUnregisterOnExit unreg{"dispatch-test"};
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::Desc slow = MakeDesc("demo.slow", Arcane::Remote::Access::Observe,
                                      Arcane::Remote::Mutates::None, Arcane::Remote::PlayPolicy::Any);
    slow.thread = Arcane::Remote::ExecThread::Job;
    slow.fn = &ArmThree;
    slow.user = &jobs;
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(slow, &err));
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.kind = "runtime";
    const auto audit = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-dispatch-waiter" / "audit.ndjson";
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit); // calls jobs.SetQueue(&queue)
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "demo.slow";
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(1, call));
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult out;
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);           // invokes: arms the job, binds waiter 1, no Complete
    CHECK_FALSE(queue.TryTake(1, out));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);           // step 1
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);           // step 2
    CHECK_FALSE(queue.TryTake(1, out));
    gate.frame.store(7);
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);           // step 3 finishes: StepAll Completes waiter 1
    REQUIRE(queue.TryTake(1, out));
    CHECK(out.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(out.envelope.data["done"] == 3);
    CHECK(out.envelope.host.kind == "runtime");   // stamped from gate.Block() at finish
    CHECK(out.envelope.host.frame == 7);
    REQUIRE(out.envelope.undo.has_value());       // the armed envelope's undo block rides through
    CHECK(out.envelope.undo->reason == "runtime-host");
}

TEST_CASE("a request-level readOnly refuses Drive", "[remote][dispatch]") {
    g_ran = 0;
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    RemoteUnregisterOnExit unreg{"dispatch-test"};
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(MakeDesc("demo.drive", Arcane::Remote::Access::Drive,
                                  Arcane::Remote::Mutates::Preferences, Arcane::Remote::PlayPolicy::Any), &err));
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(MakeDesc("demo.look", Arcane::Remote::Access::Observe,
                                  Arcane::Remote::Mutates::None, Arcane::Remote::PlayPolicy::Any), &err));
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;          // the host itself is NOT read-only
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    const auto audit = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-dispatch-callro" / "audit.ndjson";
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit);
    Arcane::Remote::Call drive;
    drive.command = "demo.drive";
    drive.readOnly = true;               // options.readOnly on the wire (ruling F15)
    Arcane::Remote::Call look;
    look.command = "demo.look";
    look.readOnly = true;
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(1, drive));
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(2, look));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult denied;
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult allowed;
    REQUIRE(queue.TryTake(1, denied));
    REQUIRE(queue.TryTake(2, allowed));
    CHECK(denied.envelope.errors[0].code == "permission.read-only");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(denied.envelope) == 3);
    CHECK(allowed.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(g_ran == 1);
}

static Arcane::Remote::Access DriveWhenBless(const Arcane::Remote::Call& call) {
    return call.args.value("bless", false) ? Arcane::Remote::Access::Drive : Arcane::Remote::Access::Observe;
}

TEST_CASE("accessFor makes a bless Drive: read-only refuses it and the audit records it", "[remote][dispatch]") {
    g_ran = 0;
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    RemoteUnregisterOnExit unreg{"dispatch-test"};
    Arcane::Remote::Desc compare = MakeDesc("demo.compare", Arcane::Remote::Access::Observe,
                                         Arcane::Remote::Mutates::Project, Arcane::Remote::PlayPolicy::Any);
    compare.accessFor = &DriveWhenBless;   // ruling F19
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(compare, &err));
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.readOnly = true;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    const auto dir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-dispatch-bless";
    std::filesystem::remove_all(dir);
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, dir / "audit.ndjson");
    Arcane::Remote::Call bless;
    bless.command = "demo.compare";
    bless.args = nlohmann::json{{"bless", true}};
    Arcane::Remote::Call plain;
    plain.command = "demo.compare";
    plain.args = nlohmann::json{{"bless", false}};
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(1, bless));
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(2, plain));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult refused;
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult ran;
    REQUIRE(queue.TryTake(1, refused));
    REQUIRE(queue.TryTake(2, ran));
    CHECK(refused.envelope.errors[0].code == "permission.read-only");
    CHECK(ran.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(g_ran == 1);
    // Not read-only: the bless runs and is audited as Drive; the plain compare is not audited.
    gate.readOnly = false;
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(3, bless));
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(4, plain));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_ran == 3);
    std::ifstream in(dir / "audit.ndjson");
    std::string body((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(in)), std::istreambuf_iterator<char>());
    std::size_t lines = 0;
    for (char c : body)
        lines += c == '\n' ? 1u : 0u;
    CHECK(lines == 1);
    CHECK(body.find("\"command\":\"demo.compare\"") != std::string::npos);
}
```
