### Task 9: Internal job table (s7.7) — executes before Task 8

**Order (ruling F1): this task executes before Task 8**, right after Task 7. Task 8's dispatcher holds a `JobTable&`, and its tests arm jobs, so the table must exist first. To break the header cycle, `Jobs.hpp` forward-declares `struct HostGate;`, and this task also creates `Dispatcher.hpp/.cpp` holding only `HostGate` (Task 8 adds `RemoteDispatcher` to those same files). `Dispatcher.hpp` includes `Jobs.hpp`; `Jobs.hpp` never includes `Dispatcher.hpp`. The task numbers are unchanged.

Public `servitor job` and `--detach` are P3. This table is what screenshot, compare, wait, and `play.step` arm.

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Jobs.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Jobs.cpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp` (`HostGate` only, plus `#include <Arcane/Remote/Jobs.hpp>`; Task 8 adds the dispatcher)
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.cpp` (`HostGate::State`, `SetState`, `Block` only)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/JobsTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Envelope`, `HostBlock`, `UndoBlock` (Task 3, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp`) and `RequestQueue`, `CommandResult` (Tasks 6 and 4; `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Listener.hpp`, `Registry.hpp`).
- Produces: `HostGate` (in `Dispatcher.hpp`; every later task that names `HostGate` includes `<Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp>`), and the s7.7 row `{id, command, state, progress{done,total,label}, startedFrame, envelope}`.

```cpp
// Dispatcher.hpp (this task creates it; Task 8 appends RemoteDispatcher below HostGate)
#include <Arcane/Remote/Jobs.hpp>
namespace Arcane::Remote {

struct ARC_REMOTE_API HostGate {
    std::atomic<bool> minimized{false};
    std::atomic<bool> readOnly{false};
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> frame{0};
    std::atomic<std::int64_t> heartbeatUnixMs{0};
    std::string state = "ready";
    mutable std::mutex stateMutex; // State() and Block() are const and lock it
    std::string world;
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::string kind = "editor";
    std::uint32_t catalog = 0;
    std::uint32_t pluginGeneration = 0;
    std::string State() const;
    void SetState(std::string s);
    HostBlock Block() const; // fills HostBlock from these fields
};
}
```

`Jobs.hpp` and `Jobs.cpp` are born inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)` (Global Constraints, Dist). `JobTable` is exported `ARC_REMOTE_API` (`#include <Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp>`); the plain structs need no export. `JobsTest.cpp` is wrapped in the same `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`: CI builds and runs ArcaneTests in Dist (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:148`, matrix `[Debug, Release, Dist]`), where no Remote symbol exists.

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {

struct JobId { std::uint64_t value = 0; };

enum class JobState : std::uint8_t { Queued, Running, Done, Failed, Cancelled };

struct JobProgress {
    std::uint64_t done = 0;
    std::uint64_t total = 0;
    std::string label;
};

struct HostGate;      // defined in Dispatcher.hpp, which includes this header (ruling F1)
class RequestQueue;   // Listener.hpp (Task 6)
struct Job;
using JobStepFn = bool (*)(Job& job, HostGate& gate, void* user); // true = finished

struct Job {
    JobId id;
    std::string command;
    JobState state = JobState::Queued;
    JobProgress progress;
    std::uint64_t startedFrame = 0;
    Envelope envelope;
    std::int64_t finishedUnixMs = 0;
    JobStepFn step = nullptr;
    void* user = nullptr;
    std::uint64_t waiterId = 0; // RequestQueue id to Complete when the job finishes; 0 = none
    std::optional<UndoBlock> undo;  // the arming envelope's undo block (BindWaiter); copied onto the final envelope
    std::uint64_t subscriberId = 0; // SSE follow jobs only; 0 = not a follow job
};

class ARC_REMOTE_API JobTable {
public:
    JobId Arm(std::string command, std::uint64_t total, JobStepFn step, void* user);
    // Task 8's Drain calls this with the queue key and the arming envelope's undo block (ruling F2).
    void BindWaiter(JobId id, std::uint64_t waiterId, std::optional<UndoBlock> undo = std::nullopt);
    void SetSubscriber(JobId id, std::uint64_t subscriberId);
    void CancelBySubscriber(std::uint64_t subscriberId); // Running rows with that id become Cancelled
    void StepAll(HostGate& gate);          // each Running job, at most one step; a finished step Completes its waiter
    void Finish(JobId id, Envelope result, JobState state);
    const Job* Find(JobId id) const;
    bool AnyRunning(std::initializer_list<std::string_view> commands) const;
    void Prune(std::int64_t nowUnixMs);    // finished older than 10 min, then cap 64
    std::size_t FinishedCount() const;
    void SetQueue(RequestQueue* queue);    // StepAll and Finish Complete waiterId when non-zero; Task 8's RemoteDispatcher constructor calls it
};
}
```

`Jobs.hpp` includes `<Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>`, `<cstdint>`, `<initializer_list>`, `<mutex>`, `<optional>`, `<string>`, `<string_view>`, `<vector>`, and forward-declares `HostGate` and `RequestQueue` (it does not include `Dispatcher.hpp` or `Listener.hpp`). `Jobs.cpp` includes `<Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp>` (for `HostGate::Block`) and `<Arcane/Remote/Listener.hpp>` (for `RequestQueue::Complete`).

**Waiter completion (ruling F2).** One rule for both finish paths. When `StepAll`'s step returns true, `StepAll` sets `job.envelope.host = gate.Block()`, sets `job.envelope.undo = job.undo` when the envelope has none and `job.undo` is set, sets the state and `finishedUnixMs`, and then, when `waiterId != 0` and a queue is set, calls `queue->Complete(waiterId, CommandResult{job.envelope, 0})`. `Finish` does the same (it has no gate, so its caller's envelope keeps the `host` it was built with). Each waiter is completed exactly once: completing clears `waiterId`. `Complete` is called after the table mutex is released (it calls the listener's wake function). `AnyRunning` returns true when any row whose `command` is one of the names is `Running`. A finished or `Cancelled` row does not count. Ruling P4 (a) uses it so a second capture or compare does not `Arm`. `Arm` assigns an id starting at 1, state `Running`, `progress.total = total`, `progress.done = 0`. `StepAll` calls `step` once. When `step` returns true, state becomes `Done` if `envelope.ok`, otherwise `Failed`, and `finishedUnixMs` is set. `Finish` does the same for a caller that already has the envelope (a capture that failed before arming does not leave a `Running` row). `Prune` drops finished jobs with `now - finishedUnixMs > 600000`, then drops the oldest finished until at most 64 remain. Running jobs are never pruned.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp>   // HostGate (this task)
#include <Arcane/Remote/Jobs.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Listener.hpp>     // RequestQueue (Task 6)
#include <chrono>
#include <vector>

TEST_CASE("finished jobs prune at 10 minutes and at 64", "[remote][job]") {
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable table;
    std::vector<Arcane::Remote::JobId> ids;
    for (int i = 0; i < 70; ++i) {
        auto id = table.Arm("capture.viewport", 1, nullptr, nullptr);
        table.Finish(id, Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("capture.viewport", nlohmann::json::object(), {}),
                     Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
        ids.push_back(id);
    }
    table.Prune(0);
    CHECK(table.FinishedCount() == 64);
    CHECK(table.Find(ids.front()) == nullptr);
    CHECK(table.Find(ids.back()) != nullptr);
    auto live = table.Arm("host.wait", 1, nullptr, nullptr);
    table.Prune(9'000'000'000);
    CHECK(table.Find(live) != nullptr);
    CHECK(table.Find(live)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
}

TEST_CASE("StepAll advances one running job by one and leaves the next for later", "[remote][job]") {
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable table;
    int hits = 0;
    auto id = table.Arm("play.step", 3,
        [](Arcane::Remote::Job& job, Arcane::Remote::HostGate&, void* user) {
            *static_cast<int*>(user) += 1;
            job.progress.done += 1;
            return job.progress.done >= job.progress.total;
        }, &hits);
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(hits == 1);
    CHECK(table.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(table.Find(id)->progress.done == 1);
    table.StepAll(gate);
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(hits == 3);
    CHECK(table.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(hits == 3);
}

TEST_CASE("AnyRunning is true only for a Running row of the named command", "[remote][job]") {
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable table;
    CHECK_FALSE(table.AnyRunning({"capture.window", "capture.compare"}));
    auto id = table.Arm("capture.window", 1, nullptr, nullptr);
    CHECK(table.AnyRunning({"capture.window"}));
    CHECK(table.AnyRunning({"capture.compare", "capture.window"}));
    CHECK_FALSE(table.AnyRunning({"capture.compare"}));
    table.Finish(id, Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("capture.window", nlohmann::json::object(), {}),
                 Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
    CHECK_FALSE(table.AnyRunning({"capture.window"}));
}

TEST_CASE("CancelBySubscriber cancels only the keyed job", "[remote][job]") {
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable table;
    int hits = 0;
    auto follow = table.Arm("log.follow", 0,
        [](Arcane::Remote::Job&, Arcane::Remote::HostGate&, void* user) {
            *static_cast<int*>(user) += 1;
            return false;
        }, &hits);
    table.SetSubscriber(follow, 7);
    auto other = table.Arm("play.step", 3, nullptr, nullptr);
    table.CancelBySubscriber(7);
    CHECK(table.Find(follow)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Cancelled);
    CHECK(table.Find(other)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(hits == 0);
    table.CancelBySubscriber(7);
    CHECK(table.Find(other)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
}

TEST_CASE("a finished job is kept for 10 minutes and dropped after", "[remote][job]") {
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable table;
    auto id = table.Arm("capture.window", 1, nullptr, nullptr);
    table.Finish(id, Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("capture.window", nlohmann::json::object(), {}),
                 Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
    REQUIRE(table.Find(id) != nullptr);
    const std::int64_t finished = table.Find(id)->finishedUnixMs;
    table.Prune(finished + 599999);
    CHECK(table.Find(id) != nullptr);
    table.Prune(finished + 600001);
    CHECK(table.Find(id) == nullptr);
}

TEST_CASE("StepAll completes the bound waiter with the gate's host block and the bound undo", "[remote][job]") {
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable table;
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    table.SetQueue(&queue);
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "play.step";
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(5, call));
    auto id = table.Arm("play.step", 2,
        [](Arcane::Remote::Job& job, Arcane::Remote::HostGate&, void*) {
            job.progress.done += 1;
            job.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(job.command, nlohmann::json{{"done", job.progress.done}}, {});
            return job.progress.done >= job.progress.total;
        }, nullptr);
    Arcane::Remote::UndoBlock undo;
    undo.reason = "runtime-host";
    table.BindWaiter(id, 5, undo);
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.kind = "server";
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult out;
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK_FALSE(queue.TryTake(5, out));
    gate.frame.store(110);
    table.StepAll(gate);
    REQUIRE(queue.TryTake(5, out));
    CHECK(out.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(out.envelope.data["done"] == 2);
    CHECK(out.envelope.host.frame == 110);
    CHECK(out.envelope.host.kind == "server");
    REQUIRE(out.envelope.undo.has_value());
    CHECK(out.envelope.undo->reason == "runtime-host");
    CHECK(table.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
    table.SetQueue(nullptr);
}
```

`StepAll` skips every state other than `Running`, so a cancelled follow job's step is not called.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the table and `HostGate`**

Create `Dispatcher.hpp` with exactly the `HostGate` block in **Produces** (includes `<Arcane/Modules/ArcaneRemoteApi.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Remote/Jobs.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>`, `<atomic>`, `<cstdint>`, `<mutex>`, `<string>`; Dist-guarded like every Remote header) and `Dispatcher.cpp` with `State()` / `SetState` (under `stateMutex`) and `Block()` (copies `kind`, `pid`, `world`, `frame`, `State()`, `catalog`, `pluginGeneration` into a `HostBlock`). Task 8 appends the dispatcher to both files.

`std::vector<Job>` plus a mutex. `Arm` with a null step is legal for the prune test; `StepAll` skips a null step and skips any state other than `Running`. `Finish` on an already-finished id is a no-op. `CancelBySubscriber` sets every `Running` job whose `subscriberId` equals the argument to `Cancelled` and does not call its step. A second call is a no-op. `subscriberId` 0 is never cancelled by that function.

The Remote code lives in the ArcaneRemote module DLL (R8-1), whose Dist build holds only the module anchor `ArcModule_ArcaneRemote`. Build that one project in Dist (its vcxproj sits at the module root, `location` = the module root in `arc_module_project`, `build/modules.lua:387`; building it builds its referenced modules), then dump its exports. `dumpbin` is found through `vswhere`, the way `scripts/ci/package.ps1:23-25` finds it:

```powershell
& "C:\Program Files\Microsoft Visual Studio\18\Community\MSBuild\Current\Bin\amd64\MSBuild.exe" D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Source\Runtime\ArcaneRemote\ArcaneRemote.vcxproj /p:Configuration=Dist /p:Platform=x64 /m:8 /nodeReuse:false /p:CL=/FS /v:m
$vswhere = Join-Path ${env:ProgramFiles(x86)} 'Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe'
$dumpbin = @(& $vswhere -latest -find 'VC\Tools\MSVC\*\bin\Hostx64\x64\dumpbin.exe') | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $dumpbin) { throw 'dumpbin.exe not found via vswhere' }
$out = & $dumpbin /nologo /EXPORTS D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Binaries\Dist-windows-x86_64-md\ArcaneRemote\ArcaneRemote.dll
$hit = $out | Select-String -Pattern '@Remote@Arcane@@|Arcane::Remote::' | Where-Object { $_.Line -notmatch '\bArcModule_ArcaneRemote\b' }
if ($hit) { $hit; throw "Dist ArcaneRemote exports Arcane::Remote" }
```

Expected: the script does not throw, and the export list holds only `ArcModule_ArcaneRemote`. The pattern is the regex `@Remote@Arcane@@|Arcane::Remote::`. `dumpbin /EXPORTS` prints MSVC-DECORATED names, where `Arcane::Remote::X` is spelled `?X@Remote@Arcane@@...` (a nested namespace is written innermost first, each part ending in `@`, the scope closed by `@@`), so the undecorated `Arcane::Remote::` alone can never match and a gate built on it passes vacuously (Task 10 found two live exports, `?FrameBudgetMsSetting@Remote@Arcane@@YANXZ` and `?QueueDepthSetting@Remote@Arcane@@YAIXZ`, that it missed). The undecorated half stays for a dump piped through `undname`, or any tool that prints demangled names. Neither half matches the module's own name (`ArcaneRemote`, `ArcModule_ArcaneRemote`), `Arcane::RemoteCVarService` (`@RemoteCVarService@Arcane@@`), or any `Arcane::Arcane...` symbol; the anchor line is excluded explicitly anyway. This export dump is the task's Dist test (ruling S3-4); there is no unit-test stand-in for it.

Ruling D-1 (Global Constraints **Dist**, **Packages**): packaged games are Dist monolithic builds, so the remote surface is compiled out of them. `ArcaneRuntimeHost` is built in both layouts and linked by every packaged `<Game>.exe` (`premake5.lua:436-442`), so every remote line in `RuntimeApp.cpp` / `RuntimeApp.hpp` (members, `Start`, `Drain`, hooks, the `exitReason` strings) sits under `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST) && !defined(ARC_MONOLITHIC)`; `build/arcane.lua` is not edited. In a monolithic non-Dist build, `RuntimeApp` sees `HostConfig::remote` and refuses at startup: it prints `the remote surface is not linked into packaged games` to stderr and exits 2 before arming anything. The editor and the server are modular-only programs and use `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)` alone.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RemoteListener`, `RemoteDispatcher`, `HostGate`, `JobTable`, `WriteDescriptorAtomic`, `Descriptor::abi` (Task 7). `kGamePluginABIVersion` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Core/AbiVersion.hpp`). `PluginHost::Generation()` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneEngine/src/Arcane/Plugin/PluginHost.hpp:115`, a non-static member: the HOST reads it from its own `PluginHost` and passes the value to `NoteReload`, ruling F10). `NetMode` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Core/NetMode.hpp:8`; the server reads `m_runtime->Mode()`, the runtime and the editor read `m_runtime->Core().Mode()` through `ClientRuntime::Core`, `Source/Client/ArcaneClient/src/Arcane/Client/ClientRuntime.hpp:67`). Contexts (`CVarContext`, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Config/CVarTypes.hpp:102`): `Editor` on the editor, `LocalHost` on the runtime, `ServerAdmin` on the server.
- Produces (`HostSession.hpp`, born inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`; `Arming` and `HostSession` exported `ARC_REMOTE_API`):

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {

class TickGate; // TickGate.hpp, Task 12

struct ARC_REMOTE_API Arming {
    static bool Allow(NetMode mode, bool distBuild, bool editorEnableCvar);
};

class ARC_REMOTE_API HostSession {
public:
    struct Config {
        HostKind kind;
        CVarContext cvarContext;
        std::filesystem::path projectRoot;
        std::filesystem::path descriptorOverride;
        std::uint32_t idleExitSeconds = 600;
        bool readOnly = false;
        bool holdSim = false;          // Task 12: --remote && maxFrames == 0
        std::vector<std::string> capabilities;
        // Appended by later tasks, kept last so the fields above keep their
        // positions. Each task that appends a field also wires, in Start, the
        // registration that reads it (ruling F12): Task 16 adds
        // `bool (*dirty)(void*)`, `bool (*settled)(void*)` and the `void* hookUser`
        // both receive; Task 19 adds `WorldTable worlds`; Task 22 adds
        // `DiagnosticStore* problemStore`; Tasks 27-29 add their host hook
        // structs. Session-owned state (the cvar command user, FrameStats)
        // is a HostSession member added by the task that needs it.
    };
    explicit HostSession(Config config);
    void Start();
    void Drain();
    void Stop(std::string exitReason);
    bool IdleExpired() const;
    HostGate& Gate();
    const HostGate& Gate() const;       // ruling F9: WaitPoll reads it through a const session
    const Config& GetConfig() const;    // ruling F9: the const hooks accessor (dirty, settled, hookUser, ...)
    TickGate& Tick();                   // defined in Task 12; declare it now
    // ruling F10: the host passes its own m_plugin->Generation(); HostSession holds no PluginHost
    void NoteReload(std::uint32_t generation, bool ok, std::string reason, std::uint64_t frame);
    std::uint16_t Port() const;
    const std::filesystem::path& DescriptorFile() const;
    bool TakeQuit() const;              // host.quit sets it; Task 16
    void RequestQuit();
};
}
```

`Arming::Allow` is false for `NetMode::Client`, for `distBuild`, or for `editorEnableCvar == false`. `Standalone`, `DedicatedServer`, and `ListenServer` return true when the other two inputs allow it. The editor process uses `CVarContext::Editor` even while play topology is a client. A process whose primary `Runtime::Mode()` is `NetMode::Client` does not call `Start`.

`editor.remote.enable`, inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, in the same anonymous namespace as `cvar_windowedFrameCapture` (`EditorAppFrame.cpp:1635-1642`) and spelled the way that neighbour spells its enums:

```cpp
ARC_CVAR(cvar_editorRemoteEnable, "editor.remote.enable", bool, true,
    .flags = ::Arcane::CVarFlags::Dev,
    .audience = ::Arcane::Audience::Editor,
    .scope = ::Arcane::SettingScope::PreferencesMachine,
    .help = "Serve the remote-control surface on 127.0.0.1.");
```

When the published value becomes false, the editor calls `Stop("remote-disabled")` and does not restart until it becomes true again.

Capabilities, sorted, written onto `HostStatus`. All three include `cvar`, `console`, `ecs`, `logs`, `problems`, `stats`, `play`. Editor also adds `capture`, `compare`, `scene`, `selection`. Runtime also adds `capture`, `compare`, `scene`. Server does **not** add `capture` or `compare`. `play` on the server is the tick gate (Task 29), not a renderer.

`Start` remembers the registry's current host kind, then calls `CommandRegistry::Get().SetHostKind(config.kind)` (ruling F-1, Task 4); `Stop` restores the remembered kind (ruling S3-9), so a test session never leaves the process registry as Server or Runtime. `Start` sizes its `RequestQueue` with `QueueDepthSetting()` (Task 8), and writes the descriptor at `DescriptorPath` unless `descriptorOverride` is set, with `Descriptor::abi = kGamePluginABIVersion` (ruling F27). `Drain` passes `FrameBudgetMsSetting()` and `kFrameBudgetCommands` to `dispatcher.Drain`. `Drain` calls `dispatcher.Drain`, republishes `HostStatus` (whose `abi` is the same constant) and the catalog JSON into the listener atomics, and calls `TouchHeartbeat` when a second has elapsed. `NoteReload(generation, ok, reason, frame)` stores `generation` into `pluginGeneration` (gate and status) plus `reloadOk`, `reloadReason`, `reloadAtFrame` (ruling F10). Each host calls it after a reload with its own `m_plugin->Generation()` (`PluginHost.hpp:115`). Do not change `Reload`'s `bool` return.

**Where later tasks wire in (ruling F12).** `Start` is the one place a host's remote commands are registered. Tasks 16, 19, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25, 26, 27, 28 and 29 each add their registration call to `HostSession::Start` (and any host hook to the host program file) in their own commit, reading the session's own members (`m_jobs`, `m_gate`, `m_queue`, `m_listener`) or a `Config` field they append. Task 30 is the end-to-end check, not the place wiring is discovered.

`exitReason`: the chain in `RuntimeApp::ShutdownGraphPath` (`Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeApp.cpp`, `std::string exitReason = "frames-complete";` at line 1285) gains `"remote-quit"` when `TakeQuit()` is true, and `"remote-idle"` when `IdleExpired()` is true, ahead of the `"stopped-early"` fallback (`else if (!completedAllFrames)` at 1325). That chain is inside `#if defined(ARC_RUNTIME_HOST_VERIFY)` (1186-1564) and runs only when `--report` was given (`if (!m_config.reportPath.empty())` at 1270). Add the same two strings to the server's exit path, the `Finish(rep, stoppedByCleanExit ? "clean-exit" : "frames-complete", 0)` at `ServerApp.cpp:236`. `IdleExpired()` is true when `idleExitSeconds > 0` and the last completed command (or `Start`, if none) is older than that many seconds. `0` disables it.

Do not add a `HostConfig` or `ServerConfig` field, and do not register a `--read-only` host flag (ruling D-3: both host parsers refuse an unregistered flag through `Arcane::Cli`, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Cli/Cli.cpp:184`, and `GetCommandLineW` is Win32-only). `Start` sets `gate.readOnly` when `std::getenv("ARC_REMOTE_READ_ONLY")` is `"1"`, on every platform. The request-level `options.readOnly` (Task 6, `Call::readOnly`, posted by Task 14's client) is enforced per call by Task 8's gate, `(gate.readOnly || call.readOnly)` (ruling F15).

### Task 12: AM1 tick gate

**Files:**

- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneServer/src/ServerApp.cpp` (in `ServerApp::Run`: around `EnsurePhysics` + `Advance` at 190-193 and `++rep.framesTicked` at 210)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeApp.cpp` (in `RuntimeApp::MainLoop`: around `AdvanceSim` at 830)
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.hpp` and `HostSession.cpp` (the `Tick()` declared in Task 11)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/TickGateTest.cpp` (wrapped in `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`; CI runs ArcaneTests in Dist)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `HostSession::Gate()` (Task 11). `RunLoop::SetPaused` / `RequestSingleStep` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneSim/src/Arcane/Sim/RunLoop.hpp:63`, `:75`) are the editor path and are not this class.
- Produces (`TickGate.hpp`, born inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`; `TickGate` exported `ARC_REMOTE_API`):

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
enum class TickDecision : std::uint8_t { Idle, Step, Run };

class ARC_REMOTE_API TickGate {
public:
    void SetRunning(bool running);
    void SetPaused(bool paused);
    void RequestSteps(std::uint32_t n);
    TickDecision Consume();
    bool Running() const;
    bool Paused() const;
    std::uint32_t StepsRemaining() const;
};
}
```

`Consume`:

| running | paused | steps | result |
|---|---|---|---|
| false | * | 0 | Idle |
| false | * | >0 | Step, then decrement |
| true | false | * | Run, and clear a leftover step count |
| true | true | 0 | Idle |
| true | true | >0 | Step, then decrement |

When `--remote` and `maxFrames == 0`, `HostSession::Config::holdSim` is true and `Start` leaves the gate Idle (`running == false`, `paused == true`). `--frames N` leaves `holdSim` false so the scripted run still finishes. Default `holdSim` is false.

`host.frame` increments by 1 only in the branch that calls `Advance` / `AdvanceSim`. Idle iterations do not increment it. `++io.frameCount` in `CaptureTail` (`Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeFrame.cpp:800`) stays where it is.

Server loop after this task (the real calls from `ServerApp.cpp:190-193`):

```cpp
if (m_remote) m_remote->Drain();
const auto decision = m_remote ? m_remote->Tick().Consume() : Arcane::Remote::TickDecision::Run;
if (decision != Arcane::Remote::TickDecision::Idle) {
    m_runtime->EnsurePhysics();
    m_runtime->Loop().Advance(m_cfg.fixedDtSeconds,
                              [&](double dt) { m_plugin->FixedUpdateAll(dt); },
                              [&](double dt, double a) { m_plugin->UpdateAll(dt, a); });
    if (m_remote) m_remote->Gate().frame.fetch_add(1);
    ++rep.framesTicked;
}
```

Keep `Advance`'s real arguments. `++rep.framesTicked` moves from `:210` into the branch; the rest of the iteration (`m_plugin->Poll()` at `:194`, the admin stdin block, `Publish` at `:208`, `Heartbeat` at `:209`, the `sleep_until` at `:211`) still runs every iteration. A host with no `m_remote` takes the `Run` branch and behaves as today. Headless runtime uses the same `Consume` check around `AdvanceSim` at line 830. An Idle headless iteration may still render; it must not bump `gate.frame`. The editor loop does not use `TickGate` for the viewport; it keeps rendering every non-minimized frame. Editor `host.frame` is written by Task 29 from the play tick, not from the ImGui frame.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp>
#include "RemoteDemoArgs.hpp"

static int g_get = 0;
static int g_shot = 0;

static Arcane::Remote::CommandResult CountGet(const Arcane::Remote::Call& call, void*) {
    ++g_get;
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult r;
    r.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(call.command, nlohmann::json::object(), {});
    return r;
}
static Arcane::Remote::CommandResult CountShot(const Arcane::Remote::Call& call, void*) {
    ++g_shot;
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult r;
    r.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(call.command, nlohmann::json::object(), {});
    return r;
}

TEST_CASE("minimized: cvar.get runs, screenshot does not, and the waiter returns", "[remote][dispatch]") {
    g_get = 0;
    g_shot = 0;
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    std::string err;
    Arcane::Remote::Desc get;
    get.name = "min.cvar";
    get.summary = "read a cvar";
    get.access = Arcane::Remote::Access::Observe;
    get.module = "min-test";
    get.argsMeta = Astra::GetMeta<RemoteDemoArgs>();
    get.fn = &CountGet;
    get.needsFrame = false;
    Arcane::Remote::Desc shot = get;
    shot.name = "min.shot";
    shot.summary = "grab the viewport";
    shot.access = Arcane::Remote::Access::Drive;
    shot.mutates = Arcane::Remote::Mutates::Project;
    shot.fn = &CountShot;
    shot.needsFrame = true;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(get, &err));
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(shot, &err));
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.minimized = true;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    const auto audit = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-min-audit" / "audit.ndjson";
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit);
    Arcane::Remote::Call a;
    a.command = "min.cvar";
    Arcane::Remote::Call b;
    b.command = "min.shot";
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(1, a));
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(2, b));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_get == 1);
    CHECK(g_shot == 0);
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult got;
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult denied;
    REQUIRE(queue.Wait(1, got, std::chrono::milliseconds(1000)));
    REQUIRE(queue.Wait(2, denied, std::chrono::milliseconds(1000)));
    CHECK(got.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(denied.envelope.errors[0].code == "host.busy");
    CHECK(denied.envelope.errors[0].detail["state"] == "minimized");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(denied.envelope) == 5);
    reg.UnregisterModule("min-test");
}
```

```cpp
namespace Servitor {

enum class Format { Json, Human, Ndjson };

struct RenderOptions {
    Format format = Format::Json;
    bool tty = false;
};

struct Printed {
    std::string stdoutText;
    std::string stderrText;
};

Printed Render(const Envelope& env, const ClientBlock& client, RenderOptions opt);
// json: ToJson(env) plus "client":{exitCode, retried, descriptor}. One object.
// ndjson: one {"type":"progress",...} line per notification that carries detail.progress,
//         then {"type":"result","count":1,"envelope":<ToJson plus client>}.
// human: one summary line on stdout ("ok <command>" or "error <code>"), data keys as
//        "key: value", artifacts as paths. Diagnostics (errors, warnings) on stderr.
// --format github is not a Format. ParseGlobals returns false and exit 2.

struct GlobalOptions {
    Format format = Format::Human; // TTY default; non-TTY default is Json
    bool json = false;
    bool verbose = false;
    bool tty = false;
    bool readOnly = false;          // --read-only; Call posts it as options.readOnly (ruling F15)
    bool yes = false;
    bool noRetry = false;
    bool retryOnDeath = false;
    std::uint32_t timeoutMs = 30000; // milliseconds. --timeout <s> is seconds; ParseGlobals scales.
    std::string project;
    std::string hostKind;       // editor | runtime | server, optional
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::string engine;         // --engine <root>; rev 8.3 V7 (HostsDir)
};

bool ParseGlobals(int argc, char** argv, GlobalOptions& out, std::string* error);
struct CallRequest {
    std::string command;
    nlohmann::json args = nlohmann::json::object();
};

struct ExchangeResult {
    Envelope envelope;
    bool connectionRefusedBeforeBody = false;
};

using ExchangeFn = ExchangeResult (*)(const std::string& requestBody, void* user);

Printed Call(const CallRequest& req, const GlobalOptions& opt, ExchangeFn exchange, void* user, ClientBlock* client);
int Run(int argc, char** argv); // servitor.exe main calls this. Run parses argv, builds an ExchangeFn over the live socket, and calls Call.

struct TargetWatch {
    std::string command;
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::uint64_t processStart = 0;
    std::filesystem::path descriptorPath;
    std::filesystem::path diagnosticsDir;
    std::int64_t requestUnixMs = 0;
};

// nullopt: the pid is live and the descriptor heartbeat is fresh.
// An engaged optional is a finished error envelope. The caller does not post it.
std::optional<Envelope> ObserveTarget(const TargetWatch& watch, std::int64_t nowUnixMs);
}
```

`Servitor/Verbs.hpp` (ruling F17: the verb table lives in the Servitor client, so `main.cpp` stays one line and `Run(argc, argv)` needs no table parameter):

```cpp
namespace Servitor {

// One row of the Servitor verb table.
struct VerbRow {
    const char* words;    // the verb as typed, space-separated: "stats frame", "cvar get"
    const char* command;  // the wire command it posts ("stats.frame"); nullptr for a client-side verb
    // Turns the tokens after the verb into `args`; false is a usage error
    // (exit 2) with *error set. nullptr: the verb takes no arguments.
    bool (*args)(std::span<const std::string> rest, nlohmann::json& out, std::string* error);
    // Client-side verbs (status, launch, quit, wait, commands, list, describe,
    // schema, call) run here and return the exit code instead of posting.
    // nullptr for a row that posts `command`.
    int (*local)(std::span<const std::string> rest, const GlobalOptions& opt);
};

std::span<const VerbRow> VerbTable(); // Servitor/Verbs.cpp: one static array
// Longest `words` prefix of `tokens` (globals already removed); null = unknown verb (Run exits 2).
const VerbRow* MatchVerb(std::span<const std::string> tokens, std::size_t* consumed);
}
```

`Run` parses the globals, matches the remaining tokens with `MatchVerb`, and either calls the row's `local` or builds `args` and calls `Call` with `row.command`. This task's table has one row, `{"stats frame", "stats.frame", nullptr, nullptr}` (Task 26 registers the host command and does not touch the table). Tasks 15-29 add their rows to `Verbs.cpp`.

`--json` equals `--format json` and wins over TTY detection (AM4). A non-TTY stdout with no format flag is JSON. `--format github` sets `error` to `unknown format github` and `Run` returns 2; it does not fall through to human. `Render` runs `RedactSecrets` on both streams with the token it was given; `Run` passes the descriptor token (on `ClientBlock::token`). `--verbose` makes `Call` log one line per post, `servitor request: POST /remote/v1/call Authorization: Bearer <token> <payload>`, passed through `RedactSecrets(line, client->token)` before it reaches `ARC_INFO`; the `ARC_*` log macros write to stderr (R8-8), and stdout carries only the printed envelope. `--read-only` sets `GlobalOptions::readOnly`. `--version` prints `Servitor 0.1.0 (remote protocol 1)` (`kVersion`, rev 8.3 V6) and returns 0. `--engine <root>` fills `GlobalOptions::engine` (V7). Dist `main` (`#if defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`) prints `Servitor is compiled out of Dist builds` and returns 2, so the exe still links.

The client retries a busy answer with backoff until `--timeout`, not for a fixed two tries. The bound is `GlobalOptions::timeoutMs`, default 30000. `--no-retry` disables busy retries. Exit 7 retries only with `--retry-on-death`, and then on that same until-timeout loop. That retry is ruling P4 (a). `host.busy` with `detail.state` `capture-pending` is exit 5 and provably not run, so it is one of the answers this retry already covers. Do not add a second retry policy. `Call` is the function the busy-host test drives. It does not open a socket. `Run` builds one `ExchangeFn` over the real connection and calls `Call`. The first sleep is 10 ms, the second is 50 ms, and each later sleep doubles, capped at 1000 ms. A retry is issued only when `elapsed + nextSleep < timeoutMs`. A connection refused before the body was sent is exit 5 and uses this loop. A connection refused after the body was sent is not retried. `ParseGlobals` sets `GlobalOptions::noRetry` from `--no-retry`. `--timeout <s>` is seconds, decimal allowed (spec s4.6, line 361). Parse the whole token with `std::strtod`. A token that is not fully consumed, `ERANGE`, a non-finite value, a negative value, an empty token, a missing value, or a rounded millisecond count above `std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max()` makes `ParseGlobals` return false, `error` contains `timeout`, and `Run` returns 2. Otherwise store `timeoutMs = std::max(1u, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::llround(seconds * 1000.0)))`. Use `std::llround` (half away from zero). `std::lround` returns `long`, which is 32-bit on Windows, so a multi-hour timeout overflows it. Check the range before the cast: `llround` of a value outside `long long` is undefined. `--timeout 0` and any positive value that rounds below 1 ms store 1. `ParseGlobals` begins by assigning `out = GlobalOptions{}`. That value is the struct's default, and its `timeoutMs` member initializer is 30000. Flags present in this argv are applied after that assignment, so an omitted `--timeout` stores 30000 even when the caller reused an `out` a previous parse had set to 500. The omitted-timeout check below passes a fresh `GlobalOptions`, not the object that just parsed `--timeout 0.5`. `1e3` is 1000 seconds because `strtod` consumes it. `2s` is a refusal because the trailing `s` is not consumed. `Call` posts `opt.timeoutMs` as the wire integer `options.timeoutMs` and does not multiply. The case that assigns `opt.timeoutMs = 200` below is 200 milliseconds of that field. The flag `--timeout 200` would store 200000.

`Call` posts `{"id","command","args","options":{"timeoutMs"}}` with `opt.timeoutMs` already in milliseconds, plus `options.readOnly: true` when `opt.readOnly` is set (ruling F15; the host's Task 8 gate refuses Drive for that call). The exit it retries on is `connectionRefusedBeforeBody ? 5 : ExitFromHost(result.envelope)`, and it retries an exit-5 envelope only when its first error code is `host.busy` or `capture.minimized` (a transient state). `host.unavailable` is also exit 5, but it means a host-side hook was never installed and will not appear later. It answers immediately with no retry, and the busy-retry test file gains one case pinning that: a mock host answering `host.unavailable` gives exit 5 after exactly one exchange, with `retried == false`. (Controller ruling, rev 8.1 follow-up.) It does not trust a side channel. There is no attempt cap. `client->retried` is set true when a retry is issued. `Render` prints that flag. `Call` has no pid and does not call `ObserveTarget`. The busy-host tests keep driving `Call` through `ExchangeFn`.

**Target watch.** `ObserveTarget` is the client's mid-call observation. It does not sleep, does not spawn, and does not terminate a process. `Arcane::Platform::IsProcessLive(pid, processStart)` (ArcaneCore; Task 7 extracts it from the three tells in `EditorLock::ReadLive`, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneEngine/src/Arcane/Project/Project.cpp:847-888`; rev 8.3 V2) is the liveness test. The client never calls `EditorLock`, which is ArcaneEngine. On Windows (`:847-877`): `OpenProcess`, creation time equals `processStart` when `processStart != 0`, and the exit time is zero. Off Windows (`:878-888`) the same three tells come from `Platform::QueryProcess` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Process.hpp:55`): `found`, `start` equals `processStart` when non-zero, and not `exited` (a zombie). A start flipped by 1 is not live.

- `!Arcane::Platform::IsProcessLive(watch.pid, watch.processStart)` → `host.died`. Died wins when the heartbeat is also stale.
- Otherwise `ReadDescriptor` fails → `host.hung`. A live process with no readable heartbeat is hung, because a fresh heartbeat cannot be proved.
- Otherwise `IsHung(desc, nowUnixMs)` → `host.hung`. `IsHung` is `nowUnixMs - heartbeatUnixMs > 10000`. `now == heartbeat` and `now == heartbeat + 10000` are not hung. `+ 10001` is hung.
- Otherwise return `nullopt`.

Both envelopes are `MakeError(watch.command, code, message, "relaunch")`. `host.pid` is `watch.pid`. When the descriptor was read, `host.state` is `desc.state` and `host.kind` is `ToString(desc.hostKind)`. `errors[0].detail.lastState` is that state, or `""` when the descriptor was not read. `detail.exitCode` is present only for `host.died`, and only when this `{pid, processStart}` opened and `GetProcessTimes` showed a non-zero exit time. `IsProcessLive` closes the handle it opens (the `::CloseHandle(h)` it inherits from `ReadLive`, `Project.cpp:860`). On Windows `ObserveTarget` opens the pid again, with `PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION`, and records `exitCode` only when that second open's creation time matches `processStart` and the exit time is non-zero. The value is `GetExitCodeProcess`'s `DWORD`, including `259` (`STILL_ACTIVE`) when the exit time already proved the process has exited. A creation-time mismatch is a different process: omit `exitCode`. An `OpenProcess` failure omits it too. That second open is `#if defined(_WIN32)`. Off Windows `detail.exitCode` is always omitted: POSIX gives a process's exit status only to its parent, and the target is not the client's child (the same limit `scripts/linux-test-exclusions.txt` records for the crash monitor). The function never calls `TerminateProcess`.

`detail.arcdiag` is the newest `*.arcdiag` directly under `watch.diagnosticsDir` whose `last_write_time`, converted with `std::chrono::file_clock::to_sys`, is strictly after `requestUnixMs`. Use `file_clock::to_sys` / `from_sys`, not `std::chrono::clock_cast`: the tree has no `clock_cast` use, and the code must compile on libc++ (macOS) as well as MSVC and libstdc++ (R8-7). Omit the key when none qualifies. Equal mtimes tie-break by the lexicographically greater `generic_string()`. `ObserveTarget` does not wait for the watchdog. `IsHung`'s 10 s is under `hangSeconds` (12) and under the 15 s sleep, so the first hung sample usually has no report yet.

`Run`'s live-socket `ExchangeFn` (installed in Task 15, once `Select` has a host) samples this function. The first sample is immediate, before `send`. Later samples run every 250 ms while the response read is outstanding: `PollSockets` with a 250 ms timeout, and every wake, including a timeout, calls `ObserveTarget` again before the socket is checked. `requestUnixMs` is `system_clock` milliseconds, taken once when that call starts, before the first sample and before the body is written. An engaged optional is returned with `connectionRefusedBeforeBody = false`, and the body is not written when it has not been written yet. A complete HTTP response already buffered is parsed and kept. A selected host that then dies or hangs is `host.died` or `host.hung` (exit 7), not `target.none` (4) and not connection-refused (5). Exit 7 still retries only with `--retry-on-death`, on `Call`'s existing loop. `servitor status` does not call `ObserveTarget`.

```cpp
Printed Servitor::Call(const CallRequest& req, const GlobalOptions& opt,
                               ExchangeFn exchange, void* user, ClientBlock* client) {
    nlohmann::json body = {
        {"id", "c1"},
        {"command", req.command},
        {"args", req.args},
        {"options", {{"timeoutMs", opt.timeoutMs}}},
    };
    if (opt.readOnly)
        body["options"]["readOnly"] = true;   // ruling F15
    const std::string payload = body.dump();
    ClientBlock local;
    if (client == nullptr)
        client = &local;
    client->retried = false;
    if (opt.verbose) {
        const std::string line = "servitor request: POST /remote/v1/call Authorization: Bearer " +
                                 client->token + " " + payload;
        ARC_INFO("{}", RedactSecrets(line, client->token));   // stderr only (R8-8)
    }
    ExchangeResult last{};
    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    int pauseMs = 10;
    for (;;) {
        last = exchange(payload, user);
        const int code = last.connectionRefusedBeforeBody ? 5 : ExitFromHost(last.envelope);
        const bool busy = code == 5 && !opt.noRetry;
        const bool death = code == 7 && opt.retryOnDeath;
        const auto elapsed = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started).count();
        if ((busy || death) && elapsed + pauseMs < static_cast<long long>(opt.timeoutMs)) {
            client->retried = true;
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(pauseMs));
            pauseMs = pauseMs < 50 ? 50 : std::min(1000, pauseMs * 2);
            continue;
        }
        client->exitCode = code;
        break;
    }
    RenderOptions ro;
    ro.format = opt.format;
    return Render(last.envelope, *client, ro);
}
```

```cpp
#include <windows.h>

TEST_CASE("json render adds client and keeps the spec envelope", "[servitor][client]") {
    Arcane::Remote::HostBlock host;
    host.kind = "runtime";
    host.frame = 3;
    host.state = "ready";
    auto env = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("cvar.get", nlohmann::json{{"value", false}}, host);
    env.command = "cvar.get";
    Arcane::Remote::ClientBlock client;
    client.exitCode = 0;
    client.descriptor = "Saved/Remote/runtime-1.json";
    Servitor::RenderOptions opt;
    opt.format = Servitor::Format::Json;
    const auto printed = Servitor::Render(env, client, opt);
    const auto j = nlohmann::json::parse(printed.stdoutText);
    CHECK(j["command"] == "cvar.get");
    CHECK(j["host"]["frame"] == 3);
    CHECK(j["data"]["value"] == false);
    CHECK(j["client"]["exitCode"] == 0);
    CHECK(j["client"]["descriptor"] == "Saved/Remote/runtime-1.json");
    CHECK_FALSE(j.contains("result"));
    CHECK_FALSE(j.contains("hostFrame"));
    CHECK(printed.stderrText.empty());
}

struct RemoteVerboseLog {
    std::mutex mu;
    std::string text;
};

static void RemoteVerboseSink(const Arcane::LogRecord& rec, void* user) {
    auto* log = static_cast<RemoteVerboseLog*>(user);
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(log->mu);
    log->text += rec.text;
    log->text.push_back('\n');
}

struct LeakyHost {
    std::string token;
};

// A host whose error message echoes the bearer back (the worst case Render must scrub).
static Servitor::ExchangeResult LeakyExchange(const std::string& body, void* user) {
    auto* host = static_cast<LeakyHost*>(user);
    const auto cmd = nlohmann::json::parse(body).at("command").get<std::string>();
    Servitor::ExchangeResult result;
    result.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeError(cmd, "auth.denied", "saw " + host->token, "check the descriptor");
    return result;
}

TEST_CASE("verbose and human output redact the bearer token", "[servitor][client]") {
    // Drives the real flags (ruling S3-4): --verbose and --format human through
    // ParseGlobals, then Call, which logs the request line and renders human.
    const char* argv[] = {"servitor", "--verbose", "--format", "human", "--no-retry", "cvar", "get", "x"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(8, const_cast<char**>(argv), opt, &err));
    REQUIRE(opt.verbose);
    REQUIRE(opt.format == Servitor::Format::Human);
    LeakyHost host;
    host.token = Arcane::Remote::MakeToken();
    Arcane::Remote::ClientBlock client;
    client.token = host.token;   // Run puts the descriptor token here
    RemoteVerboseLog log;
    const auto sub = Arcane::Log::Subscribe(&RemoteVerboseSink, &log);
    Servitor::CallRequest req;
    req.command = "cvar.get";
    req.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "x"}};
    const auto printed = Servitor::Call(req, opt, &LeakyExchange, &host, &client);
    Arcane::Log::Unsubscribe(sub);
    std::string logged;
    {
        std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(log.mu);
        logged = log.text;
    }
    CHECK(client.exitCode == 3);
    CHECK(logged.find("servitor request: POST /remote/v1/call") != std::string::npos);   // --verbose logged
    CHECK(logged.find("Bearer <redacted>") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(logged.find(host.token) == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(printed.stdoutText.find(host.token) == std::string::npos);              // human output
    CHECK(printed.stderrText.find(host.token) == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(printed.stderrText.find("<redacted>") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(printed.stderrText.find("auth.denied") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(printed.stdoutText.find("\"ok\"") == std::string::npos);              // human, not JSON
}

TEST_CASE("--read-only is posted as options.readOnly", "[servitor][client]") {
    const char* argv[] = {"servitor", "--read-only", "cvar", "set", "x", "1"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(6, const_cast<char**>(argv), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.readOnly);
    static std::string posted;
    posted.clear();
    Servitor::CallRequest req;
    req.command = "cvar.set";
    Servitor::Call(req, opt,
        [](const std::string& body, void*) {
            posted = body;
            Servitor::ExchangeResult r;
            r.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("cvar.set", nlohmann::json::object(), {});
            return r;
        }, nullptr, nullptr);
    const auto j = nlohmann::json::parse(posted);
    CHECK(j["options"]["readOnly"] == true);
    Servitor::GlobalOptions plain;
    Servitor::Call(req, plain,
        [](const std::string& body, void*) {
            posted = body;
            Servitor::ExchangeResult r;
            r.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("cvar.set", nlohmann::json::object(), {});
            return r;
        }, nullptr, nullptr);
    CHECK_FALSE(nlohmann::json::parse(posted)["options"].contains("readOnly"));
}

TEST_CASE("the verb table maps stats frame to stats.frame", "[servitor][client]") {
    const std::vector<std::string> tokens = {"stats", "frame"};
    std::size_t consumed = 0;
    const Servitor::VerbRow* row = Servitor::MatchVerb(tokens, &consumed);
    REQUIRE(row != nullptr);
    CHECK(std::string(row->command) == "stats.frame");
    CHECK(consumed == 2);
    const std::vector<std::string> unknown = {"no", "such"};
    CHECK(Servitor::MatchVerb(unknown, &consumed) == nullptr);

TEST_CASE("--json wins and github is refused", "[servitor][client]") {
    const char* argv[] = {"servitor", "--json", "status"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(3, const_cast<char**>(argv), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.format == Servitor::Format::Json);
    const char* bad[] = {"servitor", "--format", "github", "status"};
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(bad), opt, &err));
    CHECK(err.find("github") != std::string::npos);
    const char* once[] = {"servitor", "--no-retry", "status"};
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(3, const_cast<char**>(once), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.noRetry);
    const char* timed[] = {"servitor", "--timeout", "2", "status"};
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(timed), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.timeoutMs == 2000);
    const char* half[] = {"servitor", "--timeout", "0.5", "status"};
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(half), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.timeoutMs == 500);
    const char* omitted[] = {"servitor", "status"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions omittedOpt;
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(2, const_cast<char**>(omitted), omittedOpt, &err));
    CHECK(omittedOpt.timeoutMs == 30000);
    const char* zero[] = {"servitor", "--timeout", "0", "status"};
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(zero), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.timeoutMs == 1);
    const char* negative[] = {"servitor", "--timeout", "-1", "status"};
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(negative), opt, &err));
    CHECK(err.find("timeout") != std::string::npos);
    const char* words[] = {"servitor", "--timeout", "abc", "status"};
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(words), opt, &err));
    CHECK(err.find("timeout") != std::string::npos);
}

    auto env = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("logs.read", nlohmann::json::object(), {});
    Servitor::RenderOptions opt;
    opt.format = Servitor::Format::Ndjson;

    CHECK(printed.stdoutText.find("\"type\":\"result\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(printed.stdoutText.find("\"count\":1") != std::string::npos);

struct BusyHost {
    int posts = 0;
    int busyAnswers = 0;
    int busyForPosts = 0;
    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point freeAt{};
    bool useFreeAt = false;
    std::string busyState;
};

static Servitor::ExchangeResult BusyExchange(const std::string& body, void* user) {
    auto* transport = static_cast<BusyHost*>(user);
    ++transport->posts;
    Servitor::ExchangeResult result;
    const auto cmd = nlohmann::json::parse(body).at("command").get<std::string>();
    const bool stillBusy = transport->posts <= transport->busyForPosts
        || (transport->useFreeAt && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < transport->freeAt);
    if (stillBusy) {
        ++transport->busyAnswers;
        result.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeError(cmd, "host.busy", "one capture is pending", "retry");
        result.envelope.errors[0].detail = nlohmann::json{{"state", "capture-pending"}};
        transport->busyState = result.envelope.errors[0].detail["state"].get<std::string>();
        return result;
    }
    Arcane::Remote::HostBlock host;
    host.kind = "runtime";
    host.state = "ready";
    result.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(cmd, nlohmann::json{{"width", 2}, {"height", 1}}, host);
    result.envelope.command = cmd;
    return result;
}

TEST_CASE("a busy host longer than 60 ms is retried until it frees", "[servitor][client]") {
    BusyHost transport;
    transport.busyForPosts = 3;
    transport.useFreeAt = true;
    transport.freeAt = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::milliseconds(150);
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    opt.format = Servitor::Format::Json;
    opt.noRetry = false;
    opt.timeoutMs = 30000;
    Servitor::CallRequest shot;
    shot.command = "capture.window";
    shot.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    Arcane::Remote::ClientBlock client;
    const auto printed = Servitor::Call(shot, opt, &BusyExchange, &transport, &client);
    const auto elapsedMs = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started).count();
    const auto json = nlohmann::json::parse(printed.stdoutText);
    CHECK(json["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(json["command"] == "capture.window");
    CHECK(json["client"]["retried"] == true);
    CHECK(json["client"]["exitCode"] == 0);
    CHECK(client.retried == true);
    CHECK(client.exitCode == 0);
    CHECK(transport.busyAnswers >= 3);
    CHECK(transport.posts == transport.busyAnswers + 1);
    CHECK(transport.busyState == "capture-pending");
    CHECK(elapsedMs >= 60);
    CHECK(elapsedMs < 5000);

    opt.noRetry = true;
    transport.posts = 0;
    transport.busyAnswers = 0;
    transport.busyForPosts = 5;
    transport.useFreeAt = false;
    Arcane::Remote::ClientBlock refusedClient;
    const auto refused = Servitor::Call(shot, opt, &BusyExchange, &transport, &refusedClient);
    const auto refusedJson = nlohmann::json::parse(refused.stdoutText);
    CHECK(refusedClient.exitCode == 5);
    CHECK(refusedClient.retried == false);
    CHECK(transport.posts == 1);
    CHECK(transport.busyAnswers == 1);
    CHECK(refusedJson["ok"] == false);
    CHECK(refusedJson["errors"][0]["code"] == "host.busy");
    CHECK(refusedJson["errors"][0]["detail"]["state"] == "capture-pending");
    CHECK(refusedJson["client"]["exitCode"] == 5);
    CHECK(refusedJson["client"]["retried"] == false);
}

TEST_CASE("busy retries stop at --timeout", "[servitor][client]") {
    BusyHost transport;
    transport.busyForPosts = 100;
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    opt.format = Servitor::Format::Json;
    opt.noRetry = false;
    opt.timeoutMs = 200;
    Servitor::CallRequest shot;
    shot.command = "capture.window";
    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    Arcane::Remote::ClientBlock client;
    const auto printed = Servitor::Call(shot, opt, &BusyExchange, &transport, &client);
    const auto elapsedMs = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started).count();
    const auto json = nlohmann::json::parse(printed.stdoutText);
    CHECK(json["ok"] == false);
    CHECK(client.exitCode == 5);
    CHECK(client.retried == true);
    CHECK(transport.posts >= 4);
    CHECK(transport.posts < 8);
    CHECK(elapsedMs >= 60);
    CHECK(elapsedMs < 1000);
    CHECK(json["errors"][0]["detail"]["state"] == "capture-pending");
}

static std::int64_t UnixMs(std::chrono::system_clock::time_point tp) {
    return std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(tp.time_since_epoch()).count();
}

TEST_CASE("ObserveTarget reports host.hung with the newest later .arcdiag and host.died when the pid is gone", "[servitor][client]") {
    const auto root = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-observe";
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root);
    const auto diag = root / "diagnostics";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(diag);
    Arcane::Remote::Descriptor d;
    d.protocol = 1;
    d.hostKind = Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime;
    d.pid = Arcane::EditorLock::Self().pid;
    d.processStart = Arcane::EditorLock::Self().start;
    d.port = 9;
    d.state = "ready";
    d.heartbeatUnixMs = 5000;
    const auto descPath = root / "runtime.json";
    REQUIRE(Arcane::Remote::WriteDescriptorAtomic(descPath, d));
    Servitor::TargetWatch watch;
    watch.command = "cvar.get";
    watch.pid = d.pid;
    watch.processStart = d.processStart;
    watch.descriptorPath = descPath;
    watch.diagnosticsDir = diag;
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs));
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10000));
    const auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
    watch.requestUnixMs = UnixMs(now - std::chrono::minutes(30));
    auto hung = Servitor::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(hung);
    CHECK(hung->command == "cvar.get");
    CHECK(hung->errors[0].code == "host.hung");
    CHECK(hung->errors[0].remediation == "relaunch");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(*hung) == 7);
    CHECK(hung->host.pid == d.pid);
    CHECK(hung->host.state == "ready");
    CHECK(hung->errors[0].detail["lastState"] == "ready");
    CHECK_FALSE(hung->errors[0].detail.contains("arcdiag"));

    const auto older = diag / "old.arcdiag";
    const auto newer = diag / "new.arcdiag";
    { std::ofstream(older) << "old"; }
    std::filesystem::last_write_time(older,
        std::chrono::file_clock::from_sys(now - std::chrono::hours(1)));
    auto stillNone = Servitor::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(stillNone);
    CHECK_FALSE(stillNone->errors[0].detail.contains("arcdiag"));
    { std::ofstream(newer) << "new"; }
    std::filesystem::last_write_time(newer, std::chrono::file_clock::from_sys(now));
    auto withFile = Servitor::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(withFile);
    CHECK(std::filesystem::equivalent(
        std::filesystem::path(withFile->errors[0].detail["arcdiag"].get<std::string>()), newer));

    const auto a = diag / "a.arcdiag";
    const auto m = diag / "m.arcdiag";
    { std::ofstream(a) << "a"; }
    { std::ofstream(m) << "m"; }
    const auto stamp = std::chrono::file_clock::from_sys(now + std::chrono::seconds(2));
    std::filesystem::last_write_time(a, stamp);
    std::filesystem::last_write_time(m, stamp);
    auto tie = Servitor::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(tie);
    CHECK(std::filesystem::equivalent(
        std::filesystem::path(tie->errors[0].detail["arcdiag"].get<std::string>()), m));

    watch.descriptorPath = root / "absent.json";
    auto unread = Servitor::ObserveTarget(watch, UnixMs(now));
    REQUIRE(unread);
    CHECK(unread->errors[0].code == "host.hung");
    CHECK(unread->errors[0].detail["lastState"] == "");

    Servitor::TargetWatch gone;
    gone.command = "cvar.get";
    gone.pid = 1;
    gone.processStart = Arcane::EditorLock::Self().start + 1;
    gone.descriptorPath = descPath;
    gone.diagnosticsDir = diag;
    gone.requestUnixMs = watch.requestUnixMs;
    auto died = Servitor::ObserveTarget(gone, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(died);
    CHECK(died->errors[0].code == "host.died");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(*died) == 7);
    CHECK_FALSE(died->errors[0].detail.contains("exitCode"));

#if defined(_WIN32)
    STARTUPINFOW si{};
    si.cb = sizeof(si);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
    std::wstring cmd = L"C:\\Windows\\System32\\cmd.exe /c exit 3";
    std::vector<wchar_t> mutableCmd(cmd.begin(), cmd.end());
    mutableCmd.push_back(L'\0');
    REQUIRE(CreateProcessW(L"C:\\Windows\\System32\\cmd.exe", mutableCmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE,
                           CREATE_NO_WINDOW, nullptr, nullptr, &si, &pi));
    REQUIRE(WaitForSingleObject(pi.hProcess, 5000) == WAIT_OBJECT_0);
    FILETIME created{}, exited{}, kernel{}, userTime{};
    REQUIRE(GetProcessTimes(pi.hProcess, &created, &exited, &kernel, &userTime));
    Servitor::TargetWatch exitedWatch;
    exitedWatch.command = "cvar.get";
    exitedWatch.pid = pi.dwProcessId;
    exitedWatch.processStart = (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(created.dwHighDateTime) << 32) | created.dwLowDateTime;
    exitedWatch.descriptorPath = root / "absent.json";
    exitedWatch.diagnosticsDir = diag;
    exitedWatch.requestUnixMs = UnixMs(now);
    auto coded = Servitor::ObserveTarget(exitedWatch, UnixMs(now));
    REQUIRE(coded);
    CHECK(coded->errors[0].code == "host.died");
    CHECK(coded->host.pid == pi.dwProcessId);
    CHECK(coded->errors[0].detail["exitCode"] == 3);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(*coded) == 7);
    CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    CloseHandle(pi.hProcess);
#endif
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root);
}
```

The acceptance call observes hung at 10 s, before the watchdog's 12 s report, so this case is the deterministic `.arcdiag` pin. `old.arcdiag` is an hour behind `requestUnixMs` and is omitted. `new.arcdiag` is later and is attached. `a.arcdiag` and `m.arcdiag` share a later mtime, and the lexicographically greater `generic_string()` wins. The cmd process is held open until after `ObserveTarget` so its pid cannot be recycled; the handle is closed after the checks. `ObserveTarget` only queries it. That half is `#if defined(_WIN32)` (R8-7): off Windows the client cannot read a non-child's exit code, so `ObserveTarget` never sets `exitCode` there. The rest of the case runs on all three platforms: `d.pid` is `EditorLock::Self().pid` rather than `::GetCurrentProcessId()`, and pid 1 is init / launchd off Windows, whose start never equals `Self().start + 1`, so `gone` is `host.died` everywhere.

`Render` calls `RedactSecrets` on both streams with `client.token` before returning. The redaction case parses `--verbose --format human` with `ParseGlobals`, sets `client.token`, drives `Call` against a host that echoes the token, and checks both the `ARC_INFO` request line (captured with `Log::Subscribe`) and the human `Printed`. Do not redact again in the test. `ToJson(Envelope)` still omits `client` and `token`.

Create `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/ServitorBoundaryTest.cpp`, the whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`. It runs from the ArcaneTests exe dir, so `../Servitor/` is the staged Servitor closure:

```cpp
TEST_CASE("servitor's staged closure holds no engine-side module", "[servitor][boundary]") {
    const auto dir = Arcane::Platform::ExecutablePath().parent_path().parent_path() / "Servitor";
    REQUIRE(std::filesystem::is_directory(dir));
#if defined(_WIN32)
    REQUIRE(std::filesystem::exists(dir / "servitor.exe"));
#else
    REQUIRE(std::filesystem::exists(dir / "servitor"));
#endif
    constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 10> kForbidden = {
        "ArcaneRemote.", "ArcaneEngine", "ArcaneScene", "ArcaneSim", "ArcaneAssets",
        "ArcaneCrypto", "ArcaneClient", "ArcaneRender", "ArcaneEditor", "ArcaneVerify"};
    std::size_t coreFiles = 0;
    for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(dir)) {
        std::string name = entry.path().filename().string();
        if (name.starts_with("lib"))
            name.erase(0, 3);                       // POSIX shared-library prefix
        INFO(name);
        for (std::string_view bad : kForbidden)
            CHECK_FALSE(name.starts_with(bad));
        if (name.starts_with("ArcaneCore"))
            ++coreFiles;
    }
    CHECK(coreFiles > 0);                           // the closure was staged at all
}
```

`"ArcaneRemote."` (with the dot) refuses ArcaneRemote's own library and lets `ArcaneRemoteProtocol` through. If this case fails, the fix is never to edit `kForbidden`. It is to remove the dependency that pulled the module in.

Append to `FormatTest.cpp` the version and compatibility cases. `CompatExchange` returns a fixed envelope JSON parsed with `TryParseEnvelope`, the shape of `LeakyExchange` above:

```cpp
static nlohmann::json g_compatReply;
static Servitor::ExchangeResult CompatExchange(const std::string&, void*) {
    Servitor::ExchangeResult result;
    result.envelope = *Arcane::Remote::TryParseEnvelope(g_compatReply);
    return result;
}

TEST_CASE("servitor talks to newer minors and refuses unknown majors", "[servitor][client]") {
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    opt.noRetry = true;
    opt.format = Servitor::Format::Json;   // errors print on stdout in json; human sends them to stderr
    Servitor::CallRequest req;
    req.command = "stats.frame";
    // A newer minor with fields this client has never seen: still a success.
    g_compatReply = Arcane::Remote::ToJson(Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("stats.frame", nlohmann::json{{"frame", 3}}, {}));
    g_compatReply["protocolMinor"] = 9;
    g_compatReply["futureTopLevel"] = 1;
    Arcane::Remote::ClientBlock ok;
    Servitor::Call(req, opt, &CompatExchange, nullptr, &ok);
    CHECK(ok.exitCode == 0);
    // A major outside kProtocolMajors: protocol.mismatch, exit 4.
    g_compatReply["protocol"] = 2;
    Arcane::Remote::ClientBlock mismatch;
    const auto printed = Servitor::Call(req, opt, &CompatExchange, nullptr, &mismatch);
    CHECK(mismatch.exitCode == 4);
    CHECK(printed.stdoutText.find("protocol.mismatch") != std::string::npos);
    // A host without the command: command.unknown keeps exit 2 and names the host's major.minor.
    g_compatReply = Arcane::Remote::ToJson(Arcane::Remote::MakeError("stats.frame", "command.unknown", "no such command", ""));
    g_compatReply["protocolMinor"] = 4;
    Arcane::Remote::ClientBlock unknown;
    const auto missing = Servitor::Call(req, opt, &CompatExchange, nullptr, &unknown);
    CHECK(unknown.exitCode == 2);
    CHECK(missing.stdoutText.find("1.4") != std::string::npos);
}

TEST_CASE("servitor --version names its version and protocol", "[servitor][client]") {
    CHECK(std::string(Servitor::kVersion) == "0.1.0");
    CHECK(Servitor::kProtocolMajors.size() == 1);
    CHECK(Servitor::kProtocolMajors[0] == 1);
    const char* argv[] = {"servitor", "--engine", "D:/some/root", "status"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(argv), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.engine == "D:/some/root");
    const char* bare[] = {"servitor", "--engine"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions missingValue;
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(2, const_cast<char**>(bare), missingValue, &err));
}
```

### Task 15: Discovery, status, selection

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ReadDescriptor`, `IsStale`, `IsHung`, `Descriptor::abi` (Task 7). `ExitFromHost` (Task 3).
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Servitor {
struct LiveHost {
    Descriptor descriptor;
    std::filesystem::path path;
    bool hung = false;
};

struct Scan {
    std::vector<LiveHost> live;
    std::vector<std::filesystem::path> removedStale;
};

Scan ScanProject(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot);
std::optional<LiveHost> Select(const Scan& scan, const GlobalOptions& opt, Envelope* error);
nlohmann::json StatusData(const Scan& scan);
std::filesystem::path HostDiagnosticsDir(HostKind kind, const GlobalOptions& opt = {}); // defined below; resolves through HostsDir (rev 8.3 V7); Task 22's HostLogFile and its test use it
}
```

`ScanProject` reads `Saved/Remote/*.json`. Stale files (`IsStale`) are deleted and listed in `removedStale`. Hung files (`IsHung`, heartbeat older than 10 s) stay, with `hung = true`. `Select`: zero live hosts → `target.none` exit 4. Two or more and neither `--pid` nor `--host` narrows to one → `target.ambiguous` exit 2, `candidates` are `kind:pid`. `--host editor` with two editors and no `--pid` is still ambiguous. A protocol major other than 1 on the chosen descriptor → `protocol.mismatch` exit 4. `Select` still returns a hung host. Hung is not a reason to delete the file or to refuse the selection. `servitor status` prints `MakeOk("status", StatusData(scan), {})`. It lists every live host. It does not connect, and it does not call `ObserveTarget`. Each `data.hosts` object keeps the s7.1 fields (`hostKind`, `pid`, `port`, `state`, `frame`, `abi`, `project`, `heartbeatAgeMs`) and adds `hung`, copied from `LiveHost::hung`. `abi` is `descriptor.abi` (Task 7's `Descriptor::abi`, written by `HostSession::Start` from `kGamePluginABIVersion`; ruling F27). The s7.1 example omits `hung` because those rows are fresh (`heartbeatAgeMs` 410 and 120). Spec s4.4 requires a stale heartbeat to be reported hung. `heartbeatAgeMs` is `max(0, nowUnixMs - heartbeatUnixMs)` using the same `nowUnixMs` as `IsHung`. Age 10000 is not hung. Age 10001 is hung. `data.stale` lists removed files. Status does not print tokens. `abi` stays the field s7.1 already names. A status envelope is exit 0.

`Run`, after `Select` succeeds for a command other than `status`, fills a `TargetWatch` and the live `ExchangeFn` samples it on the schedule in Task 14's **Target watch**. `command` is the wire command. `pid`, `processStart`, and `descriptorPath` come from the selected `LiveHost` (`descriptor.pid`, `descriptor.processStart`, `path`). `diagnosticsDir` is the host's report directory, `Paths::Location::DiagnosticsDir` (Global Constraints, ruling AX (2)): when the selected descriptor names a project, `Paths::Resolve(Paths::Location::DiagnosticsDir, Paths::ForProject(descriptor.project))` (`<project>/Saved/Diagnostics`; `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Paths.hpp:33`, `ForProject` `:49`); otherwise the sibling host's exe-relative directory, `HostDiagnosticsDir(kind)` below. `servitor` lives in `Binaries/<Config>-<os>-<arch>-md/Servitor/` (R8-4; `Arcane::Platform::ExecutablePath()` in `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Process.hpp:26` is that exe as a `std::filesystem::path` on all three platforms; `Arcane::ExecutablePathUtf8` in `Base/Engine.hpp:24` is the same path as UTF-8 bytes, and wrapping those bytes in `std::filesystem::path` on Windows would decode them as the ANSI code page). The runtime exe is `../ArcaneRuntime/ArcaneRuntime` (`.exe` on Windows), and the same shape names the editor and the server (Task 16). `Diagnostics::ReportDirFor` with an empty `configured` (`dumpDir`) is `<exeDir>/diagnostics` on every platform (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Diagnostics.hpp:316-325`: comment `:316-322`, declaration `:323-325`). The descriptor has no exe path. Do not add one.

Define `HostDiagnosticsDir` in `Servitor`:

```cpp
std::filesystem::path HostDiagnosticsDir(Arcane::Remote::HostKind kind, const GlobalOptions& opt) {
    const auto bin = HostsDir(opt, nullptr); // rev 8.3 V7: --engine, SERVITOR_ENGINE, sibling, package
    if (!bin)
        return {};
    const char* folder = kind == Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Editor ? "ArcaneEditor"
                       : kind == Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Server ? "ArcaneServer"
                       : "ArcaneRuntime";
    return *bin / folder / "diagnostics";
}
```

**Transport (rev 8.3 V3).** The live `ExchangeFn` lives in `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Transport.hpp/.cpp`:

```cpp
// Connects to 127.0.0.1:desc.port (ConnectSocketWithTimeout), sends BuildPost("/remote/v1/call", ...),
// then loops PollSockets(250 ms) -> recv -> ParseHttpReply until a reply or a watch verdict.
// Every wake, including a timeout, samples ObserveTarget(watch, now) first (Task 14 Target watch).
// A connect refused before the body was sent sets connectionRefusedBeforeBody (the retry rule).
// A reply that is not HTTP 200, or whose body TryParseEnvelope rejects, is `internal` exit 1.
ExchangeResult LiveExchange(const std::string& requestBody, void* user); // user = LiveTarget*
struct LiveTarget { Arcane::Remote::Descriptor descriptor; TargetWatch watch; };
// GET /remote/v1/status and GET /remote/v1/catalog over the same codec.
std::optional<nlohmann::json> GetJson(const Arcane::Remote::Descriptor& desc, std::string_view target,
                                      std::uint32_t timeoutMs, Arcane::Remote::Envelope* error);
```

It uses ArcaneNet's header-only `TcpSocket.hpp` (`ConnectSocketWithTimeout`, Task 6's `SetNonBlocking` and `PollSockets`) and ArcaneRemoteProtocol's `HttpClient.hpp`, nothing else. `Run` installs `LiveExchange` once `Select` has a host. Test (`[servitor][client]`, in `DiscoverTest.cpp`): start a Task 6 `RemoteListener` on an ephemeral port with a test token and a registered no-op command (the `RemoteTestSupport.hpp` guards). Then `LiveExchange` returns that command's envelope; with a wrong token it returns `auth.denied`, exit 3; with the listener stopped it sets `connectionRefusedBeforeBody`.

**Engine root (rev 8.3 V7, spec AM8).** `Discover.hpp` also declares:

```cpp
// The directory that holds ArcaneEditor/, ArcaneRuntime/ and ArcaneServer/. First match wins:
// opt.engine, then SERVITOR_ENGINE, then the source sibling of servitor's own folder,
// then the package root three levels up. nullopt fills *error (when non-null) with
// engine.unresolved, exit 4, remediation "pass --engine <root> or set SERVITOR_ENGINE".
std::optional<std::filesystem::path> HostsDir(const GlobalOptions& opt, Arcane::Remote::Envelope* error);
// The same mapping for one explicit root (rules 1 and 2): <root>/sdk/sdk.json present ->
// <root>/Binaries/<engineConfig>-<os>-<arch>-md (engineConfig from that file; the
// -<os>-<arch>-md tail is SERVITOR_OUTPUTDIR's); absent -> <root>/Binaries/SERVITOR_OUTPUTDIR.
// A present sdk.json without a string engineConfig is nullopt.
std::optional<std::filesystem::path> HostsDirForRoot(const std::filesystem::path& root);
```

`HostDiagnosticsDir` becomes `HostDiagnosticsDir(HostKind kind, const GlobalOptions& opt = {})` over `HostsDir`. An unresolved root returns an empty path, so no `.arcdiag` is attached. It never falls back to a hard-coded `parent_path().parent_path()`. `Run` passes its parsed `GlobalOptions`.

Add `RemoteTest::ScopedEnv` (set or unset one environment variable, restore it on scope exit) to `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/RemoteTestSupport.hpp`, modelled on `ScopedEnvironmentValue` in `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/MeshNodeTest.cpp:349`. Append to `DiscoverTest.cpp`:

```cpp
TEST_CASE("HostsDir: --engine beats SERVITOR_ENGINE beats the sibling; sdk.json picks the config", "[servitor][client]") {
    namespace fs = std::filesystem;
    const fs::path tmp = fs::temp_directory_path() / ("servitor-hostsdir-" + std::to_string(Arcane::EditorLock::Self().pid));
    fs::remove_all(tmp);
    const fs::path pkg = tmp / "pkg";                       // an installed engine
    fs::create_directories(pkg / "sdk");
    { std::ofstream(pkg / "sdk" / "sdk.json") << R"({"engineConfig":"Release"})"; }
    const fs::path src = tmp / "src";                       // a source checkout (no sdk.json)
    fs::create_directories(src);
    const fs::path broken = tmp / "broken";                 // sdk.json without engineConfig
    fs::create_directories(broken / "sdk");
    { std::ofstream(broken / "sdk" / "sdk.json") << R"({})"; }

    const std::string tail = std::string(SERVITOR_OUTPUTDIR).substr(std::string(SERVITOR_OUTPUTDIR).find('-'));
    CHECK(Servitor::HostsDirForRoot(pkg) == pkg / "Binaries" / ("Release" + tail));
    CHECK(Servitor::HostsDirForRoot(src) == src / "Binaries" / SERVITOR_OUTPUTDIR);
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::HostsDirForRoot(broken).has_value());

    RemoteTest::ScopedEnv env("SERVITOR_ENGINE", src.string());
    Servitor::GlobalOptions flag;
    flag.engine = pkg.string();
    CHECK(Servitor::HostsDir(flag, nullptr) == Servitor::HostsDirForRoot(pkg));      // the flag wins
    Servitor::GlobalOptions none;
    CHECK(Servitor::HostsDir(none, nullptr) == Servitor::HostsDirForRoot(src));      // then the env var
    Arcane::Remote::Envelope error;
    Servitor::GlobalOptions bad;
    bad.engine = broken.string();
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::HostsDir(bad, &error).has_value());
    CHECK(error.errors[0].code == "engine.unresolved");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(error) == 4);
    fs::remove_all(tmp);
}

    RemoteTest::ScopedEnv env("SERVITOR_ENGINE", std::nullopt);   // unset for this case

    REQUIRE(dir.has_value());   // ArcaneTests runs from Binaries/<cfg>/ArcaneTests; its sibling folders exist
    CHECK(*dir == Arcane::Platform::ExecutablePath().parent_path().parent_path());
}
```

```cpp
    CHECK(data.dump().find("secret-token") == std::string::npos);
    d.heartbeatUnixMs = nowMs - 60000;
    REQUIRE(Arcane::Remote::WriteDescriptorAtomic(

    opt.hostKind = "runtime";
    Arcane::Remote::Envelope err;
    auto selected = Servitor::Select(scan, opt, &err);
    REQUIRE(selected);
    CHECK(selected->hung);
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root);
}
```

`DescriptorPath` includes kind, so the editor and runtime files of this pid are two paths. Both are live because pid and `processStart` match this process. The stale file uses pid 1 and `start + 1`, and `ScanProject` deletes it. Empty options are `target.ambiguous` exit 2. `--pid` of this process plus `--host runtime` selects port 2.

These cases run on all three CI lanes because Task 7's `Platform::IsProcessLive` (ArcaneCore, rev 8.3 V2) carries both branches of `ReadLive` (ruling E-4), including the POSIX three tells (`Project.cpp:878-889`, `Platform::QueryProcess` in `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Platform/Process.hpp`: `found`, `start` match, not `exited`). No exclusion entry is needed.
