In both, the child's stdout and stderr go to `<host exe dir>/<HostName>.servitor-launch.log`, truncated per launch (the `RuntimeLaunch` pattern, `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Project/RuntimeLaunch.hpp:75-80`: Windows `STARTF_USESTDHANDLES` with an inheritable file handle; POSIX `posix_spawn_file_actions_addopen` on fds 1 and 2), never to `servitor`'s stdout, which is the envelope channel (R8-8). The child's stdin is the null device. A boot failure stays readable in that file.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
struct WaitArgs {
    std::string what;          // ready | settled | frames | reload | state
    std::uint32_t frames = 0;  // Optional
    std::string state;         // Optional
    std::uint32_t timeoutMs = 30000; // milliseconds, copied from Call::timeoutMs; host.wait does not scale
};

// ruling F9: reads session.Gate() and session.GetConfig() through the const
// accessors Task 11 declared; `started` is when the wait job was armed.
ARC_REMOTE_API Envelope WaitPoll(const HostSession& session, const WaitArgs& args, std::uint64_t startedFrame,
                  std::uint32_t generationAtArm, bool hasRenderer,
                  std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point started);

// Commands/SessionCommands.hpp. Registers host.quit and host.wait. Safe to call
// twice; a later call replaces the stored pointers (ruling F11). Null pointers
// make both commands answer host.unavailable (exit 5) without dereferencing.
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterSessionCommands(HostSession* session, JobTable* jobs);
}

// Servitor/Launch.hpp (rev 8.3 V3/V7). The client half of `servitor launch`.
namespace Servitor {
// <HostsDir>/<Host>/<Host>[.exe]; nullopt fills *error with engine.unresolved (exit 4) and the
// path it tried. Launch calls this first and spawns nothing when it fails.
std::optional<std::filesystem::path> HostExePath(Arcane::Remote::HostKind kind, const GlobalOptions& opt,
                                                 Arcane::Remote::Envelope* error);
// Spawns detached (ruling E-2) with --remote (+ --remote-descriptor when given), then polls the
// descriptor until it is not IsStale and its state is "ready", or opt.timeoutMs (host.busy, exit 5).
// Returns the exit code and fills *out.
int Launch(Arcane::Remote::HostKind kind, const GlobalOptions& opt, std::span<const std::string> hostArgs,
           Arcane::Remote::Envelope* out);
}
```

`host.wait` copies `Call::timeoutMs` into `WaitArgs::timeoutMs` and does not scale it. `--timeout 1` on the CLI is 1000 ms on this job. The test that sets `args.timeoutMs = 0` is an in-process assignment meaning already timed out. `ParseGlobals` never stores 0.

`servitor launch editor|runtime|server` adds `--remote` (and `--remote-descriptor` when the user passed one) to the child command line and polls until that pid's descriptor is not `IsStale` (ArcaneRemoteProtocol, which calls `Platform::IsProcessLive`; never `EditorLock`, rev 8.3 V2) and `state` is `ready`, or `timeoutMs` elapses (`host.busy` exit 5). The exe path is `<HostsDir>/ArcaneEditor/ArcaneEditor`, `<HostsDir>/ArcaneRuntime/ArcaneRuntime` or `<HostsDir>/ArcaneServer/ArcaneServer`, each with `.exe` on Windows, where `<HostsDir>` is Task 15's `HostsDir(opt, &error)` (rev 8.3 V7). An unresolved root, or a resolved one whose host exe does not exist, answers `engine.unresolved` (exit 4) with the path it tried in `message`, and spawns nothing. Add the `[servitor][client]` case `launch with an unresolvable --engine spawns nothing` to `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/LaunchTest.cpp` (whole file inside the Dist guard). With `opt.engine` naming an empty temp root, `HostExePath(HostKind::Editor, opt, &err)` is nullopt, `err.errors[0].code == "engine.unresolved"`, and its message contains the root. `Launch(HostKind::Editor, opt, {}, &out)` returns 4 with the same code. Launch calls `HostExePath` before any spawn, so nullopt proves no child started. All three are plain `ConsoleApp` projects (`premake5.lua` projects `ArcaneEditor` `:1095`, `ArcaneRuntime` `:964`, `ArcaneServer` `:884`), so there is no app bundle on macOS.

`host.quit` sets `RequestQuit`. The host loop notices `TakeQuit()` and exits `remote-quit`. `--force` is the CLI flag that sends `host.quit` with `args.force = true`. Editor dirty documents: a function pointer `bool (*dirty)(void*)` on `HostSession::Config`. When it returns true and `force` is false and `--discard-changes` was not passed, the command returns `host.busy` `detail.state = "modal"` exit 5 and does not quit. The pointer is a function pointer, not `std::function`.

`host.wait` is `ExecThread::Job`. It arms a job and binds the waiter. Each `StepAll` calls `WaitPoll` once.

- `ready`: done when `gate.State()` is `ready`, `playing`, or `paused`.
- `settled`: if `hasRenderer` is false, return `MakeError("host.wait", "capture.no-renderer", "this host has no renderer", "wait on an editor or a runtime")` immediately, exit 2, job `Failed`. If true, done when the host's `settled()` pointer (installed by editor and runtime, calling `SettleConverged` and `ShaderCompiler::IsIdle`) returns true.
- `frames`: done when `gate.frame >= startedFrame + args.frames`.
- `reload`: done when `pluginGeneration != generationAtArm`. `NoteReload` is what changes it. `Reload`'s return type stays `bool`.
- `state`: done when `gate.State() == args.state`.

Timeout: the step sees `timeoutMs == 0 || steady_clock::now() - started > timeoutMs` (`started` is the `WaitPoll` argument the arming command recorded), finishes the job with `MakeOk("host.wait", {{"timedOut", true}, {"what", args.what}}, gate.Block())`, `ok` true, empty `errors`. `ExitFromHost` is 8. Do not put `wait.timeout` in `errors` (that would force `ok` false). The code `wait.timeout` is still mapped to 8 if present; the wire for this path is `data.timedOut`.

`n` is not this command. `what` missing → `arg.missing` exit 2.

```cpp
TEST_CASE("wait reload completes when NoteReload bumps the generation", "[remote][host]") {
    RemoteTest::ScopedHostKind kindGuard(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime);
    auto root = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-wait-reload";

    args.what = "reload";
    args.timeoutMs = 5000;
    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    auto pending = Arcane::Remote::WaitPoll(session, args, 0, gen, true, started);
    CHECK_FALSE(pending.data.value("done", false));
    session.NoteReload(gen + 1, true, "reloaded", 0);       // the host passes its PluginHost generation (ruling F10)
    auto done = Arcane::Remote::WaitPoll(session, args, 0, gen, true, started);
    CHECK(done.ok);

TEST_CASE("wait settled on a server is capture.no-renderer exit 2", "[remote][host]") {
    RemoteTest::ScopedHostKind kindGuard(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Server);
    Arcane::Remote::WaitArgs args;
    args.what = "settled";

    session.Start();
    auto env = Arcane::Remote::WaitPoll(session, args, 0, 0, false, std::chrono::steady_clock::now());

static bool RemoteSettledNever(void*) { return false; }

TEST_CASE("wait state times out with ok true and exit 8", "[remote][host]") {
    RemoteTest::ScopedHostKind kindGuard(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime);
    Arcane::Remote::HostSession::Config cfg;
    cfg.kind = Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime;
    cfg.cvarContext = Arcane::CVarContext::LocalHost;
    cfg.projectRoot = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-wait-timeout";
    cfg.settled = &RemoteSettledNever;
    Arcane::Remote::HostSession session(cfg);
    session.Start();
    session.Gate().SetState("ready");
    Arcane::Remote::WaitArgs args;
    args.what = "state";
    args.state = "playing";
    args.timeoutMs = 0;                                      // in-process: already timed out
    auto env = Arcane::Remote::WaitPoll(session, args, 0, 0, true, std::chrono::steady_clock::now());
    CHECK(env.ok);
    CHECK(env.errors.empty());
    CHECK(env.data["timedOut"] == true);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(env) == 8);
    // A real elapsed timeout through `started` (ruling F9): 50 ms budget, armed 100 ms ago.
    Arcane::Remote::WaitArgs settled;
    settled.what = "settled";
    settled.timeoutMs = 50;
    const auto armed = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - std::chrono::milliseconds(100);
    auto late = Arcane::Remote::WaitPoll(session, settled, 0, 0, true, armed);
    CHECK(late.ok);
    CHECK(late.data["timedOut"] == true);
    auto early = Arcane::Remote::WaitPoll(session, settled, 0, 0, true, std::chrono::steady_clock::now());
    CHECK_FALSE(early.data.value("done", false));             // settled() is false and 50 ms have not passed
    CHECK_FALSE(early.data.value("timedOut", false));
    session.Stop("test");
}
```

`WaitPoll` returns `data.done == false` when the condition is not met and the timeout has not fired, so the job step keeps the job `Running`. Timeout and success return a finished envelope. `timeoutMs == 0` means already timed out; otherwise the timeout is `steady_clock::now() - started > timeoutMs`. `WaitPoll` reads the gate and the `settled` / `hookUser` hooks through `session.Gate() const` and `session.GetConfig()` (Task 11, ruling F9).

`servitor launch`, `servitor wait` and `servitor quit` are rows in `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Verbs.cpp` (ruling F17) and call these functions. `HostSession::Start` calls `RegisterSessionCommands(this, &m_jobs)` (ruling F12), so every armed host serves `host.quit` and `host.wait`; the `host.wait` command records `std::chrono::steady_clock::now()` when it arms the job and passes it to every `WaitPoll`. The editor installs `dirty`, `settled` and `hookUser` on its `Config` in `EditorAppFrame.cpp`; the runtime installs `settled` and `hookUser` in `RuntimeApp.cpp`; the server installs neither.

- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.cpp` (`Start` calls `RegisterCatalogCommands()`; no `Config` field, the commands read only the process `CommandRegistry` and Astra's meta registry)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Verbs.cpp` (rows `commands`, `list`, `describe`, `schema`, `call`; ruling F17)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/CommandsCliTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CommandRegistry::Catalogue`, `Find`, `SchemaOf`, `Invoke`, `SchemaFor` (Task 4) for the **host** catalogue. `Astra::GetMetaByName(std::string_view)` (`ThirdParty/Astra/include/Astra/Reflection/MetaRegistry.hpp:924`). A static table in this cpp for the **client** manifest.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Servitor {
nlohmann::json StaticManifest(); // no socket, no descriptor; an OBJECT {version, protocolMajors, verbs, exitCodes} (rev 8.3 V8)
std::string RenderStaticManifest(const GlobalOptions& opt); // the exact text PrintStaticManifest writes (ruling F31)
int PrintStaticManifest(const GlobalOptions& opt); // writes RenderStaticManifest(opt) to stdout; exit 0
}

namespace Arcane::Remote {
// Commands/CatalogCommands.hpp (ruling F13). Registers, once per process
// (a second call is a no-op), two Observe host commands, needsFrame false:
//   catalog.describe {name}        -> data {name, summary, access, mutates, play,
//                                     thread, needsFrame, module, schema: SchemaOf(name)};
//                                     an unregistered name is command.unknown (exit 2)
//   schema.type {kind, name}       -> kind "component" or "resource"; data is
//                                     SchemaFor(*Astra::GetMetaByName(name)); an unknown
//                                     name is component.unknown / resource.not-found (exit 6)
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterCatalogCommands();
}
```

The two args structs (`CatalogDescribeArgs {std::string name;}`, `SchemaTypeArgs {std::string kind; std::string name;}`) are reflected with `ARC_REFLECT_TYPE` in `CatalogCommands.cpp` above the registration, so `argsMeta` is non-null.

`servitor commands --format json` with no host and no project prints `StaticManifest()` and returns 0. It does not call `ScanProject` and it does not return `target.none`. The manifest is a JSON object (rev 8.3 V8, spec AM8):
- `version`: `kVersion`;
- `protocolMajors`: `kProtocolMajors`;
- `verbs`;
- `exitCodes`: one row per code, 0-8, 9 and 130/143, each `{code, meaning, launcherOnly}`. Code 9 is `launcherOnly: true` with meaning `launcher: engine.not-found, package.not-installed, package.incompatible`, and servitor never returns it.

`verbs` is the array of `{name, summary, access}` for the P0 verbs: `status`, `launch`, `quit`, `wait`, `world`, `commands`, `list`, `describe`, `schema`, `call`, `cvar`, `console`, `logs`, `problems`, `entity`, `ecs`, `resource`, `scene`, `selection`, `stats`, `screenshot`, `compare`, `play`, `stop`, `pause`, `resume`, `step`. `servitor commands` without `--format json` prints the same rows as human text and also returns 0.

`servitor list`, `servitor describe <cmd>`, and `servitor schema component|resource <Name>` talk to a host: `GET /remote/v1/catalog`, then `catalog.describe` / `schema.type` via `POST /remote/v1/call`. Those two host commands are registered by `RegisterCatalogCommands()`, which `HostSession::Start` calls on every host (ruling F13). No host → `target.none` exit 4. That is the opposite of `servitor commands`.

`servitor call <command>` sends `POST /remote/v1/call` with `{"command": <command>, "args": {...}}`. Unknown CLI flags are exit 2 (`arg.unknown`), not ignored.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
TEST_CASE("servitor commands --format json needs no host", "[servitor][client]") {
    const auto manifest = Servitor::StaticManifest();
    REQUIRE(manifest.is_object());
    CHECK(manifest.at("version") == std::string(Servitor::kVersion));
    CHECK(manifest.at("protocolMajors") == nlohmann::json::array({1}));
    bool launcherNine = false;
    for (const auto& row : manifest.at("exitCodes"))
        if (row.at("code") == 9)
            launcherNine = row.at("launcherOnly").get<bool>()
                && row.at("meaning").get<std::string>().find("package.not-installed") != std::string::npos;
    CHECK(launcherNine);
    bool saw = false;
    for (const auto& row : manifest.at("verbs")) {
        if (row["name"] == "commands")
            saw = true;
        CHECK_FALSE(row["summary"].get<std::string>().empty());
    }
    CHECK(saw);
    const char* argv[] = {"servitor", "commands", "--format", "json"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(argv), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.format == Servitor::Format::Json);
    // The unit test never calls PrintStaticManifest: stdout is Catch2's
    // reporter channel (ruling F31). RenderStaticManifest is the same text.
    const std::string text = Servitor::RenderStaticManifest(opt);
    CHECK(text.find("\"name\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(text.find("\"entity\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(text).is_object());
}

TEST_CASE("catalog.describe and schema.type answer through Invoke", "[servitor][client]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterCatalogCommands();
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterCatalogCommands();   // a second call is a no-op
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    Arcane::Remote::Call describe;
    describe.command = "catalog.describe";
    describe.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "catalog.describe"}};
    const auto described = reg.Invoke(describe);
    REQUIRE(described.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(described.envelope.data["name"] == "catalog.describe");
    CHECK(described.envelope.data["access"] == "observe");
    CHECK(described.envelope.data["schema"]["properties"].contains("name"));
    describe.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "no.such"}};
    const auto missing = reg.Invoke(describe);
    CHECK(missing.envelope.errors[0].code == "command.unknown");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(missing.envelope) == 2);
    Arcane::Remote::Call schema;
    schema.command = "schema.type";
    schema.args = nlohmann::json{{"kind", "component"}, {"name", "RemoteDemoArgs"}};
    const auto typed = reg.Invoke(schema);
    REQUIRE(typed.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(typed.envelope.data["properties"].contains("position"));
    schema.args = nlohmann::json{{"kind", "component"}, {"name", "NoSuchType"}};
    const auto unknown = reg.Invoke(schema);
    CHECK(unknown.envelope.errors[0].code == "component.unknown");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(unknown.envelope) == 6);
}
```

### Task 18: `--all` fan-out (AM2)

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/FanOut.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/FanOut.cpp`
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/FanOutTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scan`, `WorstExit`, `ExitFromHost`. `std::function` is allowed only in this client helper. Registered commands stay function pointers.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Servitor {
struct FanOutResult {
    nlohmann::json wrapper; // {"hosts":[{"pid","hostKind","envelope"}]}
    int exitCode = 0;
};

using FanOutPost = int (*)(const Descriptor& descriptor, const nlohmann::json& call,
                           Envelope* out, void* user);

FanOutResult FanOut(const Scan& scan, std::string_view hostKindFilter, bool allKinds,
                    const nlohmann::json& call, bool drive, bool yes, Format format,
                    FanOutPost post, void* user);
}
```

`--all` is every live host of the project. `--all editor` (the token after `--all` when it is a kind) filters. A Drive command (the static manifest's `access` is `drive`, or the call's command is in the drive set `cvar.set`, `console.exec`, `host.quit`, `play.*`, `capture.compare` with bless) requires `yes == true`. Otherwise `arg.inapplicable` exit 2 and **zero** sends. Observe does not require `--yes`.

The wrapper is `{hosts:[{pid, hostKind, envelope}]}`. Ndjson is one frame per host, then the result frame. `exitCode` is `WorstExit` of the per-host exits. A busy host (5) beside a dead host (7) exits 7.

The test does not open sockets. `post` is the function pointer in **Produces**. The first call's `drive` flag is false (`cvar.get` is observe). The second passes `drive = true` and `yes = false`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
TEST_CASE("fan-out worst exit is 7 when one host is dead and one is busy", "[servitor][client]") {
    Servitor::Scan scan;
    Servitor::LiveHost a;
    a.descriptor.pid = 1;
    a.descriptor.hostKind = Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime;
    a.descriptor.protocol = 1;
    Servitor::LiveHost b = a;
    b.descriptor.pid = 2;
    b.descriptor.hostKind = Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Server;
    scan.live = {a, b};
    int sends = 0;
    auto post = [](const Arcane::Remote::Descriptor& d, const nlohmann::json&, Arcane::Remote::Envelope* out, void* user) {
        *static_cast<int*>(user) += 1;
        if (d.pid == 1)
            *out = Arcane::Remote::MakeError("cvar.get", "host.busy", "full", "retry");
        else
            *out = Arcane::Remote::MakeError("cvar.get", "host.died", "gone", "relaunch");
        return Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(*out);
    };
    // FanOut's post parameter is the pointer above. Bind `sends` as user.
    const auto result = Servitor::FanOut(scan, {}, true,
        nlohmann::json{{"command", "cvar.get"}, {"args", {{"name", "render.vsync"}}}},
        false, false, Servitor::Format::Json, post, &sends);
    CHECK(sends == 2);
    CHECK(result.exitCode == 7);
    CHECK(result.wrapper["hosts"].size() == 2);
}

TEST_CASE("drive fan-out without --yes sends nothing", "[servitor][client]") {
    Servitor::Scan scan;
    Servitor::LiveHost a;
    a.descriptor.pid = 1;
    a.descriptor.protocol = 1;
    a.descriptor.hostKind = Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Editor;
    scan.live = {a};
    int sends = 0;
    auto post = [](const Arcane::Remote::Descriptor&, const nlohmann::json&, Arcane::Remote::Envelope*, void* user) {
        *static_cast<int*>(user) += 1;
        return 0;
    };
    const auto result = Servitor::FanOut(scan, {}, true,
        nlohmann::json{{"command", "cvar.set"}},
        true, false, Servitor::Format::Json, post, &sends);
    CHECK(sends == 0);
    CHECK(result.exitCode == 2);
}
```

### Task 19: `world.list` and `--world` (before any command that takes a world)

ECS, resource, and scene commands consume this. It lands first so their tests can pass a world.

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Worlds.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Worlds.cpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/WorldCommands.hpp` (`RegisterWorldCommands`)
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/WorldCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.hpp` (append `WorldTable worlds` to `HostSession::Config`, last, in the room Task 11 left)
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.cpp` (wiring, ruling F12: `Start` calls `SetCommandWorlds(config.worlds)` and `RegisterWorldCommands()`; `Stop` calls `SetCommandWorlds(WorldTable{})`)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/App/EditorAppFrame.cpp`, `Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeApp.cpp` (under the D-1 guard) and `Source/Programs/ArcaneServer/src/ServerApp.cpp` (where Task 11 builds each `Config`: install `worlds` = a `WorldListFn` returning the host's one world, `{"main", ToString(mode), &registry}`, with `user` = the app)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Client.hpp` and `Client.cpp` (ruling F14: `GlobalOptions::world`, `--world <name>` in `ParseGlobals`, and `Call` posts `options.world` when it is non-empty)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Verbs.cpp` (row `world list` -> `world.list`; ruling F17)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/WorldCommandTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GlobalOptions`, `ParseGlobals`, `Call` (Task 14), `ScopedRemotePointer` (Task 4's `RemoteTestSupport.hpp`). `Arcane::NetMode` and `Arcane::ToString(NetMode)`, now in ArcaneCore: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Core/NetMode.hpp` (enum `:8`, `ToString` `:9`, returns `const char*`), strings in `NetMode.cpp:5-15` (`DedicatedServer` → `"DedicatedServer"`). `Plugin/SystemFactory.hpp` no longer declares either. The host fills `role` from `Runtime::Mode()` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneEngine/src/Arcane/Base/Runtime.hpp:110`) in host code; `Worlds.hpp` itself needs only ArcaneCore plus a forward declaration of `Astra::Registry` (Astra is a public third-party of ArcaneCore, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/ArcaneCore.arcmodule`).
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
struct WorldInfo {
    std::string name;
    std::string role;                    // ToString(Runtime::Mode())
    Astra::Registry* registry = nullptr; // not serialized
};

using WorldListFn = std::vector<WorldInfo> (*)(void* user);

struct WorldTable {
    WorldListFn fn = nullptr;
    void* user = nullptr;
};

ARC_REMOTE_API Envelope WorldList(const WorldTable& table);
// data.worlds = [{name, role}] sorted by name. registry pointers are not in JSON.

enum class WorldPick { Ok, Missing, Unknown, None };
struct PickedWorld {
    WorldPick pick = WorldPick::Missing;
    WorldInfo world;
    Envelope error;
};

ARC_REMOTE_API PickedWorld ResolveWorld(const WorldTable& table, const std::string& requested);
// requested empty and one world → that world.
// requested empty and several → arg.missing exit 2, error.data.worlds lists names.
// requested names one → that world.
// requested names none → arg.unknown exit 2, candidates are the names.
// table.fn null, or zero worlds → WorldPick::None, host.unavailable exit 5 (nothing ran; ruling S3-9).

// The ONE world table every world-taking command reads (ruling F16). Copies the
// table. Safe to call twice; a later call replaces the stored table (ruling F11).
// HostSession::Start installs config.worlds; Stop installs an empty table.
ARC_REMOTE_API void SetCommandWorlds(const WorldTable& table);
ARC_REMOTE_API WorldTable CommandWorlds();   // a copy of the stored table

// Commands/WorldCommands.hpp: registers world.list (reads CommandWorlds()). A second call is a no-op.
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterWorldCommands();
}
```

`HostSession::Config` gains `WorldTable worlds`. P0 hosts install one world. The check still handles two, so a second world cannot silently win. `world.list` is Observe, `needsFrame` false. Command name `world.list`.

**One world path (ruling F16).** Every world-taking command (Tasks 23, 24, 25) starts with `const PickedWorld picked = ResolveWorld(CommandWorlds(), call.world);` and returns `picked.error` unless `picked.pick == WorldPick::Ok`. There is no per-command world setter (no `SetEcsWorld`, `SetResourceWorld` or `SetSceneWorld`). Tests install a one-row table (the `OneWorld` shape below, with `registry` pointing at the test's registry) through `SetCommandWorlds`, held by an `RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const WorldTable>` whose setter is `[](const WorldTable* t) { SetCommandWorlds(t ? *t : WorldTable{}); }`, so the table is cleared when the case ends.

**`--world` on the client (ruling F14).** `GlobalOptions` gains `std::string world;` (Task 14's struct, `Servitor/Client.hpp`). `ParseGlobals` reads `--world <name>` (a missing value is exit 2, `error` contains `world`). `Call` adds `options.world` to the posted body when `opt.world` is non-empty; the listener already copies `options.world` onto `Call::world` (Task 6).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`WorldCommandTest.cpp` includes `<catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Remote/Worlds.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Remote/Commands/WorldCommands.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Remote/Registry.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>`, `<Servitor/Client.hpp>`, `<Arcane/Core/NetMode.hpp>`, `<Json.hpp>`, and `"RemoteTestSupport.hpp"`. Every case is tagged `[remote][world]`.

```cpp
static std::vector<Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo> TwoWorlds(void*) {
    Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo main;
    main.name = "main";
    main.role = Arcane::ToString(Arcane::NetMode::DedicatedServer);
    Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo other;
    other.name = "other";
    other.role = Arcane::ToString(Arcane::NetMode::Client);
    return {other, main};
}

TEST_CASE("world.list sorts by name and uses NetMode strings", "[remote][world]") {
    Arcane::Remote::WorldTable table;
    table.fn = &TwoWorlds;
    auto env = Arcane::Remote::WorldList(table);
    CHECK(env.ok);
    REQUIRE(env.data["worlds"].size() == 2);
    CHECK(env.data["worlds"][0]["name"] == "main");
    CHECK(env.data["worlds"][0]["role"] == "DedicatedServer");
    CHECK(env.data["worlds"][1]["role"] == "Client");
}

TEST_CASE("a missing world name is arg.missing when two worlds exist", "[remote][world]") {
    Arcane::Remote::WorldTable table;
    table.fn = &TwoWorlds;
    auto missing = Arcane::Remote::ResolveWorld(table, "");
    CHECK(missing.pick == Arcane::Remote::WorldPick::Missing);
    CHECK(missing.error.errors[0].code == "arg.missing");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(missing.error) == 2);
    CHECK(missing.error.data["worlds"].size() == 2);
    auto ok = Arcane::Remote::ResolveWorld(table, "main");
    CHECK(ok.pick == Arcane::Remote::WorldPick::Ok);
    CHECK(ok.world.name == "main");
    auto bad = Arcane::Remote::ResolveWorld(table, "nope");
    CHECK(bad.pick == Arcane::Remote::WorldPick::Unknown);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(bad.error) == 2);
}

static std::vector<Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo> OneWorld(void*) {
    Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo main;
    main.name = "main";
    main.role = "Standalone";
    return {main};
}

TEST_CASE("one world is the default", "[remote][world]") {
    Arcane::Remote::WorldTable table;
    table.fn = &OneWorld;
    auto picked = Arcane::Remote::ResolveWorld(table, "");
    CHECK(picked.pick == Arcane::Remote::WorldPick::Ok);
    CHECK(picked.world.name == "main");
    CHECK(picked.world.role == "Standalone");
    auto none = Arcane::Remote::ResolveWorld(Arcane::Remote::WorldTable{}, "");
    CHECK(none.pick == Arcane::Remote::WorldPick::None);
    CHECK(none.error.errors[0].code == "host.unavailable");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(none.error) == 5);
}

TEST_CASE("world.list reads the command world table and the guard clears it", "[remote][world]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterWorldCommands();
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "world.list";
    {
        Arcane::Remote::WorldTable table;
        table.fn = &TwoWorlds;
        RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable> worlds(
            [](const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable* t) { Arcane::Remote::SetCommandWorlds(t ? *t : Arcane::Remote::WorldTable{}); },
            &table);
        const auto listed = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
        REQUIRE(listed.envelope.ok);
        CHECK(listed.envelope.data["worlds"].size() == 2);
    }
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::CommandWorlds().fn == nullptr);   // cleared on scope exit
    const auto empty = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(empty.envelope.errors[0].code == "host.unavailable");
}

TEST_CASE("--world is parsed and posted as options.world", "[remote][world]") {
    const char* argv[] = {"servitor", "--world", "main", "ecs", "stats"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    std::string err;
    REQUIRE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(5, const_cast<char**>(argv), opt, &err));
    CHECK(opt.world == "main");
    const char* bare[] = {"servitor", "ecs", "stats", "--world"};
    Servitor::GlobalOptions bareOpt;
    CHECK_FALSE(Servitor::ParseGlobals(4, const_cast<char**>(bare), bareOpt, &err));
    CHECK(err.find("world") != std::string::npos);
    static std::string posted;
    posted.clear();
    Servitor::CallRequest req;
    req.command = "ecs.stats";
    Servitor::Call(req, opt,
        [](const std::string& body, void*) {
            posted = body;
            Servitor::ExchangeResult r;
            r.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk("ecs.stats", nlohmann::json::object(), {});
            return r;
        }, nullptr, nullptr);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(posted)["options"]["world"] == "main");
}
```

`OneWorld` is a file-scope function (a `WorldListFn` is a plain function pointer) that returns the single `main` / `Standalone` row. `ResolveWorld(table, "")` then picks `Ok` and `name == "main"`.

### Task 20: `cvar.get`, `cvar.set`, `cvar.list`, `cvar.explain`

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/CvarCommands.hpp` (the step 5 commit stages it; the test includes it)
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/CvarCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.hpp` (a private member `CvarCommandUser m_cvarUser;`)
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.cpp` (wiring, ruling F12: `Start` sets `m_cvarUser = {&CVarRegistry::Get(), config.cvarContext}` (the process registry every host's cvars live in; the context from `Config::cvarContext`), calls `SetCvarCommandUser(&m_cvarUser)` and `RegisterCvarCommands()`; `Stop` calls `SetCvarCommandUser(nullptr)`)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Verbs.cpp` (rows `cvar get|set|list|explain`; ruling F17)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/CvarCommandTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RemoteCVarService::Handle` (Task 0; ArcaneCore, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Config/RemoteCVarService.hpp`, `Handle` declared `:60`, defined `RemoteCVarService.cpp:131`). The cvar types are all ArcaneCore, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Config/`: `CVarDesc` (`CVarRegistry.hpp:29`; `name` is a `std::string_view`). `CVarRegistry::Register` (`CVarRegistry.hpp:352`) returns a `CVarHandle` whose `IsStale()` is `CVarHandle.hpp:14`. `CVarFlags::Protected` (`CVarTypes.hpp:50`). `ApplyMode::Restart` (`CVarTypes.hpp:129`). `CVarContext` (`CVarTypes.hpp:102`). `render.vsync` is PlayerSafe and inherits `ApplyMode::Restart` from the type attribute (`Source/Client/ArcaneRender/src/Arcane/Render/RenderDeviceSettings.hpp:90`, field `:94`, `PlayerSafe` `:95`); the test registers its own `render.vsync` and does not include that Client header. The spec example that says `"apply":"Live"` is the old shape. Today's service text for a protected read is `<name> is protected: never readable remotely` (`RemoteCVarService.cpp:148`) and the mask is `kProtected` `"<protected>"` (`:11`).
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
struct CvarCommandUser {
    CVarRegistry* registry = nullptr;
    CVarContext context = CVarContext::Editor;
};
// Safe to call twice; a later call replaces the stored pointer (ruling F11).
// While it is null (or its registry is), every cvar.* command answers
// host.unavailable (exit 5) and dereferences nothing (ruling S3-9).
ARC_REMOTE_API void SetCvarCommandUser(CvarCommandUser* user);
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterCvarCommands(); // HostSession::Start calls this. A second call returns.
}
```

Contexts the hosts pass: `Editor` on ArcaneEditor, `LocalHost` on ArcaneRuntime, `ServerAdmin` on ArcaneServer.

`cvar.get` / `cvar.explain` / `cvar.list` are Observe, `needsFrame` false. `cvar.set` is Drive and `Mutates::Preferences` (`Register` rejects `Drive` with `Mutates::None`). Do not use `ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND` here: that macro runs at static init and collides with Task 4. `CommandRegistry::Get()` is the process singleton Task 4 owns, and `UnregisterModule("")` does not drop a host command, so a `cvar.get` registered before `main` stays registered in every later test. `RegisterCvarCommands` runs from `HostSession::Start` (Task 11).

The command builds a `RemoteCVarRequest` (`op`, `name`, `value`, `callerId`, `context` last) and copies the response. Protected get, in every context including Editor: `ok` false, `data.value` JSON null, `data.masked` `"<protected>"`, error code `cvar.protected`, `ExitFromHost` 3. `errors[0].message` is the service sentence (it contains `protected` and does not contain the secret). Unknown name: `cvar.unknown`, exit 6. A value the registry rejects as the wrong type: `cvar.type-mismatch`, exit 6. A successful set's `data` is `{name, type, value, previous, setBy, audience, apply, context}` and `undo` is `{label: null, undoable: false, reason: "not-undoable-command"}`. Explain fills `setBy`, `audience`, `apply`, `published`, `pending`, `history` from the structured response.

**`cvar.set` is three `Handle` calls (ruling F18).** Task 0 fills the structured fields only on explain, so `cvar.set` does, through the same `RemoteCVarService` and the same context: (1) `Handle({"get", name, "", callerId, context})`; its parsed `valueJson` is `data.previous` (a Protected cvar's get is refused; `previous` is then JSON null and the set still proceeds, its audit masked by Task 0). (2) `Handle({"set", name, value, callerId, context})`; a failure maps to `cvar.type-mismatch` / `cvar.unknown` / `cvar.protected` as above and stops. (3) `Handle({"explain", name, "", callerId, context})`; its `type`, `setBy`, `audience` and `apply` fill `data`, and its `valueJson` is `data.value`. A Protected explain is refused; then `data.value` is null, `setBy`, `audience` and `apply` are omitted, and `data.masked` is `"<protected>"`. `data.context` is the context's enumerator name. `ToJson` of `UndoBlock` emits `label`, `undoable`, `transaction`, and `reason` when `reason` is non-empty.

CLI: `servitor cvar get <name>`, `servitor cvar set <name> <value>`, `servitor cvar list [prefix]`, `servitor cvar explain <name>`.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/CvarCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Protocol.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Config/CVarRegistry.hpp>
#include "RemoteTestSupport.hpp"

static void AddBool(Arcane::CVarRegistry& reg, const char* name, bool def,
                     Arcane::Audience audience, Arcane::ApplyMode apply,
                     Arcane::CVarFlags flags = Arcane::CVarFlags::None) {
    Arcane::CVarDesc d;
    d.name = name;
    d.type = Arcane::CVarType::Bool;
    d.defaultValue = Arcane::CVarValue::Bool(def);
    d.audience = audience;
    d.apply = apply;
    d.flags = flags;
    d.help = name;
    REQUIRE_FALSE(reg.Register(d).IsStale());
}

TEST_CASE("Protected get in the Editor context is cvar.protected exit 3", "[remote][cvar]") {
    Arcane::CVarRegistry reg;
    Arcane::CVarDesc secret;
    secret.name = "editor.secret";
    secret.type = Arcane::CVarType::String;
    secret.defaultValue = Arcane::CVarValue::String("editorhorse");
    secret.audience = Arcane::Audience::Editor;
    secret.flags = Arcane::CVarFlags::Protected;
    secret.help = "editor.secret";
    REQUIRE_FALSE(reg.Register(secret).IsStale());
    Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser user;
    user.registry = &reg;
    user.context = Arcane::CVarContext::Editor;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser> userGuard(&Arcane::Remote::SetCvarCommandUser, &user);
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterCvarCommands();
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "cvar.get";
    call.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "editor.secret"}};
    const auto env = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call).envelope;
    CHECK_FALSE(env.ok);
    CHECK(env.data["value"].is_null());
    CHECK(env.data["masked"] == "<protected>");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(env.errors.empty());
    CHECK(env.errors[0].code == "cvar.protected");
    CHECK(env.errors[0].message.find("protected") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(env.errors[0].message.find("editorhorse") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(env) == 3);
    CHECK_FALSE(env.data.contains("result"));
}

TEST_CASE("explain of render.vsync reports Restart and PlayerSafe", "[remote][cvar]") {
    Arcane::CVarRegistry reg;
    AddBool(reg, "render.vsync", true, Arcane::Audience::PlayerSafe, Arcane::ApplyMode::Restart);
    Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser user{&reg, Arcane::CVarContext::LocalHost};
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser> userGuard(&Arcane::Remote::SetCvarCommandUser, &user);
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterCvarCommands();
    // The case's name is what it tests (ruling F18): a real cvar.explain.
    Arcane::Remote::Call explain;
    explain.command = "cvar.explain";
    explain.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "render.vsync"}};
    const auto explained = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(explain).envelope;
    REQUIRE(explained.ok);
    CHECK(explained.data["apply"] == "Restart");
    CHECK(explained.data["audience"] == "PlayerSafe");
    CHECK(explained.data["setBy"] == "Default");
    CHECK(explained.data["published"] == true);
    REQUIRE(explained.data["history"].is_array());
    CHECK(explained.data["history"].front()["by"] == "Default");
    Arcane::Remote::Call set;
    set.command = "cvar.set";
    set.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "render.vsync"}, {"value", false}};
    const auto wrote = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(set).envelope;
    CHECK(wrote.ok);
    CHECK(wrote.data["previous"] == true);     // from the leading get
    CHECK(wrote.data["value"] == false);       // from the trailing explain
    CHECK(wrote.data["type"] == "bool");
    CHECK(wrote.data["apply"] == "Restart");
    CHECK(wrote.data["audience"] == "PlayerSafe");
    CHECK(wrote.data["setBy"] == "Console");
    CHECK(wrote.data["context"] == "LocalHost");
    CHECK(wrote.undo->undoable == false);
    CHECK(wrote.undo->reason == "not-undoable-command");
    const auto dumped = Arcane::Remote::ToJson(wrote);
    CHECK(dumped["undo"]["reason"] == "not-undoable-command");
    CHECK_FALSE(dumped.contains("hostFrame"));
    Arcane::Remote::Call bad;
    bad.command = "cvar.set";
    bad.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "render.vsync"}, {"value", "nope"}};
    const auto mismatch = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(bad).envelope;
    CHECK(mismatch.errors[0].code == "cvar.type-mismatch");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(mismatch) == 6);
    Arcane::Remote::Call missing;
    missing.command = "cvar.get";
    missing.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "no.such.cvar"}};
    const auto unknown = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(missing).envelope;
    CHECK(unknown.errors[0].code == "cvar.unknown");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(unknown) == 6);
}

TEST_CASE("cvar commands with no user answer host.unavailable", "[remote][cvar]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterCvarCommands();
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser> userGuard(&Arcane::Remote::SetCvarCommandUser, nullptr);
    Arcane::Remote::Call get;
    get.command = "cvar.get";
    get.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "render.vsync"}};
    const auto env = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(get).envelope;
    CHECK(env.errors[0].code == "host.unavailable");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(env) == 5);
}
```

Command name `console.exec`, Drive, `Mutates::Preferences`, `needsFrame` false. CLI: `servitor console <line>`.

`Execute(line, context, SetBy::Console, &caller)` with `caller.id` equal to `call.callerId`. `cvar_explain` is refused: the envelope is `ok` false, code `command.unavailable`, and the registry command does not run. A command registered with `ServerCanExecute` runs and its text lands in `data.text`. A command without that flag is `permission.denied` exit 3 and does not run (ruling E-1). `undo.reason` is `not-undoable-command`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/ConsoleCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Config/CVarRegistry.hpp>
#include "RemoteTestSupport.hpp"

static int g_flush = 0;
static Arcane::CommandResult FlushCmd(std::string_view, void*) {
    ++g_flush;
    return {true, "flushed"};
}

TEST_CASE("console.exec runs test.flush and refuses cvar_explain", "[remote]") {
    g_flush = 0;
    Arcane::CVarRegistry reg;
    REQUIRE(reg.RegisterCommand("test.flush", Arcane::CVarFlags::ServerCanExecute,
                                "flush", "remote-test", &FlushCmd, nullptr));
    Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser user{&reg, Arcane::CVarContext::ServerAdmin};
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser> userGuard(&Arcane::Remote::SetConsoleCommandUser, &user);
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterConsoleCommand();
    Arcane::Remote::Call ok;
    ok.command = "console.exec";
    ok.callerId = "remote-test";
    ok.args = nlohmann::json{{"line", "test.flush"}};
    const auto ran = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(ok).envelope;
    CHECK(ran.ok);
    CHECK(ran.data["text"] == "flushed");
    CHECK(g_flush == 1);
    CHECK(ran.undo->reason == "not-undoable-command");
    Arcane::Remote::Call denied;
    denied.command = "console.exec";
    denied.args = nlohmann::json{{"line", "cvar_explain render.vsync"}};
    const auto refused = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(denied).envelope;
    CHECK_FALSE(refused.ok);
    CHECK(refused.errors[0].code == "command.unavailable");
    CHECK(g_flush == 1);
}

static int g_secret = 0;
static Arcane::CommandResult SecretCmd(std::string_view, void*) {
    ++g_secret;
    return {true, "ran"};
}

TEST_CASE("console.exec refuses a command without ServerCanExecute", "[remote]") {
    g_secret = 0;
    Arcane::CVarRegistry reg;
    REQUIRE(reg.RegisterCommand("test.secret", Arcane::CVarFlags::None,
                                "not remote", "remote-test", &SecretCmd, nullptr));
    Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser user{&reg, Arcane::CVarContext::ServerAdmin};
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::CvarCommandUser> userGuard(&Arcane::Remote::SetConsoleCommandUser, &user);
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterConsoleCommand();
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "console.exec";
    call.callerId = "remote-test";
    call.args = nlohmann::json{{"line", "test.secret now"}};
    const auto env = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call).envelope;
    CHECK_FALSE(env.ok);
    CHECK(env.errors[0].code == "permission.denied");
    CHECK(env.errors[0].message.find("not remotely executable") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(env) == 3);
    CHECK(g_secret == 0);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate and Debug build per Global Constraints, filter `console.exec runs test.flush*`. Expected: FAIL to compile.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

If the trimmed line starts with `cvar_explain`, return `command.unavailable` and do not call `Execute`. Ruling E-1: `CVarRegistry::Execute` does not check `ServerCanExecute` (`CVarRegistry.cpp:1344-1362` runs any live command and gates only `Cheat`, `:1348-1349`); the flag check and its text live in `RemoteCVarService::RunCommand` (`RemoteCVarService.cpp:113-129`, text `:118`). So before `Execute`, look up the line's first token in `CVarRegistry::ListCommands()` (`CVarRegistry.hpp:483`; each `CVarListEntry` carries `name` and `flags`, `:75-81`). A registered command without `CVarFlags::ServerCanExecute` returns `permission.denied` (exit 3) with `RunCommand`'s sentence, `command '<name>' is not remotely executable (no ServerCanExecute)`, and does not call `Execute`. This applies in every context. A first token that is not a command (a cvar line) falls through to `Execute`, whose context rules already gate Protected and policy. Otherwise call `Execute` with `SetBy::Console` and a `CVarCaller` whose `id` is `call.callerId`. Copy `ExecResult::text` into `data.text` and `ExecResult::ok` into `envelope.ok`. A null user answers `host.unavailable`. Wire `HostSession` as the Files list says, and add the `console` row to `Verbs.cpp`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Filters `console.exec runs test.flush*` and `console.exec refuses a command without ServerCanExecute*`. Expected: PASS. `g_flush` is 1; `g_secret` stays 0 and the refusal exits 3.

Not `Log::FileSinkPath()` (`Log.hpp:103`): in `servitor` that is Servitor's own sink (ruling E-3). `DiagnosticStore::Filtered` and `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` after Task 1 moves the store into ArcaneCore beside `Diagnostics` (`<Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp>`, namespace `Arcane`); today they are `Arcane::Editor` in `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/DiagnosticStore.hpp` (`Filtered` `:69`, `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` `:48`, `SeverityMask` `:23`, `InstallAsEngineSink` `:79`, `UninstallEngineSink` `:80`). `Diagnostics::Publish` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Diagnostics.hpp:819`). `DiagLocator::Entity` (`Diagnostics.hpp:767`; `DiagLocator` `:754`, `Diagnostic` `:786`). The editor's store instance is `EditorApp::m_consoleDiag.store` (`Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/App/EditorApp.hpp:871`, member `:877`), installed as the engine sink at `EditorApp.cpp:1970`. `JobTable::Arm`, `SetSubscriber`, `CancelBySubscriber` (Task 9). `RemoteListener`, `RemoteListener::Atoms`, `RemoteListener::EnqueueSse`, `TakeSseEvents`, `SseLife` (Task 6). `Log::LatestSeq` (Task 2). `HostDiagnosticsDir` (Task 15). The test socket helpers `RemoteTest::ConnectLoopback`, `SendAll`, `RecvUntil` (Task 6's `RemoteTestHttp.hpp`) and `RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer` (Task 4's `RemoteTestSupport.hpp`).
- Produces (ruling F32: every setter and registrar the tests call, each `ARC_REMOTE_API`):

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
// Commands/LogCommands.hpp
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterLogCommand();                  // log.read; a second call returns
ARC_REMOTE_API void SetLogJobs(JobTable* jobs);            // safe to call twice; a later call replaces the stored pointer
// Commands/ProblemCommands.hpp
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterProblemCommand();              // problems.list; a second call returns
ARC_REMOTE_API void SetProblemStore(DiagnosticStore* store); // safe to call twice; a later call replaces the stored pointer;
                                                          // null: problems.list answers host.unavailable (exit 5)
// Follow.hpp (ruling F5: the listener, not its atoms, so the step can reach EnqueueSse)
ARC_REMOTE_API void ApplyFollowEvents(JobTable& jobs, RemoteListener& listener);
}
```

`log.read` and `problems.list`, both Observe, `needsFrame` false, one-shot. `args.follow == true` on either command is `arg.inapplicable`, exit 2, `completesJob` 0, and arms nothing. Ruling P2 (f): the only follow path is `GET /remote/v1/stream`. `ApplyFollowEvents(jobs, listener)` takes the SSE events with `TakeSseEvents(listener.Atoms())`. An `Opened` event whose topic is `logs` or `problems` arms one job with `user = &listener`, `SetSubscriber` to that event's id, and the step returns false. **The follow job starts at the newest record (ruling F4):** at `Opened`, a `log.follow` job's `progress.done` is set to `Log::LatestSeq()`, so its first step publishes only lines logged after the stream opened, never the retained ring; a `problem.follow` job's `progress.done` is set to the store's current `Snapshot().size()`, so it publishes only rows added after it opened. A `Closed` event calls `CancelBySubscriber` for that id. The step publishes new records with `static_cast<RemoteListener*>(job.user)->EnqueueSse(job.subscriberId, line)`, advances `progress.done` to the last seq it sent, and does not outlive the socket. CLI: `servitor logs [--since N] [--level info|warn|error] [--follow]`, `servitor problems [--severity error|warning|info] [--search TEXT] [--follow]`. `--follow` opens `GET /remote/v1/stream?topics=logs` or `topics=problems` and prints each SSE `data:` line as ndjson until the process timeout or the socket closes. It does not `POST /remote/v1/call` `log.read` or `problems.list` with `follow`.

`log.read` data is `{records:[{seq, time, level, logger, text}], dropped}`. `--since` is exclusive. A gap between `since` and the oldest retained seq sets `dropped` to that gap. `--level` drops records below it. When the client has no live host, it reads the dead host's log file if that file exists and adds notification `{code:"logs.from-file", message: <path>}`. It does not invent lines. The path is NOT `Log::FileSinkPath()`, which in `servitor` is `servitor`'s own sink. `HostLogFile(kind, project)` derives the host's path exactly the way `AttachLogSink` does (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Diagnostics.cpp:2307-2323`, POSIX `DiagnosticsPosix.cpp:1025`). AttachLogSink takes the log directory from the report directory (`:2318-2320`), so a host that had a project open logged to `<project>/Saved/Logs/<appName>.log`. HostLogFile resolves that path through `Paths` like Task 15's `diagnosticsDir`, using the dead host's last descriptor `project` when one is known. Otherwise the path is `<exeDir>/Logs/<appName>.log`. Test both branches (ruling F28; the `HostLogFile picks*` case below). `<exeDir>` is the sibling host's directory beside `servitor` (the `HostDiagnosticsDir(kind).parent_path()` of Task 15) and `<appName>` is the host's `Diagnostics` app name, `ArcaneEditor` / `ArcaneRuntime` / `ArcaneServer` (`Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/main.cpp:403`, `Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeHost.cpp:137`, `Source/Programs/ArcaneServer/src/main.cpp:121`). `ReadLogFile(path, env)` is the tested seam.

`problems.list` calls `store.Filtered(mask, search)`. `--search` is that second argument, the same string `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` sees. `--severity error` is `SeverityMask::Error`. A row whose `locator.kind` is `Entity`: if that entity is alive and has `Identity`, `data` carries `locator.guid` as `{"hi","lo"}`; otherwise `locator.guid` is null, `locator.handle` is the packed id, and `warnings` contains `entity.no-identity`.

```cpp
TEST_CASE("log.read returns the ring and follow on the call is refused", "[remote]") {
    // Read from just before the marker: a full-suite ring holds thousands of
    // older lines and log.read returns the oldest window after `since`.
    const std::uint64_t since = Arcane::Log::LatestSeq();
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("remote-log-read-marker");
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterLogCommand();
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "log.read";
    call.args = nlohmann::json{{"since", since}};
    const auto once = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(once.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(once.completesJob == 0);
    bool saw = false;
    for (const auto& row : once.envelope.data["records"]) {
        if (row["text"].get<std::string>().find("remote-log-read-marker") != std::string::npos)
            saw = true;
    }
    CHECK(saw);
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::JobTable> logJobs(&Arcane::Remote::SetLogJobs, &jobs);
    call.args["follow"] = true;
    const auto follow = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(follow.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(follow.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(follow.envelope.errors[0].code == "arg.inapplicable");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(follow.envelope) == 2);
    CHECK(jobs.FinishedCount() == 0);
}

TEST_CASE("the SSE subscriber owns the follow job", "[remote][listener]") {
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<const Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>());
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    // Lines already in the ring must not be replayed into the stream (ruling F4).
    for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i)
        Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("remote-follow-old-line {}", i);
    const SocketType client = RemoteTest::ConnectLoopback(listener.Port());
    RemoteTest::SendAll(client, "GET /remote/v1/stream?topics=logs HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:" +
        std::to_string(listener.Port()) + "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + listener.Token() + "\r\n\r\n");
    const std::string headers = RemoteTest::RecvUntil(client, "\r\n\r\n", std::chrono::seconds(1));
    CHECK(headers.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(headers.find("text/event-stream") != std::string::npos);
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::JobTable> logJobs(&Arcane::Remote::SetLogJobs, &jobs);
    const std::uint64_t seqBeforeOpen = Arcane::Log::LatestSeq();
    Arcane::Remote::JobId followId{};
    const Arcane::Remote::Job* opened = nullptr;
    const auto openDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(1);
    while (opened == nullptr && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < openDeadline) {
        Arcane::Remote::ApplyFollowEvents(jobs, listener);   // ruling F5: the listener, not its atoms
        for (std::uint64_t id = 1; id < 8 && opened == nullptr; ++id) {
            const Arcane::Remote::Job* candidate = jobs.Find(Arcane::Remote::JobId{id});
            if (candidate && candidate->subscriberId != 0) {
                opened = candidate;
                followId = candidate->id;
            }
        }
        if (opened == nullptr)
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
    }
    REQUIRE(opened != nullptr);
    CHECK(opened->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(opened->command == "log.follow");
    CHECK(opened->progress.done >= seqBeforeOpen);       // starts at the newest seq, not 0
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("remote-follow-while-open");
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    // Loop recv until the marker arrives or 1 s passes (ruling F4).
    const std::string pushed = RemoteTest::RecvUntil(client, "remote-follow-while-open", std::chrono::seconds(1));
    CHECK(pushed.find("remote-follow-while-open") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(pushed.find("remote-follow-old-line") == std::string::npos);
    CloseSocket(client);
    Arcane::Remote::JobState state = Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running;
    for (int i = 0; i < 50; ++i) {
        Arcane::Remote::ApplyFollowEvents(jobs, listener);
        const Arcane::Remote::Job* now = jobs.Find(followId);
        REQUIRE(now != nullptr);
        state = now->state;
        if (state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Cancelled)
            break;
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(20));
    }
    CHECK(state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Cancelled);
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("remote-follow-after-close");
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(followId)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Cancelled);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("a dead host falls back to the file sink", "[servitor][client]") {
    const auto path = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-logs-fallback.log";
    std::filesystem::remove(path);
    {
        std::ofstream out(path);
        out << "remote-file-line\n";
    }
    Arcane::Remote::Envelope env;
    Servitor::ReadLogFile(path, env);
    CHECK(env.ok);
    CHECK(env.notifications[0].code == "logs.from-file");
    CHECK(env.notifications[0].message == path.string());
    CHECK(env.data["text"].get<std::string>().find("remote-file-line") != std::string::npos);
    std::filesystem::remove(path);
}

TEST_CASE("HostLogFile picks the project log and falls back to the exe-dir log", "[servitor][client]") {
    // Ruling F28: both branches of the AttachLogSink derivation.
    const auto project = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-logfile-project";
    const auto withProject = Servitor::HostLogFile(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Runtime, project);
    CHECK(withProject.lexically_normal().generic_string() ==
          (project / "Saved" / "Logs" / "ArcaneRuntime.log").lexically_normal().generic_string());
    CHECK(Servitor::HostLogFile(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Editor, project).filename() == "ArcaneEditor.log");
    const auto exeDir = Servitor::HostDiagnosticsDir(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Server).parent_path();
    const auto noProject = Servitor::HostLogFile(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Server);
    CHECK(noProject.lexically_normal().generic_string() ==
          (exeDir / "Logs" / "ArcaneServer.log").lexically_normal().generic_string());
    // rev 8.3 V7: --engine naming a package root whose sdk.json lacks engineConfig is unresolved.
    const auto broken = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-logfile-broken";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(broken / "sdk");
    { std::ofstream(broken / "sdk" / "sdk.json") << "{}"; }
    Servitor::GlobalOptions opt;
    opt.engine = broken.string();
    CHECK(Servitor::HostLogFile(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Server, {}, opt).empty());
    std::filesystem::remove_all(broken);
}
```

- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
ARC_REMOTE_API std::string ShortName(std::string_view qualified); // text after the last "::", or the whole string
enum class NamePick : std::uint8_t { One, None, Many };
struct NameResolution {
    NamePick pick = NamePick::None;
    std::string qualified;
    std::vector<std::string> candidates; // sorted qualified names
};
ARC_REMOTE_API NameResolution PickComponent(const std::vector<std::string>& qualifiedNames, std::string_view requested);
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterEcsCommands(); // entity.get, ecs.query, ecs.stats, ecs.archetypes; a second call returns
}
```

There is no ECS-specific world setter (ruling F16): each command starts with `ResolveWorld(CommandWorlds(), call.world)` and returns the pick's error (`arg.missing`, `arg.unknown`, or `host.unavailable` when no table is installed) unless it is `Ok`.

`PickComponent`: one qualified name whose `ShortName` equals `requested`, or whose full string equals `requested`, is `One`. Two or more short-name hits are `Many` and `candidates` lists the qualified names. Zero is `None`.

`entity.get` args: `entity` (`guid:` or `name:` or a raw integer handle), `components` (array; empty means every reflected component on the entity). `guid:` matches `Identity.id`. `name:` must match exactly one `Identity.name`, else `entity.ambiguous` exit 2 with `candidates`. Unknown guid or name: `entity.not-found` exit 6. A raw handle addresses an entity with no `Identity` and adds warning `entity.no-identity`; `data.entity.guid` is null and `data.entity.handle` is the id. `data.entity.guid` when present is `{"hi","lo"}`. `data.components` is an object keyed by short name. Rotation arrays have 4 numbers, x,y,z,w.

`ecs.query` args: `with` (array), `without` (array), `fields` (array of `Component.member`), `limit` (default 100), `strict` (default true). Unknown component when `strict`: `component.unknown` exit 6. `--no-strict` (`strict:false`): warning `component.unknown`, `ok` true, `rows` empty. `data` is `{count, truncated, rows:[{guid, name, components}]}`. `count` is the match count before the limit. `truncated` is true when `rows.size() < count` or the JSON exceeded the inline cap. Default inline cap is 262144 bytes. Over the cap, `rows` is `[]`, `truncated` is true, and one artifact `kind:"ecs"` holds the rows. Sort matches by `guid.hi`, then `guid.lo`, then `name`. `--fields Transform.position` keeps `position` and drops `rotation` and `scale`.

`ecs.stats` data is `{entityCount, archetypeCount, chunkCount, bytesUsed}` from `Inspector::Capture`. `ecs.archetypes` data is `{archetypes:[{signature, entityCount, chunkCount}]}`. All four commands are Observe, `needsFrame` false, and call `ResolveWorld(CommandWorlds(), call.world)` first.

CLI: `servitor entity get <ref> [--component C]`, `servitor ecs query --with A,B [--without C] [--fields A.x,B] [--limit N] [--no-strict]`, `servitor ecs stats`, `servitor ecs archetypes`.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <catch2/catch_approx.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/EcsCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Worlds.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Scene/Components.hpp>
#include <Astra/Registry/Registry.hpp>
#include "RemoteTestSupport.hpp"
#include <vector>

using Catch::Approx;

// The one-row world table every world-taking command reads (Task 19, ruling F16).
static std::vector<Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo> RemoteTestWorld(void* user) {
    Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo world;
    world.name = "main";
    world.role = "Standalone";
    world.registry = static_cast<Astra::Registry*>(user);
    return {world};
}

TEST_CASE("short names collide as component.ambiguous", "[remote][ecs]") {
    const auto many = Arcane::Remote::PickComponent({"GameA::Health", "GameB::Health"}, "Health");
    CHECK(many.pick == Arcane::Remote::NamePick::Many);
    REQUIRE(many.candidates.size() == 2);
    CHECK(many.candidates[0] == "GameA::Health");
    CHECK(many.candidates[1] == "GameB::Health");
    const auto one = Arcane::Remote::PickComponent({"Arcane::Transform"}, "Transform");
    CHECK(one.pick == Arcane::Remote::NamePick::One);
    CHECK(one.qualified == "Arcane::Transform");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ShortName("Arcane::Transform") == "Transform");
    const auto none = Arcane::Remote::PickComponent({"Arcane::Transform"}, "Nope");
    CHECK(none.pick == Arcane::Remote::NamePick::None);
}

TEST_CASE("ecs.query --fields keeps one member and entity.get reads Transform", "[remote][ecs]") {
    Astra::Registry reg;
    const auto entity = reg.CreateEntity();
    Arcane::Identity id;
    id.id = Arcane::Guid{1, 2};
    id.name = "MeshCube";
    reg.AddComponent<Arcane::Identity>(entity, id);
    Arcane::Transform xf;
    xf.position = glm::vec3(0.4f, -0.3f, 0.0f);
    xf.rotation = glm::quat(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
    xf.scale = glm::vec3(4.0f, 5.0f, 6.0f);
    reg.AddComponent<Arcane::Transform>(entity, xf);
    Arcane::Remote::WorldTable worlds{&RemoteTestWorld, &reg};   // one-row table (ruling F16)
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable> worldGuard(
        [](const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable* t) { Arcane::Remote::SetCommandWorlds(t ? *t : Arcane::Remote::WorldTable{}); },
        &worlds);
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterEcsCommands();

    Arcane::Remote::Call query;
    query.command = "ecs.query";
    query.args = nlohmann::json{
        {"with", nlohmann::json::array({"Transform"})},
        {"fields", nlohmann::json::array({"Transform.position"})},
        {"limit", 100}};
    const auto rows = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(query).envelope;
    CHECK(rows.ok);
    REQUIRE(rows.data["rows"].size() == 1);
    const auto& comp = rows.data["rows"][0]["components"]["Transform"];
    CHECK(comp["position"][0].get<double>() == Approx(0.4).margin(1e-4));
    CHECK(comp["position"][1].get<double>() == Approx(-0.3).margin(1e-4));
    CHECK_FALSE(comp.contains("scale"));
    CHECK_FALSE(comp.contains("rotation"));
    CHECK(rows.data["rows"][0]["name"] == "MeshCube");

    Arcane::Remote::Call get;
    get.command = "entity.get";
    get.args = nlohmann::json{{"entity", "name:MeshCube"}, {"components", nlohmann::json::array({"Transform"})}};
    const auto got = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(get).envelope;
    CHECK(got.ok);
    CHECK(got.data["components"]["Transform"]["rotation"][0] == 0);
    CHECK(got.data["components"]["Transform"]["rotation"][3] == 1);
    CHECK(got.data["entity"]["guid"]["hi"] == 1);
    CHECK(got.data["entity"]["guid"]["lo"] == 2);

    Arcane::Remote::Call loose;
    loose.command = "ecs.query";
    loose.args = nlohmann::json{{"with", nlohmann::json::array({"NoSuchComponent"})}, {"strict", false}};
    const auto warned = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(loose).envelope;
    CHECK(warned.ok);
    CHECK(warned.errors.empty());
    CHECK(warned.warnings[0].code == "component.unknown");
    CHECK(warned.data["rows"].empty());

    Arcane::Remote::Call strict;
    strict.command = "ecs.query";
    strict.args = nlohmann::json{{"with", nlohmann::json::array({"NoSuchComponent"})}};
    const auto failed = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(strict).envelope;
    CHECK_FALSE(failed.ok);
    CHECK(failed.errors[0].code == "component.unknown");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(failed) == 6);

    Arcane::Remote::Call missing;
    missing.command = "entity.get";
    missing.args = nlohmann::json{{"entity", "name:NoSuchEntity"}};
    const auto absent = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(missing).envelope;
    CHECK(absent.errors[0].code == "entity.not-found");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(absent) == 6);

    Arcane::Remote::Call wrongWorld;
    wrongWorld.command = "ecs.stats";
    wrongWorld.world = "nope";                          // --world names no world
    const auto unknownWorld = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(wrongWorld).envelope;
    CHECK(unknownWorld.errors[0].code == "arg.unknown");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(unknownWorld) == 2);
}
```

`Approx` is Catch2 v3's `Catch::Approx` (`<catch2/catch_approx.hpp>`); the `using` line is the existing test convention (`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/AuthoredTransformSyncTest.cpp:25`).

### Task 25: `scene.tree` and `selection.get`

`selection.set` is P1. Do not register it.

R8-2: `scene.tree` is ArcaneRemote. `editor.selection.get` reads the editor program's `SelectionContext`, so it registers from the editor program (`Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Remote/`, the home spec s4.2 gives editor commands). ArcaneRemote never includes an editor header.

Ruling F-1 (the spec wins, s6.4): runtime and server do not carry `editor.selection.get`. Invoking it there answers `command.host-kind`, exit 2, from the Task 4 registry, which answers that code for any unregistered `editor.`-prefixed name on a non-editor host (`CommandRegistry::SetHostKind`, set by `HostSession::Start`). Task 4's `"an editor command on a runtime host is command.host-kind"` pins the rule with a never-registered name (ruling F7). Other unregistered names stay `command.unknown`.

Ruling F8: `scene.tree` IS registered on every host, so the registry rule above does not cover it. Its own function answers `command.host-kind` (exit 2) when `CommandRegistry::Get().GetHostKind() == HostKind::Server`, before it resolves a world.

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/SceneCommands.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/SceneCommands.cpp` (`scene.tree` only)
- Create: `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Remote/SelectionCommand.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Remote/SelectionCommand.cpp` (`editor.selection.get`, inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`; the editor project already compiles `src/**.cpp`)
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.cpp` (wiring, ruling F12: `Start` calls `RegisterSceneCommands()`; the world comes from Task 19's `SetCommandWorlds(config.worlds)`)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Verbs.cpp` (rows `scene tree`, `selection`; ruling F17)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/App/EditorAppFrame.cpp` (the editor's Task 11 arming site: right after `HostSession::Start`, install the view from `EditorApp::m_selection`, `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/App/EditorApp.hpp:1088`, then call `RegisterSelectionCommand`; ruling F33)
- Modify: `premake5.lua` project "ArcaneTests" `files` (source-compile `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Remote/SelectionCommand.cpp` beside `EntityList.cpp`, line 1257)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/SceneCommandTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Registry::HasParent` (`ThirdParty/Astra/include/Astra/Registry/Registry.hpp:1634`), `GetChildren` (`:1597`), `SetParent` (`:1528`). `SelectionContext::Select` (`Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Scene/SelectionContext.hpp:43`), `Toggle` (`:52`), `Entities` (`:30`), `Primary` (`:29`), `Count` (`:28`). There is no `Parent` component. Do not call `BuildOutlinerRows` (`Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/EntityList.hpp:71`, root walk `EntityList.cpp:117-123`) from ArcaneRemote; it is editor-program code.
- Produces: `scene.tree` (ArcaneRemote; editor and runtime; on the server its function returns `command.host-kind` exit 2, ruling F8) and `editor.selection.get` (editor program only). Both Observe, `needsFrame` false. In the editor, a null selection view returns `permission.denied` exit 3, not `command.unknown`. CLI: `servitor scene tree [--depth N]`, `servitor selection`.
- Produces, from `SceneCommands.hpp` (each `ARC_REMOTE_API`; ruling F32):

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterSceneCommands();  // scene.tree; a second call returns
// The resolved world (ruling F16): ResolveWorld(CommandWorlds(), requested).
// There is no SetSceneWorld; tests install a one-row table through SetCommandWorlds.
ARC_REMOTE_API PickedWorld SceneWorld(const std::string& requested = {});
// The {guid, name, handle} object scene.tree writes per node (without children).
ARC_REMOTE_API nlohmann::json EntityRef(Astra::Registry& reg, Astra::Entity e);
}
```

The selection command uses `SceneWorld` and `EntityRef`, so the two commands cannot disagree on the world or on the entity shape.

`scene.tree` data is `{roots:[{guid, name, handle, children:[...]}]}`. Roots are entities where `HasParent` is false. Children come from `GetChildren`. Siblings are sorted by `Identity.id` (`hi`, then `lo`); an entity without `Identity` sorts last and adds warning `entity.no-identity` with `guid` null. `--depth` stops the walk. Depth 0 is roots only.

`editor.selection.get` data is `{entities:[...], primary}`. Order is `Entities()` order, not sorted. `primary` is the primary entity's guid object, or null when the selection is empty.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/SceneCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Registry.hpp>

#include <vector>

// The one-row world table every world-taking command reads (Task 19, ruling F16).
static std::vector<Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo> RemoteTestWorld(void* user) {
    Arcane::Remote::WorldInfo world;
    world.name = "main";
    world.role = "Standalone";
    world.registry = static_cast<Astra::Registry*>(user);
    return {world};
}

static Arcane::Editor::SelectionContext* g_sel = nullptr;
static std::size_t SelCount(void*) { return g_sel ? g_sel->Count() : 0; }
static Astra::Entity SelAt(std::size_t i, void*) { return g_sel->Entities()[i]; }
static Astra::Entity SelPrimary(void*) {
    return g_sel ? g_sel->Primary() : Astra::Entity::Invalid();
}

TEST_CASE("scene.tree roots are parentless and siblings sort by guid", "[remote][scene]") {
    Astra::Registry reg;
    const auto first = reg.CreateEntity();
    const auto second = reg.CreateEntity();
    const auto child = reg.CreateEntity();
    REQUIRE(reg.SetParent(child, second));
    Arcane::Identity a;
    a.id = Arcane::Guid{1, 9};
    a.name = "later";
    Arcane::Identity b;
    b.id = Arcane::Guid{1, 2};
    b.name = "earlier";
    Arcane::Identity c;
    c.id = Arcane::Guid{1, 3};
    c.name = "child";
    reg.AddComponent<Arcane::Identity>(first, a);
    reg.AddComponent<Arcane::Identity>(second, b);
    reg.AddComponent<Arcane::Identity>(child, c);
    Arcane::Remote::WorldTable worlds{&RemoteTestWorld, &reg};   // one-row table (ruling F16)
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable> worldGuard(
        [](const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable* t) { Arcane::Remote::SetCommandWorlds(t ? *t : Arcane::Remote::WorldTable{}); },
        &worlds);
    Arcane::Remote::RegisterSceneCommands();
    RemoteTest::ScopedHostKind editorKind(Arcane::Remote::HostKind::Editor);
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "scene.tree";
    call.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto env = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call).envelope;
    CHECK(env.ok);
    REQUIRE(env.data["roots"].size() == 2);
    CHECK(env.data["roots"][0]["name"] == "earlier");
    CHECK(env.data["roots"][1]["name"] == "later");
    REQUIRE(env.data["roots"][0]["children"].size() == 1);
    CHECK(env.data["roots"][0]["children"][0]["name"] == "child");
}

TEST_CASE("scene.tree on a server is command.host-kind", "[remote][scene]") {

        [](const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable* t) { Arcane::Remote::SetCommandWorlds(t ? *t : Arcane::Remote::WorldTable{}); },
        &worlds);

    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "scene.tree";
    const auto env = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call).envelope;
    CHECK_FALSE(env.ok);
    CHECK(env.errors[0].code == "command.host-kind");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(env) == 2);
}

TEST_CASE("selection.get keeps user order and a null store is permission.denied", "[remote][scene]") {
    Astra::Registry reg;
    const auto e1 = reg.CreateEntity();
    const auto e2 = reg.CreateEntity();
    Arcane::Identity a;
    a.id = Arcane::Guid{2, 1};
    a.name = "first";
    Arcane::Identity b;
    b.id = Arcane::Guid{2, 2};
    b.name = "second";
    reg.AddComponent<Arcane::Identity>(e1, a);
    reg.AddComponent<Arcane::Identity>(e2, b);
    Arcane::Remote::WorldTable worlds{&RemoteTestWorld, &reg};   // one-row table (ruling F16)
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable> worldGuard(
        [](const Arcane::Remote::WorldTable* t) { Arcane::Remote::SetCommandWorlds(t ? *t : Arcane::Remote::WorldTable{}); },
        &worlds);
    Arcane::Editor::SelectionContext sel;
    sel.Select(e1);
    sel.Toggle(e2);
    g_sel = &sel;
    Arcane::Editor::SelectionView view;
    view.count = &SelCount;
    view.at = &SelAt;
    view.primary = &SelPrimary;
    // Cleared on scope exit even if a REQUIRE aborts (ruling S3-9).
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Editor::SelectionView> viewGuard(
        [](const Arcane::Editor::SelectionView* v) {
            Arcane::Editor::SetSelectionView(v ? *v : Arcane::Editor::SelectionView{});
        },
        &view);
    Arcane::Editor::RegisterSelectionCommand();
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "editor.selection.get";
    const auto env = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call).envelope;
    CHECK(env.ok);
    REQUIRE(env.data["entities"].size() == 2);
    CHECK(env.data["entities"][0]["name"] == "first");
    CHECK(env.data["entities"][1]["name"] == "second");
    CHECK(env.data["primary"]["lo"] == 2);
    g_sel = nullptr;
    Arcane::Editor::SetSelectionView({});
    const auto denied = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call).envelope;
    CHECK_FALSE(denied.ok);
    CHECK(denied.errors[0].code == "permission.denied");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(denied) == 3);
}
```

`SelectionContext.hpp` is `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Scene/SelectionContext.hpp`. ArcaneTests already has `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src` on its include path (`premake5.lua` ArcaneTests `includedirs`, line 1754), so both `"Scene/SelectionContext.hpp"` and `"Remote/SelectionCommand.hpp"` resolve. The test includes them. ArcaneRemote includes neither. The editor-side header `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Remote/SelectionCommand.hpp` declares:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Editor {
struct SelectionView {
    std::size_t (*count)(void*) = nullptr;
    Astra::Entity (*at)(std::size_t index, void*) = nullptr;
    Astra::Entity (*primary)(void*) = nullptr;
    void* user = nullptr;
};
void SetSelectionView(SelectionView view);
void RegisterSelectionCommand(); // editor.selection.get; module "" (host), safe to call twice
}
```

A null `count` is `permission.denied` exit 3. The editor installs the view from `m_selection` (`EditorApp.hpp:1088`) and calls `RegisterSelectionCommand` at its Task 11 arming site in `EditorAppFrame.cpp`, right after `HostSession::Start`. `HostSession` is ArcaneRemote and cannot see `SelectionContext`, so the install is not inside `Start`. The selection command reads the world through `Arcane::Remote::SceneWorld(call.world)`, which is `ResolveWorld(CommandWorlds(), call.world)`, the same table the tree uses (ruling F16). The test's one-row table points `registry` at `reg`, or the guid lookup has no registry. Each entry of `entities`, and `primary`'s guid, comes from `Arcane::Remote::EntityRef`.

Sort siblings with `Guid::operator<` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Guid.hpp:47`). The editor installs the view right after `HostSession::Start`, in `EditorAppFrame.cpp`. Runtime and server do not register `editor.selection.get`; there it answers `command.host-kind` exit 2 (ruling F-1, Task 4). `scene.tree` is absent from the server capability list (Task 11), and its function returns `command.host-kind` when `CommandRegistry::Get().GetHostKind() == HostKind::Server` (ruling F8). Add `RegisterSceneCommands();` to `HostSession::Start` and the two rows to `Verbs.cpp`.

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
struct FrameStats {
    std::uint64_t frame = 0;
    double dtSeconds = 0;
    double tickMs = 0;
    double renderMs = 0;
};
ARC_REMOTE_API void NoteAdvance(FrameStats& stats, std::uint64_t frame, double dtSeconds, double tickMs, double renderMs);
// Registers stats.frame on the first call and stores the pointer. Safe to call
// twice; a later call replaces the stored pointer (ruling F11). Null: stats.frame
// answers host.unavailable (exit 5) and dereferences nothing (ruling S3-9).
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterStatsCommand(FrameStats* stats);
}
```

`NoteAdvance` is called only from the branch that actually advances sim. Idle, minimized, and render-only iterations do not call it. The server passes `renderMs = 0`. Each host calls `NoteAdvance(m_remote->Stats(), ...)`, the session's one `FrameStats` (ruling F12). Command `stats.frame` data is those four fields.
