- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
struct CaptureMapped {
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0, rowPitch = 0;
    bool bgra = false;
    const std::uint8_t* mapped = nullptr; // null and drain returns true: the map failed
    std::uint32_t mappedBytes = 0;
};
using ArmCopyFn = bool (*)(std::uint64_t ticket, void* user);
using DrainSlotFn = bool (*)(std::uint64_t ticket, CaptureMapped* raw, void* user);
struct CaptureHooks {
    std::uint32_t framesInFlight = 2; // host ring only; the job does not wait on host.frame
    ArmCopyFn armViewport = nullptr, armWindow = nullptr, armHost = nullptr;
    DrainSlotFn drainViewport = nullptr, drainWindow = nullptr, drainHost = nullptr;
    bool occludedRisk = false;
    void* user = nullptr;
    std::filesystem::path outDir;
};
// Both are safe to call twice; a later call replaces the stored hooks / pointer
// (ruling F11). A null JobTable* makes capture.* answer host.unavailable (exit 5).
ARC_REMOTE_API void SetCaptureHooks(CaptureHooks hooks);
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterCaptureCommands(JobTable* jobs);
ARC_REMOTE_API CommandResult CaptureBusy(const Call& call);
ARC_REMOTE_API bool CaptureInFlight(const JobTable& jobs);
}

namespace Arcane {
inline void NormalizeCaptureRows(const std::uint8_t* mapped,
    std::uint32_t width, std::uint32_t height,
    std::uint32_t rowPitch, bool bgra,
    std::vector<unsigned char>& rgba)
{
    rgba.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(width) * height * 4);
    for (std::uint32_t y = 0; y < height; ++y)
    {
        const std::uint8_t* src = mapped + static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * rowPitch;
        unsigned char* dst = rgba.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * width * 4;
        std::memcpy(dst, src, static_cast<std::size_t>(width) * 4);
        if (bgra)
        {
            for (std::uint32_t x = 0; x < width; ++x)
            {
                unsigned char* p = dst + static_cast<std::size_t>(x) * 4;
                const unsigned char b = p[0];
                p[0] = p[2];
                p[2] = b;
            }
        }
    }
}
}
```

`capture.viewport` and `capture.window` are Observe, `ExecThread::Job`, `needsFrame` true. The command validates args. The matching source is one pair: viewport is `armViewport` + `drainViewport`, window is `armWindow` + `drainWindow`. `armHost` + `drainHost` are the pixels `--compare` captures (Task 28): the editor installs its viewport pair there (ruling F20), and the runtime its one target. Server leaves all six pointers null. When either pointer of the pair is null the command returns immediately: `ok` false, code `capture.no-renderer`, `ExitFromHost` 2, `completesJob` 0, and it does not `Arm`. When both are set, it `Arm`s a job with `total` 1 and returns `completesJob` set. The command function does not call either pointer. Ruling P4 (a). One capture or compare is in flight per host, not per source. `capture.viewport`, `capture.window`, and `capture.compare` share that slot, because the ring has one `wanted`. `armViewport`, `armWindow`, and `armHost` are the same slot. After the null-hook `capture.no-renderer` return and before `JobTable::Arm`, `CaptureInFlight(*jobs)` true means the command returns `CaptureBusy(call)`: `ok` false, code `host.busy`, `errors[0].detail["state"]` = `"capture-pending"`, `ExitFromHost` 5, `completesJob` 0. It does not call `Arm` and it does not call `arm`, so `ring.wanted` is not overwritten by a second ticket and no second drain waits on a lost ticket. Minimized `host.busy` with `state` `"minimized"` (exit 5) still happens first, in the dispatcher `needsFrame` check (Task 13), and does not reach this command. `CaptureInFlight` is `jobs.AnyRunning({"capture.viewport", "capture.window", "capture.compare"})`. `CaptureBusy` is the helper below. A host passes one `JobTable*` into both `RegisterCaptureCommands` and `RegisterCompareCommand`.

The job step is the PickNode ticket, split across render submissions. Ruling P3 (a). `PickNode::RecordReadback` (`Source/Client/ArcaneRender/src/Arcane/Render/Nri/nodes/PickOutlineNodes.cpp:617-693`) drains the slot with `memcpy` at `:639-641`, publishes `m_ticket[frameSlot]` at `:645`, then records `CmdReadbackTextureToBuffer` and stores the ticket at `:692`. The comment at `:628-635` is the rule: no fence query, no idle. The job does not compute a slot and does not read `gate.frame`. The host render loop owns the slot (`submission % framesInFlight`). The command `new`s a `CaptureJobState { arm, drain, hookUser, ticket, outDir, occludedRisk }` and passes it as `job.user`. `hookUser` is `CaptureHooks::user` (the test's `CapUser`), not the state itself. `progress.label` is the phase. `progress.done` stays the job-progress counter; it is not the ticket. The step `delete`s the state when it returns true. It does not write `job.startedFrame`.

- Label is not `"copied"`. `ticket = NextCaptureTicket()`, a monotonic `std::atomic<std::uint64_t>` starting at 1. `0` is never issued, so "not armed" stays distinct from a stored PickNode ticket. Call `arm(ticket, hookUser)`. Store `state.ticket`. Set `progress.label = "copied"`. Return false. The job stays `Running`. A false arm finishes `Failed` on this step: `capture.no-frame`, exit 6, the drain pointer is not called, and the state is deleted.
- Label is `"copied"`. Call `drain(state.ticket, &raw, hookUser)`. Do not read `gate.frame`. Do not call `arm` again. A false return means that ticket has not been retired yet: stay `Running`, leave the state allocated, and do not finish `capture.no-frame`.
- A true return with `raw.mapped == nullptr` finishes `Failed`, `capture.no-frame`, exit 6, and deletes the state.
- A true return with `raw.mapped != nullptr` calls `NormalizeCaptureRows(raw.mapped, raw.width, raw.height, raw.rowPitch, raw.bgra, rgba)`, then `WritePngRgba` and `Sha256Hex` of the PNG bytes. The artifact is `{kind:"capture", path, bytes, sha256, width, height, format:"RGBA8_UNORM"}`. The PNG is tight RGBA, so the format string is `RGBA8_UNORM`. `occludedRisk` adds warning `capture.occluded-risk` and still `ok` true. Delete the state and return true.

`CaptureGraphViewportPng` (`EditorApp.cpp:2056`) calls `ReadCapture` (`NriGraphContext.cpp:2081`, call at `EditorApp.cpp:2086`), and `ReadCapture` calls `DeviceWaitIdle` at `:2101`. That path stays the cover and the runtime `--screenshot` path. The remote hooks do not call `ReadCapture`, do not call `DeviceWaitIdle`, and do not query a fence. `ReadCapture`'s swizzle flag and loop at `:2115-2132` are replaced by one call. `m_captureRowPitch` is `std::uint64_t` (`NriGraphContext.hpp:1518`), so the narrowing is explicit:

```cpp
NormalizeCaptureRows(mapped, m_captureWidth, m_captureHeight,
                     static_cast<std::uint32_t>(m_captureRowPitch),
                     IsBgraFormat(m_format), rgba);
core.UnmapBuffer(*m_capture);
```

`IsBgraFormat` stays at `:79-82`. Do not leave a second copy of the loop.

Ruling F-2: with the ring in ArcaneRender (R8-2), each `NriGraphContext` a hook targets owns one ring: the editor's viewport context and chrome context each hold one, and the runtime's single context holds one. Each ring's slot follows its own context's `m_frameIndex` pacing, which is what keeps the pacing-wait argument (`PickOutlineNodes.cpp:628-635`) sound. ONE capture in flight per host is still enforced before anything is armed: `CaptureInFlight` in ArcaneRemote gates all three commands before any `arm`.

Each `NriGraphContext` a hook targets owns one `HOST_READBACK` of `kMaxFramesInFlight` slices and one ring. The `capture` buffer `EnsureCaptureBuffer` makes today (`NriGraphContext.cpp:1182`) is one slice and stays the `ReadCapture` path's. The ring, a member of `NriGraphContext` in namespace `Arcane`:

```cpp
struct CaptureRing {
    std::uint64_t ticket[3]{};
    bool pending[3]{};
    std::uint64_t wanted = 0;
    std::uint64_t retiredTicket = 0;
    bool haveRetired = false;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> retiredBytes;
    std::uint32_t retiredW = 0, retiredH = 0, retiredPitch = 0;
    bool retiredBgra = false;
    std::uint32_t submission = 0;
    std::uint32_t framesInFlight = 2;
};
```

`arm` (the host hook, forwarding to `ArmTicketCapture`) stores `ring.wanted = ticket` and returns true. It does not record a copy itself. A second capture or compare does not reach this assignment: the command returns `host.busy` `capture-pending` before `JobTable::Arm`, so `arm` is not called and `wanted` is never overwritten by a second ticket. There is no second drain waiting on a lost ticket. The graph context's own frame recording calls `NoteRenderSubmission`, once per recorded frame, and nothing else does. Slot is `ring.submission % ring.framesInFlight`, which matches the context's own slot, `m_frameIndex % FramesInFlight()` (`NriGraphContext.cpp:1806` windowed, `:1945` offscreen). If `pending[slot]`, `memcpy` that slice the way `PickOutlineNodes.cpp:639-641` copies its dword, set `retiredTicket = ticket[slot]`, `haveRetired = true`, and clear `pending[slot]`. If `wanted != 0`, record `CmdReadbackTextureToBuffer` with `layout.offset = slot * slicePitch`, store `ticket[slot] = wanted`, set `pending[slot]`, and clear `wanted`. Today's single copy is the `capture` copy node's execute lambda: `layout` at `NriGraphContext.cpp:1574`, `CmdReadbackTextureToBuffer` at `:1583`. Then increment `submission`. `drain` (forwarding to `DrainTicketCapture`) returns false until `haveRetired && retiredTicket == ticket`. It then fills `CaptureMapped` from the retired bytes and returns true. The pacing wait inside backbuffer acquire is what makes the slot safe. In the editor, the recording frames are the viewport submit, `EditorApp::RenderSceneToViewport` (`EditorAppFrame.cpp:1651`, `RenderFrameOffscreen` at `:1767`), and the chrome submit, `EditorApp::PresentChromeFrame` (`:4026`, `RenderFrame` at `:4108-4109`). Both run on every non-minimized frame, including while the editor is not playing. The runtime's is `RuntimeFrame::RenderGraph` (`RuntimeFrame.cpp:454`, submit at `:753-754`), called at `RuntimeApp.cpp:845`. That call still runs when the sim is idle: `AdvanceSim` is `:830`, and the `skipFrame` continues at `:825` and `:839` are the minimized path, which does not render. `NoteRenderSubmission` is not reachable from `Advance`, `AdvanceSim`, `Consume`, or the play tick. `host.frame` still counts sim advances only. A headless runtime has one target, so its viewport, window, and host pairs are the same two functions. The server installs null hooks. Idle editor and idle headless runtime therefore retire tickets. Task 30 is the live proof: the editor bless, before any play or step, keeps `host.frame` equal to the settled frame, and a runtime `screenshot window` with no `--frames` exits 0 while `stats.frame` stays put.

CLI: `servitor screenshot viewport [--out FILE]`, `servitor screenshot window [--out FILE]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/CaptureCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Listener.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Assets/ImageIo.hpp>
#include "RemoteDemoArgs.hpp"
#include "RemoteTestSupport.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <vector>

// Installs one case's hooks and JobTable* and clears both on scope exit, so a
// later case never sees this case's stack objects (rulings F11, S3-9).
struct RemoteCaptureScope {
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks> hooks;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::JobTable> jobs;
    RemoteCaptureScope(const Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks& h, Arcane::Remote::JobTable& j)
        : hooks([](const Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks* p) {
                    Arcane::Remote::SetCaptureHooks(p ? *p : Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks{});
                }, &h),
          jobs(&Arcane::Remote::RegisterCaptureCommands, &j) {}
};

static const std::uint8_t kProbe[12] = {10, 20, 30, 255, 1, 2, 3, 40, 9, 9, 9, 9};
static const std::uint8_t kProbeB[12] = {40, 50, 60, 255, 7, 8, 9, 40, 9, 9, 9, 9};

struct CapUser {
    int copies = 0;
    int reads = 0;
    int idles = 0;
    std::uint64_t armedTicket = 0;
    std::uint64_t wanted = 0;
    bool retired = false;
    bool failMap = false;
};

static bool ArmSlot(std::uint64_t ticket, void* user) {
    auto* u = static_cast<CapUser*>(user);
    ++u->copies;
    u->armedTicket = ticket;
    u->wanted = ticket;
    return true;
}
static bool DrainSlot(std::uint64_t ticket, Arcane::Remote::CaptureMapped* raw, void* user) {
    auto* u = static_cast<CapUser*>(user);
    if (!u->retired || ticket != u->armedTicket)
        return false;
    raw->width = 2;
    raw->height = 1;
    raw->rowPitch = 12;
    raw->bgra = true;
    if (u->failMap) {
        raw->mapped = nullptr;
        raw->mappedBytes = 0;
        return true;
    }
    ++u->reads;
    raw->mapped = kProbe;
    raw->mappedBytes = sizeof(kProbe);
    return true;
}
static Arcane::Remote::CommandResult Ping(const Arcane::Remote::Call& call, void*) {
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult r;
    r.envelope = Arcane::Remote::MakeOk(call.command, nlohmann::json::object(), {});
    return r;
}
static bool ArmNo(std::uint64_t, void* user) {
    ++static_cast<CapUser*>(user)->copies;
    return false;
}

TEST_CASE("NormalizeCaptureRows drops padding and swaps BGRA", "[remote][capture]") {
    std::vector<unsigned char> rgba;
    Arcane::NormalizeCaptureRows(kProbe, 2, 1, 12, true, rgba);
    REQUIRE(rgba.size() == 8);
    CHECK(rgba[0] == 30);
    CHECK(rgba[1] == 20);
    CHECK(rgba[2] == 10);
    CHECK(rgba[3] == 255);
    CHECK(rgba[4] == 3);
    CHECK(rgba[5] == 2);
    CHECK(rgba[6] == 1);
    CHECK(rgba[7] == 40);
}

TEST_CASE("the copy step reads nothing and the job completes when the ticket is published", "[remote][capture]") {
    CapUser user;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks hooks;
    hooks.framesInFlight = 2;
    hooks.armWindow = &ArmSlot;
    hooks.drainWindow = &DrainSlot;
    hooks.user = &user;
    hooks.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-cap";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(hooks.outDir);
    RemoteCaptureScope scope(hooks, jobs);
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    std::string err;
    Arcane::Remote::Desc ping;
    ping.name = "cap.ping";
    ping.summary = "observe while a capture is in flight";
    ping.access = Arcane::Remote::Access::Observe;
    ping.module = "cap-test";
    ping.argsMeta = Astra::GetMeta<RemoteDemoArgs>();
    ping.fn = &Ping;
    ping.needsFrame = false;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(ping, &err));
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "capture.window";
    call.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = reg.Invoke(call);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    CHECK(user.copies == 0);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.idles == 0);
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 10;
    gate.minimized = false;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto id = Arcane::Remote::JobId{armed.completesJob};
    REQUIRE(jobs.Find(id) != nullptr);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->progress.label == "copied");
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->startedFrame == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.armedTicket != 0);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.idles == 0);
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    const auto audit = hooks.outDir / "audit.ndjson";
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit);
    Arcane::Remote::Call pingCall;
    pingCall.command = "cap.ping";
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(2, pingCall));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult pinged;
    REQUIRE(queue.Wait(2, pinged, std::chrono::milliseconds(1000)));
    CHECK(pinged.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.idles == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.idles == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    user.retired = true;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    REQUIRE(jobs.Find(id) != nullptr);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(user.reads == 1);
    CHECK(user.idles == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    const auto& art = jobs.Find(id)->envelope.artifacts[0];
    CHECK(art.format == "RGBA8_UNORM");
    CHECK(art.width == 2);
    CHECK(art.height == 1);
    CHECK(art.sha256.size() == 64);
    std::uint32_t w = 0, h = 0;
    std::vector<unsigned char> png;
    REQUIRE(Arcane::LoadPngRgba(art.path, w, h, png));
    CHECK(w == 2);
    CHECK(h == 1);
    REQUIRE(png.size() == 8);
    CHECK(png[0] == 30);
    CHECK(png[1] == 20);
    CHECK(png[2] == 10);
    CHECK(png[3] == 255);
    CHECK(png[4] == 3);
    CHECK(png[5] == 2);
    CHECK(png[6] == 1);
    CHECK(png[7] == 40);
    reg.UnregisterModule("cap-test");
}

struct OverlapUser {
    int copies = 0;
    int reads = 0;
    int frames = 0;
    int framesInFlight = 2;
    std::uint64_t wanted = 0;
    std::uint64_t ticket[2]{};
    int retireFrame[2]{-1, -1};
};

static bool ArmOverlap(std::uint64_t ticket, void* user) {
    auto* u = static_cast<OverlapUser*>(user);
    REQUIRE(u->copies < 2);
    u->ticket[u->copies] = ticket;
    u->wanted = ticket;
    u->retireFrame[u->copies] = u->frames + u->framesInFlight;
    ++u->copies;
    return true;
}

static bool DrainOverlap(std::uint64_t ticket, Arcane::Remote::CaptureMapped* raw, void* user) {
    auto* u = static_cast<OverlapUser*>(user);
    int slot = -1;
    if (u->copies > 0 && ticket == u->ticket[0])
        slot = 0;
    else if (u->copies > 1 && ticket == u->ticket[1])
        slot = 1;
    if (slot < 0 || u->frames < u->retireFrame[slot])
        return false;
    raw->width = 2;
    raw->height = 1;
    raw->rowPitch = 12;
    raw->bgra = true;
    raw->mapped = slot == 0 ? kProbe : kProbeB;
    raw->mappedBytes = 12;
    ++u->reads;
    return true;
}

static void ExpectPng(const Arcane::Remote::Artifact& art, unsigned char r, unsigned char g,
                       unsigned char b) {
    CHECK(art.format == "RGBA8_UNORM");
    CHECK(art.width == 2);
    CHECK(art.height == 1);
    CHECK(art.sha256.size() == 64);
    std::uint32_t w = 0, h = 0;
    std::vector<unsigned char> png;
    REQUIRE(Arcane::LoadPngRgba(art.path, w, h, png));
    CHECK(w == 2);
    CHECK(h == 1);
    REQUIRE(png.size() == 8);
    CHECK(png[0] == r);
    CHECK(png[1] == g);
    CHECK(png[2] == b);
    CHECK(png[3] == 255);
}

TEST_CASE("overlapping captures: B is capture-pending until A's ticket retires, then the retry succeeds", "[remote][capture]") {
    OverlapUser user;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks hooks;
    hooks.framesInFlight = 2;
    hooks.armViewport = &ArmOverlap;
    hooks.drainViewport = &DrainOverlap;
    hooks.armWindow = &ArmOverlap;
    hooks.drainWindow = &DrainOverlap;
    hooks.user = &user;
    hooks.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-cap-busy";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(hooks.outDir);
    RemoteCaptureScope scope(hooks, jobs);
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 10;

    Arcane::Remote::Call captureA;
    captureA.command = "capture.window";
    captureA.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = reg.Invoke(captureA);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto idA = Arcane::Remote::JobId{armed.completesJob};
    REQUIRE(jobs.Find(idA) != nullptr);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idA)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.frames == 0);
    CHECK(user.wanted != 0);
    const auto ticketA = user.wanted;
    CHECK(user.retireFrame[0] == 2);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);

    Arcane::Remote::Call captureB;
    captureB.command = "capture.viewport";
    captureB.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto busy = reg.Invoke(captureB);
    CHECK(busy.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(busy.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(busy.envelope.errors[0].code == "host.busy");
    CHECK(busy.envelope.errors[0].detail["state"] == "capture-pending");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(busy.envelope) == 5);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.wanted == ticketA);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idA)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);

    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        ++user.frames;
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
    }
    REQUIRE(jobs.Find(idA) != nullptr);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idA)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idA)->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(user.reads == 1);
    CHECK(user.frames == 2);
    CHECK(user.wanted == ticketA);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    ExpectPng(jobs.Find(idA)->envelope.artifacts[0], 30, 20, 10);

    const auto retried = reg.Invoke(captureB);
    REQUIRE(retried.completesJob != 0);
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto idB = Arcane::Remote::JobId{retried.completesJob};
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idB)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(user.copies == 2);
    CHECK(user.wanted != ticketA);
    CHECK(user.retireFrame[1] == user.frames + 2);
    CHECK(user.reads == 1);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        ++user.frames;
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
    }
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idB)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Done);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idB)->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(user.reads == 2);
    CHECK(user.frames == 4);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    ExpectPng(jobs.Find(idB)->envelope.artifacts[0], 60, 50, 40);
    ExpectPng(jobs.Find(idA)->envelope.artifacts[0], 30, 20, 10);
}

TEST_CASE("a server with null hooks returns capture.no-renderer and arms nothing", "[remote][capture]") {
    CapUser user;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    const Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks none{};   // a server: all six pointers null
    RemoteCaptureScope scope(none, jobs);
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "capture.window";
    const auto result = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(result.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(result.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(result.envelope.errors[0].code == "capture.no-renderer");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(result.envelope) == 2);
    CHECK(user.copies == 0);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(jobs.FinishedCount() == 0);
}

TEST_CASE("a false arm finishes capture.no-frame without a read", "[remote][capture]") {
    CapUser user;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks hooks;
    hooks.framesInFlight = 2;
    hooks.armWindow = &ArmNo;
    hooks.drainWindow = &DrainSlot;
    hooks.user = &user;
    hooks.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-cap-arm";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(hooks.outDir);
    RemoteCaptureScope scope(hooks, jobs);
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "capture.window";
    call.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 4;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto* job = jobs.Find(Arcane::Remote::JobId{armed.completesJob});
    REQUIRE(job != nullptr);
    CHECK(job->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Failed);
    CHECK_FALSE(job->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(job->envelope.errors[0].code == "capture.no-frame");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(job->envelope) == 6);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 4);
}

TEST_CASE("a published ticket with a null map finishes capture.no-frame", "[remote][capture]") {
    CapUser user;
    user.failMap = true;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks hooks;
    hooks.framesInFlight = 2;
    hooks.armWindow = &ArmSlot;
    hooks.drainWindow = &DrainSlot;
    hooks.user = &user;
    hooks.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-cap-drain";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(hooks.outDir);
    RemoteCaptureScope scope(hooks, jobs);
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "capture.window";
    call.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 4;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto id = Arcane::Remote::JobId{armed.completesJob};
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 4);
    user.retired = true;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto* job = jobs.Find(id);
    REQUIRE(job != nullptr);
    CHECK(job->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Failed);
    CHECK_FALSE(job->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(job->envelope.errors[0].code == "capture.no-frame");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(job->envelope) == 6);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.idles == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 4);
}
```

`RegisterCaptureCommands` is safe to call twice. The second call keeps the latest hooks and the latest `JobTable*`. Every case installs through `RemoteCaptureScope`, which resets the hooks to `{}` and the `JobTable*` to null when the case ends (ruling S3-9). `DrainSlot` increments `reads` only when it copies bytes. A false return is "this ticket is not retired", not a failure. `failMap` returns true with a null `mapped` and does not increment `reads`. The `idles` field has no writer. It stays 0 because the step has no idle call. `gate.frame` is set to 10, or to 4, and every later check requires the same value. A step that waits for `gate.frame` to advance never finishes this test.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate and build Debug per Global Constraints, then run filter `the copy step reads nothing*`. Expected: FAIL to compile.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the ticket job and the shared normalizer**

Paste `NormalizeCaptureRows` into `ImageIo.hpp` and replace the `ReadCapture` loop with the call in **Interfaces**. The command does not call the hooks. The first step only calls `arm(ticket)`. Later steps only call `drain` for that same ticket. `drain` returning false leaves the job `Running`. `drain` returning true with a null map finishes `Failed` with `capture.no-frame` and exit 6. `drain` returning true with bytes calls `NormalizeCaptureRows`, then `WritePngRgba` and `Sha256Hex`. Do not call `NriGraphContext::ReadCapture` (`NriGraphContext.cpp:2081`) or `DeviceWaitIdle` (`:2101`) from this job, from the editor hook, from the runtime hook, or from `ArmTicketCapture` / `DrainTicketCapture`. Do not query a fence. `CaptureGraphViewportPng` (`EditorApp.cpp:2056`) and the runtime `--screenshot` writes (`RuntimeFrame.cpp:937-940` and `:1078-1081`) stay synchronous and are not on this path. Do not read `gate.frame` and do not write `startedFrame` in this step. The ring is `CaptureRing` above, an `NriGraphContext` member in ArcaneRender (R8-2). `NoteRenderSubmission` runs inside that context's `RenderFrame` / `RenderFrameOffscreen` recording: the editor's viewport and chrome submits, and `RuntimeFrame::RenderGraph` (called at `RuntimeApp.cpp:845`). It does not run from `AdvanceSim` (`:830`). Before `Arm`, `CaptureInFlight` of `capture.viewport`, `capture.window`, and `capture.compare` returns `CaptureBusy` and does not call `arm`. `CaptureBusy` sets `detail.state` to `capture-pending` and leaves `completesJob` 0. Wire `HostSession` (`Config::capture`, `SetCaptureHooks`, `RegisterCaptureCommands(&m_jobs)` in `Start`; cleared in `Stop`), the editor's six pointers (host pair = viewport pair, ruling F20) and the runtime's in their arming sites, and the two `screenshot` rows in `Verbs.cpp`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Filter `[remote][capture]`. Expected: PASS. On the arm step, copies is 1, reads is 0, idles is 0, `startedFrame` is 0, `gate.frame` is still 10, and `cap.ping` has already returned. The next step, with the ticket not retired, stays `Running` and reads stays 0. Reads becomes 1 only after the test sets `retired`. The PNG is width 2, height 1, and the first pixel is RGBA `(30, 20, 10, 255)`. Exit on the null hooks is 2. Exit on a false arm is 6 with reads 0. Exit on a published null map is 6 with reads 0. `gate.frame` is unchanged in every case. The overlap case arms capture A, refuses capture B with exit 5 and `capture-pending` while A's ticket is not retired (`copies` stays 1, so B was not run), advances two render frames until A retires, then retries B. Both PNGs are valid: A's first pixel is RGBA `(30, 20, 10, 255)` and B's is `(60, 50, 40, 255)`. `gate.frame` stays 10.

### Task 28: `compare`, pixel ratio, and the `--bless` round trip

ABI: next ABI after main at execution time; main is 59 today (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Core/AbiVersion.hpp:10`), so expected 60 after Task 0 and 61 after Task 10; re-read main; Tasks 0 and 10 restamp `ReferenceProject.arcproj` and the arcbuild fixture. This task does not bump.

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/CompareCommands.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/CompareCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.hpp` (append `CompareHooks compare` to `HostSession::Config`; the server leaves it default)
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.cpp` (wiring, ruling F12: `Start` calls `SetCompareHooks(config.compare)` and `RegisterCompareCommand(&m_jobs)` with the same `m_jobs` Task 27 passes to `RegisterCaptureCommands`; `Stop` calls `SetCompareHooks({})` and `RegisterCompareCommand(nullptr)`)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/App/EditorAppFrame.cpp` (the editor's `ResolveFn` / `BlessFn`, defined beside the anonymous-namespace `CompareBackendName` helper at `:116` they call, and installed on `Config::compare` at the Task 11 arming site with `projectRoot` and `user = this`; ruling F33: this file is committed by this task)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeApp.cpp` (the runtime's `ResolveFn` / `BlessFn` on `Config::compare`, under the D-1 guard, built the way its own `--compare` path builds the adapter set and resolution, `RuntimeApp.cpp:658-663`, and blesses, `:1191`)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Verbs.cpp` (row `compare`; ruling F17)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/CompareCommandTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `Desc::accessFor` (Task 4) and the Task 8 gate and audit that read it (ruling F19).
  - `ImageCompareOptions` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneAssets/src/Arcane/Assets/ImageCompare.hpp:209`: `maxDiffPixels`, `maxDiffPixelRatio`; both unset means the budget is zero; if both are set the smaller budget wins).
  - `CompareImages` (`:273`). It takes `(const PixelData& expected, const PixelData& actual, const ImageCompareOptions&)`. `PixelData` is `Source/Runtime/ArcaneAssets/src/Arcane/Assets/ImageIo.hpp:22`.
  - `ImageCompareResult` (`:228`). Its fields are `passed`, `diffCount`, `diffRatio` and `maxLocalDifference`; the envelope's `diffPixels` is `diffCount`.
  - `LoadPngRgba` / `WritePngRgba` (`ImageIo.hpp:38` / `:44`). These are the functions that `Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeApp.cpp:691` and `RuntimeFrame.cpp:937` already call.
  - `CaptureHooks` and `NormalizeCaptureRows` (Task 27; `NormalizeCaptureRows` sits beside `WritePngRgba` in `ImageIo.hpp`).
  - `BlessReference` (`Source/Developer/ArcaneVerify/src/Arcane/Host/ReferenceImages.hpp:122`) and `ResolveReference` (`:95`, plus the adapter-set overload at `:109`) stay in ArcaneVerify. ArcaneVerify is a Developer module, and ArcaneRemote never depends on it (R8-2). ArcaneRemote calls function pointers.
  - The verdict names (`Passed`, `PassedOnFallback`, `Skipped`, `Failed`) are also an ArcaneVerify enum (`Source/Developer/ArcaneVerify/src/Arcane/Host/Verdict.hpp`, `PassedOnFallback` at `:37`). For the same R8-2 reason, ArcaneRemote writes those strings itself and does not include that header.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
struct ResolvedReference {
    bool found = false;                   // false: no reference file at any level
    std::filesystem::path path;           // the PNG the verdict compares against
    std::string level;                    // the resolved verdict level (ReferenceResolution's level name)
    std::vector<std::string> triedPaths;  // every path the resolver looked at, in order; the envelope's triedPaths
};
using ResolveFn = ResolvedReference (*)(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot, const std::string& slot,
                                        void* user);
using BlessFn = bool (*)(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot, const std::string& slot,
                         std::uint32_t width, std::uint32_t height, const unsigned char* rgba, void* user);
struct CompareHooks {
    ResolveFn resolve = nullptr;
    BlessFn bless = nullptr;
    std::filesystem::path projectRoot;
    void* user = nullptr;
};
// Both are safe to call twice; a later call replaces the stored hooks / pointer
// (ruling F11). A null JobTable*, or a null resolve or bless, makes
// capture.compare answer host.unavailable (exit 5) after the capture.no-renderer check.
ARC_REMOTE_API void SetCompareHooks(CompareHooks hooks);
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterCompareCommand(JobTable* jobs);
}
```

Command `capture.compare`: static `access` Observe, `mutates` `Project`, and `accessFor = &CompareAccess`, which returns `Access::Drive` when `call.args.value("bless", false)` and `Access::Observe` otherwise (ruling F19). Task 8's gate and audit use that per-call access, so a bless under `ARC_REMOTE_READ_ONLY` or `options.readOnly` is `permission.read-only` (exit 3) and never reaches the command, and an allowed bless is audited as Drive; Task 18's `--yes` rule treats it as Drive on the client. `ExecThread::Job`, `needsFrame` true. Args: `reference` (required), `source` (`host` default, or `viewport`, or `window`), `maxDiffPixels`, `maxDiffPixelRatio`, `bless`. The source selects one Task 27 pair: `host` is `armHost`/`drainHost`, `viewport` is `armViewport`/`drainViewport`, `window` is `armWindow`/`drainWindow`. The job uses that same ticket step. The copy step calls `arm` only. `CompareImages` and `BlessFn` run only on the drain that returns the retired ticket's bytes. The job does not read `gate.frame`. Data is `{verdict, reference, resolvedLevel, triedPaths, diffPixels, diffRatio, maxLocalDifference, thresholds:{maxDiffPixels, maxDiffPixelRatio}}`. `resolvedLevel` and `triedPaths` are copied from the `ResolvedReference` the hook returned (G gap 2): the editor hook fills `triedPaths` from `ReferenceResolution::triedPaths` (`ReferenceImages.hpp:87`), so ArcaneRemote never searches the reference tree itself. Verdict `Passed` / `PassedOnFallback` / `Skipped` is `ok` true, empty errors, exit 0. Verdict `Failed` is `ok` true, empty errors, exit 8.

When `args.bless` is false, the command calls `resolve` before `Arm`. A resolve whose `found` is false returns immediately: `ok` false, `compare.missing-reference`, exit 6, `completesJob` 0, and it does not call `arm`, `drain`, or `CompareImages`. When `args.bless` is true, a not-found resolve does not return that error. The job still arms, the completing drain still reads the pixels, `BlessFn` writes them, and the envelope is `ok` true, verdict `Passed`, exit 0. `CompareImages` is not called on that creating bless. A null arm or drain pointer for the source is `capture.no-renderer`, exit 2, `completesJob` 0, no job left `Running`. After that null-hook return and before resolve and before `Arm`, compare calls `CaptureInFlight`. True returns `CaptureBusy`: `host.busy`, `detail.state` `capture-pending`, exit 5, `completesJob` 0, and `arm` is not called. A screenshot that is `Running` and a compare that is `Running` are the same slot. `wanted` stays the first ticket. `RegisterCompareCommand`, like `RegisterCaptureCommands`, stores that `JobTable*` and a later call replaces it. A busy return does not call `resolve`.

A bless of a reference that did resolve calls `BlessFn` with the captured RGBA after `CompareImages`, on the completing drain. The editor's function calls `ResolveReference` and then `BlessReference` (`ReferenceImages.hpp:122`). It uses the adapter-set overload (`ReferenceImages.hpp:109`), the one the editor's `--compare` path calls at `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/App/EditorAppFrame.cpp:295-296`.

Ruling G-2: the editor's bless/compare hook calls the adapter-set `ResolveReference` overload (`ReferenceImages.hpp:109`) with a `ReferenceAdapterSet` it computes itself, exactly as `--compare` does. A software adapter (WARP, lavapipe) therefore never blesses into the shared or backend level; that is the header's contract (`ReferenceImages.hpp:99-108`).

A missing safe name on a hardware adapter blesses the shared file: `ResolveReferenceIn` sets `blessTarget` to `projectRoot/Verify/References/<name>.png` when nothing exists and the adapter set is empty (`Source/Developer/ArcaneVerify/src/Arcane/Host/ReferenceImages.cpp:105-110`). On a software adapter (WARP, lavapipe) the target is `Verify/References/<adapter set>/<name>.png`. The unit test does not use the slot `editor-ui`. It uses `servitor-bless-probe` under a temp project. Acceptance (Task 30) uses `servitor-accept-scratch`.

CLI: `servitor compare <reference> [--source host|viewport|window] [--bless] [--max-diff-pixels N] [--max-diff-pixel-ratio R]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/CompareCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/CaptureCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Listener.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Assets/ImageCompare.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Assets/ImageIo.hpp>
#include "RemoteTestSupport.hpp"
#include <Json.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

// Installs one case's capture hooks, compare hooks and JobTable* and clears all
// of them on scope exit (rulings F11, S3-9).
struct RemoteCompareScope {
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks> capture;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Remote::CompareHooks> compare;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::JobTable> captureJobs;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::JobTable> compareJobs;
    RemoteCompareScope(const Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks& cap, const Arcane::Remote::CompareHooks& cmp,
                    Arcane::Remote::JobTable& jobs)
        : capture([](const Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks* p) {
                      Arcane::Remote::SetCaptureHooks(p ? *p : Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks{});
                  }, &cap),
          compare([](const Arcane::Remote::CompareHooks* p) {
                      Arcane::Remote::SetCompareHooks(p ? *p : Arcane::Remote::CompareHooks{});
                  }, &cmp),
          captureJobs(&Arcane::Remote::RegisterCaptureCommands, &jobs),
          compareJobs(&Arcane::Remote::RegisterCompareCommand, &jobs) {}
};

static std::filesystem::path g_ref;
static int g_blesses = 0;
static int g_copies = 0;
static int g_reads = 0;
static std::uint64_t g_ticket = 0;
static bool g_retired = false;
static const std::uint8_t kWhite[4] = {255, 255, 255, 255};

static bool ArmWhite(std::uint64_t ticket, void*) {
    ++g_copies;
    g_ticket = ticket;
    g_retired = false;
    return true;
}
static bool DrainWhite(std::uint64_t ticket, Arcane::Remote::CaptureMapped* raw, void*) {
    if (!g_retired || ticket != g_ticket)
        return false;
    ++g_reads;
    raw->width = 1;
    raw->height = 1;
    raw->rowPitch = 4;
    raw->bgra = true;
    raw->mapped = kWhite;
    raw->mappedBytes = 4;
    return true;
}
static Arcane::Remote::ResolvedReference ResolveProbe(const std::filesystem::path&, const std::string& slot, void*) {
    Arcane::Remote::ResolvedReference out;
    out.triedPaths.push_back(g_ref.generic_string());
    if (slot != "servitor-bless-probe" || !std::filesystem::exists(g_ref))
        return out;
    out.found = true;
    out.path = g_ref;
    out.level = "temp";
    return out;
}
static bool BlessProbe(const std::filesystem::path&, const std::string&,
                       std::uint32_t w, std::uint32_t h, const unsigned char* rgba, void*) {
    ++g_blesses;
    g_ref = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "servitor-bless-probe.png";
    return Arcane::WritePngRgba(g_ref, w, h, rgba);
}

TEST_CASE("compare Failed is ok true exit 8, and bless then matches", "[remote][compare]") {
    g_blesses = 0;
    g_ref = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "servitor-bless-probe-black.png";
    unsigned char black[4] = {0, 0, 0, 255};
    REQUIRE(Arcane::WritePngRgba(g_ref, 1, 1, black));
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks cap;
    cap.framesInFlight = 2;
    cap.armHost = &ArmWhite;
    cap.drainHost = &DrainWhite;
    cap.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-compare-out";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(cap.outDir);
    Arcane::Remote::CompareHooks hooks;
    hooks.resolve = &ResolveProbe;
    hooks.bless = &BlessProbe;
    hooks.projectRoot = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path();
    RemoteCompareScope scope(cap, hooks, jobs);
    auto run = [&](bool bless, double ratio) {
        g_copies = 0;
        g_reads = 0;
        g_retired = false;
        Arcane::Remote::Call call;
        call.command = "capture.compare";
        call.args = nlohmann::json{{"reference", "servitor-bless-probe"}, {"source", "host"}, {"bless", bless}};
        if (ratio >= 0.0)
            call.args["maxDiffPixelRatio"] = ratio;
        auto result = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
        REQUIRE(result.completesJob != 0);
        const auto id = Arcane::Remote::JobId{result.completesJob};
        Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
        gate.frame = 10;
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
        CHECK(g_copies == 1);
        CHECK(g_reads == 0);
        CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
        REQUIRE(jobs.Find(id) != nullptr);
        CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
        CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->startedFrame == 0);
        CHECK_FALSE(jobs.Find(id)->envelope.data.contains("verdict"));
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
        CHECK(g_reads == 0);
        CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
        CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Remote::JobState::Running);
        g_retired = true;
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
        CHECK(g_reads == 1);
        CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
        return jobs.Find(id)->envelope;
    };
    const auto failed = run(false, -1.0);
    CHECK(failed.ok);
    CHECK(failed.errors.empty());
    CHECK(failed.data["verdict"] == "Failed");
    CHECK(failed.data["resolvedLevel"] == "temp");
    REQUIRE(failed.data["triedPaths"].size() == 1);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(failed) == 8);
    const auto ratioPass = run(false, 1.0);
    CHECK(ratioPass.ok);
    CHECK(ratioPass.data["verdict"] == "Passed");
    CHECK(ratioPass.data["thresholds"]["maxDiffPixelRatio"] == 1.0);
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(ratioPass) == 0);
    const auto blessed = run(true, -1.0);
    CHECK(g_blesses == 1);
    CHECK(blessed.ok);
    const auto again = run(false, -1.0);
    CHECK(again.ok);
    CHECK(again.data["verdict"] == "Passed");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(again) == 0);
    g_ref.clear();
    const auto created = run(true, -1.0);
    CHECK(created.ok);
    CHECK(created.errors.empty());
    CHECK(created.data["verdict"] == "Passed");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(created) == 0);
    CHECK(g_blesses == 2);
    CHECK(std::filesystem::exists(g_ref));
    g_copies = 0;
    g_reads = 0;
    g_ref.clear();
    Arcane::Remote::Call missingCall;
    missingCall.command = "capture.compare";
    missingCall.args = nlohmann::json{{"reference", "servitor-bless-probe"}, {"source", "host"}, {"bless", false}};
    const auto missing = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(missingCall);
    CHECK(missing.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK(g_copies == 0);
    CHECK(g_reads == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(missing.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(missing.envelope.errors[0].code == "compare.missing-reference");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(missing.envelope) == 6);
}

TEST_CASE("compare while a screenshot is in flight is host.busy capture-pending", "[remote][compare]") {
    g_copies = 0;
    g_reads = 0;
    g_retired = false;
    g_ticket = 0;
    g_blesses = 0;
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks cap;
    cap.framesInFlight = 2;
    cap.armWindow = &ArmWhite;
    cap.drainWindow = &DrainWhite;
    cap.armHost = &ArmWhite;
    cap.drainHost = &DrainWhite;
    cap.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-compare-busy";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(cap.outDir);
    Arcane::Remote::CompareHooks hooks;
    hooks.resolve = &ResolveProbe;
    hooks.bless = &BlessProbe;
    hooks.projectRoot = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path();
    RemoteCompareScope scope(cap, hooks, jobs);
    g_ref = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "servitor-bless-probe-busy.png";
    unsigned char black[4] = {0, 0, 0, 255};
    REQUIRE(Arcane::WritePngRgba(g_ref, 1, 1, black));

    Arcane::Remote::Call shot;
    shot.command = "capture.window";
    shot.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(shot);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 10;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(g_copies == 1);
    CHECK(g_reads == 0);
    const auto firstTicket = g_ticket;
    CHECK(firstTicket != 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);

    Arcane::Remote::Call cmp;
    cmp.command = "capture.compare";
    cmp.args = nlohmann::json{{"reference", "servitor-bless-probe"}, {"source", "host"}, {"bless", false}};
    const auto busy = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(cmp);
    CHECK(busy.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(busy.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(busy.envelope.errors[0].code == "host.busy");
    CHECK(busy.envelope.errors[0].detail["state"] == "capture-pending");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(busy.envelope) == 5);
    CHECK(g_copies == 1);
    CHECK(g_ticket == firstTicket);
    CHECK(g_reads == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
}

TEST_CASE("a read-only bless is refused and a read-only compare still runs", "[remote][compare]") {
    // Ruling F19: through the real dispatcher gate, which reads Desc::accessFor.
    g_copies = 0;
    g_reads = 0;
    g_retired = false;
    g_ticket = 0;
    g_blesses = 0;
    g_ref = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "servitor-bless-probe-ro.png";
    unsigned char black[4] = {0, 0, 0, 255};
    REQUIRE(Arcane::WritePngRgba(g_ref, 1, 1, black));
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::CaptureHooks cap;
    cap.framesInFlight = 2;
    cap.armHost = &ArmWhite;
    cap.drainHost = &DrainWhite;
    cap.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-compare-ro";
    std::filesystem::remove_all(cap.outDir);
    std::filesystem::create_directories(cap.outDir);
    Arcane::Remote::CompareHooks hooks;
    hooks.resolve = &ResolveProbe;
    hooks.bless = &BlessProbe;
    hooks.projectRoot = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path();
    RemoteCompareScope scope(cap, hooks, jobs);
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.readOnly = true;                                  // ARC_REMOTE_READ_ONLY on this host
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get(), gate, jobs,
                                          cap.outDir / "audit.ndjson");
    Arcane::Remote::Call bless;
    bless.command = "capture.compare";
    bless.args = nlohmann::json{{"reference", "servitor-bless-probe"}, {"source", "host"}, {"bless", true}};
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(1, bless));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult refused;
    REQUIRE(queue.TryTake(1, refused));
    CHECK(refused.envelope.errors[0].code == "permission.read-only");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(refused.envelope) == 3);
    CHECK(g_copies == 0);
    CHECK(g_blesses == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(jobs.AnyRunning({"capture.compare"}));
    Arcane::Remote::Call plain = bless;
    plain.args["bless"] = false;
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(2, plain));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);                           // runs the command: arms the job
    CHECK(jobs.AnyRunning({"capture.compare"}));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);                           // copy step
    CHECK(g_copies == 1);
    g_retired = true;
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);                           // completing drain Completes waiter 2
    Arcane::Remote::CommandResult ran;
    REQUIRE(queue.TryTake(2, ran));
    CHECK(ran.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(ran.envelope.data["verdict"] == "Failed");       // white vs black, zero budget
    CHECK(g_blesses == 0);
}
```

The lambda `run` is test code. The first `StepAll` arms, at frame 10, and it must not read pixels or store a verdict. The second `StepAll` is the same frame with the ticket not retired, so reads stays 0 and there is still no verdict. The third `StepAll`, after `g_retired` is set, is the only step that calls `CompareImages` or `BlessFn`. `gate.frame` stays 10. White BGRA `(255, 255, 255, 255)` is still white after `NormalizeCaptureRows`, so the black reference fails and the ratio pass still passes. `Invoke` itself must not call either hook. The ratio `1.0` is the only budget set, so a fully different pixel still passes. The no-budget call fails. Bless writes the white capture over the reference the next resolve sees. A bless of a missing file still grabs, returns `Passed` and exit 0, and does not return `compare.missing-reference`. The same missing file without `--bless` returns before `Arm` and reads nothing. Do not call `BlessReference` on `editor-ui`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate and build Debug per Global Constraints, then filter `compare Failed is ok*`. Expected: FAIL to compile.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the job**

**Budget.** Copy `maxDiffPixels` and `maxDiffPixelRatio` onto `ImageCompareOptions` only when the arg is present. Leave them unset otherwise, which is the zero budget.

**Arm and drain.**
- The compare job calls the Task 27 arm on its first step and the Task 27 drain on later steps.
- `drain` returning false leaves the job `Running`.
- The job does not read `gate.frame`.

**Compare and bless on the completing drain.**
- `CompareImages` runs only on the drain that returns bytes, and only when resolve already returned `found`.
- Before `CompareImages` or `BlessFn`, call `NormalizeCaptureRows` on those bytes. It is the same function Task 27's capture step calls. White BGRA `(255, 255, 255, 255)` stays white.
- Wrap the normalized capture, and the reference read with `LoadPngRgba`, as `PixelData` for `CompareImages`.
- `BlessFn` runs on that same drain when `args.bless` is true and the drain returned pixels. It receives the normalized RGBA.
- A creating bless (resolve not found, `args.bless` true) skips `CompareImages`, calls `BlessFn`, and completes `Passed` / exit 0.

**The editor `BlessFn`.**
- It calls `ResolveReference` (`ReferenceImages.hpp:109`), then `BlessReference` (`:122`). The `ResolveFn` the editor installs calls the same overload and copies `ReferenceResolution`'s path, level and `triedPaths` into the `ResolvedReference` it returns.
- The backend argument is `CompareBackendName(m_config.backend)`. That is the same call `EditorAppFrame.cpp:285` already makes; the helper is anonymous-namespace code at `EditorAppFrame.cpp:116`.
- The adapter set is `ReferenceAdapterSet(backendName, caps.adapterName, caps.softwareAdapter)`, computed the way `EditorAppFrame.cpp:286-290` does from `ChromeGraph()->Device().Caps()`. The editor's `m_compareAdapterSet` (`EditorApp.hpp:790`) is filled only under `--compare`, so the hook does not read it.
- An empty backend is refused: `ResolveReferenceIn` checks `ReferenceNameIsSafe(backend)` (`ReferenceImages.cpp:66`), and its empty check is `:28`.
- When the resolution's level is `None` on a hardware adapter, `blessTarget` is already `projectRoot/Verify/References/<name>.png` (`ReferenceImages.cpp:105-110`), so the first bless creates that shared file.
- Install the hooks from the editor program: define them in `EditorAppFrame.cpp` beside `CompareBackendName` (`:116`, anonymous namespace, which they call) and put them on `Config::compare` at the Task 11 arming site in that same file, next to the Task 27 capture hooks. ArcaneRemote does not include `ReferenceImages.hpp` (R8-2). The runtime installs its own pair in `RuntimeApp.cpp` the same way, from its `--compare` code (`:658-663`, `:1191`).

**State.** The compare step keeps the armed ticket on its own `CaptureJobState` (Task 27), and it does not store that ticket in `progress.done`. It does not write `startedFrame`.

**Test.** The test's `BlessProbe` does not call `BlessReference`. The test touches only temp files and has no Win32 primitive, so it runs on all three CI lanes with no exclusion (R8-7).

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Filter `[remote][compare]`. Expected: PASS. A bless on a read-only host is `permission.read-only` exit 3 before anything is armed, and a plain compare on that host runs to a `Failed` verdict (ruling F19). After the arm step, reads is 0, `gate.frame` is still 10, and there is no verdict. A second step without a retired ticket stays `Running` with reads 0. Reads becomes 1 only after the ticket is published. Failed compare exits 8 with `ok` true. A missing reference without bless exits 6 and reads nothing. Bless of a missing name exits 0 with verdict `Passed`. The bless round trip exits 0. A compare issued while `capture.window` is `Running` exits 5 with `capture-pending` and does not call `arm`.

### Task 29: `play`, `stop`, `pause`, `resume`, `step` as frame-spanning jobs

**Files:**
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/PlayCommands.hpp`
- Create: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/Commands/PlayCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/TickGate.hpp` and `TickGate.cpp` (add `void ClearSteps()`)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeApp.cpp`. The loop around `RuntimeFrame::AdvanceSim` at line 830 already `Consume`s, from Task 12. ArcaneRuntimeHost is the static library that ArcaneRuntime.exe links (`premake5.lua:444`). The monolithic packaged games link it too, so this wiring sits under `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST) && !defined(ARC_MONOLITHIC)` (Global Constraints **Packages**, ruling D-1).
- Modify: `Source/Programs/ArcaneServer/src/ServerApp.cpp` (the loop before `m_runtime->Loop().Advance` at line 191, and the `--frames` bound of the `for` at line 173)
- Modify: the ArcaneEditor play loop.
  - `PlaySession::Play` / `Stop` are at `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/App/PlayMode.hpp:108` and `:115`. The instance is `EditorApp::m_play` (`EditorApp.hpp:686`).
  - Pause and step go through `RunLoop::SetPaused` (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneSim/src/Arcane/Sim/RunLoop.hpp:63`) and `RequestSingleStep` (`:75`), reached as `m_runtime->Loop()`, the way the toolbar does at `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/EditorPanels.cpp:1016` and `:1022`.
  - The file is `Source/Programs/ArcaneEditor/src/App/EditorAppFrame.cpp` (ruling F33): the editor's `PlayHooks` functions are defined there and installed on `Config::play` at the Task 11 arming site.
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.hpp` (append `PlayHooks play` to `HostSession::Config`)
- Modify: `Source/Runtime/ArcaneRemote/src/Arcane/Remote/HostSession.cpp` (wiring, ruling F12: `Start` copies `config.play`, sets `gate = &Tick()` on runtime and server and `stats = &m_stats` (Task 26), then calls `SetPlayHooks(...)`, `SetPlayGate(&m_gate)` and `RegisterPlayCommands(&m_jobs)`; `Stop` calls `SetPlayHooks({})`, `SetPlayGate(nullptr)` and `RegisterPlayCommands(nullptr)`)
- Modify: `Source/Programs/Servitor/src/Servitor/Verbs.cpp` (rows `play`, `stop`, `pause`, `resume`, `step`; ruling F17)
- Test: `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/Remote/PlayCommandTest.cpp` (whole file inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`, ruling D-2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TickGate::Consume` (Task 12). `JobTable`, `AnyRunning` and the waiter completion in `StepAll` (Task 9, ruling F2). `RemoteDispatcher` (Task 8; its constructor calls `jobs.SetQueue(&queue)` and its `Drain` binds the waiter with the arming envelope's undo block). `RequestQueue::Wait` (Task 6). `FrameStats`, `RegisterStatsCommand` (Task 26). `RemoteTest::HttpExchange` (Task 6's `RemoteTestHttp.hpp`) and `RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer` (Task 4). `PlaySession` has no pause and no step (`PlayMode.hpp:92-140`). `PlayTopology` is `PlayMode.hpp:90`.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Remote {
struct PlayHooks {
    TickGate* gate = nullptr;                 // runtime and server
    bool (*editorPlay)(void*, std::string_view topology) = nullptr; // PlaySession::Play; topology is the CLI spelling
    bool (*editorStop)(void*) = nullptr;      // PlaySession::Stop
    void (*editorSetPaused)(bool paused, void*) = nullptr;
    void (*editorRequestSingleStep)(void*) = nullptr;
    void* editorUser = nullptr;
    bool editor = false;
    FrameStats* stats = nullptr;
    std::string undoReason; // "play-mode" on the editor, "runtime-host" on runtime and server
};
// All three are safe to call twice; a later call replaces the stored hooks /
// pointer (ruling F11). A null JobTable* or HostGate* makes play.* answer
// host.unavailable (exit 5) without dereferencing it (ruling S3-9).
ARC_REMOTE_API void SetPlayHooks(PlayHooks hooks);
ARC_REMOTE_API void SetPlayGate(HostGate* gate);       // the gate play.step reads its arm-time frame from
ARC_REMOTE_API void RegisterPlayCommands(JobTable* jobs);
}
```

All five are Drive, `Mutates::PlayMode`, `needsFrame` false. `play.step` is `ExecThread::Job`. The others return in the command.

`play.start` does not call `Advance`. Editor: `editorPlay(editorUser, topology)`, where `topology` is `args.topology` (default `standalone`); the command checks the spelling first, and anything other than `standalone`, `listen` or `client+server` answers `arg.invalid`, usage exit 2, without calling the hook. The editor's hook maps the spelling to `PlayTopology` and calls `PlaySession::Play` with it (G gap 1). Runtime and server: `TickGate::SetRunning(true)` and `SetPaused(false)`; their topology is fixed at launch, so a `topology` argument other than the default answers `arg.inapplicable`, exit 2, and does not start the gate. `play.stop`: editor `editorStop`; runtime and server `SetRunning(false)`, `SetPaused(true)`, `ClearSteps()`. `play.pause`: `SetPaused(true)` (editor function or the gate). `play.resume`: `SetPaused(false)` and `Running` stays true. `play.step`: `n` from `args.n`, default 1. `n < 1` or `n > 100000` is `arg.inapplicable`, exit 2, and it does not `Arm`. Otherwise it arms a job with `progress.total = n`, calls `RequestSteps(n)` (runtime and server) or stores `n` for the editor, and returns. It does not loop `Advance`. Each later `Drain` steps that job once. The job step reads `HostGate::frame` minus the frame at arm time. When the delta reaches `n` it completes the waiter. The host loop's `Consume` performs one `Advance` per iteration. After the job, `Consume` is `Idle`.

`undo` is `{label: null, undoable: false, reason: <PlayHooks::undoReason>}`. Do not emit a `recorded` field.

CLI: `servitor play [--topology standalone|listen|client+server]`, `servitor stop`, `servitor pause`, `servitor resume`, `servitor step [N]`. `client+server` is `PlayTopology::EmbeddedServer`. `listen` is `ListenServer`. `standalone` is `Standalone`. The enum also has `ClientOnly` (`PlayMode.hpp:90`). P0 gives it no CLI spelling.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/PlayCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Commands/StatsCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Listener.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/Dispatcher.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/TickGate.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Remote/FrameStats.hpp>
#include "RemoteTestHttp.hpp"     // the one HttpExchange (ruling S3-5); includes RemoteTestSupport.hpp
#include <Json.hpp>
#include <chrono>
#include <filesystem>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <thread>

// Installs one case's play hooks, play gate and JobTable* and clears all of
// them on scope exit, so no later case reads this case's stack objects
// (rulings F11, S3-9).
struct RemotePlayScope {
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<const Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks> hooks;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::JobTable> jobs;
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::HostGate> gate;
    RemotePlayScope(const Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks& h, Arcane::Remote::JobTable& j, Arcane::Remote::HostGate* g)
        : hooks([](const Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks* p) {
                    Arcane::Remote::SetPlayHooks(p ? *p : Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks{});
                }, &h),
          jobs(&Arcane::Remote::RegisterPlayCommands, &j),
          gate(&Arcane::Remote::SetPlayGate, g) {}
};

TEST_CASE("step 10 advances host.frame by exactly 10 while status still answers", "[remote][play]") {
    Arcane::Remote::TickGate tick;
    tick.SetRunning(false);
    tick.SetPaused(true);
    Arcane::Remote::FrameStats stats;
    Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks hooks;
    hooks.gate = &tick;
    hooks.stats = &stats;
    hooks.undoReason = "runtime-host";
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 100;
    gate.kind = "server";
    gate.state = "ready";
    RemotePlayScope play(hooks, jobs, &gate);   // SetPlayGate(&gate): the arm-time frame is 100 (ruling F11)
    Arcane::Remote::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    snap->frame = 100;
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    auto& reg = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get();
    const auto audit = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-play-audit" / "audit.ndjson";
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit);   // calls jobs.SetQueue(&queue)
    Arcane::Remote::RemoteListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Remote::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    const std::string body = R"({"command":"play.step","args":{"n":10}})";
    std::string posted;
    std::thread worker([&] {
        posted = RemoteTest::HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "", "POST /remote/v1/call HTTP/1.1", body);
    });
    const auto start = gate.frame.load();
    for (int spin = 0; spin < 200 && queue.Depth() == 0; ++spin)
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(5));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);   // runs play.step: arms the job, binds the parked POST as its waiter
    // The step job is Running and its POST is parked while /status answers (ruling F30).
    CHECK(jobs.AnyRunning({"play.step"}));
    const std::string status = RemoteTest::HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "", "GET /remote/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(status.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(jobs.AnyRunning({"play.step"}));
    int advanced = 0;
    while (tick.Consume() != Arcane::Remote::TickDecision::Idle) {
        gate.frame.fetch_add(1);
        Arcane::Remote::NoteAdvance(stats, gate.frame.load(), 0.016, 1.0, 0.0);
        ++advanced;
        dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
        snap->frame = gate.frame.load();
        atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<Arcane::Remote::HostStatus>(*snap));
    }
    worker.join();
    listener.Stop();
    CHECK(advanced == 10);
    CHECK(gate.frame.load() - start == 10);
    CHECK_FALSE(jobs.AnyRunning({"play.step"}));
    CHECK(tick.Consume() == Arcane::Remote::TickDecision::Idle);
    CHECK(posted.find("\"ok\":true") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(posted.find("\"frame\":110") != std::string::npos);   // StepAll stamped gate.Block() (ruling F2)
    CHECK(posted.find("runtime-host") != std::string::npos);      // the armed undo block rode through
    CHECK(stats.frame == 110);
    CHECK(stats.renderMs == 0.0);
    Arcane::Remote::Call later;
    later.command = "stats.frame";
    RemoteTest::ScopedRemotePointer<Arcane::Remote::FrameStats> statsGuard(&Arcane::Remote::RegisterStatsCommand, &stats);
    const auto after = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(later).envelope;
    CHECK(after.data["frame"] == 110);
}

TEST_CASE("step rejects n outside 1..100000 and does not arm", "[remote][play]") {
    Arcane::Remote::TickGate tick;
    Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks hooks;
    hooks.gate = &tick;
    hooks.undoReason = "runtime-host";
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    RemotePlayScope play(hooks, jobs, &gate);   // SetPlayGate(&gate) (ruling F11)
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "play.step";
    call.args = nlohmann::json{{"n", 0}};
    const auto zero = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(zero.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK(zero.envelope.errors[0].code == "arg.inapplicable");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(zero.envelope) == 2);
    CHECK(tick.StepsRemaining() == 0);
    call.args["n"] = 100001;
    const auto huge = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(huge.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK(tick.StepsRemaining() == 0);
}

TEST_CASE("play start does not advance, and stop returns to Idle", "[remote][play]") {
    Arcane::Remote::TickGate tick;
    tick.SetRunning(false);
    tick.SetPaused(true);
    Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks hooks;
    hooks.gate = &tick;
    hooks.undoReason = "runtime-host";
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    RemotePlayScope play(hooks, jobs, &gate);
    Arcane::Remote::Call start;
    start.command = "play.start";
    const auto began = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(start).envelope;
    CHECK(began.ok);
    CHECK(tick.Running());
    CHECK_FALSE(tick.Paused());
    CHECK(tick.Consume() == Arcane::Remote::TickDecision::Run);
    Arcane::Remote::Call stop;
    stop.command = "play.stop";
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(stop).envelope.ok);
    CHECK_FALSE(tick.Running());
    CHECK(tick.Consume() == Arcane::Remote::TickDecision::Idle);
    Arcane::Remote::Call listen;
    listen.command = "play.start";
    listen.args = nlohmann::json{{"topology", "listen"}};
    const auto refused = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(listen).envelope;
    CHECK(refused.errors[0].code == "arg.inapplicable");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(refused) == 2);
    CHECK_FALSE(tick.Running());
}

static std::string g_topology;
static bool EditorPlayTopology(void*, std::string_view topology) {
    g_topology = std::string(topology);
    return true;
}

TEST_CASE("editor play passes the topology and refuses an unknown one", "[remote][play]") {
    g_topology.clear();
    Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks hooks;
    hooks.editor = true;
    hooks.editorPlay = &EditorPlayTopology;
    hooks.undoReason = "play-mode";
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    RemotePlayScope play(hooks, jobs, &gate);
    Arcane::Remote::Call start;
    start.command = "play.start";
    start.args = nlohmann::json{{"topology", "client+server"}};
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(start).envelope.ok);
    CHECK(g_topology == "client+server");
    g_topology.clear();
    start.args = nlohmann::json{{"topology", "mesh"}};
    const auto bad = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(start).envelope;
    CHECK(bad.errors[0].code == "arg.invalid");
    CHECK(Arcane::Remote::ExitFromHost(bad) == 2);
    CHECK(g_topology.empty());
}

TEST_CASE("server --frames counts sim advances, so a remote pause does not spend it", "[remote][play]") {
    // Documentation of the ServerApp loop shape after this task (ruling S3-3):
    // the --frames bound is rep.framesTicked, which only the Advance branch
    // increments. This models the loop; Task 30's live
    // "server --frames 1200 ticks exactly 1200 across a remote pause, step and resume"
    // case drives the real ArcaneServer and is the proof.
    constexpr std::uint64_t kFrames = 8;
    Arcane::Remote::TickGate tick;
    tick.SetRunning(true);
    tick.SetPaused(false);
    std::uint64_t framesTicked = 0;
    std::uint64_t iterations = 0;
    auto iterate = [&] {
        ++iterations;
        if (tick.Consume() != Arcane::Remote::TickDecision::Idle)
            ++framesTicked;
    };
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) iterate();
    tick.SetPaused(true);
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) iterate();      // paused: idle iterations
    CHECK(framesTicked == 2);
    tick.RequestSteps(3);
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; ++i) iterate();      // three steps, then idle again
    CHECK(framesTicked == 5);
    tick.SetPaused(false);
    while (framesTicked < kFrames) iterate();   // resume until the budget is ticked
    CHECK(framesTicked == kFrames);
    CHECK(iterations > kFrames);
}

static int g_editorSteps = 0;
static void EditorOneStep(void*) { ++g_editorSteps; }

TEST_CASE("editor step calls RequestSingleStep once per drain, not n times", "[remote][play]") {
    g_editorSteps = 0;
    Arcane::Remote::PlayHooks hooks;
    hooks.editor = true;
    hooks.editorRequestSingleStep = &EditorOneStep;
    hooks.undoReason = "play-mode";
    Arcane::Remote::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Remote::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 4;
    RemotePlayScope play(hooks, jobs, &gate);
    Arcane::Remote::Call call;
    call.command = "play.step";
    call.args = nlohmann::json{{"n", 3}};
    const auto armed = Arcane::Remote::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(armed.completesJob != 0);
    CHECK(g_editorSteps == 0);
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(g_editorSteps == 1);
    gate.frame = 5;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(g_editorSteps == 2);
    REQUIRE(armed.envelope.undo.has_value());
    CHECK(armed.envelope.undo->reason == "play-mode");
}
```

`SetPlayGate` stores the `HostGate*` the command reads the arm-time frame from; every case that arms or rejects a step passes its own gate through `RemotePlayScope`, which nulls it again on exit (ruling F11). The command function does not call `EditorOneStep`. One `StepAll` calls it once. The worker thread in the step-10 case blocks inside `POST /remote/v1/call` until the job finishes. `GET /remote/v1/status` is answered on that same listener while the POST is outstanding, and `jobs.AnyRunning({"play.step"})` is true on both sides of that status call (ruling F30). `play.start` must not increment `gate.frame`. The editor envelope's `undo.reason` is `play-mode`. Write that reason onto the envelope inside the command, before the job is armed, so `armed.envelope.undo->reason` is `play-mode` even while the job is still running.

`HostStatus` needs a copy constructor the `shared_ptr` store can use. It already is a normal struct (Task 3). `atoms.status` is `atomic<shared_ptr<const HostStatus>>`. Storing a fresh `shared_ptr` each advance is the publish. The POST body the listener parses uses the field `command`.

R8-7: the test's socket calls are Task 6's `RemoteTestHttp.hpp` (`RemoteTest::HttpExchange`, ruling S3-5), which uses the portable aliases in `Source/Runtime/ArcaneNet/src/Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp`. The test has no Win32-only call, so every case runs on all three CI lanes with no exclusion.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the five commands**

`ClearSteps` sets the remaining count to 0. The job records `startedFrame` from `gate.frame` at arm time. The step sets `progress.done` to the delta and returns true only when `delta >= total`. Do not call `Advance`, `AdvanceSim`, or `RequestSingleStep` inside `play.step`'s command function. The editor job step calls `editorRequestSingleStep` once and returns false until the frame delta reaches `n`. Wire the runtime (`Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeApp.cpp`, `AdvanceSim` at `:830`) and the server (`Source/Programs/ArcaneServer/src/ServerApp.cpp`, `Advance` at `:191`). In both, the loop order from Task 12 is `Drain`, then `Consume`, then at most one `Advance`. `NoteAdvance` runs in that `Advance` branch only. On the server, `--frames N` counts sim advances, not loop iterations (AM1): the `for` at `ServerApp.cpp:173` becomes a loop whose bound is `m_cfg.frames == 0 || rep.framesTicked < m_cfg.frames`, and `rep.framesTicked` is incremented only in the `Advance` branch (Task 12). An remote pause therefore never spends the frame budget: a paused `--frames N --remote` server idles until it is resumed or stepped, and still ticks exactly N. The `"server --frames counts sim advances"` case pins that loop shape. The editor's `PlayHooks` functions wrap `m_play.Play(*m_runtime, ...)`, `m_play.Stop(*m_runtime, ...)`, `m_runtime->Loop().SetPaused(...)` and `m_runtime->Loop().RequestSingleStep()`. They are installed from the editor program, not from ArcaneRemote (R8-2): defined in `EditorAppFrame.cpp` and placed on `Config::play` at the Task 11 arming site. Wire `HostSession` as the Files list says (`SetPlayHooks`, `SetPlayGate(&m_gate)`, `RegisterPlayCommands(&m_jobs)` in `Start`; cleared in `Stop`), and add the five rows to `Verbs.cpp`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Filter `[remote][play]`. Expected: PASS. `advanced` is 10. `gate.frame` delta is 10. The step job is `Running` around the status call, which answers `200` while the POST is parked. The POST body carries `host.frame` 110 and `runtime-host`. A later `stats.frame` is 110. The editor hook receives `client+server`, an unknown topology is exit 2, and the paused server shape ticks exactly 8.
