A Debug-CRT game DLL is the one the Debug hosts load.

Hosts, all Debug, working directory the exe's own directory. The exes are `Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\<Host>\<Host>.exe`, and `servitor.exe` is `Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\Servitor\servitor.exe` (R8-4):

- `ArcaneEditor.exe --headless --project <ReferenceProject> --remote`
- `ArcaneRuntime.exe --headless --project <ReferenceProject> --remote`
- `ArcaneServer.exe --project <ReferenceProject> --remote`

`<ReferenceProject>` is the repo-root `ReferenceProject`, given as an absolute path. Each host's postbuild also stages a copy of ReferenceProject beside its exe (for example ArcaneServer's `arcane_copydir` at `premake5.lua:942`). The acceptance does not use those copies, so all three hosts and `servitor.exe` share one `Saved/Remote` and one `Verify/References`. The server flag is `ServerConfig::remote` (Task 10; `Source/Programs/ArcaneServer/src/ServerConfig.hpp:17`). A server started without `--remote` is a failed acceptance, not a skipped one. `--frames` is absent, so the tick gate starts Idle (Task 12).

`servitor.exe` argv, `--format json` on every call, `--project` the ReferenceProject root. Every `--timeout` value in this task is seconds: `--timeout 60` stores 60000, `--timeout 1` stores 1000, and `--timeout 10` stores 10000.

**The server cvar probe (ruling F21).** `render.vsync` is a field of ArcaneRender's `RenderSettings` (a Client module), and ArcaneServer links only Engine/Net/Crypto (`premake5.lua:887`). Before running the acceptance, the executor VERIFIES whether the live ArcaneServer registers `render.vsync` (for example `servitor --host server cvar get render.vsync` against a server started with `--remote`: exit 0 means it does; `cvar.unknown` exit 6 means it does not). If it does, `kServerProbe` below stays `render.vsync`. If it does not, `kServerProbe` is a cvar registered by ArcaneCore or ArcaneEngine that the Editor, LocalHost and ServerAdmin contexts can all read, that is any cvar whose audience is not `Editor` and that is neither `Hidden` nor `Protected` (`DefaultAccess`, `CVarRegistry.cpp:105-112`); the suggested one is ArcaneCore's `log.file.flushLevel` (`LogFileSettings.hpp`, `Audience::Game`). The `--host server cvar get` call and the `--all cvar get` call both use `kServerProbe`. The task report records which cvar was used and why.

- `--host editor wait settled` (timeout 60 s)
- `--host editor entity get name:MeshCube --component Transform`
- `--host editor cvar set render.vsync false`
- `--host editor cvar explain render.vsync`
- `--host editor compare servitor-accept-scratch --source host --bless` (the scratch file is deleted first)
- `--host editor compare servitor-accept-scratch --source host` (no `--bless`; exit 0, verdict `Passed`)
- `--host editor compare servitor-accept-scratch --source host` again, after one pixel of that PNG is flipped (no `--bless`; exit 8)
- `--host editor problems`
- `--host runtime stats frame`, then `--host runtime screenshot window`, then `--host runtime stats frame` again (no `--frames` on the runtime, so this is the idle headless capture; exit 0; the two `data.frame` values are equal)
- `--host editor quit` (timeout 10 s, then kill)
- `--host runtime cvar get render.vsync` (exit 0; the runtime links ArcaneRender)
- `--host runtime entity get name:MeshCube --component Transform`
- `--host server cvar get <kServerProbe>` (exit 0; ruling F21)
- `--host server entity get name:MeshCube --component Transform`
- `--all cvar get <kServerProbe>` (exit 0)
- `--host runtime step 10`, then `--host runtime stats frame`, then `--host runtime stop` (exit 0), then `--host runtime stats frame` again (unchanged by the stop; ruling F34)
- `--host server step 10`, then `--host server stats frame`
- `--host runtime entity get name:NoSuchEntity`
- `--host runtime wait state playing --timeout 1`
- `--host server screenshot window`

MeshCube in `ReferenceProject/Content/scenes/main.arcscene` is `Arcane::Transform` position `[0.4, -0.3, 0]` (the entity name is line 257, `Arcane::Transform` line 269, the position lines 270–274). Rotation in that file is `[0, 0, 0, 1]` (lines 275–280). Assert those numbers with absolute error `1e-4`. Assert `data.components.Transform`. Assert `host.frame` on the step calls moved by exactly 10, and that the following `stats.frame` `data.frame` is that same number. The compare slot is `servitor-accept-scratch`, not `editor-ui`. Delete `ReferenceProject/Verify/References/servitor-accept-scratch.png` and any `Verify/References/<subdir>/servitor-accept-scratch.png` before the bless and again in cleanup. The subdirectories cover the backend level and the adapter-set level (`ReferenceImages.hpp:44-50`). The acceptance runs on the desk's hardware adapter, where a creating bless writes the shared path (`ReferenceImages.cpp:105-110`). That is the path the poisoning step below reads. The bless call is the only one whose argv contains `--bless`. It must exit 0. The next call, the same slot without `--bless`, must exit 0 with verdict `Passed`. Flipping one pixel of that PNG and comparing again, still without `--bless`, must exit 8 with `ok` true, verdict `Failed`, and empty `errors`. There is no branch that treats exit 8 as an acceptable bless failure. The 60 s compare timeout covers the render-ticket readback. The job does not wait on `host.frame`. The editor bless runs before any play or step, so it is the idle-editor capture: `blessJson["host"]["frame"]` equals `settledJson["host"]["frame"]`. The runtime is started without `--frames`. Before `step`, `screenshot window` on that runtime exits 0 and the `stats.frame` value after it equals the value before it. Record each call's wall time with `INFO` and append a line to `servitor-latency.txt` next to the test exe. Do not fail the test on that number. Do not bless `editor-ui`. The golden gate later in this task is the check for that slot, and it is not re-blessed.

Self-test exits, in this Catch2 case, not in a `.servitor` file (`servitor run` is P2):

- `entity get name:NoSuchEntity` exits 6, code `entity.not-found`.
- `wait state playing --timeout 1` while the host is not playing exits 8, `data.timedOut` true, `ok` true, `errors` empty.
- `screenshot window` on the server exits 2, code `capture.no-renderer`.
- `screenshot window` on the idle headless runtime (no `--frames`, before `step`) exits 0, and `stats.frame` does not move.

Death: spawn `ArcaneRuntime.exe --headless --project <ReferenceProject> --remote --hang-main 60`. A bare `--hang-main` stays 0 (`Source/Client/ArcaneClient/src/Arcane/Host/HostConfig.cpp:177`, stored at `:272`; Dist-guarded, so the hosts must be Debug), and then `Source/Programs/ArcaneRuntimeHost/src/RuntimeFrame.cpp:486` never sleeps. The counter compared there is `io.frameCount` (incremented at `RuntimeFrame.cpp:800`), not `host.frame`. There is no `--frames`, so the tick gate stays Idle and `RenderGraph` (`:454`) still runs. Poll `servitor status --format json` until the `data.hosts` row whose `pid` equals this child's `pi.dwProcessId` has `hostKind` `runtime` and `hung` true. The deadline is 90 s (boot, 60 frames, the 10 s heartbeat, and margin). A non-zero status, or a body that is not JSON yet, keeps polling. Then `cvar get render.vsync` with `--host runtime`, `--pid` of that same pid, and `--timeout 10` (10 seconds, so the harness wait of 15000 ms is longer than the client). Require that this child's hung row was seen, then require exit 7, `errors[0].code` `host.hung`, and `client.exitCode` 7. `GetExitCodeProcess` on the runtime is `STILL_ACTIVE`: the sleep is 15 s (`kHangMainSeconds`, `HostConfig.hpp:411`, pinned at `Tests/ArcaneTests/src/HostConfigTest.cpp:203`), and a zero-exit hang report leaves the process running (`Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Diagnostics.hpp:241-243`). The call is issued on the same iteration that first sees hung, with no extra sleep. The hung window is the 5 s from the 10 s heartbeat until the sleep ends. If `detail.arcdiag` is present, the file exists, its extension is `.arcdiag`, and its mtime is after the cvar call started. On a host with a project open, reports land under `<project>/Saved/Diagnostics` (`Tests/ArcaneTests/src/CrashWitnessTest.cpp:100`: "RuntimeApp retargets here on project open"). The first hung sample is usually before the watchdog's 12 s report (threshold `g_cfg.hangSeconds`, `Source/Runtime/ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Diagnostics.cpp:1902`), so the key is often absent. Task 14's `ObserveTarget` case is the deterministic `.arcdiag` pin. A client that blocks makes `RunServitor` return 124, and `CHECK(code == 7)` fails. The case's destructor deletes `ReferenceProject/Saved/Remote` so a `REQUIRE` abort still removes the descriptors. `HostGuard` is not widened to do that. `--crash-gpu` is a separate case, gated like G1 (`CrashWitnessTest.cpp:122-124`): when `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK` is unset it `SKIP`s with `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK not set -- desk-only TDR lane`. When the variable is set, spawn `ArcaneRuntime.exe --headless --project <ReferenceProject> --frames 60 --crash-gpu 30` and require the process to exit 1 (the hosts' device-loss code, `CrashWitnessTest.cpp:113-114`). An agent must not set `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK=1`. A deliberate GPU fault triggers a machine-wide TDR (ruling R106, `CrashWitnessTest.cpp:116-121`).

The token inside `Saved/Remote/*.json` must not appear in any captured `servitor.exe` stdout or stderr (ruling F23): `RunServitor` appends every call's stdout and stderr to one transcript, the case snapshots all three descriptor tokens right after the settle (once the editor, runtime and server descriptors all exist), and at the end asserts that none of the three appears anywhere in the transcript. Delete the descriptor files in the test's cleanup: the main case holds a `DescriptorSweep` RAII object over `ReferenceProject/Saved/Remote` (ruling F34), and clears that directory before it spawns, so no stale descriptor from an earlier run is read. Ruling P4 (b): the acceptance owns its child hosts through an RAII scope guard (`HostGuard`). The destructor kills every child (`TerminateProcess`, then `CloseHandle`) and calls `DeleteScratch`. Catch2 `REQUIRE` aborts by throwing, so the destructor runs on an assertion failure and on the success path. Do not use a local cleanup lambda; a later `REQUIRE` skips it. `DeleteScratch` only removes the scratch PNGs and a second call is a no-op. Handles that are already null are skipped, so the destructor does not double-close. Timeouts are 60 s for settled, 20 s for a cvar call, 10 s for quit. A hung child is a test failure after the kill. Screenshots and compares in this case each wait for `RunServitor` to return before the next one starts, so two captures are not in flight together. The `crash-gpu` child and the `--hang-main` child use the same guard.

Dist, after the Debug acceptance (Windows only):
- The `Arcane::Remote` code now lives in the ArcaneRemote module (R8-1), so the check reads both `ArcaneRemote.dll` and `ArcaneCore.dll`. Module DLLs land at `Binaries/<cfg>-<os>-<arch>-md/<Module>/` (`build/modules.lua:391`).
- The module vcxproj is generated at the module root (`build/modules.lua:387`). Task 30 (C7) builds the whole Dist solution instead of that vcxproj with its project references: Task 10 found that a vcxproj-only Dist build breaks ArcaneRender's shader step. A slnx `/t:` target is not used: the modules sit in solution folders (`premake5.lua:429-430`).
- The match is the regex `@Remote@Arcane@@|Arcane::Remote::` (Task 10's corrected gate). `dumpbin /EXPORTS` prints MSVC-decorated names (`?X@Remote@Arcane@@...`), so the undecorated `Arcane::Remote::` alone never matches; the decorated half is the one that bites. A bare `Arcane::Remote` would also match today's `Arcane::ArcModuleAttachRegistrar` and `Arcane::ArcaneWorkScheduler` in ArcaneCore. The only export a Dist ArcaneRemote.dll may carry is the anchor `ArcModule_ArcaneRemote`.
- Task 30 (C7) extends the gate to the protocol module (rev 8.3 V1): a Dist `ArcaneRemoteProtocol.dll` carries no `Arcane::Remote` export either, and its only export is its anchor `ArcModule_ArcaneRemoteProtocol`. For each module DLL the gate also requires that the whole export list is exactly that one anchor (the pattern alone would miss a non-Remote export), with each DLL's own anchor; `\bArcModule_ArcaneRemote\b` does not match `ArcModule_ArcaneRemoteProtocol`, so the two anchors are named per DLL, never by one exclusion. ArcaneCore.dll keeps the pattern check only (it exports the rest of the engine core).
- `dumpbin` is found through `vswhere`, as `scripts/ci/package.ps1` finds it.

```powershell
& "C:\Program Files\Microsoft Visual Studio\18\Community\MSBuild\Current\Bin\amd64\MSBuild.exe" D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Arcane.slnx /p:Configuration=Dist /m:8 /nodeReuse:false /p:CL=/FS /v:m
$vswhere = Join-Path ${env:ProgramFiles(x86)} 'Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe'
$dumpbin = @(& $vswhere -latest -find 'VC\Tools\MSVC\*\bin\Hostx64\x64\dumpbin.exe') | Select-Object -First 1
if (-not $dumpbin) { throw 'dumpbin.exe not found via vswhere' }
$dist = "D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Binaries\Dist-windows-x86_64-md"
$gates = @(
    @{ Dll = "ArcaneRemote\ArcaneRemote.dll";                 Anchor = "ArcModule_ArcaneRemote" },
    @{ Dll = "ArcaneRemoteProtocol\ArcaneRemoteProtocol.dll"; Anchor = "ArcModule_ArcaneRemoteProtocol" },
    @{ Dll = "ArcaneCore\ArcaneCore.dll";                     Anchor = $null }
)
foreach ($g in $gates) {
    $path = Join-Path $dist $g.Dll
    if (-not (Test-Path $path)) { throw "Dist $($g.Dll) was not built" }
    $out = @(& $dumpbin /nologo /EXPORTS $path)
    $hit = $out | Select-String -Pattern '@Remote@Arcane@@|Arcane::Remote::'
    if ($hit) { $hit; throw "Dist $($g.Dll) exports Arcane::Remote" }
    if ($g.Anchor) {
        # An export row is: ordinal, hint, RVA, name.
        $names = @($out | ForEach-Object { if ($_ -match '^\s+\d+\s+[0-9A-Fa-f]+\s+[0-9A-Fa-f]{8}\s+(\S+)') { $Matches[1] } })
        if ($names.Count -ne 1 -or $names[0] -ne $g.Anchor) { $names; throw "Dist $($g.Dll) exports more than its anchor $($g.Anchor)" }
    }
    "GATE PASS: $($g.Dll)"
}
```

Golden gate, Debug hosts, do not re-bless (Windows only):
- `scripts/golden-gate.ps1` defaults to `-Configuration Debug` (`:188`). It rebuilds `ReferenceProject.slnx` itself through `ci\msbuild.cmd` (`:446-487`) and restages it beside the hosts.
- It runs fourteen dx12 and vulkan lanes (header `:23-37`).
- Its header's step 3 (`:11`) is a precondition: delete the exe-dir `imgui.ini`. The acceptance above launches `ArcaneEditor.exe` from `Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\ArcaneEditor`, so delete `Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\ArcaneEditor\imgui.ini` before the gate.

```powershell
Remove-Item D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\ArcaneEditor\imgui.ini -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\scripts\golden-gate.ps1
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
// Windows-only acceptance (rev 8, R8-7): P0 is functional on Windows, and this
// file spawns, redirects and kills hosts with CreateProcessW/TerminateProcess.
// Off Windows it compiles to nothing, so no exclusion-list entry is needed.
// Dist compiles it out too (ruling D-2).
#if defined(_WIN32) && !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)
#ifndef WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#endif
#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#include <windows.h>
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Render/RenderErrorLatch.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Assets/ImageIo.hpp>
#include <Json.hpp>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <iterator>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

static void DeleteScratch(const std::filesystem::path& project) {
    const auto refs = project / "Verify" / "References";
    std::error_code ec;
    std::filesystem::remove(refs / "servitor-accept-scratch.png", ec);
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(refs, ec)) return;
    for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(refs, ec)) {
        if (!entry.is_directory()) continue;
        std::filesystem::remove(entry.path() / "servitor-accept-scratch.png", ec);
    }
}

struct HostGuard {
    HANDLE editor = nullptr;
    HANDLE runtime = nullptr;
    HANDLE server = nullptr;
    std::filesystem::path project;
    ~HostGuard() {
        for (HANDLE h : {editor, runtime, server}) {
            if (h) {
                TerminateProcess(h, 1);
                CloseHandle(h);
            }
        }
        editor = runtime = server = nullptr;
        if (!project.empty())
            DeleteScratch(project);
    }
    HostGuard() = default;
    HostGuard(const HostGuard&) = delete;
    HostGuard& operator=(const HostGuard&) = delete;
};

// Every RunServitor call appends its stdout and stderr here (ruling F23).
static std::string g_servitorTranscript;

// Removes ReferenceProject/Saved/Remote on scope exit, so a REQUIRE abort still
// removes the descriptors (ruling F34). Shared by every case in this file.
struct DescriptorSweep {
    std::filesystem::path dir;
    ~DescriptorSweep() {
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::remove_all(dir, ec);
    }
};

// Every 64-char token in the live descriptors under `dir`.
static std::vector<std::string> ReadTokens(const std::filesystem::path& dir) {
    std::vector<std::string> tokens;
    std::error_code ec;
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(dir, ec))
        return tokens;
    for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::directory_iterator(dir, ec)) {
        if (entry.path().extension() != ".json")
            continue;
        std::ifstream in(entry.path());
        const auto j = nlohmann::json::parse(in, nullptr, false);
        if (j.is_object() && j.contains("token") && j["token"].is_string()
            && j["token"].get<std::string>().size() == 64)
            tokens.push_back(j["token"].get<std::string>());
    }
    return tokens;
}

static HANDLE SpawnSleeper() {
    wchar_t sys[MAX_PATH];
    REQUIRE(GetSystemDirectoryW(sys, MAX_PATH) > 0);
    std::wstring cmd = std::wstring(L"\"") + sys + L"\\ping.exe\" 127.0.0.1 -n 30";
    STARTUPINFOW si{};
    si.cb = sizeof(si);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
    std::vector<wchar_t> mutableCmd(cmd.begin(), cmd.end());
    mutableCmd.push_back(L'\0');
    REQUIRE(CreateProcessW(nullptr, mutableCmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE,
                           CREATE_NO_WINDOW, nullptr, nullptr, &si, &pi));
    CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    return pi.hProcess;
}

static HANDLE DuplicateProcess(HANDLE process) {
    HANDLE dup = nullptr;
    REQUIRE(DuplicateHandle(GetCurrentProcess(), process, GetCurrentProcess(), &dup,
                            0, FALSE, DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS));
    return dup;
}

static int RunServitor(const std::filesystem::path& servitorExe, const std::vector<std::string>& args,
                  std::string* stdoutText, std::string* stderrText, std::uint32_t timeoutMs);

TEST_CASE("P0 hosts answer the spec envelope", "[remote][gpu]") {
    const auto testsDir = std::filesystem::current_path();   // <repo>\Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\ArcaneTests
    const auto bin = testsDir.parent_path();                  // <repo>\Binaries\Debug-windows-x86_64-md
    const auto repo = bin.parent_path().parent_path();
    const auto project = repo / "ReferenceProject";
    const auto servitor = bin / "Servitor" / "servitor.exe";
    const auto editor = bin / "ArcaneEditor" / "ArcaneEditor.exe";
    const auto runtime = bin / "ArcaneRuntime" / "ArcaneRuntime.exe";
    const auto server = bin / "ArcaneServer" / "ArcaneServer.exe";
    REQUIRE(std::filesystem::exists(servitor));
    REQUIRE(std::filesystem::exists(editor));
    REQUIRE(std::filesystem::exists(runtime));
    REQUIRE(std::filesystem::exists(server));
    DeleteScratch(project);
    DescriptorSweep saved{project / "Saved" / "Remote"};   // ruling F34
    {
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::remove_all(saved.dir, ec);     // no stale descriptor from an earlier run
    }
    g_servitorTranscript.clear();
    // Ruling F21: set after the Step 3 verification; render.vsync only if the server registers it.
    const std::string kServerProbe = "render.vsync";

    auto spawn = [](const std::filesystem::path& exe, const std::vector<std::wstring>& args) -> HANDLE {
        std::wstring cmd = L"\"" + exe.wstring() + L"\"";
        for (const auto& a : args) cmd += L" " + a;
        STARTUPINFOW si{};
        si.cb = sizeof(si);
        PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
        std::vector<wchar_t> mutableCmd(cmd.begin(), cmd.end());
        mutableCmd.push_back(L'\0');
        REQUIRE(CreateProcessW(exe.c_str(), mutableCmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, 0,
                               nullptr, exe.parent_path().c_str(), &si, &pi));
        CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
        return pi.hProcess;
    };
    const std::wstring proj = project.wstring();
    HostGuard hosts;
    hosts.project = project;
    hosts.editor = spawn(editor, {L"--headless", L"--project", proj, L"--remote"});
    hosts.runtime = spawn(runtime, {L"--headless", L"--project", proj, L"--remote"});
    hosts.server = spawn(server, {L"--project", proj, L"--remote"});

    std::string out, err;
    const int settled = RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json",
                                     "--host", "editor", "--timeout", "60", "wait", "settled"},
                               &out, &err, 60000);
    REQUIRE(settled == 0);
    const auto settledJson = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(settledJson["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(settledJson["command"] == "host.wait");
    CHECK(settledJson["host"].contains("frame"));
    CHECK_FALSE(settledJson.contains("hostFrame"));
    CHECK_FALSE(settledJson.contains("result"));
    CHECK(settledJson["client"]["exitCode"] == 0);

    // Snapshot all three hosts' tokens now (ruling F23); the editor's descriptor
    // is gone after `quit`, so a check at the end could not read it.
    std::vector<std::string> tokens;
    const auto tokenDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(60);
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < tokenDeadline) {
        tokens = ReadTokens(saved.dir);
        if (tokens.size() >= 3)
            break;
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(250));
    }
    REQUIRE(tokens.size() == 3);

    out.clear();
    const int got = RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json",
                                 "--host", "editor", "entity", "get", "name:MeshCube",
                                 "--component", "Transform"},
                           &out, &err, 20000);
    REQUIRE(got == 0);
    const auto entity = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    const auto& pos = entity["data"]["components"]["Transform"]["position"];
    CHECK(std::abs(pos[0].get<double>() - 0.4) < 1e-4);
    CHECK(std::abs(pos[1].get<double>() - (-0.3)) < 1e-4);
    CHECK(std::abs(pos[2].get<double>() - 0.0) < 1e-4);
    const auto& rot = entity["data"]["components"]["Transform"]["rotation"];
    CHECK(rot[0] == 0);
    CHECK(rot[1] == 0);
    CHECK(rot[2] == 0);
    CHECK(rot[3] == 1);

    out.clear();
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "editor",
                         "cvar", "set", "render.vsync", "false"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    out.clear();
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "editor",
                         "cvar", "explain", "render.vsync"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    const auto explained = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(explained["data"]["audience"] == "PlayerSafe");
    CHECK(explained["data"]["setBy"] == "Console");
    CHECK(explained["data"]["apply"] == "Restart");

    out.clear();
    const int blessed = RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "editor",
                                     "compare", "servitor-accept-scratch", "--source", "host", "--bless"},
                               &out, &err, 60000);
    REQUIRE(blessed == 0);
    const auto blessJson = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(blessJson["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(blessJson["command"] == "capture.compare");
    CHECK(blessJson["data"]["verdict"] == "Passed");
    CHECK(blessJson["host"]["frame"] == settledJson["host"]["frame"]);

    out.clear();
    const int passed = RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "editor",
                                    "compare", "servitor-accept-scratch", "--source", "host"},
                              &out, &err, 60000);
    REQUIRE(passed == 0);
    CHECK(out.find("--bless") == std::string::npos);
    const auto passJson = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(passJson["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(passJson["command"] == "capture.compare");
    CHECK(passJson["data"]["verdict"] == "Passed");

    const auto scratch = project / "Verify" / "References" / "servitor-accept-scratch.png";
    std::uint32_t pw = 0, ph = 0;
    std::vector<unsigned char> pixels;
    REQUIRE(std::filesystem::exists(scratch));
    REQUIRE(Arcane::LoadPngRgba(scratch, pw, ph, pixels));
    REQUIRE(pixels.size() >= 4);
    pixels[0] = static_cast<unsigned char>(pixels[0] ^ 0xFFu);
    REQUIRE(Arcane::WritePngRgba(scratch, pw, ph, pixels.data()));
    out.clear();
    const int poisoned = RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "editor",
                                      "compare", "servitor-accept-scratch", "--source", "host"},
                                &out, &err, 60000);
    REQUIRE(poisoned == 8);
    CHECK(out.find("--bless") == std::string::npos);
    const auto poisonJson = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(poisonJson["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(poisonJson["data"]["verdict"] == "Failed");
    CHECK(poisonJson["errors"].empty());

    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                       "entity", "get", "name:NoSuchEntity"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 6);
    const auto missing = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(missing["errors"][0]["code"] == "entity.not-found");

    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                       "--timeout", "1", "wait", "state", "playing"}, &out, &err, 5000) == 8);
    const auto timed = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(timed["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(timed["data"]["timedOut"] == true);
    CHECK(timed["errors"].empty());

    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "server",
                       "screenshot", "window"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 2);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(out)["errors"][0]["code"] == "capture.no-renderer");

    out.clear();
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                         "stats", "frame"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    const auto idleBefore = nlohmann::json::parse(out)["data"]["frame"];
    out.clear();
    const int shot = RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                                  "screenshot", "window"}, &out, &err, 60000);
    REQUIRE(shot == 0);
    const auto shotJson = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(shotJson["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(shotJson["command"] == "capture.window");
    out.clear();
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                         "stats", "frame"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(out)["data"]["frame"] == idleBefore);

    auto stepHost = [&](const char* host) {
        std::string before, after, sink;
        REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", host,
                             "stats", "frame"}, &before, &sink, 20000) == 0);
        const auto frame0 = nlohmann::json::parse(before)["data"]["frame"].get<std::uint64_t>();
        std::string stepOut;
        REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", host,
                             "step", "10"}, &stepOut, &sink, 20000) == 0);
        const auto stepped = nlohmann::json::parse(stepOut);
        CHECK(stepped["host"]["frame"].get<std::uint64_t>() - frame0 == 10);
        REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", host,
                             "stats", "frame"}, &after, &sink, 20000) == 0);
        CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(after)["data"]["frame"].get<std::uint64_t>() - frame0 == 10);
    };
    stepHost("runtime");

    // Ruling F34: stop exits 0 and does not move the frame.
    std::string stopBefore, stopAfter, stopSink;
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                         "stats", "frame"}, &stopBefore, &stopSink, 20000) == 0);
    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                       "stop"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(out)["command"] == "play.stop");
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                         "stats", "frame"}, &stopAfter, &stopSink, 20000) == 0);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(stopAfter)["data"]["frame"] == nlohmann::json::parse(stopBefore)["data"]["frame"]);

    stepHost("server");

    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                       "cvar", "get", "render.vsync"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(out)["command"] == "cvar.get");
    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "server",
                       "cvar", "get", kServerProbe}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(out)["command"] == "cvar.get");

    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--all",
                       "cvar", "get", kServerProbe}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    const auto fan = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(fan.contains("hosts"));
    CHECK(fan["hosts"].size() >= 2);

    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "runtime",
                       "entity", "get", "name:MeshCube", "--component", "Transform"},
                 &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "server",
                       "entity", "get", "name:MeshCube", "--component", "Transform"},
                 &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "editor",
                       "problems"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(out)["command"] == "problems.list");
    out.clear();
    CHECK(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "editor",
                       "quit"}, &out, &err, 10000) == 0);
    CHECK(nlohmann::json::parse(out)["command"] == "host.quit");

    // Ruling F23: no host's token appears in ANY servitor.exe output of this case.
    REQUIRE_FALSE(g_servitorTranscript.empty());
    for (const auto& token : tokens)
        CHECK(g_servitorTranscript.find(token) == std::string::npos);

    CHECK(Arcane::RenderErrorCount() == 0);
}
```

`RunServitor` is the next function in this file. It uses `CreateProcessW`, redirected stdout and stderr files, and `WaitForSingleObject`. On timeout it calls `TerminateProcess` and returns 124. It appends one line, `milliseconds command`, to `servitor-latency.txt` beside the test exe, and appends the call's stdout and stderr to `g_servitorTranscript` (ruling F23). `RenderErrorCount()` covers this process. The children's GPU validation is the golden gate in Step 4.

```cpp
static int RunServitor(const std::filesystem::path& servitorExe, const std::vector<std::string>& args,
                  std::string* stdoutText, std::string* stderrText, std::uint32_t timeoutMs) {
    const auto dir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-p0-accept";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(dir);
    const auto outPath = dir / "stdout.txt";
    const auto errPath = dir / "stderr.txt";
    SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES sa{};
    sa.nLength = sizeof(sa);
    sa.bInheritHandle = TRUE;
    HANDLE outFile = CreateFileW(outPath.c_str(), GENERIC_WRITE, FILE_SHARE_READ, &sa,
                                 CREATE_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    HANDLE errFile = CreateFileW(errPath.c_str(), GENERIC_WRITE, FILE_SHARE_READ, &sa,
                                 CREATE_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    std::wstring cmd = L"\"" + servitorExe.wstring() + L"\"";
    for (const auto& a : args) {
        cmd += L" \"";
        cmd += std::wstring(a.begin(), a.end());
        cmd += L"\"";
    }
    STARTUPINFOW si{};
    si.cb = sizeof(si);
    si.dwFlags = STARTF_USESTDHANDLES;
    si.hStdOutput = outFile;
    si.hStdError = errFile;
    si.hStdInput = GetStdHandle(STD_INPUT_HANDLE);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
    std::vector<wchar_t> mutableCmd(cmd.begin(), cmd.end());
    mutableCmd.push_back(L'\0');
    const auto t0 = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    if (!CreateProcessW(servitorExe.c_str(), mutableCmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, TRUE, 0,
                        nullptr, servitorExe.parent_path().c_str(), &si, &pi)) {
        CloseHandle(outFile);
        CloseHandle(errFile);
        return 125;
    }
    CloseHandle(outFile);
    CloseHandle(errFile);
    const DWORD wait = WaitForSingleObject(pi.hProcess, timeoutMs);
    int code = 124;
    if (wait == WAIT_OBJECT_0) {
        DWORD exitCode = 124;
        GetExitCodeProcess(pi.hProcess, &exitCode);
        code = static_cast<int>(exitCode);
    } else {
        TerminateProcess(pi.hProcess, 124);
    }
    CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    CloseHandle(pi.hProcess);
    const auto ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - t0).count();
    std::ofstream latency("remote-latency.txt", std::ios::app);
    latency << ms;
    for (const auto& a : args) latency << ' ' << a;
    latency << '\n';
    std::string capturedOut;
    std::string capturedErr;
    {
        std::ifstream in(outPath);
        capturedOut = std::string(std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(in), {});
    }
    {
        std::ifstream in(errPath);
        capturedErr = std::string(std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(in), {});
    }
    g_servitorTranscript += capturedOut;   // every call, success or failure (ruling F23)
    g_servitorTranscript += capturedErr;
    if (stdoutText)
        *stdoutText = capturedOut;
    if (stderrText)
        *stderrText = capturedErr;
    return code;
}
```

`--hang-main` is a second `TEST_CASE` in the same file:

```cpp
TEST_CASE("a hung runtime is exit 7", "[remote][gpu]") {
    const auto bin = std::filesystem::current_path().parent_path();
    const auto repo = bin.parent_path().parent_path();
    const auto project = repo / "ReferenceProject";
    const auto runtime = bin / "ArcaneRuntime" / "ArcaneRuntime.exe";
    const auto servitor = bin / "Servitor" / "servitor.exe";
    DescriptorSweep saved{project / "Saved" / "Remote"};
    std::wstring cmd = L"\"" + runtime.wstring() + L"\" --headless --project \"" + project.wstring()
        + L"\" --remote --hang-main 60";
    STARTUPINFOW si{};
    si.cb = sizeof(si);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
    std::vector<wchar_t> mutableCmd(cmd.begin(), cmd.end());
    mutableCmd.push_back(L'\0');
    HostGuard hosts;
    REQUIRE(CreateProcessW(runtime.c_str(), mutableCmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, 0,
                           nullptr, runtime.parent_path().c_str(), &si, &pi));
    hosts.runtime = pi.hProcess;
    CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    bool sawHung = false;
    const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(90);
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline) {
        std::string statusOut, statusErr;
        const int statusCode = RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "status"},
                                      &statusOut, &statusErr, 5000);
        if (statusCode == 0) {
            const auto status = nlohmann::json::parse(statusOut, nullptr, false);
            if (!status.is_discarded() && status.contains("data") && status["data"].contains("hosts")
                && status["data"]["hosts"].is_array()) {
                for (const auto& host : status["data"]["hosts"]) {
                    if (host.value("hostKind", "") == "runtime"
                        && host.contains("pid")
                        && host["pid"] == static_cast<std::uint32_t>(pi.dwProcessId)
                        && host.value("hung", false) == true) {
                        sawHung = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if (sawHung) break;
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(250));
    }
    REQUIRE(sawHung);
    const auto callStart = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
    std::string out, err;
    const int code = RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json",
                                  "--host", "runtime", "--pid", std::to_string(pi.dwProcessId),
                                  "--timeout", "10", "cvar", "get", "render.vsync"},
                            &out, &err, 15000);
    CHECK(code == 7);
    if (code == 7) {
        const auto body = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
        CHECK(body["errors"][0]["code"] == "host.hung");
        CHECK(body["client"]["exitCode"] == 7);
        if (body["errors"][0]["detail"].contains("arcdiag")) {
            const auto path = std::filesystem::path(body["errors"][0]["detail"]["arcdiag"].get<std::string>());
            CHECK(std::filesystem::exists(path));
            CHECK(path.extension() == ".arcdiag");
            // file_clock::to_sys, as Task 14 does; never clock_cast (ruling S3-8, R8-7).
            const auto mtime = std::chrono::file_clock::to_sys(std::filesystem::last_write_time(path));
            CHECK(mtime > callStart);
        }
    }
    DWORD procCode = 0;
    REQUIRE(GetExitCodeProcess(hosts.runtime, &procCode));
    CHECK(procCode == STILL_ACTIVE);
}
```

The live server `--frames` check (ruling F22; ruling S3-3 keeps Task 29's unit model as documentation and makes this case the proof) is a third `TEST_CASE` in the same file. N is 1200 (20 s at 60 Hz), so the descriptor wait and `servitor pause` land long before the budget is spent; the case also asserts that the paused frame is below N. `pause`, `step 3` and `resume` must each exit 0, and `step 3` must move `host.frame` by exactly 3:

```cpp
TEST_CASE("server --frames 1200 ticks exactly 1200 across a remote pause, step and resume", "[remote][gpu]") {
    const auto bin = std::filesystem::current_path().parent_path();
    const auto repo = bin.parent_path().parent_path();
    const auto project = repo / "ReferenceProject";
    const auto server = bin / "ArcaneServer" / "ArcaneServer.exe";
    const auto servitor = bin / "Servitor" / "servitor.exe";
    constexpr std::uint64_t kFrames = 1200;
    DescriptorSweep saved{project / "Saved" / "Remote"};
    {
        std::error_code ec;
        std::filesystem::remove_all(saved.dir, ec);
    }
    const auto scratch = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "remote-server-frames";
    std::filesystem::remove_all(scratch);
    std::filesystem::create_directories(scratch);
    const auto report = scratch / "server-report.json";
    std::wstring cmd = L"\"" + server.wstring() + L"\" --project \"" + project.wstring() +
        L"\" --frames 1200 --report \"" + report.wstring() + L"\" --remote";
    STARTUPINFOW si{};
    si.cb = sizeof(si);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
    std::vector<wchar_t> mutableCmd(cmd.begin(), cmd.end());
    mutableCmd.push_back(L'\0');
    HostGuard hosts;
    REQUIRE(CreateProcessW(server.c_str(), mutableCmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, 0,
                           nullptr, server.parent_path().c_str(), &si, &pi));
    hosts.server = pi.hProcess;
    CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    const std::string pid = std::to_string(pi.dwProcessId);

    bool live = false;
    const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(60);
    while (!live && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline) {
        std::string statusOut, statusErr;
        if (RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "status"},
                   &statusOut, &statusErr, 5000) == 0) {
            const auto status = nlohmann::json::parse(statusOut, nullptr, false);
            if (!status.is_discarded() && status.contains("data") && status["data"].contains("hosts")) {
                for (const auto& host : status["data"]["hosts"]) {
                    if (host.value("hostKind", "") == "server"
                        && host["pid"] == static_cast<std::uint32_t>(pi.dwProcessId))
                        live = true;
                }
            }
        }
        if (!live)
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(100));
    }
    REQUIRE(live);

    std::string out, err;
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "server",
                         "--pid", pid, "pause"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    const auto pausedFrame = nlohmann::json::parse(out)["host"]["frame"].get<std::uint64_t>();
    REQUIRE(pausedFrame < kFrames);                       // the pause landed before the budget was spent
    out.clear();
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "server",
                         "--pid", pid, "step", "3"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);
    const auto stepped = nlohmann::json::parse(out);
    CHECK(stepped["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(stepped["host"]["frame"].get<std::uint64_t>() - pausedFrame == 3);
    out.clear();
    REQUIRE(RunServitor(servitor, {"--project", project.string(), "--format", "json", "--host", "server",
                         "--pid", pid, "resume"}, &out, &err, 20000) == 0);

    REQUIRE(WaitForSingleObject(hosts.server, 60000) == WAIT_OBJECT_0);
    DWORD exitCode = 1;
    REQUIRE(GetExitCodeProcess(hosts.server, &exitCode));
    CHECK(exitCode == 0);
    std::ifstream in(report);
    const auto rep = nlohmann::json::parse(in, nullptr, false);
    REQUIRE(rep.is_object());
    REQUIRE(rep.contains("framesTicked"));
    CHECK(rep["framesTicked"].get<std::uint64_t>() == kFrames);   // the pause spent none of the budget
}
```

`--crash-gpu` follows the G1 gate. It is not an unconditional `SKIP`. An agent must not set `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK`.

```cpp
TEST_CASE("crash-gpu is the desk-only TDR lane", "[remote][gpu]") {
    if (!std::getenv("ARCANE_DIAG_DESK"))
        SKIP("ARCANE_DIAG_DESK not set -- desk-only TDR lane");
    const auto bin = std::filesystem::current_path().parent_path();
    const auto repo = bin.parent_path().parent_path();
    const auto project = repo / "ReferenceProject";
    const auto runtime = bin / "ArcaneRuntime" / "ArcaneRuntime.exe";
    std::wstring cmd = L"\"" + runtime.wstring() + L"\" --headless --project \"" + project.wstring() +
        L"\" --frames 60 --crash-gpu 30";
    STARTUPINFOW si{};
    si.cb = sizeof(si);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
    std::vector<wchar_t> mutableCmd(cmd.begin(), cmd.end());
    mutableCmd.push_back(L'\0');
    HostGuard hosts;
    REQUIRE(CreateProcessW(runtime.c_str(), mutableCmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, 0,
                           nullptr, runtime.parent_path().c_str(), &si, &pi));
    hosts.runtime = pi.hProcess;
    CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    const DWORD wait = WaitForSingleObject(hosts.runtime, 120000);
    DWORD exitCode = 0;
    GetExitCodeProcess(hosts.runtime, &exitCode);
    CHECK(wait == WAIT_OBJECT_0);
    CHECK(exitCode == 1);
}
```

The resource test in Task 24 has no `SKIP`. This case skips only when `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK` is unset.

`HostGuard`'s destructor is what kills the children. Catch2 `REQUIRE` aborts by throwing, so a forced exception leaving the scope is the same cleanup. This case throws `std::runtime_error` rather than `REQUIRE(false)`, so the suite stays green, and it checks the three sleeper processes have exited. It does not start the editor, the runtime, or the server, and it does not read `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK`. `DuplicateHandle` runs before the throw because `~HostGuard` closes the original handles.

```cpp
TEST_CASE("the host guard kills children when a REQUIRE aborts", "[remote][accept]") {
    HANDLE watchEditor = nullptr;
    HANDLE watchRuntime = nullptr;
    HANDLE watchServer = nullptr;
    bool threw = false;
    try {
        HostGuard guard;
        guard.editor = SpawnSleeper();
        guard.runtime = SpawnSleeper();
        guard.server = SpawnSleeper();
        watchEditor = DuplicateProcess(guard.editor);
        watchRuntime = DuplicateProcess(guard.runtime);
        watchServer = DuplicateProcess(guard.server);
        throw std::runtime_error("forced failure");
    } catch (const std::runtime_error&) {
        threw = true;
    }
    REQUIRE(threw);
    for (HANDLE h : {watchEditor, watchRuntime, watchServer}) {
        REQUIRE(h != nullptr);
        const DWORD wait = WaitForSingleObject(h, 5000);
        CHECK(wait == WAIT_OBJECT_0);
        DWORD exitCode = STILL_ACTIVE;
        CHECK(GetExitCodeProcess(h, &exitCode));
        CHECK(exitCode != STILL_ACTIVE);
        CloseHandle(h);
    }
}
#endif // defined(_WIN32) && !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)
```

The `#endif` closes the `#if defined(_WIN32) && !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)` that opens the file. The `[remote][accept]` guard case is untagged `[gpu]`, so the Windows `~[gpu]` lane runs it. The Linux and macOS lanes never see it.
